FILE: .impeccable.md (7.1 KB)

# PDF Master — design direction

**Direction:** Apple macOS Sequoia / iPadOS Freeform & Preview precision glass studio.
**One line:** the page is the hero; every control is a translucent instrument floating a few millimetres above it, and nothing competes with the document.

This file is the contract for anything visual. If a change does not fit here, change this file first.

---

## 1. Personality

| Trait | It means | It never means |
|---|---|---|
| **Precise** | Millimetre truth, crisp 1 px borders, tabular numbers, calibrated scales. | Clinical, grey, spreadsheet. |
| **Calm** | One accent colour, quiet motion, generous space around the sheet. | Empty, low contrast, hiding controls. |
| **Tactile** | Glass, soft inner light, paper-like shadows, real hover/press states. | Skeuomorphic leather, fake stitching, embossed bevels. |
| **Confident** | Controls sit where a professional's hand expects them; labels are verbs. | Shouting, badges everywhere, decoration as hierarchy. |
| **Alive** | Depth comes from light (glows, blur, saturation), not from more boxes. | Neon, animation for its own sake. |

Voice: short, literal, no exclamation marks. "Save as stamp", not "Save your stamp!". Units and numbers are shown as values (`1:50`, `300 DPI`), never explained in prose inside a control.

---

## 2. Spatial system

- **Frame:** 8 px outer margin around every surface. Panels are `m-2`, corners `rounded-2xl` (16 px).
- **Radii:** 16 px panels, 18 px tool rail, 12 px pills/fields, 10 px icon buttons, 999 px chips, 2 px paper (paper is paper).
- **Density:** 26 px chips, 28–32 px icon buttons, 34 px pills, 56 px top bar, 13 px base text (11.5 px in chips, 10.5 px in status metadata).
- **Spacing:** 4 px grid; 6 px between icon buttons, 8 px between groups, 12 px inside panels.
- **Elevation:** three steps only — `--shadow-soft` (hover), `--shadow` (floating chrome), `--paper-shadow` (the sheet). Never invent a fourth.

---

## 3. Colour

Three themes, one token set. Accent is system blue in light/dark, burnt sienna in sepia; nothing else is allowed to be saturated.

| Token | Light | Dark | Sepia | Use |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--bg` | `#dfe5ef` | `#090b10` | `#efe4cf` | App backdrop |
| `--glass` | `rgba(255,255,255,.58)` | dark glass | warm glass | Panels, toolbars |
| `--glass-strong` | `.78` | stronger | stronger | Popovers, floating bars |
| `--fg` | `#14161b` | near-white | `#3a2f22` | Text |
| `--muted` | `#6a7280` | grey | `#8a775e` | Secondary text, idle icons |
| `--border` | 10 % ink | 2–3 % white | 14 % ink | Every hairline |
| `--accent` | `#0a84ff` | `#0a84ff` | `#b0552a` | Selection, active tool, focus |
| `--paper` | `#fffdf8` | `#f7f7f5` | `#fffaf0` | The document sheet |
| `--danger` / `--success` | `#ff453a` / `#30d158` | same | `#b3261e` / `#4c7a34` | Destructive / confirmation |

Rules:
1. Max **one** accent-filled surface in view at a time (active tool, or primary button).
2. Ink for annotation colour comes from the document's palette, never from the chrome palette.
3. Glows (`--glow-1..3`) are ambient light behind the sheet. They never sit on a control.
4. Text on glass must clear 4.5:1; `--muted` is for metadata, not for the only label of a control.
5. `color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 24%, transparent)` is the standard accent tint — do not hand-pick new alphas.

---

## 4. Type

- Family: `Inter Variable`, fallback SF Pro / Segoe UI. No second family, no display serif.
- Scale: 10.5 metadata · 11.5 chips/labels · 13 body · 15 panel titles · 18 dialog titles. No 20+ except empty-state headings.
- Weight: 500 for controls, 600 for titles, 400 for prose. Never 700+.
- Numbers that move (page count, zoom, measurements, dimensions) use tabular figures.
- Labels are sentence case. Micro-labels may be ALL CAPS at 10 px with 0.06 em tracking — maximum two per panel.

---

## 5. Light, glass, paper

- Glass = translucent fill + 1 px `--border` + `--shadow` + `inset 0 1px 0 var(--edge)` + `backdrop-filter: blur(26px) saturate(170%)` (`34px` for `.glass-strong`).
- Blur is for chrome over the canvas. Never blur the paper or anything inside it.
- The sheet gets `--paper-shadow` and a 2 px radius; it is the only object allowed a large shadow.
- Paper recipes (129 parametric styles) are drawn as SVG ink, never as background images.
- No gradients on controls. Gradients belong to the ambient glows and the app icon only.

---

## 6. Motion

- Durations: 120–160 ms for state, 220–260 ms for entrances (`anim-in`), 0 ms for measurement readouts.
- Easing: `ease` / `ease-out`. No bounce, no spring, no overshoot on chrome.
- What moves: panel entrance (opacity + 6 px rise), toast, popover origin, selection handles. What does not: the sheet during zoom (transform only), ink while drawing, text while typing.
- `prefers-reduced-motion` collapses everything to 0.01 ms (already enforced globally).
- Print is motionless: `@media print` hides `.top-bar`, `.sidebar`, `.tool-rail`, `.inspector`, `.status-line`, `.selection-chrome`, `.snapshot-bar`, `.crop-overlay`, `.presentation-bar` and flattens `.viewport`, `.page-stage`, `.page-paper`.

---

## 7. Component grammar

- **Icon button:** Lucide, 16 px, `strokeWidth 1.75`, `--muted` → `--fg` on hover, accent tint + accent icon when `aria-pressed`.
- **Chip:** pill, 26 px, transparent with hairline; active state is accent tint, never solid accent.
- **Segmented control:** track (`--track`) with a 24 px thumb per option; the chosen option is the only filled child.
- **Tool rail:** vertical glass column, groups separated by a 24 px hairline, tooltip to the right with name + shortcut.
- **Panels:** header row (icon + title + close), scroll body, footer actions. Hairlines between regions, never cards inside cards.
- **Popovers:** `.glass-strong`, 16 px radius, 8 px offset from trigger, arrowless.
- **Toast:** bottom centre, 10 px from the edge, auto-dismiss, one line, no icon except on failure.
- **Focus:** 2 px `--accent` outline at 2 px offset on `:focus-visible` only.

Every icon-only control carries `aria-label` and `title`. Every toggle carries `aria-pressed`.

---

## 8. Anti-patterns (reject on sight)

- More than one accent colour, or accent used for decoration.
- Solid opaque panels on the canvas; a second card inside a panel.
- Pure `#000` text or `#fff` panels in light theme; pure `#fff` text in dark theme.
- Drop shadows on text, borders on borders, or radii mixed inside one row.
- Bouncy springs, blur-in animations, skeleton shimmer.
- Sentence-length helper text inside a toolbar. Help belongs in tooltips, empty states, or Settings.
- New bespoke button/input classes when `.chip`, `.icon-btn`, `.pill`, `.seg` already cover it.
- Hiding the page behind chrome; the sheet must always keep at least 60 % of the canvas width at 1440 px.

---

## 9. Review checklist

1. Does it read at a glance in all three themes?
2. Is the page still the brightest, largest object?
3. One accent, one shadow step, one radius family?
4. Keyboard reachable, labelled, focus-visible?
5. Does it survive `@media print` and `prefers-reduced-motion`?
6. Would removing it lose information? If not, remove it.

FILE: PDFMaster-Desktop.cmd (297 B)
================================================================================
@echo off
rem PDF Master desktop launcher: builds if needed, serves the production bundle
rem and opens it in a frameless Edge/Chrome app window.
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0"
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0scripts\launch-desktop.ps1" %*
if errorlevel 1 pause
endlocal

================================================================================
FILE: README.md (17.0 KB)
================================================================================
# PDF Master

A precision glass studio for PDF and paper annotation: vector ink, shapes, text, LaTeX formulas, calibrated measurements, 464 stamps, snapshots, cropping, bookmarks and presentation mode — in the browser, as an installed PWA, or as a desktop window.

PDF Master is a ground-up rebuild of the behaviour and data model of **PDF Annotator 10.0.0.1011**, replacing its Delphi VCL + PDF-XChange stack with React 19, PDF.js and pdf-lib. The design direction is defined in [`.impeccable.md`](./.impeccable.md).

---

## Quick start

```bash
npm install
npm run dev        # http://localhost:5173
npm run build      # type-check + production bundle in dist/
npm run preview    # serve dist/ on http://localhost:4173
npm test           # 79 unit tests
```

Requires Node 20+ (developed on Node 26, Windows 11).

---

## Feature tour

### Annotation tools

| Tool | Shortcut | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| Select | `V` | Select, move, resize, rotate, flip, align; `Ctrl+A` selects the page, `Esc` clears. |
| Lasso | `Q` | Freeform selection over ink and shapes. |
| Pen | `P` | Pressure-sensitive vector ink; snapping, scratch-out erase, ink-on-text. |
| Highlighter | `M` | Broad marker with its own property set (never synchronised with the pen). |
| Text | `T` | In-place text boxes with fill, frame and type styles; `Ctrl+B/I/U`, `Ctrl+M` fit. |
| Line / Arrow | `L` / `A` | 15° snapping with `Shift`, 10 arrowhead and tail shapes. |
| Rectangle / Ellipse | `R` / `O` | Fill, opacity and dash styles; `Shift` for square/circle. |
| Polygon | `G` | Click to add points, `Enter` to close. |
| Dimension | `D` | Distance or area with extension lines, editable scale and unit. |
| Stamp | `S` | 464 factory stamps plus your own; drag, resize, rotate, flip. |
| Eraser | `E` | Whole annotation or split strokes; rapid scribble erases ink underneath. |
| Measure | `U` | Distance, perimeter, area, angle, with calibration scales and unit conversion. |
| Formula | `F` | LaTeX via KaTeX, live preview, editable after insertion. |
| Crop | `C` | Crop the visible page box. |
| Snapshot | `Ctrl+Shift+S` | Copy a region, download it, or save it as a custom stamp. |
| Image | `I` | Place PNG, JPEG, WebP or SVG; paste from the clipboard. |
| Hand | `H` | Pan; hold `Space` from any tool. |

### Documents, pages and storage

- Multi-document tabs, per-tab page lists, reorder/duplicate/rotate/delete pages, per-page rotation and crop.
- 129 parametric paper recipes (Blank, Ruled, Squared, Millimetre, Grid) for A4 and Letter, drawn as SVG ink — not images.
- Autosave to IndexedDB, manual `.pmbackup.json` backup (version 2) with tabs, pages, annotations **and** custom stamps, scales, shortcuts, preferences and theme.
- Editable PDF export writes real vector ink, text and shapes plus a `PDFMasterAnnotations` sidecar; flattened export bakes everything into the page tree.
- The page tree is rebuilt on export, so page order, duplicates, crops and rotation survive the round trip.
- Bookmarks and the custom outline are read from imported PDFs and kept in the sidecar across an export/reopen cycle.

### Presentation and output

- Presentation mode (`Ctrl+Shift+P`): full-screen sheet, minimal bottom bar, page stepping.
- Print (`Ctrl+P`, top bar, command palette) prints the active sheet through `@media print`, hiding all chrome.
- PWA manifest with `display: standalone`, `window-controls-overlay` and an inline SVG icon.
- Desktop launch contract compatible with the original: `?new=A4+Landscape&fullscreen=1&print=1`.

---

## PDF Annotator 10 → PDF Master

### Why a rebuild

Phase 1 reconnaissance of the installed product found four stacks glued into one process: a Delphi VCL + DevExpress shell, the closed-source **PDF-XChange Editor SDK 10.8.4.409** (`PDFXEditSimple.x64.dll`, 41.5 MB), **ImageEn 8.1.5.0** for a second imaging path, and an MPRESS-packed executable. The whole application is 93.9 MB across 18 files, with an **8.4 MB XML file** holding 617 tool presets, and annotations persisted as **transparent raster images** at 72–1200 DPI. That is why saving is slow, files bloat, quality depends on a save-time DPI setting, and "melting" annotations exists as a separate command.

PDF Master keeps what was worth keeping — the tool model, presets, stamps, paper recipes and shortcut contract — and replaces the rest with web technology: vector-first, no install-time binary blob, no per-save rasterisation.

### Side-by-side

| Dimension | PDF Annotator 10.0.0.1011 | PDF Master |
|---|---|---|
| Platform | Windows only (x86/x64 native) | Browser, installable PWA, Windows desktop window (Tauri v2) |
| UI stack | Delphi VCL + DevExpress, ~690 bitmap glyphs | React 19 + Tailwind 4, Lucide icons, CSS glass tokens |
| PDF engine | PDF-XChange Editor SDK 10.8.4.409 (closed, GDI) | PDF.js 6 (render) + pdf-lib 1.17 (write), both open |
| Annotation model | Editable sidecar data, saved as transparent images | Vector geometry + images, kept as annotation data and written as vector PDF content |
| Save quality | Fixed by the chosen DPI (72/150/300/600/1200) | Zoom-independent vectors; images at source resolution |
| Install footprint | 93.9 MB, 18 files + language modules | 2.0 MB of JavaScript across 9 chunks (564 kB gzip); 6.9 MB with KaTeX/Inter webfonts |
| Tool presets | 617 presets in an 8.67 MB XML parsed at startup | Same 617 presets and 21 groups, bundled as JSON and split from app code |
| Stamps | 464 GIF stamps hex-encoded inside the XML | 464 PNG stamps in a lazy catalogue chunk, plus user stamps |
| Paper templates | 129 templates in `PAStyleTemplates.xml` | 129 parametric recipes as JSON, drawn as SVG |
| Text | Text boxes rasterised on save | Real PDF text with the same layout model on screen and in export |
| Formulas | LaTeX as ink | KaTeX rendered to PNG at export (same on-screen result) |
| Measurement | Distance/perimeter/area/angle, scale manager | Same four modes, calibrated scale store, unit conversion |
| Persistence | PDF + private editable data; "melt" required | Editable PDF with a `PDFMasterAnnotations` sidecar, or flattened PDF |
| Undo | Per-document history | Ctrl+Z/Ctrl+Y over pages, annotations and document state |
| Themes | Windows/skin based (PA_DARK / PA_LIGHT / PA_W11) | Light, dark and sepia glass themes |
| Shortcuts | Rebindable with conflict detection | Rebindable with conflict detection, per-command reset, backup included |
| Command line | `PDFAnnotator.exe [file] [/print] [/new:"A4 Landscape"] [/fullscreen]` | Same contract as URL query or arguments, via browser or Tauri |
| Updates | Bundled update wizard | Web deployment; desktop build rebuilt from source |

### What carried over

- Tool families, property semantics (mm, opacity, dash, arrowheads), and the "highlighter never syncs" rule.
- Default shortcuts (`Ctrl+O/S/P`, tool letters, `Shift` modifiers) and the rebindable keymap.
- 129 paper recipes, 617 presets, 464 stamps, 21 groups — migrated in Phase 2, not recreated by hand.
- Print, presentation, page operations, bookmarks, measurement scales and the launch contract.
- The visual goal: a professional annotation desk, not a generic web form.

---

## Keyboard shortcuts

| Scope | Shortcut → action |
|---|---|
| File | `Ctrl+O` open · `Ctrl+S` save editable PDF · `Ctrl+P` print |
| Tools | `H` hand · `V` select · `Q` lasso · `P` pen · `M` highlighter · `T` text · `L` line · `A` arrow · `R` rectangle · `O` ellipse · `G` polygon · `D` dimension · `S` stamp · `E` eraser · `U` measure · `F` formula · `C` crop · `Ctrl+Shift+S` snapshot · `I` image |
| View | `Ctrl+K` command palette · `Ctrl+,` settings · `Ctrl+Shift+P` presentation · `0` fit page |
| Edit | `Ctrl+Z` undo · `Ctrl+Y` redo · `Ctrl+A` select page · `Delete` remove · arrow keys nudge |
| Text editing | `Ctrl+B/I/U` bold/italic/underline · `Ctrl+M` fit box · `Ctrl+Enter` commit · `Esc` cancel |
| Modifiers | `Shift` 15° snap / square / circle · `Space` temporary pan · `Esc` cancel polygon/measure |

Every binding is editable in **Settings → Shortcuts**, with duplicate detection and per-action reset.

---

## Desktop builds

### 1. Zero-dependency launcher (no Rust, no install)

`PDFMaster-Desktop.cmd` → `scripts/launch-desktop.ps1`:

- builds `dist/` when missing or stale (or pass `-Rebuild`),
- serves it with Vite preview on port 4173,
- opens it in a **frameless Edge/Chrome `--app` window** at 1440×900 with its own profile (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\PDFMaster\DesktopProfile`),
- closes the preview server when the window exits.

No address bar, no tabs, no Node prompt. Windows only, requires Edge or Chrome.

### 2. Tauri v2 shell (native installer)

Verified with Rust 1.98.1 (`cargo check` finishes clean).

```bash
winget install Rustlang.Rustup      # once, then reopen the terminal
npm install
npm run build
npx @tauri-apps/cli dev             # development window (loads Vite on 5173)
npx @tauri-apps/cli build           # MSI/NSIS installer + executable
```

`src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`:

- product name **PDF Master**, identifier **com.pdfmaster.desktop**,
- 1440×900 window, minimum 1024×680, centred, resizable, drag-and-drop enabled,
- `frontendDist: ../dist`, `devUrl: http://localhost:5173`,
- bundle target `all` with 32/128/256 px PNG, `.ico` and `.icns` icons.

`src-tauri/src/main.rs` forwards arguments (`--new "A4 Landscape" --fullscreen --print`) into the same query contract the web build understands. Icons are generated from the app mark with `python scripts/make_icons.py` (Pillow); rerun it after changing the logo.

---

## Launch contract (browser and desktop)

| Intent | URL | Arguments |
|---|---|---|
| New paper, portrait | `?new=A4` | `--new "A4"` or `/new:"A4"` |
| New paper, landscape | `?new=A4+Landscape` | `--new "A4 Landscape"` |
| Presentation | `?fullscreen=1` | `--fullscreen` |
| Print on open | `?print=1` | `--print` |

- Paper names resolve to the least busy matching recipe (`Blank` → `Ruled` → `Squared` → `Grid`); A4 has a Blank recipe, Letter resolves to a Letter ruled/squared recipe.
- Flags accept `1/true/yes/on`; `0`/`no` keep them off. Empty values are ignored.
- Both spellings (`--flag`, `/flag`, `flag=value`) are parsed by the same tested parser (`src/lib/bootArgs.ts`).

---

## Architecture

### Stack

| Layer | Libraries |
|---|---|
| UI | React 19, Tailwind CSS 4, Lucide icons, Inter Variable |
| State | Zustand 5 stores (documents, tools, annotations, UI) |
| PDF read | PDF.js 6 (`pdfjs-dist`) |
| PDF write | pdf-lib 1.17 |
| Ink | `perfect-freehand` + custom vector paths |
| Formulas | KaTeX |
| Build | Vite 8, TypeScript 7 |
| Tests | Vitest 5 (unit), Playwright/Chromium (headless E2E, run ad hoc) |

### Directory map

```
src/
  App.tsx                  shell: layout, shortcut router, boot args, autosave, export
  components/
    TopBar, Sidebar, ToolBar, Inspector, StatusLine, ZoomPill
    CommandPalette, SettingsModal, NewDocumentModal, StampGallery, PresetGallery
    PresentationBar, ThemeSwitcher
    canvas/
      PaperViewport        pan/zoom/fit, page stage
      PaperSheet           SVG paper recipe
      PdfBackground        rasterised source page
      AnnotationLayer      drawing, selection, snapshot, tools
      SelectionOverlay     handles and mini action bar (Save as stamp, …)
      TextView/TextEditor, FormulaView/FormulaEditor
      MeasureOverlay, CropOverlay, SnapshotBar
    ui/controls.tsx        shared chips, switches, segmented controls
  stores/                  useDocStore, useToolStore, useAnnotationStore, useUIStore
  lib/
    pdfEngine              inspect, render, export, sidecar (encode/load)
    annotationTypes, annotationGeometry, textLayout, ink, paper, pageUtils
    presets, data, tools, sync, images, raster, formula
    measure, customStamps, prefs, shortcuts, bootArgs
    storage               IndexedDB autosave, v1/v2 workspace backups
  data/                    617 presets, 464 stamps, 129 paper recipes, 21 groups
src-tauri/                 Tauri v2 shell (config, capabilities, icons, Rust entry)
scripts/                   launch-desktop.ps1, make_icons.py, conversion tooling
public/                    manifest.webmanifest, icon.svg, stamp assets
```

### Data flow

1. **Open** — PDF.js renders the source page into a canvas; `inspectPdf` reads page sizes and outline entries; the sidecar is restored if present.
2. **Annotate** — the SVG annotation layer keeps geometry in page millimetres; zoom scales the wrapper, never the data.
3. **Autosave** — documents and annotations are written to IndexedDB; preference stores (stamps, scales, shortcuts, prefs, theme) live in `localStorage`.
4. **Export** — `exportPdf` rebuilds the page tree, draws paper, annotations and rotation, then embeds either the editable sidecar or flattened content.
5. **Backup** — `workspaceBackup` collects documents plus every preference store into a version 2 `.pmbackup.json`.

### Persistence

| Layer | Key / file | Holds |
|---|---|---|
| IndexedDB `pdf-master/workspace/current` | `current` | tabs, pages, annotations, active tab |
| IndexedDB `pdf-master/pdf-blobs` | tab id | imported PDF bytes, kept out of the structural save |
| `localStorage` | `pm.customStamps`, `pm.measure.scales`, `pm.measure.active`, `pm.shortcuts`, `pm.prefs`, `pa.theme` | studio preferences |
| PDF sidecar | `/PDFMasterAnnotations` info key, `pm1:` + base64 JSON | page map, annotations, bookmarks |
| Workspace backup | `*.pmbackup.json` (version 2) | everything above |

Version 1 backups (tabs only) still import; missing stores are left untouched.

## Testing and verification

| Check | Command | Result |
|---|---|---|
| Type-check | `npx tsc -b` | clean |
| Unit tests | `npm test` | 14 files, 79 tests passing |
| Production build | `npm run build` | clean, no warnings, 997 ms |
| Tauri shell | `cargo check` (in `src-tauri`) | `Finished dev profile` |
| Headless E2E | Chromium via Playwright | 28/28 checks, 0 console/page errors |

The E2E pass covers: PWA manifest, drawing ink, selection → save as stamp, stamp-tool switch, v2 backup export, backup import restoring stamps/scales/shortcuts/preferences/theme, `Ctrl+P` firing print, `@media print` hiding the chrome, `?new=Letter+Landscape&fullscreen=1` boot, and `?print=1`.

Unit coverage includes annotation geometry, text layout, ink paths, measurement conversion, shortcut normalisation/conflicts, PDF export and sidecar round-trips (including bookmarks), boot-argument parsing, workspace backup round-trips, and store-level undo/selection.

---

## Known limitations

- **Formula export is raster.** KaTeX depends on fonts/styles that do not load inside the isolated SVG raster document; native MathML renders at the cost of a PNG. Text stays vector.
- **Bookmarks are sidecar-only.** pdf-lib has no outline API, so custom bookmarks survive reopen and export but are not written as `/Outlines`; other readers see the original outline only.
- **Letter has no Blank recipe** in the migrated data; `?new=Letter` resolves to a ruled or squared Letter sheet.
- **Print uses the DOM print pipeline**, so hidden PDF canvas fragments that have not painted print blank; pages are rasterised at a 2× floor to keep them sharp.
- **Crop handles assume an unrotated viewer.** 0°/180° are exact; 90°/270° use the rotated screen box and can feel slightly off at high zoom.
- **Snapshot capture is best-effort** for PDF pages that have not painted yet.
- **Desktop windows are Chromium** (WebView2 for Tauri, Edge/Chrome for the launcher); Safari/Firefox are untested.
- **Stamp catalogue is one ~569 kB chunk** (59 kB gzip); the Vite warning limit is raised to 600 kB rather than lazy-loading synchronous stamp lookup everywhere.

ib/raster.ts","./src/lib/save.test.ts","./src/lib/save.ts","./src/lib/shortcuts.test.ts","./src/lib/shortcuts.ts","./src/lib/storage.test.ts","./src/lib/storage.ts","./src/lib/sync.test.ts","./src/lib/sync.ts","./src/lib/textlayout.ts","./src/lib/tools.ts","./src/lib/types.ts","./src/stores/annotationstore.test.ts","./src/stores/useannotationstore.ts","./src/stores/usedocstore.test.ts","./src/stores/usedocstore.ts","./src/stores/usefavoritesstore.test.ts","./src/stores/usefavoritesstore.ts","./src/stores/usetoolstore.ts","./src/stores/useuistore.ts"],"packageJsons":["./node_modules/@fontsource-variable/inter/package.json","./node_modules/@pdf-lib/standard-fonts/package.json","./node_modules/@types/chai/package.json","./node_modules/@types/deep-eql/package.json","./node_modules/@types/react-dom/package.json","./node_modules/@types/react/package.json","./node_modules/@vitest/mocker/package.json","./node_modules/assertion-error/package.json","./node_modules/chai/package.json","./node_modules/csstype/package.json","./node_modules/expect-type/package.json","./node_modules/jiti/package.json","./node_modules/katex/package.json","./node_modules/lucide-react/package.json","./node_modules/pdf-lib/package.json","./node_modules/pdfjs-dist/package.json","./node_modules/perfect-freehand/package.json","./node_modules/react-dom/package.json","./node_modules/react/package.json","./node_modules/tinybench/package.json","./node_modules/vite/package.json","./node_modules/vitest/package.json","./node_modules/zustand/package.json","./package.json"],"missingPackageJsons":["./node_modules/@edge-runtime/vm/package.json","./node_modules/@opentelemetry/api/package.json","./node_modules/@pdf-lib/standard-fonts/lib/package.json","./node_modules/@types/katex/dist/katex.min.css/package.json","./node_modules/@types/katex/package.json","./node_modules/@types/node/package.json","./node_modules/@types/pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.min.mjs?url/package.json","./node_modules/@types/pdfjs-dist/package.json" import { defineConfig } from "vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import tailwindcss from "@tailwindcss/vite";

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  server: { port: 5173 },
  build: {
    // The stamp catalogue is a single synchronous JSON payload (569 kB, 59 kB gzip); it is split
    // from the app code but cannot be split further without lazy-loading stamp lookup everywhere.
    chunkSizeWarningLimit: 600,
    rollupOptions: {
      output: {
        manualChunks(id) {
          if (id.includes("node_modules")) {
            if (id.includes("pdfjs-dist")) return "pdfjs";
            if (id.includes("pdf-lib")) return "pdf-lib";
            if (id.includes("katex")) return "katex";
            if (id.includes("lucide-react")) return "icons";
            if (id.includes("react")) return "react";
            return "vendor";
          }
          if (/[\\/]src[\\/]lib[\\/]data\.ts$/.test(id)) return "stamp-data";
          return undefined;
        },
      },
    },
  },
});

FILE: src\App.tsx (13.9 KB)

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { CommandPalette } from "./components/CommandPalette";
import { FavoriteToolsBar } from "./components/FavoriteToolsBar";
import { Inspector } from "./components/Inspector";
import { NewDocumentModal } from "./components/NewDocumentModal";
import { SearchBar } from "./components/SearchBar";
import { SettingsModal } from "./components/SettingsModal";
import { PaperViewport } from "./components/canvas/PaperViewport";
import { PresentationBar } from "./components/PresentationBar";
import { Sidebar } from "./components/Sidebar";
import { StampLibrary } from "./components/StampLibrary";
import { ToolBar } from "./components/ToolBar";
import { TopBar } from "./components/TopBar";
import { addImageStamp } from "./lib/images";
import { parseBootArgs, resolvePaperTarget } from "./lib/bootArgs";
import { paperTemplates } from "./lib/data";
import { loadMirroredStamps, useCustomStampStore } from "./lib/customStamps";
import { readImageFile } from "./lib/raster";
import { saveActiveDocument } from "./lib/save";
import { bindingFromEvent, normalizeBinding, useShortcutStore } from "./lib/shortcuts";
import { loadWorkspace, saveWorkspace } from "./lib/storage";
import { TOOL_BY_ID } from "./lib/tools";
import type { ToolId } from "./lib/types";
import { useAnnotationStore } from "./stores/useAnnotationStore";
import { useDocStore } from "./stores/useDocStore";
import { useToolStore } from "./stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore } from "./stores/useUIStore";

function isTyping(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  return (
    target instanceof HTMLElement &&
    (target.tagName === "INPUT" ||
      target.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ||
      target.tagName === "SELECT" ||
      target.isContentEditable)
  );
}

export default function App() {
  const presentation = useUIStore((state) => state.presentation);
  const setPresentation = useUIStore((state) => state.setPresentation);
  const setPaletteOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setPaletteOpen);
  const toast = useUIStore((state) => state.toast);
  const toastAction = useUIStore((state) => state.toastAction);
  const setTool = useToolStore((state) => state.setTool);
  const zoomBy = useDocStore((state) => state.zoomBy);
  const setFit = useDocStore((state) => state.setFit);
  const setPage = useDocStore((state) => state.setPage);
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const ready = useRef(false);
  const saveFailed = useRef(false);

  // Restore the last workspace, then autosave every change to IndexedDB.
  useEffect(() => {
    const boot = parseBootArgs(typeof window === "undefined" ? "" : window.location.search);
    void loadWorkspace().then((saved) => {
      if (saved) useDocStore.getState().restore(saved.tabs, saved.activeId);
      // The IndexedDB mirror survives a cleared localStorage.
      void loadMirroredStamps().then((mirrored) => {
        if (mirrored) useCustomStampStore.getState().restore(mirrored);
      });
      ready.current = true;
      // Desktop launch contract: a requested paper size, presentation, or print run.
      if (boot.newDoc) {
        const target = resolvePaperTarget(boot.newDoc, paperTemplates);
        if (target) useDocStore.getState().newTab(target.templateId, boot.newDoc.trim(), target.landscape);
      }
      if (boot.fullscreen) useUIStore.getState().setPresentation(true);
      if (boot.print) window.setTimeout(() => window.print(), 500);
    });
    let timer = 0;
    let pending: { tabs: import("./lib/types").DocumentTab[]; activeId: string } | null = null;
    // Structural changes only: zoom, page and tool updates do not touch `tabs`.
    let lastTabs = useDocStore.getState().tabs;
    let lastActiveId = useDocStore.getState().activeId;
    const flush = () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = 0;
      const value = pending;
      pending = null;
      if (!value) return;
      void saveWorkspace(value.tabs, value.activeId).then((ok) => {
        if (ok || saveFailed.current) return;
        saveFailed.current = true;
        useUIStore.getState().setToast("Workspace autosave failed — storage may be full. Export a backup from Settings.");
      });
    };
    const unsubscribe = useDocStore.subscribe((state) => {
      if (!ready.current) return;
      if (state.tabs === lastTabs && state.activeId === lastActiveId) return;
      lastTabs = state.tabs;
      lastActiveId = state.activeId;
      pending = { tabs: state.tabs, activeId: state.activeId };
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = window.setTimeout(flush, 1000);
    });
    // A hidden page or a closed window must not lose the last second of edits.
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === "hidden") flush();
    };
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
    window.addEventListener("pagehide", flush);
    return () => {
      unsubscribe();
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
      window.removeEventListener("pagehide", flush);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => useUIStore.getState().setToast(null), 3400);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast]);

  // An unpinned tool popover collapses as soon as the next stroke starts.
  useEffect(() => {
    const onStrokeBegin = () => {
      const ui = useUIStore.getState();
      if (!ui.toolPopoverPinned) ui.closeToolPopover();
    };
    window.addEventListener("pm:stroke-begin", onStrokeBegin);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("pm:stroke-begin", onStrokeBegin);
  }, []);

  // Images arrive through the clipboard as files, not annotations.
  useEffect(() => {
    const onPaste = (event: ClipboardEvent) => {
      if (isTyping(event.target)) return;
      const items = [...(event.clipboardData?.items ?? [])];
      const item = items.find((entry) => entry.type.startsWith("image/"));
      const file = item?.getAsFile();
      if (!file) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      void readImageFile(file)
        .then((image) => addImageStamp(image))
        .catch(() => useUIStore.getState().setToast("That clipboard image could not be read"));
    };
    window.addEventListener("paste", onPaste);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("paste", onPaste);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (isTyping(event.target)) return;
      const annotations = useAnnotationStore.getState();
      const pressed = bindingFromEvent(event);
      const action = pressed ? useShortcutStore.getState().resolve().get(normalizeBinding(pressed)) : undefined;

      switch (action) {
        case "palette":
          event.preventDefault();
          useUIStore.getState().setPaletteOpen(!useUIStore.getState().paletteOpen);
          return;
        case "settings":
          event.preventDefault();
          useUIStore.getState().setSettingsOpen(true);
          return;
        case "presentation":
          event.preventDefault();
          setPresentation(true);
          return;
        case "open":
          event.preventDefault();
          fileRef.current?.click();
          return;
        case "save":
          event.preventDefault();
          void saveActiveDocument("editable");
          return;
        case "print":
          event.preventDefault();
          window.print();
          return;
        case "undo":
          event.preventDefault();
          useDocStore.getState().undo();
          return;
        case "redo":
          event.preventDefault();
          useDocStore.getState().redo();
          return;
        case "copy":
          if (annotations.selection.length === 0) return;
          event.preventDefault();
          annotations.copySelection();
          return;
        case "cut":
          if (annotations.selection.length === 0) return;
          event.preventDefault();
          annotations.cutSelection();
          return;
        case "paste":
          if (annotations.clipboard.length === 0) return;
          event.preventDefault();
          annotations.paste();
          return;
        case "duplicate":
          event.preventDefault();
          annotations.duplicateSelection();
          return;
        case "select-all": {
          event.preventDefault();
          const state = useDocStore.getState();
          const tab = state.tabs.find((item) => item.id === state.activeId);
          const page = tab?.pages[state.page - 1];
          if (page) {
            annotations.select(
              page.annotations.filter((annotation) => !annotation.hidden && !annotation.locked).map((annotation) => annotation.id),
            );
          }
          return;
        }
        case "group":
          event.preventDefault();
          annotations.groupSelection();
          return;
        case "ungroup":
          event.preventDefault();
          annotations.ungroupSelection();
          return;
        case "search":
          event.preventDefault();
          useUIStore.getState().setSearchOpen(true);
          return;
        case "view-one-page":
          event.preventDefault();
          useUIStore.getState().setViewMode("one-page");
          return;
        case "view-one-continuous":
          event.preventDefault();
          useUIStore.getState().setViewMode("one-continuous");
          return;
        case "view-two-continuous":
          event.preventDefault();
          useUIStore.getState().setViewMode("two-continuous");
          return;
        case "view-two-page":
          event.preventDefault();
          useUIStore.getState().setViewMode("two-page");
          return;
        case "toggle-cover-page":
          event.preventDefault();
          useUIStore.getState().toggleSeparateCoverPage();
          return;
      }

      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        const ui = useUIStore.getState();
        if (ui.searchOpen) ui.setSearchOpen(false);
        else if (ui.paletteOpen) ui.setPaletteOpen(false);
        else if (ui.libraryOpen) ui.setLibraryOpen(false);
        else if (ui.settingsOpen) ui.setSettingsOpen(false);
        else if (ui.newDocOpen) ui.setNewDocOpen(false);
        else if (ui.toolPopoverOpen) ui.closeToolPopover();
        else if (ui.sidebarOpen) ui.toggleSidebar();
        else if (ui.presentation) setPresentation(false);
        else useAnnotationStore.getState().clearSelection();
        return;
      }

      if (event.key === "Delete" || event.key === "Backspace") {
        if (annotations.selection.length === 0) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        annotations.deleteSelection();
        return;
      }

      if (event.key.startsWith("Arrow")) {
        if (annotations.selection.length === 0) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        const step = event.shiftKey ? 5 : 1;
        const dx = event.key === "ArrowLeft" ? -step : event.key === "ArrowRight" ? step : 0;
        const dy = event.key === "ArrowUp" ? -step : event.key === "ArrowDown" ? step : 0;
        annotations.nudge(dx, dy);
        return;
      }

      if (action === "fit-page") {
        setFit("page");
        return;
      }
      if (action && TOOL_BY_ID.has(action as ToolId)) {
        setTool(action as ToolId);
        return;
      }

      if (event.altKey) return;

      if (event.code === "Equal" || event.code === "NumpadAdd" || event.key === "+") {
        zoomBy(1);
        return;
      }
      if (event.code === "Minus" || event.code === "NumpadSubtract" || event.key === "-") {
        zoomBy(-1);
        return;
      }
      if (event.key === "PageDown" || event.key === "PageUp") {
        event.preventDefault();
        setPage(useDocStore.getState().page + (event.key === "PageDown" ? 1 : -1));
        return;
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [setFit, setPage, setPaletteOpen, setPresentation, setTool, zoomBy]);

  return (
    <div className="app-bg flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden">
      <input
        ref={fileRef}
        id="pm-pdf-input"
        type="file"
        accept="application/pdf,.pdf"
        className="hidden"
        onChange={(event) => {
          const file = event.target.files?.[0];
          if (file) void useDocStore.getState().openPdfFile(file);
          event.target.value = "";
        }}
      />
      {presentation ? (
        <div className="relative h-full w-full overflow-hidden">
          <PaperViewport />
          <PresentationBar />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <TopBar />
          <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1">
            <Sidebar />
            <FavoriteToolsBar />
            <main className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
              <PaperViewport />
              <ToolBar />
              <Inspector />
            </main>
            <SearchBar />
          </div>
          <CommandPalette />
          <StampLibrary />
          <NewDocumentModal />
          <SettingsModal />
        </>
      )}
      {toast && (
        <div className={`fixed bottom-5 left-1/2 z-[60] -translate-x-1/2 ${toastAction ? "pointer-events-auto" : "pointer-events-none"}`}>
          <div className="glass-strong flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-[11.5px] shadow-lg">
            <span>{toast}</span>
            {toastAction && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="chip h-6 px-2"
                onClick={() => {
                  toastAction();
                  useUIStore.getState().setToast(null);
                }}
              >
                Undo
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { AnnotationPatch } from "../../lib/annotationTypes";

  selectionBounds,

import { angleAt, PRESET_SCALES, useScaleStore } from "../../lib/measure";

type Draft =

  | { kind: "shape"; shape: ShapeAnnotation }
  | { kind: "marquee"; start: Pt; end: Pt }
