import { useMemo } from "react";
import { PX_PER_MM, pageMm, paperGeometry } from "../../lib/paper";
import type { PaperTemplate } from "../../lib/types";

interface PaperSheetProps {
  template: PaperTemplate;
  landscape?: boolean;
  /** Dashed margin and centre guides for aligning annotations. */
  aux?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

/**
 * Parametric paper engine: one SVG per recipe, drawn in millimetres so every
 * line width and margin survives zooming without re-rasterising.
 */
export function PaperSheet({ template, landscape = false, aux = false, className = "" }: PaperSheetProps) {
  const geometry = useMemo(() => paperGeometry(template, landscape), [template, landscape]);
  const page = pageMm(template, landscape);
  const widthPx = page.widthMm * PX_PER_MM;
  const heightPx = page.heightMm * PX_PER_MM;
  const bounds = template.bounds;

  return (
    <div
      className={`paper-shadow overflow-hidden ${className}`}
      style={{ width: widthPx, height: heightPx }}
      data-template={template.id}
    >
      <svg
        viewBox={`0 0 ${geometry.widthMm} ${geometry.heightMm}`}
        width={widthPx}
        height={heightPx}
        role="img"
        aria-label={`${template.name} paper`}
        style={{ display: "block" }}
      >
        <rect width={geometry.widthMm} height={geometry.heightMm} fill="var(--paper)" />
        {geometry.hasGrid && (
          <g fill="none" stroke={geometry.color}>
            {/* Screen hairlines: non-scaling keeps 0.1 mm rules readable at every zoom. */}
            {geometry.base && (
              <path d={geometry.base} strokeWidth={0.7} vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke" opacity={0.6} />
            )}
            {geometry.minor && (
              <path d={geometry.minor} strokeWidth={0.9} vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke" opacity={0.8} />
            )}
            {geometry.major && (
              <path d={geometry.major} strokeWidth={1.1} vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke" />
            )}
          </g>
        )}
        {aux && (
          <g
            fill="none"
            stroke="var(--accent)"
            strokeWidth={1}
            strokeDasharray="4 3"
            vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"
            opacity={0.55}
          >
            <line x1={geometry.widthMm / 2} y1="0" x2={geometry.widthMm / 2} y2={geometry.heightMm} />
            <line x1="0" y1={geometry.heightMm / 2} x2={geometry.widthMm} y2={geometry.heightMm / 2} />
            {bounds && (
              <rect
                x={bounds.left}
                y={bounds.top}
                width={geometry.widthMm - bounds.left - bounds.right}
                height={geometry.heightMm - bounds.top - bounds.bottom}
              />
            )}
          </g>
        )}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { FileText, Image as ImageIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { clamp, getWorkspaceInsets, PX_PER_MM, workspaceSafeArea } from "../../lib/paper";
import { CANVAS_TOUCH_ACTION, pansWithPointer } from "../../lib/canvasInput";
import { pageSizeMm, pageTemplate } from "../../lib/pageUtils";
import { addImageStamp } from "../../lib/images";
import { readImageFile } from "../../lib/raster";
import { useSearchStore } from "../../lib/pdfSearch";
import { usePrefsStore } from "../../lib/prefs";
import { useDocStore } from "../../stores/useDocStore";
import { useToolStore } from "../../stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore, type GridStyle } from "../../stores/useUIStore";
import type { DocumentPage, DocumentTab, ToolId } from "../../lib/types";
import { AnnotationLayer } from "./AnnotationLayer";
import { CropOverlay } from "./CropOverlay";
import { PaperSheet } from "./PaperSheet";
import { PdfBackground } from "./PdfBackground";

function buildRows(count: number, twoPage: boolean, cover: boolean): number[][] {
  if (!twoPage) return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => [index]);
  const rows: number[][] = [];
  let start = 0;
  if (cover && count > 0) {
    rows.push([0]);
    start = 1;
  }
  for (let index = start; index < count; index += 2) {
    rows.push(index + 1 < count ? [index, index + 1] : [index]);
  }
  return rows;
}

/** Non-printing screen grid drawn over any page; never exported or printed. */
function GridOverlay({
  styleName,
  widthMm,
  heightMm,
  prefix,
}: {
  styleName: GridStyle;
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
  prefix: string;
}) {
  if (styleName === "none") return null;
  const id = `grid-${prefix.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "")}`;
  const minorId = `${id}-minor`;
  const step = styleName === "squared-10" ? 10 : styleName === "ruled-8" ? 8 : styleName === "millimeter" ? 1 : 5;
  const dots = styleName === "dots-5";
  const ruled = styleName === "ruled-8";
  const path = ruled ? `M 0 ${step} H ${step}` : `M ${step} 0 V ${step} H 0`;
  return (
    <svg
      className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-[1] h-full w-full"
      viewBox={`0 0 ${widthMm} ${heightMm}`}
      preserveAspectRatio="none"
      aria-hidden="true"
      data-grid={styleName}
    >
      <defs>
        <pattern id={dots ? id : minorId} width={step} height={step} patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
          {dots ? (
            <circle cx={0.5} cy={0.5} r={0.24} fill="var(--grid-ink)" opacity={0.34} />
          ) : (
            <path d={path} fill="none" stroke="var(--grid-ink)" strokeOpacity={0.22} strokeWidth={0.12} />
          )}
        </pattern>
        {styleName === "millimeter" && (
          <pattern id={id} width={10} height={10} patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
            <path d="M 10 0 V 10 H 0" fill="none" stroke="var(--grid-ink)" strokeOpacity={0.38} strokeWidth={0.2} />
          </pattern>
        )}
      </defs>
      <rect width={widthMm} height={heightMm} fill={`url(#${dots ? id : minorId})`} />
      {styleName === "millimeter" && <rect width={widthMm} height={heightMm} fill={`url(#${id})`} />}
    </svg>
  );
}

/** Amber hit boxes for the current search, in page millimetres. */
function SearchHighlights({ pageIndex, widthMm, heightMm }: { pageIndex: number; widthMm: number; heightMm: number }) {
  const matches = useSearchStore((state) => state.matches);
  const current = useSearchStore((state) => state.current);
  const hits = matches.map((match, index) => ({ match, index })).filter(({ match }) => match.pageIndex === pageIndex);
  if (hits.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-[2]" aria-hidden="true">
      {hits.map(({ match, index }) => (
        <span
          key={index}
          className="absolute rounded-[2px]"
          style={{
            left: `${(match.xMm / widthMm) * 100}%`,
            top: `${(match.yMm / heightMm) * 100}%`,
            width: `${(match.widthMm / widthMm) * 100}%`,
            height: `${(match.heightMm / heightMm) * 100}%`,
            background: index === current ? "rgba(255, 196, 0, 0.5)" : "rgba(255, 214, 10, 0.24)",
            boxShadow: index === current ? "0 0 0 2px #ffb300" : undefined,
            mixBlendMode: "multiply",
          }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

interface PageViewProps {
  tab: DocumentTab;
  page: DocumentPage;
  index: number;
  scale: number;
  observe: boolean;
  panMode: boolean;
  auxLines: boolean;
  gridStyle: GridStyle;
  activeTool: ToolId;
  activePageIndex: number;
  touchScroll: boolean;
  register: (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => void;
  onActivate: (index: number) => void;
}

function PageView({
  tab,
  page,
  index,
  scale,
  observe,
  panMode,
  auxLines,
  gridStyle,
  activeTool,
  activePageIndex,
  touchScroll,
  register,
  onActivate,
}: PageViewProps) {
  const size = pageSizeMm(tab, page);
  const template = pageTemplate(page);
  const rotated = page.rotation === 90 || page.rotation === 270;
  const baseW = size.widthMm * PX_PER_MM;
  const baseH = size.heightMm * PX_PER_MM;
  const displayW = (rotated ? size.heightMm : size.widthMm) * PX_PER_MM * scale;
  const displayH = (rotated ? size.widthMm : size.heightMm) * PX_PER_MM * scale;
  const nodeRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(!observe);

  // Hundreds of pages must not each mount an interactive ink layer.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!observe) {
      setVisible(true);
      return;
    }
    const node = nodeRef.current;
    if (!node || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") {
      setVisible(true);
      return;
    }
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries.some((entry) => entry.isIntersecting)) setVisible(true);
      },
      { rootMargin: "1200px" },
    );
    observer.observe(node);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [observe]);

  const crop = page.cropBox;
  const clipPath = crop
    ? `inset(${crop.yMm * PX_PER_MM}px ${(size.widthMm - crop.xMm - crop.widthMm) * PX_PER_MM}px ${(size.heightMm - crop.yMm - crop.heightMm) * PX_PER_MM}px ${crop.xMm * PX_PER_MM}px)`
    : undefined;

  return (
    <div
      ref={(node) => {
        nodeRef.current = node;
        register(node);
      }}
      data-page-index={index}
      data-page-number={index + 1}
      className="relative shrink-0"
      style={{ width: displayW, height: displayH }}
      onPointerDownCapture={(event) => {
        if (event.button === 0) onActivate(index);
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="page-paper absolute"
        style={{
          width: baseW,
          height: baseH,
          left: (displayW - baseW * scale) / 2,
          top: (displayH - baseH * scale) / 2,
          transform: `scale(${scale})${page.rotation ? ` rotate(${page.rotation}deg)` : ""}`,
          transformOrigin: "center",
          touchAction: CANVAS_TOUCH_ACTION,
        }}
      >
        <div className="relative h-full w-full" style={{ clipPath, overflow: "hidden" }}>
          {page.pdf ? (
            <div className="paper-shadow relative h-full w-full overflow-hidden" style={{ background: "var(--paper)" }}>
              <PdfBackground page={page.pdf} widthMm={size.widthMm} heightMm={size.heightMm} zoom={scale} />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <PaperSheet template={template} landscape={tab.landscape} aux={auxLines} />
          )}
          <GridOverlay styleName={gridStyle} widthMm={size.widthMm} heightMm={size.heightMm} prefix={`${tab.id}-${page.id}`} />
          <SearchHighlights pageIndex={index} widthMm={size.widthMm} heightMm={size.heightMm} />
          {visible && (
            <AnnotationLayer
              tab={tab}
              page={page}
              widthMm={size.widthMm}
              heightMm={size.heightMm}
              zoom={scale}
              panMode={panMode}
              touchScroll={touchScroll}
            />
          )}
        </div>
        {activeTool === "crop" && activePageIndex === index && (
          <CropOverlay
            key={`${tab.id}:${page.id}:${page.cropBox ? `${page.cropBox.xMm},${page.cropBox.yMm},${page.cropBox.widthMm},${page.cropBox.heightMm}` : "full"}`}
            widthMm={size.widthMm}
            heightMm={size.heightMm}
            cropBox={page.cropBox}
            zoom={scale}
            onApply={(box) => {
              const doc = useDocStore.getState();
              doc.pushHistory(tab.id);
              doc.updatePage(tab.id, index, { cropBox: box ?? undefined });
              useToolStore.getState().setTool("select");
            }}
            onCancel={() => useToolStore.getState().setTool("select")}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function PaperViewport() {
  const tabs = useDocStore((state) => state.tabs);
  const activeId = useDocStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const zoom = useDocStore((state) => state.zoom);
  const fitMode = useDocStore((state) => state.fitMode);
  const pageIndex = useDocStore((state) => state.page);
  const activeTool = useToolStore((state) => state.activeTool);
  const measureMode = useUIStore((state) => state.measureMode);
  const auxLines = useUIStore((state) => state.auxLines);
  const viewMode = useUIStore((state) => state.viewMode);
  const separateCoverPage = useUIStore((state) => state.separateCoverPage);
  const gridStyle = useUIStore((state) => state.gridStyle);
  const lastError = useDocStore((state) => state.lastError);
  const touchDrawEnabled = usePrefsStore((state) => state.touchDrawEnabled);
  const sidebarOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.sidebarOpen);
  const favoritesOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.favoritesOpen);
  const toolPopoverOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.toolPopoverOpen);
  const toolPopoverPinned = useUIStore((state) => state.toolPopoverPinned);
  const presentation = useUIStore((state) => state.presentation);

  const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === activeId) ?? tabs[0];
  const pageNumber = clamp(pageIndex, 1, tab.pages.length);
  const page = tab.pages[pageNumber - 1] ?? tab.pages[0];
  const size = pageSizeMm(tab, page);
  const rotated = page.rotation === 90 || page.rotation === 270;
  const fitSize = rotated ? { widthMm: size.heightMm, heightMm: size.widthMm } : size;
  const continuous = viewMode === "one-continuous" || viewMode === "two-continuous";
  const twoPage = viewMode === "two-page" || viewMode === "two-continuous";
  // Floating controls cover part of the canvas; fitting must centre in the rest.
  const { leftInset, rightInset } = presentation
    ? { leftInset: 0, rightInset: 0 }
    : getWorkspaceInsets({
        sidebarOpen,
        favoritesOpen,
        inspectorOpen: toolPopoverOpen,
        inspectorPinned: toolPopoverPinned,
      });

  const rows = useMemo(() => buildRows(tab.pages.length, twoPage, separateCoverPage), [tab.pages.length, twoPage, separateCoverPage]);
  const displayRows = useMemo(() => {
    if (continuous) return rows;
    const activeRow = rows.find((row) => row.includes(pageNumber - 1));
    return [activeRow ?? [pageNumber - 1]];
  }, [continuous, rows, pageNumber]);

  const viewportRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const pageRefs = useRef(new Map<number, HTMLDivElement>());
  const drag = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; panX: number; panY: number; scroll: boolean } | null>(null);
  const programmaticScrollUntil = useRef(0);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const panRef = useRef(pan);
  panRef.current = pan;
  // A second finger takes over from the one-finger pan; its pointerdown must not start one.
  const pinching = useRef(false);
  const [spaceDown, setSpaceDown] = useState(false);
  const [dropActive, setDropActive] = useState(false);

  // Two-finger pinch pans and zooms around the midpoint; one finger cancels any draft.
  useEffect(() => {
    const element = viewportRef.current;
    if (!element) return;
    const pointers = new Map<number, { x: number; y: number }>();
    let pinch: { distance: number; zoom: number; center: { x: number; y: number }; pan: { x: number; y: number } } | null = null;
    const midpoint = (): { x: number; y: number } => {
      const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
      const points = [...pointers.values()];
      const x = (points[0].x + points[1].x) / 2 - rect.left - rect.width / 2;
      const y = (points[0].y + points[1].y) / 2 - rect.top - rect.height / 2;
      return { x, y };
    };
    const onDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      pointers.set(event.pointerId, { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY });
      if (pointers.size !== 2) return;
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event("pm:cancel-draft"));
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event("pm:pinch-start"));
      drag.current = null;
      pinching.current = true;
      const points = [...pointers.values()];
      pinch = {
        distance: Math.hypot(points[0].x - points[1].x, points[0].y - points[1].y) || 1,
        zoom: useDocStore.getState().zoom,
        center: midpoint(),
        pan: { ...panRef.current },
      };
    };
    const onMove = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (!pointers.has(event.pointerId)) return;
      pointers.set(event.pointerId, { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY });
      if (!pinch || pointers.size < 2) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      const points = [...pointers.values()];
      const distance = Math.hypot(points[0].x - points[1].x, points[0].y - points[1].y) || 1;
      const zoom = clamp(pinch.zoom * (distance / pinch.distance), 0.1, 6);
      const ratio = zoom / pinch.zoom;
      const center = midpoint();
      setPan({
        x: center.x - (pinch.center.x - pinch.pan.x) * ratio,
        y: center.y - (pinch.center.y - pinch.pan.y) * ratio,
      });
      useDocStore.setState({ zoom, fitMode: null });
    };
    const onUp = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      pointers.delete(event.pointerId);
      if (pointers.size >= 2) return;
      if (pinch) window.dispatchEvent(new Event("pm:pinch-end"));
      pinch = null;
      pinching.current = false;
    };
    element.addEventListener("pointerdown", onDown);
    element.addEventListener("pointermove", onMove, { passive: false });
    element.addEventListener("pointerup", onUp);
    element.addEventListener("pointercancel", onUp);
    return () => {
      element.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onDown);
      element.removeEventListener("pointermove", onMove);
      element.removeEventListener("pointerup", onUp);
      element.removeEventListener("pointercancel", onUp);
    };
  }, []);

  // Fit to the safe workspace between the floating side rails whenever that fit
  // was requested, or the page/rotation/sidebars change.
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!fitMode) return;
    const element = viewportRef.current;
    if (!element) return;
    const apply = () => {
      const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (rect.width === 0 || rect.height === 0) return;
      const rowWidthPx = (twoPage ? fitSize.widthMm * 2 + 12 : fitSize.widthMm) * PX_PER_MM;
      const rowHeightPx = fitSize.heightMm * PX_PER_MM;
      const { safeWidth } = workspaceSafeArea(rect.width, leftInset, rightInset);
      // 12px of breathing room on each side of the safe strip.
      const byWidth = (safeWidth - 24) / rowWidthPx;
      const byHeight = (rect.height - 16) / rowHeightPx;
      const zoom = fitMode === "width" ? byWidth : Math.min(byWidth, byHeight);
      useDocStore.setState({ zoom: clamp(zoom, 0.1, 6) });
      setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
      if (continuous) {
        const active = useDocStore.getState().page;
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          pageRefs.current.get(active - 1)?.scrollIntoView({ block: "start" });
          // A stale horizontal offset would leave the fitted page under a rail.
          if (scrollRef.current) scrollRef.current.scrollLeft = 0;
        });
      }
    };
    apply();
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(apply);
    observer.observe(element);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [fitMode, fitSize.widthMm, fitSize.heightMm, twoPage, viewMode, leftInset, rightInset, continuous]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  }, [viewMode]);

  // Ctrl/⌘ + wheel zooms about the cursor; a bare wheel scrolls the document.
  useEffect(() => {
    const element = viewportRef.current;
    if (!element) return;
    const onWheel = (event: WheelEvent) => {
      if (continuous && !(event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      if (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) {
        const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
        const cursorX = event.clientX - rect.left - rect.width / 2;
        const cursorY = event.clientY - rect.top - rect.height / 2;
        const next = clamp(zoom * Math.exp(-event.deltaY * 0.0016), 0.1, 6);
        const ratio = next / zoom;
        setPan((current) => ({
          x: cursorX - (cursorX - current.x) * ratio,
          y: cursorY - (cursorY - current.y) * ratio,
        }));
        useDocStore.setState({ zoom: next, fitMode: null });
      } else {
        setPan((current) => ({ x: current.x - event.deltaX, y: current.y - event.deltaY }));
      }
    };
    element.addEventListener("wheel", onWheel, { passive: false });
    return () => element.removeEventListener("wheel", onWheel);
  }, [zoom, continuous]);

  // Hold space to pan with any tool active.
  useEffect(() => {
    const isTyping = (target: EventTarget | null) =>
      target instanceof HTMLElement &&
      (target.tagName === "INPUT" || target.tagName === "TEXTAREA" || target.tagName === "SELECT" || target.isContentEditable);
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.code === "Space" && !isTyping(event.target)) {
        event.preventDefault();
        setSpaceDown(true);
      }
    };
    const onKeyUp = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.code === "Space") setSpaceDown(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
    };
  }, []);

  // A search hit scrolls its page into the middle of a continuous view.
  useEffect(() => {
    const onFocus = (event: Event) => {
      const detail = (event as CustomEvent<{ pageIndex?: number }>).detail;
      if (!detail || typeof detail.pageIndex !== "number") return;
      if (!continuous) return;
      pageRefs.current.get(detail.pageIndex)?.scrollIntoView({ block: "center", behavior: "smooth" });
    };
    window.addEventListener("pm:search-focus", onFocus);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("pm:search-focus", onFocus);
  }, [continuous]);

  // An explicit page jump (top bar, sidebar, PageUp/PageDown) scrolls the
  // continuous view to that page; the scroll listener must not undo it.
  useEffect(() => {
    const onGoto = (event: Event) => {
      const detail = (event as CustomEvent<{ page?: number }>).detail;
      if (!continuous || !detail || typeof detail.page !== "number") return;
      programmaticScrollUntil.current = Date.now() + 400;
      const node = scrollRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-page-number="${detail.page}"]`);
      node?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    };
    window.addEventListener("pm:goto-page", onGoto);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("pm:goto-page", onGoto);
  }, [continuous]);

  const canPan = activeTool === "hand" || spaceDown;
  const cursor = drag.current ? "grabbing" : canPan ? "grab" : "default";

  const scrollToCenterPage = () => {
    const element = scrollRef.current;
    if (!element) return;
    if (Date.now() < programmaticScrollUntil.current) return;
    const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
    const center = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
    let best = useDocStore.getState().page;
    let distance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    for (const [index, node] of pageRefs.current) {
      const box = node.getBoundingClientRect();
      const delta = Math.abs((box.top + box.bottom) / 2 - center);
      if (delta < distance) {
        distance = delta;
        best = index + 1;
      }
    }
    // Scrolling is not navigation: update the page without a goto event.
    if (best !== useDocStore.getState().page) useDocStore.setState({ page: best });
  };

  const onPointerDown = (event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (pinching.current) return;
    const touchPan = pansWithPointer(event.pointerType, touchDrawEnabled, canPan);
    if (continuous) {
      if (!touchPan && event.button !== 1) return;
      drag.current = {
        x: event.clientX,
        y: event.clientY,
        panX: scrollRef.current?.scrollLeft ?? 0,
        panY: scrollRef.current?.scrollTop ?? 0,
        scroll: true,
      };
      event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
      return;
    }
    if (!touchPan && event.button !== 1) return;
    drag.current = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, panX: pan.x, panY: pan.y, scroll: false };
    event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
  };

  const onPointerMove = (event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const current = drag.current;
    if (!current) return;
    if (current.scroll) {
      const element = scrollRef.current;
      if (!element) return;
      element.scrollLeft = current.panX - (event.clientX - current.x);
      element.scrollTop = current.panY - (event.clientY - current.y);
      return;
    }
    setPan({ x: current.panX + event.clientX - current.x, y: current.panY + event.clientY - current.y });
  };

  const onPointerUp = (event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    drag.current = null;
    if (event.currentTarget.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) event.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);
  };

  const activate = (index: number) => {
    // Clicking a page on screen is not a jump request.
    if (useDocStore.getState().page !== index + 1) useDocStore.setState({ page: index + 1 });
  };

  const pageNodes = (row: number[], observe: boolean) =>
    row.map((index) => (
      <PageView
        key={tab.pages[index]?.id ?? index}
        tab={tab}
        page={tab.pages[index]}
        index={index}
        scale={zoom}
        observe={observe}
        panMode={canPan}
        auxLines={auxLines}
        gridStyle={gridStyle}
        activeTool={activeTool}
        activePageIndex={pageNumber - 1}
        touchScroll={!touchDrawEnabled}
        register={(node) => {
          if (node) pageRefs.current.set(index, node);
          else pageRefs.current.delete(index);
        }}
        onActivate={activate}
      />
    ));

  return (
    <div
      ref={viewportRef}
      className="viewport relative h-full w-full overflow-hidden"
      style={{ cursor, touchAction: CANVAS_TOUCH_ACTION }}
      onPointerDown={onPointerDown}
      onPointerMove={onPointerMove}
      onPointerUp={onPointerUp}
      onPointerCancel={() => {
        drag.current = null;
      }}
      onDoubleClick={() => useDocStore.getState().setFit("page")}
      onDragOver={(event) => {
        if (!event.dataTransfer.types.includes("Files")) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        setDropActive(true);
      }}
      onDragLeave={() => setDropActive(false)}
      onDrop={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        setDropActive(false);
        const file = event.dataTransfer.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        if (file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
          void readImageFile(file)
            .then((image) => addImageStamp(image))
            .catch(() => undefined);
          return;
        }
        if (file.type === "application/pdf" || /\.pdf$/i.test(file.name)) {
          void useDocStore.getState().openPdfFile(file);
        }
      }}
    >
      {continuous ? (
        <div
          ref={scrollRef}
          className="doc-scroll absolute inset-0 overflow-y-scroll overflow-x-auto"
          onScroll={scrollToCenterPage}
        >
          <div
            className="flex min-h-full flex-col gap-5 py-8"
            style={{
              width: "max-content",
              minWidth: "100%",
              // border-box: the rail padding must come out of the content box, not add to it,
              // or the fitted page is pushed right and clipped.
              boxSizing: "border-box",
              paddingLeft: leftInset,
              paddingRight: rightInset,
            }}
          >
            {displayRows.map((row, rowIndex) => (
              <div key={rowIndex} className="flex items-start justify-center gap-5" style={{ marginLeft: "auto", marginRight: "auto" }}>
                {pageNodes(row, true)}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div
          className="page-stage absolute top-1/2 will-change-transform"
          style={{
            // Centre of the safe strip: leftInset + (100% - leftInset - rightInset) / 2.
            left: `calc(${leftInset}px + (100% - ${leftInset + rightInset}px) / 2)`,
            transform: `translate(-50%, -50%) translate(${pan.x}px, ${pan.y}px)`,
          }}
        >
          <div className="flex items-start justify-center gap-5">{pageNodes(displayRows[0], false)}</div>
        </div>
      )}

      {dropActive && (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-4 z-30 grid place-items-center rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-soft)]">
          <div className="glass-strong flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-[12.5px] font-medium">
            <FileText size={16} /> Drop a PDF to open a tab, or an image to place it <ImageIcon size={16} />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {lastError && (
        <div className="glass pointer-events-auto absolute left-1/2 top-14 z-30 flex max-w-[80%] -translate-x-1/2 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-[11.5px]">
          <span style={{ color: "var(--danger)" }}>{lastError}</span>
          <button type="button" className="chip h-6" onClick={() => useDocStore.setState({ lastError: null })}>
            Dismiss
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {(activeTool === "measure" || activeTool === "snapshot") && (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-4 z-20 max-w-[80%] -translate-x-1/2">
          <div className="glass rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-muted">
            {activeTool === "snapshot"
              ? "Drag a region to copy, download, or save it as a stamp."
              : measureMode === "angle"
                ? "Click the vertex, then one point on each arm."
                : measureMode === "distance"
                  ? "Drag a line, then Enter or Commit."
                  : "Click each corner; Enter closes the shape."}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div
        className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-px"
        style={{ background: "linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, var(--border), transparent)" }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\canvas\PdfBackground.tsx (1.3 KB)

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { renderPdfPage } from "../../lib/pdfEngine";
import { PX_PER_MM } from "../../lib/paper";
import type { PdfPageSource } from "../../lib/types";

interface Props {
  page: PdfPageSource;
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
  zoom: number;
}

/** Rasterises a source PDF page at the current zoom; the vector ink layer stays on top. */
export function PdfBackground({ page, widthMm, heightMm, zoom }: Props) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  // Quantised so small zoom steps reuse the same raster instead of re-rendering the page.
  const renderQuality = Math.min(4, Math.max(2, Math.ceil(zoom * (typeof window === "undefined" ? 1 : window.devicePixelRatio || 1))));

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (cancelled || !canvasRef.current) return;
      void renderPdfPage(page.url, page.pageNumber, canvasRef.current, widthMm * PX_PER_MM, heightMm * PX_PER_MM, renderQuality);
    }, 70);
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [page.url, page.pageNumber, widthMm, heightMm, renderQuality]);

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full" aria-label={`PDF page ${page.pageNumber}`} />;
}

FILE: src\components\canvas\SelectionOverlay.tsx (6.4 KB)

import {
  BringToFront,
  Copy,
  FlipHorizontal2,
  FlipVertical2,
  Group,
  Lock,
  RotateCw,
  SendToBack,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Stamp,
  Trash2,
  Ungroup,
  Unlock,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { BBox, Pt } from "../../lib/annotationTypes";
import { PX_PER_MM } from "../../lib/paper";

export type HandleId = "nw" | "n" | "ne" | "e" | "se" | "s" | "sw" | "w" | "rotate";

export function handlePoints(box: BBox, rotateGapMm: number): Record<HandleId, Pt> {
  const x0 = box.x;
  const y0 = box.y;
  const x1 = box.x + box.width;
  const y1 = box.y + box.height;
  const cx = box.x + box.width / 2;
  const cy = box.y + box.height / 2;
  return {
    nw: [x0, y0],
    n: [cx, y0],
    ne: [x1, y0],
    e: [x1, cy],
    se: [x1, y1],
    s: [cx, y1],
    sw: [x0, y1],
    w: [x0, cy],
    rotate: [cx, y0 - rotateGapMm],
  };
}

export type SelectionAction =
  | "style"
  | "duplicate"
  | "rotate"
  | "flip-h"
  | "flip-v"
  | "group"
  | "ungroup"
  | "lock"
  | "front"
  | "back"
  | "save-stamp"
  | "delete";

const BUTTONS: { action: SelectionAction; label: string; Icon: typeof Copy }[] = [
  { action: "style", label: "Style / properties", Icon: SlidersHorizontal },
  { action: "duplicate", label: "Duplicate (Ctrl+D)", Icon: Copy },
  { action: "rotate", label: "Rotate 90°", Icon: RotateCw },
  { action: "flip-h", label: "Flip horizontal", Icon: FlipHorizontal2 },
  { action: "flip-v", label: "Flip vertical", Icon: FlipVertical2 },
  { action: "group", label: "Group (Ctrl+G)", Icon: Group },
  { action: "ungroup", label: "Ungroup (Ctrl+Shift+G)", Icon: Ungroup },
  { action: "lock", label: "Lock / unlock", Icon: Lock },
  { action: "front", label: "Bring to front", Icon: BringToFront },
  { action: "back", label: "Send to back", Icon: SendToBack },
  { action: "save-stamp", label: "Save as stamp", Icon: Stamp },
  { action: "delete", label: "Delete", Icon: Trash2 },
];

interface Props {
  bounds: BBox | null;
  count: number;
  zoom: number;
  pageWidthMm: number;
  locked: boolean;
  grouped: boolean;
  onHandleDown: (handle: HandleId, event: React.PointerEvent) => void;
  onAction: (action: SelectionAction) => void;
}

/** Screen-space selection chrome; the page wrapper's CSS zoom would scale it, so it counter-scales. */
export function SelectionOverlay({ bounds, count, zoom, pageWidthMm, locked, grouped, onHandleDown, onAction }: Props) {
  if (!bounds) return null;
  const scale = PX_PER_MM;
  const left = bounds.x * scale;
  const top = bounds.y * scale;
  const width = Math.max(1, bounds.width * scale);
  const height = Math.max(1, bounds.height * scale);
  const size = 9 / zoom;
  const hit = 24 / zoom;
  const rotateGap = 26 / scale;
  const handles = handlePoints(bounds, rotateGap);
  // 48 screen px above the box top: keeps the bar 22 px clear of the rotate grip.
  const lift = 48 / zoom;
  const below = top < lift;
  // Anchor the bar to the near edge so its 12 buttons stay inside the page.
  const anchorRight = left + width / 2 > (pageWidthMm * scale) / 2;
  const barLeft = anchorRight ? left + width : left;
  const barShift = below
    ? `translate(${anchorRight ? "-100%" : "0"}, 0)`
    : `translate(${anchorRight ? "-100%" : "0"}, -100%)`;
  const barOrigin = `${below ? "top" : "bottom"} ${anchorRight ? "right" : "left"}`;

  return (
    <div className="selection-chrome pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-20">
      <div
        className="absolute"
        style={{ left, top, width, height, border: `${1.25 / zoom}px solid var(--accent)`, borderRadius: 1 }}
      />
      {(Object.keys(handles) as HandleId[]).map((handle) => {
        const [x, y] = handles[handle];
        const isRotate = handle === "rotate";
        return (
          <button
            key={handle}
            type="button"
            aria-label={isRotate ? "Rotate selection" : `Resize ${handle}`}
            className="pointer-events-auto absolute bg-transparent"
            style={{
              left: x * scale - hit / 2,
              top: y * scale - hit / 2,
              width: hit,
              height: hit,
              cursor: isRotate ? "grab" : `${handle}-resize`,
              touchAction: "none",
            }}
            onPointerDown={(event) => {
              event.stopPropagation();
              onHandleDown(handle, event);
            }}
          >
            <span
              className="block rounded-full border border-[var(--accent)]"
              style={{
                width: isRotate ? size + 3 : size,
                height: isRotate ? size + 3 : size,
                margin: "auto",
                background: isRotate ? "var(--glass-strong)" : "var(--paper)",
              }}
            />
          </button>
        );
      })}
      {!below && (
        <div
          className="absolute"
          style={{
            left: left + width / 2,
            top: top - lift,
            height: lift,
            width: 1 / zoom,
            background: "var(--accent)",
            transform: "translateX(-50%)",
          }}
        />
      )}
      <div
        className="absolute"
        style={{
          left: barLeft,
          top: below ? top + height + 12 / zoom : top - lift,
          transform: barShift,
          transformOrigin: barOrigin,
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ transform: `scale(${1 / zoom})`, transformOrigin: barOrigin }}>
          <div className="glass-strong pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-lg px-1 py-0.5 shadow-lg">
            {BUTTONS.map(({ action, label, Icon }) => (
              <button
                key={action}
                type="button"
                className="icon-btn h-6 w-6"
                title={label}
                aria-label={label}
                style={action === "delete" ? { color: "var(--danger)" } : undefined}
                onPointerDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
                onClick={() => onAction(action)}
              >
                <Icon size={12.5} strokeWidth={1.9} />
              </button>
            ))}
            <span className="ml-1 flex items-center gap-1 pr-1 text-[10px] text-muted">
              {locked ? <Lock size={10} /> : grouped ? <Group size={10} /> : <Unlock size={10} />}
              {count}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\canvas\SnapshotBar.tsx (3.7 KB)
================================================================================
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Check, Copy, Download, Stamp, X } from "lucide-react";
import type { BBox } from "../../lib/annotationTypes";
import { clamp, PX_PER_MM } from "../../lib/paper";
import { usePrefsStore, type SnapshotDpi } from "../../lib/prefs";
import { Segmented } from "../ui/controls";

const DPI: { value: string; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "72", label: "72" },
  { value: "150", label: "150" },
  { value: "300", label: "300" },
];

const EDGE = 12;

interface Props {
  region: BBox;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  zoom: number;
  /** Page size in millimetres; keeps the bar inside the sheet at any edge. */
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
  status: string | null;
  onCopy: () => void;
  onDownload: () => void;
  onSaveStamp: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

/** Floating action bar for a snapshot region, in page millimetres. */
export function SnapshotBar({ region, imageUrl, zoom, widthMm, heightMm, status, onCopy, onDownload, onSaveStamp, onClose }: Props) {
  const snapshotDpi = usePrefsStore((state) => state.snapshotDpi);
  const setPrefs = usePrefsStore((state) => state.set);
  const barRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [bar, setBar] = useState({ width: 280, height: 36 });

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const element = barRef.current;
    if (!element) return;
    const measure = () => setBar({ width: element.offsetWidth, height: element.offsetHeight });
    measure();
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(measure);
    observer.observe(element);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [imageUrl, status]);

  const pageWidth = widthMm * PX_PER_MM;
  const pageHeight = heightMm * PX_PER_MM;
  // The bar is scaled by 1/zoom, so its page-local footprint grows when zoomed out.
  const barWidth = bar.width / Math.max(0.1, zoom);
  const barHeight = bar.height / Math.max(0.1, zoom);
  const preferredLeft = (region.x + region.width) * PX_PER_MM;
  const preferredTop = (region.y + region.height) * PX_PER_MM + 6;
  const left = clamp(preferredLeft, EDGE, Math.max(EDGE, pageWidth - barWidth - EDGE));
  const top = clamp(preferredTop, EDGE, Math.max(EDGE, pageHeight - barHeight - EDGE));

  return (
    <div
      ref={barRef}
      className="snapshot-bar absolute z-[95] inline-flex w-max"
      style={{ left, top, transform: `scale(${1 / zoom})`, transformOrigin: "top left" }}
      onPointerDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
    >
      <div className="glass-strong flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl p-1.5 shadow-xl">
        {imageUrl && <img src={imageUrl} alt="Snapshot preview" className="checker h-8 w-8 shrink-0 rounded-md object-contain" />}
        <span title="Snapshot resolution">
          <Segmented
            value={String(snapshotDpi)}
            options={DPI}
            onChange={(value) => setPrefs({ snapshotDpi: Number(value) as SnapshotDpi })}
          />
        </span>
        <button type="button" className="chip h-7 shrink-0 px-2" onClick={onCopy} aria-label="Copy snapshot PNG">
          <Copy size={12} /> Copy
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="chip h-7 shrink-0 px-2" onClick={onDownload} aria-label="Download snapshot PNG">
          <Download size={12} /> PNG
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="chip h-7 shrink-0 px-2" onClick={onSaveStamp} aria-label="Save snapshot as stamp">
          <Stamp size={12} /> Save stamp
        </button>
        {status && (
          <span className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 px-1 text-[10px] text-muted">
            <Check size={10} /> {status}
          </span>
        )}
        <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7 shrink-0" aria-label="Close snapshot bar" onClick={onClose}>
          <X size={12} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\canvas\TextEditor.tsx (4.4 KB)
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { AnnotationPatch, TextAnnotation } from "../../lib/annotationTypes";
import { PX_PER_MM } from "../../lib/paper";
import { editorHeightMm, PT_TO_MM, TEXT_PADDING_MM, textFontCss } from "../../lib/textLayout";

interface Props {
  draft: TextAnnotation;
  onPatch: (patch: AnnotationPatch) => void;
  onFit: (text: string) => void;
  onCommit: (text: string) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

/**
 * In-place editor aligned to the text box: same mm position, font and frame,
 * scaled by the page's CSS zoom because it lives inside the page wrapper.
 */
export function TextEditor({ draft, onPatch, onFit, onCommit, onCancel }: Props) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(draft.text);
  const done = useRef(false);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const input = inputRef.current;
    if (!input) return;
    input.focus();
    input.setSelectionRange(input.value.length, input.value.length);
  }, []);

  const finish = (commit: boolean) => {
    if (done.current) return;
    done.current = true;
    if (commit) onCommit(value);
    else onCancel();
  };

  const fontSizePx = draft.fontSizePt * PT_TO_MM * PX_PER_MM;
  const radius = Math.min(3, draft.widthMm / 4, draft.heightMm / 4) * PX_PER_MM;
  const width = Math.max(20, draft.widthMm) * PX_PER_MM;
  const height = Math.max(8, editorHeightMm({ ...draft, text: value })) * PX_PER_MM;

  return (
    <div
      className="absolute z-30"
      style={{
        left: draft.xMm * PX_PER_MM,
        top: draft.yMm * PX_PER_MM,
        width,
        height,
        transform: draft.rotation ? `rotate(${draft.rotation}deg)` : undefined,
        transformOrigin: "center",
        opacity: draft.opacity,
      }}
    >
      {draft.frameType === 3 && (
        <div
          className="absolute inset-0"
          style={{ transform: "translate(1.2mm, 1.2mm)", background: "rgba(20,22,27,0.2)", borderRadius: radius }}
        />
      )}
      <textarea
        ref={inputRef}
        value={value}
        aria-label="Edit text box"
        spellCheck={false}
        onChange={(event) => setValue(event.target.value)}
        onBlur={() => finish(true)}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === "Escape") {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            finish(false);
            return;
          }
          if (event.key === "Enter" && (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey)) {
            event.preventDefault();
            finish(true);
            return;
          }
          if (!(event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey)) return;
          const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
          if (key === "b") {
            event.preventDefault();
            onPatch({ bold: !draft.bold });
          } else if (key === "i") {
            event.preventDefault();
            onPatch({ italic: !draft.italic });
          } else if (key === "u") {
            event.preventDefault();
            onPatch({ underline: !draft.underline });
          } else if (key === "m") {
            event.preventDefault();
            onFit(value);
          }
        }}
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          inset: 0,
          width: "100%",
          height: "100%",
          resize: "none",
          overflow: "hidden",
          padding: `${TEXT_PADDING_MM * PX_PER_MM}px`,
          fontFamily: textFontCss(draft.fontFamily, draft.bold, draft.italic),
          fontSize: fontSizePx,
          fontStyle: draft.italic ? "italic" : "normal",
          fontWeight: draft.bold ? 700 : 400,
          textDecoration: `${draft.underline ? "underline " : ""}${draft.strikeOut ? "line-through" : ""}`.trim() || "none",
          lineHeight: 1.25,
          color: draft.textColor,
          textAlign: draft.align,
          background: draft.boxFillTransparent ? "transparent" : draft.boxFillColor,
          border: draft.frameType === 0 ? "1px dashed var(--accent)" : `${Math.max(1, draft.lineWidthMm * PX_PER_MM)}px solid ${draft.frameColor}`,
          borderRadius: draft.frameType === 2 ? radius : 0,
          outline: "none",
          caretColor: draft.textColor,
          boxShadow: draft.frameType === 0 ? "0 0 0 1px var(--accent-soft)" : undefined,
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src\components\canvas\TextView.tsx (3.3 KB)
================================================================================
import type { TextAnnotation } from "../../lib/annotationTypes";
import { layoutText, measureTextWidthMm, PT_TO_MM, TEXT_PADDING_MM, textFontCss } from "../../lib/textLayout";

/** Vector text box: shared by the canvas and (mirrored in pdf-lib) the export. */
export function TextView({ annotation }: { annotation: TextAnnotation }) {
  const layout = layoutText(annotation);
  const cx = annotation.xMm + annotation.widthMm / 2;
  const cy = annotation.yMm + annotation.heightMm / 2;
  const fontSizeMm = layout.fontSizePt * PT_TO_MM;
  const padding = TEXT_PADDING_MM;
  const frameStroke = annotation.frameType === 0 ? "none" : annotation.frameColor;
  const fill = annotation.boxFillTransparent ? "none" : annotation.boxFillColor;
  const radius = Math.min(3, annotation.widthMm / 4, annotation.heightMm / 4);

  return (
    <g transform={`rotate(${annotation.rotation} ${cx} ${cy})`} opacity={annotation.opacity}>
      {annotation.frameType === 3 && (
        <rect
          x={annotation.xMm + 1.3}
          y={annotation.yMm + 1.3}
          width={annotation.widthMm}
          height={annotation.heightMm}
          rx={0}
          fill="#14161b"
          opacity={0.18}
        />
      )}
      <rect
        x={annotation.xMm}
        y={annotation.yMm}
        width={annotation.widthMm}
        height={annotation.heightMm}
        rx={annotation.frameType === 2 ? radius : 0}
        fill={fill}
        stroke={frameStroke}
        strokeWidth={annotation.frameType === 0 ? 0 : annotation.lineWidthMm}
      />
      {layout.lines.map((line, index) => {
        if (!line.text) return null;
        const widthMm = measureTextWidthMm(line.text, annotation.fontFamily, annotation.bold, layout.fontSizePt);
        let x = annotation.xMm + padding;
        if (annotation.align === "center") x = annotation.xMm + (annotation.widthMm - widthMm) / 2;
        if (annotation.align === "right") x = annotation.xMm + annotation.widthMm - padding - widthMm;
        const scriptShift =
          annotation.script === "sub" ? fontSizeMm * 0.22 : annotation.script === "super" ? -fontSizeMm * 0.3 : 0;
        const baseline = annotation.yMm + padding + line.topMm + fontSizeMm * 0.82 + scriptShift;
        const size = annotation.script === "normal" ? fontSizeMm : fontSizeMm * 0.72;
        return (
          <g key={index}>
            <text
              x={x}
              y={baseline}
              fontSize={size}
              fill={annotation.textColor}
              style={{ fontFamily: textFontCss(annotation.fontFamily, annotation.bold, annotation.italic), whiteSpace: "pre" }}
            >
              {line.text}
            </text>
            {annotation.underline && (
              <line x1={x} y1={baseline + 0.55} x2={x + widthMm} y2={baseline + 0.55} stroke={annotation.textColor} strokeWidth={Math.max(0.25, fontSizeMm * 0.07)} />
            )}
            {annotation.strikeOut && (
              <line
                x1={x}
                y1={baseline - fontSizeMm * 0.28}
                x2={x + widthMm}
                y2={baseline - fontSizeMm * 0.28}
                stroke={annotation.textColor}
                strokeWidth={Math.max(0.22, fontSizeMm * 0.06)}
              />
            )}
          </g>
        );
      })}
    </g>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Command, FileText, Printer, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { paperTemplates } from "../lib/data";
import { TOOLS } from "../lib/tools";
import type { ToolId } from "../lib/types";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { TOOL_ICONS, type IconComponent } from "./toolIcons";

interface CommandItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  hint: string;
  group: "Tools" | "Actions" | "Paper";
  Icon: IconComponent;
  run: () => void;
}

export function CommandPalette() {
  const open = useUIStore((state) => state.paletteOpen);
  const setPaletteOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setPaletteOpen);
  const setTheme = useUIStore((state) => state.setTheme);
  const setNewDocOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setNewDocOpen);
  const setLibraryOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setLibraryOpen);
  const toggleSidebar = useUIStore((state) => state.toggleSidebar);
  const setPresentation = useUIStore((state) => state.setPresentation);
  const setSettingsOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setSettingsOpen);
  const setTool = useToolStore((state) => state.setTool);
  const newTab = useDocStore((state) => state.newTab);
  const setFit = useDocStore((state) => state.setFit);
  const setZoom = useDocStore((state) => state.setZoom);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const items = useMemo<CommandItem[]>(() => {
    const close = () => setPaletteOpen(false);
    const toolItems = TOOLS.map((tool) => ({
      id: `tool-${tool.id}`,
      label: tool.name,
      hint: tool.shortcut,
      group: "Tools" as const,
      Icon: TOOL_ICONS[tool.id],
      run: () => {
        setTool(tool.id as ToolId);
        close();
      },
    }));
    const actions: CommandItem[] = [
      {
        id: "new-document",
        label: "New document…",
        hint: "129 paper recipes",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: FileText,
        run: () => {
          setNewDocOpen(true);
          close();
        },
      },
      {
        id: "stamp-library",
        label: "Open stamp library",
        hint: "Factory and own stamps",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: TOOL_ICONS.stamp,
        run: () => {
          setLibraryOpen(true);
          close();
        },
      },
      {
        id: "settings",
        label: "Open settings",
        hint: "Shortcuts, preferences",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: Command,
        run: () => {
          setSettingsOpen(true);
          close();
        },
      },
      {
        id: "print",
        label: "Print current page",
        hint: "Ctrl+P",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: Printer,
        run: () => {
          close();
          window.print();
        },
      },
      {
        id: "fit-page",
        label: "Fit page",
        hint: "Zoom",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: Command,
        run: () => {
          setFit("page");
          close();
        },
      },
      {
        id: "fit-width",
        label: "Fit width",
        hint: "Zoom",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: Command,
        run: () => {
          setFit("width");
          close();
        },
      },
      {
        id: "zoom-100",
        label: "Zoom to 100 %",
        hint: "⌘0",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: Command,
        run: () => {
          setZoom(1);
          close();
        },
      },
      {
        id: "toggle-sidebar",
        label: "Toggle sidebar",
        hint: "Panels",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: FileText,
        run: () => {
          toggleSidebar();
          close();
        },
      },
      {
        id: "presentation",
        label: "Enter presentation mode",
        hint: "Full screen canvas",
        group: "Actions",
        Icon: Command,
        run: () => {
          setPresentation(true);
          close();
        },
      },
      { id: "theme-light", label: "Theme: Apple Light", hint: "Appearance", group: "Actions", Icon: Command, run: () => { setTheme("light"); close(); } },
      { id: "theme-dark", label: "Theme: Apple Dark", hint: "Appearance", group: "Actions", Icon: Command, run: () => { setTheme("dark"); close(); } },
      { id: "theme-sepia", label: "Theme: Sepia", hint: "Appearance", group: "Actions", Icon: Command, run: () => { setTheme("sepia"); close(); } },
    ];
    const paperItems: CommandItem[] = paperTemplates.map((template) => ({
      id: `paper-${template.id}`,
      label: template.name,
      hint: `${template.styleLabel} · ${template.detail || template.page.size}`,
      group: "Paper",
      Icon: FileText,
      run: () => {
        newTab(template.id, template.name);
        close();
      },
    }));
    return [...toolItems, ...actions, ...paperItems];
  }, [newTab, setFit, setLibraryOpen, setNewDocOpen, setPaletteOpen, setPresentation, setSettingsOpen, setTheme, setTool, setZoom, toggleSidebar]);

  const results = useMemo(() => {
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    const matches = needle
      ? items.filter((item) => `${item.label} ${item.hint}`.toLowerCase().includes(needle))
      : items.filter((item) => item.group !== "Paper").concat(items.filter((item) => item.group === "Paper").slice(0, 6));
    return matches.slice(0, 60);
  }, [items, query]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setQuery("");
    setIndex(0);
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 20);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIndex(0);
  }, [query]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const run = (item: CommandItem | undefined) => {
    if (!item) return;
    item.run();
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex justify-center bg-black/25 px-4 pt-[10vh] backdrop-blur-[3px]"
      onPointerDown={(event) => {
        if (event.target === event.currentTarget) setPaletteOpen(false);
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="glass-strong anim-in flex max-h-[70vh] w-full max-w-[580px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label="Command palette"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-3.5 py-3">
          <Search size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} className="text-muted" />
          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            className="flex-1 border-0 bg-transparent text-[13.5px] outline-none placeholder:text-muted"
            placeholder="Search tools, stamps, paper and actions…"
            value={query}
            onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === "ArrowDown") {
                event.preventDefault();
                setIndex((current) => Math.min(results.length - 1, current + 1));
              } else if (event.key === "ArrowUp") {
                event.preventDefault();
                setIndex((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1));
              } else if (event.key === "Enter") {
                event.preventDefault();
                run(results[index]);
              } else if (event.key === "Escape") {
                event.preventDefault();
                setPaletteOpen(false);
              }
            }}
            aria-label="Command search"
          />
          <kbd className="rounded border border-[var(--border)] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9.5px] text-muted">Esc</kbd>
        </div>

        <div className="scroll-thin min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-1.5">
          {results.length === 0 && (
            <p className="px-3 py-8 text-center text-[12px] text-muted">Nothing matches “{query}”.</p>
          )}
          {results.map((item, itemIndex) => {
            const showGroup = itemIndex === 0 || results[itemIndex - 1].group !== item.group;
            const Icon = item.Icon;
            return (
              <div key={item.id}>
                {showGroup && (
                  <div className="px-2.5 pb-1 pt-2 text-[9.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">
                    {item.group}
                  </div>
                )}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left"
                  style={itemIndex === index ? { background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)" } : undefined}
                  onPointerEnter={() => setIndex(itemIndex)}
                  onClick={() => run(item)}
                >
                  <Icon size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                  <span className="flex-1 truncate text-[12.5px]">{item.label}</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-muted">{item.hint}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-t border-[var(--border)] px-3.5 py-2 text-[10px] text-muted">
          <span>↑↓ navigate</span>
          <span>↵ open</span>
          <span className="ml-auto">{items.length} commands</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Pencil, X } from "lucide-react";
import { penStyleOptions, inkPath } from "../lib/ink";
import type { InkPoint } from "../lib/annotationTypes";
import { useFavoritesStore, type FavoriteTool } from "../stores/useFavoritesStore";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { TOOL_ICONS } from "./toolIcons";

/** A small ink swatch that shows the saved colour, nib and stroke style. */
function FavoriteGlyph({ item }: { item: FavoriteTool }) {
  const color = item.props.color ?? "#101114";
  const style = item.penStyle ?? item.props.penStyle ?? "pro";
  const level = item.props.pressureLevel ?? 55;
  // Capped so a 16 mm nib still reads as a delicate wave inside a 28 px icon.
  const nib = Math.max(1.4, Math.min(3, 1.4 + Math.sqrt(item.props.thicknessMm ?? 1) * 0.4));
  const opacity = item.props.opacity ?? 1;

  const wave = useMemo(() => {
    const points: InkPoint[] = [];
    for (let index = 0; index <= 24; index += 1) {
      const t = index / 24;
      points.push([3 + t * 26, 14 + Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2) * 6, 0.35 + Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 3) * 0.5 + 0.5]);
    }
    return inkPath(points, nib, { ...penStyleOptions(style, level), simulatePressure: false });
  }, [level, nib, style]);

  if (item.tool === "pen" || item.tool === "formula") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 32 28" className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true">
        <path d={wave} fill={color} opacity={opacity} />
      </svg>
    );
  }
  if (item.tool === "marker") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 32 28" className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true">
        <circle cx="16" cy="14" r="9" fill={color} opacity={0.28} />
        <circle cx="16" cy="14" r="6" fill={color} opacity={Math.min(1, opacity + 0.35)} />
      </svg>
    );
  }
  if (item.tool === "text") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 32 28" className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true">
        <text x="16" y="21" textAnchor="middle" fontFamily="Georgia, 'Times New Roman', serif" fontSize="18" fill={color}>
          T
        </text>
      </svg>
    );
  }
  if (item.tool === "line") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 32 28" className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true">
        <line x1="6" y1="22" x2="26" y2="7" stroke={color} strokeWidth={nib} strokeLinecap="round" />
      </svg>
    );
  }
  if (item.tool === "arrow") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 32 28" className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true">
        <line x1="5" y1="14" x2="24" y2="14" stroke={color} strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" />
        <path d="M20 9l7 5-7 5z" fill={color} />
      </svg>
    );
  }
  const Icon = TOOL_ICONS[item.tool];
  return <Icon size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />;
}

export function FavoriteToolsBar() {
  const open = useUIStore((state) => state.favoritesOpen);
  const sidebarOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.sidebarOpen);
  const favorites = useFavoritesStore((state) => state.favorites);
  const remove = useFavoritesStore((state) => state.remove);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);

  if (!open) return null;

  const activate = (item: FavoriteTool) => {
    const tools = useToolStore.getState();
    tools.setTool(item.tool);
    tools.setProps({ ...item.props, ...(item.penStyle ? { penStyle: item.penStyle } : {}) });
    useUIStore.getState().openToolPopover();
  };

  return (
    <div className="favorite-dock absolute top-1/2 z-30 -translate-y-1/2" style={{ left: sidebarOpen ? 348 : 68 }}>
      <div className="glass no-scrollbar flex max-h-[calc(100vh-140px)] w-11 flex-col items-center gap-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto rounded-full px-0 py-1.5">
        {favorites.map((item) => (
          <div key={item.id} className="tooltip relative">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-full border border-transparent transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--hover)]"
              aria-label={item.label}
              title={item.label}
              onContextMenu={(event) => {
                event.preventDefault();
                remove(item.id);
              }}
              onClick={() => activate(item)}
            >
              <FavoriteGlyph item={item} />
            </button>
            {editing && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 grid h-4 w-4 place-items-center rounded-full"
                style={{ background: "var(--danger)", color: "#fff" }}
                aria-label={`Remove ${item.label}`}
                onClick={(event) => {
                  event.stopPropagation();
                  remove(item.id);
                }}
              >
                <X size={9} strokeWidth={2.5} />
              </button>
            )}
            <span className="tooltip-body">{item.label}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="my-0.5 h-px w-5 bg-[var(--border)]" />
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
          aria-label={editing ? "Finish editing favourites" : "Edit favourites"}
          aria-pressed={editing}
          title={editing ? "Done" : "Edit favourites"}
          onClick={() => setEditing((value) => !value)}
        >
          <Pencil size={12} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { AlignCenterHorizontal, AlignCenterVertical, AlignEndHorizontal, AlignEndVertical, AlignStartHorizontal, AlignStartVertical, BringToFront, Columns3, Ellipsis, Eraser, Group, HeartPlus, Lock, PanelRightClose, RotateCcw, Ruler, Rows3, Save, SendToBack, Trash2, Ungroup, Unlock } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
