const ALIGNMENTS: { mode: AlignMode; label: string; Icon: typeof AlignStartVertical }[] = [
  { mode: "left", label: "Align left", Icon: AlignStartVertical },
  { mode: "center", label: "Align centre", Icon: AlignCenterVertical },
  { mode: "right", label: "Align right", Icon: AlignEndVertical },
  { mode: "top", label: "Align top", Icon: AlignStartHorizontal },
  { mode: "middle", label: "Align middle", Icon: AlignCenterHorizontal },
  { mode: "bottom", label: "Align bottom", Icon: AlignEndHorizontal },
];

function firstValue<T>(annotations: Annotation[], pick: (annotation: Annotation) => T | undefined): T | undefined {
  for (const annotation of annotations) {
    const value = pick(annotation);
    if (value !== undefined) return value;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function MeasureInspector({ pageWidthMm }: { pageWidthMm: number }) {
  const measureMode = useUIStore((state) => state.measureMode);
  const setMeasureMode = useUIStore((state) => state.setMeasureMode);
  const measureUnit = usePrefsStore((state) => state.measureUnit);
  const setPrefs = usePrefsStore((state) => state.set);
  const custom = useScaleStore((state) => state.custom);
  const activeId = useScaleStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const setActive = useScaleStore((state) => state.setActive);
  const addCustom = useScaleStore((state) => state.addCustom);
  const [pageLength, setPageLength] = useState(() => Number(pageWidthMm.toFixed(1)));
  const [realLength, setRealLength] = useState(210);

  const denominator = calibrateDenominator(pageLength, realLength, measureUnit);
  const scales = [...PRESET_SCALES, ...custom];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)] p-2.5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] font-medium">
        <Ruler size={14} /> Measure
      </div>
      <Segmented value={measureMode} options={MEASURE_MODES} onChange={setMeasureMode} />

      <div className="field">
        <span className="field-label">Scale</span>
        <select className="text-input" value={activeId} onChange={(event) => setActive(event.target.value)} aria-label="Measurement scale">
          {scales.map((scale) => (
            <option key={scale.id} value={scale.id}>
              {scale.label}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="field">
        <span className="field-label">Default unit</span>
        <Segmented
          value={measureUnit}
          options={MEASURE_UNITS}
          onChange={(value) => setPrefs({ measureUnit: value })}
        />
      </div>

      <details className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)] px-2.5 py-2">
        <summary className="cursor-pointer text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">Calibrate</summary>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-2.5">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
            <label className="field">
              <span className="field-label">On page (mm)</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                min={1}
                step={0.1}
                className="text-input"
                value={pageLength}
                onChange={(event) => setPageLength(Number(event.target.value))}
              />
            </label>
            <label className="field">
              <span className="field-label">Real length ({measureUnit})</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                min={0.1}
                step={0.1}
                className="text-input"
                value={realLength}
                onChange={(event) => setRealLength(Number(event.target.value))}
              />
            </label>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1">
            <button type="button" className="chip h-7 justify-center" onClick={() => setPageLength(Number(pageWidthMm.toFixed(1)))}>
              From page
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="chip h-7 justify-center"
              disabled={!denominator}
              onClick={() => {
                if (!denominator) return;
                addCustom({ id: `custom-${Date.now().toString(36)}`, label: customScaleLabel(denominator), denominator });
              }}
            >
              <Save size={11} /> Save scale
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </details>
    </div>
  );
}

function EraserPanel({ count }: { count: number }) {
  const split = useUIStore((state) => state.eraserSplit);
  const setSplit = useUIStore((state) => state.setEraserSplit);
  const size = useUIStore((state) => state.eraserSizeMm);
  const setSize = useUIStore((state) => state.setEraserSize);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)] p-2.5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] font-medium">
        <Eraser size={14} /> Eraser
      </div>
      <Segmented
        value={split ? "split" : "whole"}
        options={[
          { value: "whole", label: "Whole" },
          { value: "split", label: "Split" },
        ]}
        onChange={(value) => setSplit(value === "split")}
      />
      <SliderRow label="Size" value={size} min={2} max={20} step={0.2} unit="mm" onChange={setSize} />
      <button
        type="button"
        className="chip h-7 justify-center"
        disabled={count === 0}
        onClick={() => useAnnotationStore.getState().mutate(() => [])}
      >
        <Trash2 size={12} /> Clear all
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function SelectionInspector({ annotations }: { annotations: Annotation[] }) {
  const update = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.update);
  const align = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.align);
  const distribute = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.distribute);
  const groupSelection = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.groupSelection);
  const ungroupSelection = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.ungroupSelection);
  const setLocked = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.setLocked);
  const bringToFront = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.bringToFront);
  const sendToBack = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.sendToBack);
  const deleteSelection = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.deleteSelection);
  const ids = annotations.map((annotation) => annotation.id);

  const color = firstValue(annotations, (annotation) =>
    annotation.type === "shape"
      ? annotation.strokeColor
      : annotation.type === "text"
        ? annotation.textColor
        : annotation.type === "stamp"
          ? undefined
          : annotation.color,
  );
  const thickness = firstValue(annotations, (annotation) =>
    annotation.type === "shape"
      ? annotation.lineWidthMm
      : annotation.type === "pen" || annotation.type === "marker"
        ? annotation.thicknessMm
        : undefined,
  );
  const opacity = annotations[0]?.opacity ?? 1;
  const fill = firstValue(annotations, (annotation) => (annotation.type === "shape" ? annotation.fillColor : undefined));
  const lineStyle = firstValue(annotations, (annotation) => (annotation.type === "shape" ? annotation.lineStyle : undefined));
  const locked = annotations.every((annotation) => annotation.locked);
  const grouped = annotations.some((annotation) => annotation.groupId);
  const kinds = [...new Set(annotations.map((annotation) => annotation.type === "shape" ? SHAPE_LABEL[annotation.shapeType] : annotation.type === "pen" ? "Pen" : annotation.type === "marker" ? "Highlighter" : annotation.type === "text" ? "Text" : annotation.type === "formula" ? "Formula" : "Stamp"))];

  const patch = (next: AnnotationPatch) => update(ids, next);
  const singleText = annotations.length === 1 && annotations[0].type === "text" ? annotations[0] : null;
  const singleFormula = annotations.length === 1 && annotations[0].type === "formula" ? annotations[0] : null;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)] p-2.5" style={{ background: "var(--panel)" }}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-lg" style={{ background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)" }}>
          <Group size={14} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        </span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="text-[12px] font-semibold">
            {annotations.length} selected
          </div>
          <div className="truncate text-[10px] text-muted">{kinds.join(" · ")}</div>
        </div>
        <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-6 w-6" title="Delete" aria-label="Delete selection" onClick={deleteSelection}>
          <Trash2 size={12} />
        </button>
      </div>

      {color !== undefined && (
        <ColorRow
          label="Colour"
          value={color}
          onChange={(value) => value && patch({ color: value, strokeColor: value, textColor: value })}
        />
      )}
      {thickness !== undefined && (
        <SliderRow
          label="Width"
          value={thickness}
          min={0.2}
          max={32}
          step={0.1}
          unit="mm"
          onChange={(value) => patch({ thicknessMm: value, lineWidthMm: value })}
        />
      )}
      <SliderRow
        label="Opacity"
        value={Math.round(opacity * 100)}
        min={5}
        max={100}
        step={1}
        unit="%"
        onChange={(value) => patch({ opacity: value / 100 })}
      />
      {fill !== undefined && (
        <ColorRow
          label="Fill"
          value={fill}
          allowNone
          onChange={(value) => patch({ fillColor: value, fillTransparent: value === null })}
        />
      )}
      {lineStyle !== undefined && (
        <div className="field">
          <span className="field-label">Line style</span>
          <Segmented
            value={lineStyle}
            options={[
              { value: "solid", label: "Solid" },
              { value: "dash", label: "Dash" },
              { value: "dot", label: "Dot" },
              { value: "dash-dot", label: "Dash-dot" },
            ]}
            onChange={(value) => patch({ lineStyle: value })}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {singleText && (
        <>
          <SectionTitle>Text</SectionTitle>
          <div className="field">
            <span className="field-label">Font</span>
            <select
              className="text-input"
              value={singleText.fontFamily}
              aria-label="Text font"
              onChange={(event) => patch({ fontFamily: event.target.value })}
            >
              {TEXT_FONTS.map((font) => (
                <option key={font} value={font}>
                  {font}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <SliderRow
            label="Size"
            value={singleText.fontSizePt}
            min={6}
            max={72}
            step={1}
            unit="pt"
            onChange={(value) => patch({ fontSizePt: value })}
          />
          <ColorRow label="Text" value={singleText.textColor} onChange={(value) => value && patch({ textColor: value })} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1">
            {[
              { label: "Bold", active: singleText.bold, toggle: () => patch({ bold: !singleText.bold }) },
              { label: "Italic", active: singleText.italic, toggle: () => patch({ italic: !singleText.italic }) },
              { label: "Underline", active: singleText.underline, toggle: () => patch({ underline: !singleText.underline }) },
              { label: "Strike", active: singleText.strikeOut, toggle: () => patch({ strikeOut: !singleText.strikeOut }) },
            ].map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item.label}
                type="button"
                className="chip h-7 justify-center"
                aria-pressed={item.active}
                onClick={item.toggle}
              >
                {item.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="field">
            <span className="field-label">Align</span>
            <Segmented<TextAlign>
              value={singleText.align}
              options={[
                { value: "left", label: "Left" },
                { value: "center", label: "Centre" },
                { value: "right", label: "Right" },
              ]}
              onChange={(value) => patch({ align: value })}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="field">
            <span className="field-label">Frame</span>
            <Segmented
              value={String(singleText.frameType)}
              options={[
                { value: "0", label: "None" },
                { value: "1", label: "Box" },
                { value: "2", label: "Round" },
                { value: "3", label: "Shadow" },
              ]}
              onChange={(value) => patch({ frameType: Number(value) as 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 })}
            />
          </div>
          <ColorRow
            label="Text box fill"
            value={singleText.boxFillTransparent ? null : singleText.boxFillColor}
            allowNone
            onChange={(value) =>
              patch({ boxFillColor: value ?? "#ffffff", boxFillTransparent: value === null })
            }
          />
        </>
      )}

      {singleFormula && (
        <>
          <SectionTitle>Formula</SectionTitle>
          <label className="field">
            <span className="field-label">LaTeX</span>
            <input
              className="text-input"
              value={singleFormula.latex}
              aria-label="Formula LaTeX"
              onChange={(event) => patch({ latex: event.target.value })}
            />
          </label>
          <SliderRow
            label="Size"
            value={singleFormula.fontSizePt}
            min={6}
            max={72}
            step={1}
            unit="pt"
            onChange={(value) => patch({ fontSizePt: value })}
          />
          <ColorRow label="Ink" value={singleFormula.color} onChange={(value) => value && patch({ color: value })} />
          <ColorRow
            label="Fill"
            value={singleFormula.fillTransparent ? null : singleFormula.fillColor}
            allowNone
            onChange={(value) => patch({ fillColor: value ?? "#ffffff", fillTransparent: value === null })}
          />
        </>
      )}

      {annotations.length >= 2 && (
        <>
          <SectionTitle>Align</SectionTitle>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-6 gap-1">
            {ALIGNMENTS.map(({ mode, label, Icon }) => (
              <button key={mode} type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-full" title={label} aria-label={label} onClick={() => align(mode)}>
                <Icon size={13} />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
      {annotations.length >= 3 && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1">
          <button type="button" className="chip h-7 justify-center" onClick={() => distribute("x")}>
            <Columns3 size={12} /> Distribute H
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="chip h-7 justify-center" onClick={() => distribute("y")}>
            <Rows3 size={12} /> Distribute V
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1">
        <button type="button" className="chip h-7 justify-center" onClick={grouped ? ungroupSelection : groupSelection} title={grouped ? "Ungroup" : "Group"}>
          {grouped ? <Ungroup size={12} /> : <Group size={12} />} {grouped ? "Ungroup" : "Group"}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="chip h-7 justify-center" onClick={() => setLocked(!locked)}>
          {locked ? <Unlock size={12} /> : <Lock size={12} />} {locked ? "Unlock" : "Lock"}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="chip h-7 justify-center" onClick={locked ? sendToBack : bringToFront}>
          {locked ? <SendToBack size={12} /> : <BringToFront size={12} />} {locked ? "Back" : "Front"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function Inspector() {
  const activeTool = useToolStore((state) => state.activeTool);
  const selectedStampId = useToolStore((state) => state.selectedStampId);
  const props = useToolStore((state) => state.props[state.activeTool]);
  const setProps = useToolStore((state) => state.setProps);
  const addFavorite = useFavoritesStore((state) => state.add);
  const toolPopoverOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.toolPopoverOpen);
  const toolPopoverPinned = useUIStore((state) => state.toolPopoverPinned);
  const toggleToolPopoverPinned = useUIStore((state) => state.toggleToolPopoverPinned);
  const closeToolPopover = useUIStore((state) => state.closeToolPopover);
  const setToast = useUIStore((state) => state.setToast);
  const [presetsOpen, setPresetsOpen] = useState(false);

  const tabs = useDocStore((state) => state.tabs);
  const activeId = useDocStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const pageIndex = useDocStore((state) => state.page);
  const selection = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.selection);

  const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === activeId) ?? tabs[0];
  const pageNumber = Math.min(Math.max(1, pageIndex), tab.pages.length);
  const page = tab.pages[pageNumber - 1] ?? tab.pages[0];
  const size = pageSizeMm(tab, page);
  const chosen = page.annotations.filter((annotation) => selection.includes(annotation.id));
  const customStamps = useCustomStampStore((state) => state.stamps);

  const meta = TOOL_BY_ID.get(activeTool);
  const Icon = TOOL_ICONS[activeTool];

  const selectedStamp = useMemo(() => {
    const stamp = resolveStamp(selectedStampId);
    if (!stamp) return null;
    const sub = stamp.source === "factory" ? "Factory stamp" : stamp.source === "user" ? "My stamps" : "Saved stamp";
    return { name: stamp.name, imageUrl: stamp.imageUrl, width: stamp.width, height: stamp.height, sub };
    // customStamps is read so a store update re-resolves the preview.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [customStamps, selectedStampId]);

  if (!toolPopoverOpen) return null;
  // Direct-acting tools stay bare unless something is selected.
  if (chosen.length === 0 && NO_POPOVER_TOOLS.includes(activeTool)) return null;

  const addCurrentFavorite = () => {
    addFavorite({ label: meta?.name ?? "Tool", tool: activeTool, props: { ...props }, penStyle: props.penStyle });
    setToast(`${meta?.name ?? "Tool"} added to favourite tools`);
  };

  const resetTool = () => {
    const factory = presetsFor(activeTool, presets)[0];
    setProps({ ...BASE_PROPS, ...(factory ? presetPatch(factory) : {}) });
  };

  return (
    <aside className="inspector glass anim-in absolute right-[78px] top-1/2 z-30 flex max-h-[calc(100vh-96px)] w-[320px] -translate-y-1/2 flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-3 py-2.5">
        <div className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl" style={{ background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)" }}>
          <Icon size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]">{chosen.length > 0 ? "Selection" : meta?.name}</span>
          {chosen.length === 0 && (
            <kbd className="rounded border border-[var(--border)] px-1 py-px text-[9px] font-medium text-muted">{meta?.shortcut}</kbd>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5">
          {chosen.length === 0 && meta?.needsProperties && activeTool !== "stamp" && (
            <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7" aria-label="Add to favourite tools" title="Add to favourite tools" onClick={addCurrentFavorite}>
              <HeartPlus size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
          )}
          {chosen.length === 0 && meta?.needsProperties && (
            <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7" aria-label="Reset tool defaults" title="Reset tool defaults" onClick={resetTool}>
              <RotateCcw size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
          )}
          {chosen.length === 0 && meta?.needsProperties && activeTool !== "stamp" && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
              aria-label="Factory presets"
              aria-pressed={presetsOpen}
              title="Factory presets"
              onClick={() => setPresetsOpen((open) => !open)}
            >
              <Ellipsis size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
          )}
          <button
            type="button"
            className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
            aria-label={toolPopoverPinned ? "Unpin popover" : "Pin popover"}
            aria-pressed={toolPopoverPinned}
            title={toolPopoverPinned ? "Pinned: stays open while drawing" : "Unpinned: collapses when you draw"}
            onClick={toggleToolPopoverPinned}
          >
            {toolPopoverPinned ? <Lock size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> : <Unlock size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
            aria-label="Close popover"
            title="Close"
            onClick={closeToolPopover}
          >
            <PanelRightClose size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="no-scrollbar flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-4 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto px-3 py-3">
        {chosen.length > 0 ? (
          <SelectionInspector annotations={chosen} />
        ) : (
          <>
            {activeTool === "eraser" && <EraserPanel count={page.annotations.length} />}
            {activeTool === "measure" && <MeasureInspector pageWidthMm={size.widthMm} />}

            {meta?.needsProperties &&
              (activeTool === "stamp" ? (
                <>
                  {selectedStamp && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)] p-2">
                      <span className="checker grid h-11 w-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg p-1">
                        {selectedStamp.imageUrl && <img src={selectedStamp.imageUrl} alt={selectedStamp.name} className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain" />}
                      </span>
                      <span className="min-w-0">
                        <span className="block truncate text-[12px] font-medium">{selectedStamp.name}</span>
                        <span className="block text-[10px] text-muted">
                          {selectedStamp.sub} · {selectedStamp.width} × {selectedStamp.height} px
                        </span>
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <StampGallery />
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <PropertyControls tool={activeTool} />

                  {presetsOpen && (
                    <>
                      <div className="divider" />
                      <PresetGallery tool={activeTool} />
                    </>
                  )}
                </>
              ))}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { FileText, Search, X } from "lucide-react";
import { pdfTemplates, paperById, paperTemplates } from "../lib/data";
import type { PaperTemplate } from "../lib/types";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { Segmented } from "./ui/controls";

function TemplateThumb({ template }: { template: PaperTemplate }) {
  const { widthMm, heightMm, landscape } = template.page;
  const w = landscape ? heightMm : widthMm;
  const h = landscape ? widthMm : heightMm;
  const ratio = w / h;
  const maxW = 58;
  const maxH = 82;
  const boxW = ratio >= maxW / maxH ? maxW : maxH * ratio;
  const boxH = ratio >= maxW / maxH ? maxW / ratio : maxH;
  const x = (70 - boxW) / 2;
  const y = (94 - boxH) / 2;
  const grid = template.grid;
  const stepMm = grid ? (grid.deltaYMm ?? grid.deltaMm ?? (grid.deltaXMm ? grid.deltaXMm : 5)) : 0;
  const rows = grid ? (stepMm <= 2 ? 11 : stepMm <= 6 ? 7 : 4) : 0;
  const cols = grid && (grid.deltaXMm || grid.kind === "grid") ? (stepMm <= 2 ? 8 : stepMm <= 6 ? 5 : 3) : 0;
  const bounds = template.bounds;

  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 70 94" className="h-[86px] w-[64px]" aria-hidden="true">
      <rect x={x} y={y} width={boxW} height={boxH} rx="1" fill="var(--paper)" stroke="var(--border)" />
      {rows > 0 &&
        Array.from({ length: rows - 1 }, (_, index) => (
          <line
            key={`r${index}`}
            x1={x + 2}
            x2={x + boxW - 2}
            y1={y + (boxH / rows) * (index + 1)}
            y2={y + (boxH / rows) * (index + 1)}
            stroke="#a9adb5"
            strokeWidth="0.5"
            opacity="0.65"
          />
        ))}
      {cols > 0 &&
        Array.from({ length: cols - 1 }, (_, index) => (
          <line
            key={`c${index}`}
            y1={y + 2}
            y2={y + boxH - 2}
            x1={x + (boxW / cols) * (index + 1)}
            x2={x + (boxW / cols) * (index + 1)}
            stroke="#a9adb5"
            strokeWidth="0.5"
            opacity="0.65"
          />
        ))}
      {bounds && (
        <rect
          x={x + (bounds.left / w) * boxW}
          y={y + (bounds.top / h) * boxH}
          width={boxW * (1 - (bounds.left + bounds.right) / w)}
          height={boxH * (1 - (bounds.top + bounds.bottom) / h)}
          fill="none"
          stroke="var(--accent)"
          strokeWidth="0.5"
          strokeDasharray="2 1.5"
          opacity="0.7"
        />
      )}
      <text x="35" y="92" textAnchor="middle" fontSize="4.6" fill="var(--muted)">
        {w} × {h} mm
      </text>
    </svg>
  );
}

export function NewDocumentModal() {
  const open = useUIStore((state) => state.newDocOpen);
  const setNewDocOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setNewDocOpen);
  const tabs = useDocStore((state) => state.tabs);
  const activeId = useDocStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const activePageIndex = useDocStore((state) => state.page);
  const applyTemplate = useDocStore((state) => state.applyTemplate);
  const newTab = useDocStore((state) => state.newTab);

  const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === activeId) ?? tabs[0];
  const activePage = tab.pages[activePageIndex - 1] ?? tab.pages[0];
  const [size, setSize] = useState("All");
  const [style, setStyle] = useState("All");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(activePage.templateId);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      const state = useDocStore.getState();
      const current = state.tabs.find((item) => item.id === state.activeId);
      setSelectedId(current?.pages[state.page - 1]?.templateId ?? current?.templateId ?? tab.templateId);
    }
  }, [open, tab.templateId]);

  const styles = useMemo(() => {
    const seen: string[] = [];
    for (const recipe of paperTemplates) if (!seen.includes(recipe.styleLabel)) seen.push(recipe.styleLabel);
    return seen;
  }, []);
  const sizes = useMemo(() => ["All", ...new Set(paperTemplates.map((recipe) => recipe.page.size))], []);

  const visible = useMemo(() => {
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return paperTemplates.filter(
      (recipe) =>
        (size === "All" || recipe.page.size === size) &&
        (style === "All" || recipe.styleLabel === style) &&
        (!needle || recipe.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle)),
    );
  }, [query, size, style]);

  const selected = paperById.get(selectedId) ?? paperTemplates[0];

  if (!open) return null;

  const close = () => setNewDocOpen(false);
  const place = (asNewTab: boolean) => {
    const landscape = selected.page.landscape;
    if (asNewTab) newTab(selected.id, selected.name, landscape);
    else applyTemplate(selected.id, selected.name, landscape);
    useDocStore.getState().setPage(1);
    close();
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/25 p-4 backdrop-blur-[3px]"
      onPointerDown={(event) => {
        if (event.target === event.currentTarget) close();
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="glass-strong anim-in flex max-h-[88vh] w-full max-w-[860px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label="New document"
      >
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-4 py-3">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]">New document</h2>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted">
              {paperTemplates.length} parametric paper recipes migrated from PAStyleTemplates.xml — every line is drawn as
              vector, not bitmap.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button type="button" className="icon-btn" aria-label="Close" onClick={close}>
            <X size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-4 py-2.5">
          <Segmented
            value={size}
            options={sizes.map((value) => ({ value, label: value }))}
            onChange={setSize}
          />
          <div className="scroll-thin flex max-w-full items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto">
            <button type="button" className="chip" aria-pressed={style === "All"} onClick={() => setStyle("All")}>
              All styles
            </button>
            {styles.map((label) => (
              <button
                key={label}
                type="button"
                className="chip"
                aria-pressed={style === label}
                onClick={() => setStyle(label)}
              >
                {label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <label className="relative ml-auto block w-[170px]">
            <Search size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" />
            <input
              className="text-input pl-7"
              placeholder="Find a recipe…"
              value={query}
              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              aria-label="Find a paper recipe"
            />
          </label>
        </div>

        <div className="scroll-thin min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-3">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(124px,1fr))] gap-2">
            {visible.map((recipe) => (
              <button
                key={recipe.id}
                type="button"
                className="tile flex flex-col items-center gap-1 px-2 pb-2 pt-1.5 text-center"
                aria-pressed={selectedId === recipe.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedId(recipe.id)}
                onDoubleClick={() => place(false)}
                title={`${recipe.name}${recipe.detail ? ` · ${recipe.detail}` : ""}`}
              >
                <TemplateThumb template={recipe} />
                <span className="w-full truncate text-[11px] font-medium leading-tight">{recipe.name}</span>
                <span className="w-full truncate text-[9.5px] text-muted">
                  {recipe.detail ? `${recipe.styleLabel} · ${recipe.detail}` : `${recipe.styleLabel} · ${recipe.page.size}`}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          {visible.length === 0 && (
            <p className="py-10 text-center text-[12px] text-muted">No recipe matches these filters.</p>
          )}

          <div className="mt-4 border-t border-[var(--border)] pt-3">
            <h3 className="mb-1.5 text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">Note PDF templates</h3>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {pdfTemplates.map((pdf) => (
                <button
                  key={pdf.name}
                  type="button"
                  className="chip h-7"
                  title={`${Math.round(pdf.bytes / 1024)} kB · sha256 ${pdf.sha256.slice(0, 12)}…`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    void useDocStore.getState().openPdfUrl(pdf.url, pdf.name);
                    close();
                  }}
                >
                  <FileText size={12} strokeWidth={1.75} /> {pdf.name}
                </button>
              ))}
              <button
                type="button"
                className="chip h-7"
                onClick={() => {
                  document.getElementById("pm-pdf-input")?.click();
                  close();
                }}
              >
                <FileText size={12} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Open another PDF…
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--border)] px-4 py-3">
          <div className="min-w-0 text-[11.5px]">
            <span className="text-muted">Selected </span>
            <span className="font-medium">{selected.name}</span>
            <span className="text-muted"> · {selected.page.landscape ? "landscape" : "portrait"}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button type="button" className="chip h-8 px-3" onClick={close}>
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="h-8 rounded-[10px] border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--hover)] px-3 text-[12px] font-medium hover:bg-[var(--active)]"
              onClick={() => place(true)}
            >
              Open in new tab
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="h-8 rounded-[10px] px-3.5 text-[12px] font-semibold"
              style={{ background: "var(--accent)", color: "var(--accent-ink)" }}
              onClick={() => place(false)}
            >
              Use template
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Minimize2 } from "lucide-react";
import { presets } from "../lib/data";
import type { ToolId } from "../lib/types";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { TOOL_ICONS } from "./toolIcons";

/** Category 6 holds the presentation recipes shipped with PDF Annotator. */
const presentationPresets = presets
  .filter((preset) => preset.category === 6 && preset.family !== "stamp")
  .filter(
    (preset, index, list) =>
      list.findIndex((other) => other.family === preset.family && other.title === preset.title) === index,
  );

export function PresentationBar() {
  const page = useDocStore((state) => state.page);
  const setPage = useDocStore((state) => state.setPage);
  const tabs = useDocStore((state) => state.tabs);
  const activeId = useDocStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const setTool = useToolStore((state) => state.setTool);
  const selectPreset = useToolStore((state) => state.selectPreset);
  const setPresentation = useUIStore((state) => state.setPresentation);

  const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === activeId) ?? tabs[0];

  return (
    <div className="presentation-bar glass-strong absolute bottom-5 left-1/2 z-30 flex -translate-x-1/2 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl px-2 py-1.5">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="icon-btn"
        aria-label="Previous page"
        disabled={page <= 1}
        onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}
      >
        <ChevronLeft size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>
      <span className="min-w-[86px] text-center text-[11.5px] tabular-nums text-muted">
        Page {page} / {tab.pageCount}
      </span>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="icon-btn"
        aria-label="Next page"
        disabled={page >= tab.pageCount}
        onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}
      >
        <ChevronRight size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>

      <span className="mx-0.5 h-6 w-px bg-[var(--border)]" />

      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        {presentationPresets.slice(0, 6).map((preset) => {
          const Icon = TOOL_ICONS[preset.family as ToolId] ?? TOOL_ICONS.pen;
          return (
            <button
              key={preset.id}
              type="button"
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] hover:bg-[var(--hover)]"
              title={`${preset.family} · ${preset.title}`}
              onClick={() => {
                setTool(preset.family as ToolId);
                selectPreset(preset);
              }}
            >
              <Icon size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
              <span className="hidden whitespace-nowrap sm:inline">{preset.title}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <span className="mx-0.5 h-6 w-px bg-[var(--border)]" />

      <button
        type="button"
        className="icon-btn"
        aria-label="Exit presentation mode"
        title="Exit presentation mode (Esc)"
        onClick={() => setPresentation(false)}
      >
        <Minimize2 size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { groupById, presets } from "../lib/data";
import { presetsFor } from "../lib/presets";
import type { Preset, ToolId } from "../lib/types";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { SectionTitle } from "./ui/controls";

function PresetSwatch({ preset }: { preset: Preset }) {
  const props = preset.props;
  const color = props.color?.hex ?? props.fontColor?.hex ?? "var(--fg)";
  const back = props.backColor && !props.backColor.transparent ? props.backColor.hex : null;
  const stroke = Math.min(11, Math.max(1, (props.thicknessMm ?? props.lineWidthMm ?? 1) * 1.6));
  const opacity = 1 - (props.inkTransparency ?? 0) / 100;

  if (preset.family === "text") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 100 40" className="h-full w-full" aria-hidden="true">
        <text
          x="50"
          y="26"
          textAnchor="middle"
          fill={color}
          fontSize={Math.min(22, 8 + (props.fontSize ?? 10) * 0.8)}
          fontFamily={props.fontName ?? "Arial"}
          fontWeight={props.bold ? 700 : 400}
          fontStyle={props.italic ? "italic" : "normal"}
        >
          Aa
        </text>
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (preset.family === "stamp") {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 100 40" className="h-full w-full" aria-hidden="true">
        <circle cx="50" cy="20" r="12" fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth="1.6" opacity="0.8" />
        <path d="M44 20.5l4.5 4.5L57 14.5" fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth="2.2" strokeLinecap="round" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (preset.family === "graphical") {
    const common = { fill: "none", stroke: color, strokeWidth: stroke, opacity };
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 100 40" className="h-full w-full" aria-hidden="true">
        {preset.typeCode === 0 && <line x1="14" y1="28" x2="86" y2="12" {...common} strokeLinecap="round" />}
        {preset.typeCode === 1 && (
          <g {...common} strokeLinecap="round">
            <line x1="14" y1="28" x2="82" y2="12" />
            <path d="M82 12l-10-1 4 9z" fill={color} stroke="none" />
          </g>
        )}
        {preset.typeCode === 2 && (
          <rect x="22" y="8" width="56" height="24" rx="2" {...common} fill={back ?? "none"} />
        )}
        {preset.typeCode === 3 && <ellipse cx="50" cy="20" rx="28" ry="12" {...common} fill={back ?? "none"} />}
        {preset.typeCode === 6 && (
          <g {...common} strokeLinecap="round">
            <line x1="16" y1="20" x2="84" y2="20" />
            <path d="M16 20l5-3M16 20l5 3M84 20l-5-3M84 20l-5 3" />
          </g>
        )}
      </svg>
    );
  }

  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 100 40" className="h-full w-full" aria-hidden="true">
      <line
        x1="12"
        y1="20"
        x2="88"
        y2="20"
        stroke={color}
        strokeWidth={stroke}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        opacity={preset.family === "marker" ? Math.min(1, opacity * 0.75) : opacity}
      />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function PresetGallery({ tool }: { tool: ToolId }) {
  const list = useMemo(() => presetsFor(tool, presets), [tool]);
  const activePreset = useToolStore((state) => state.activePreset[tool]);
  const selectPreset = useToolStore((state) => state.selectPreset);

  const grouped = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Preset[]>();
    for (const preset of list) {
      const bucket = map.get(preset.groupSlug) ?? [];
      bucket.push(preset);
      map.set(preset.groupSlug, bucket);
    }
    return [...map.entries()];
  }, [list]);

  if (list.length === 0) {
    return <p className="text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted">This tool has no factory presets; it uses the live controls below.</p>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <SectionTitle aside={<span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{list.length} factory</span>}>Presets</SectionTitle>
      {grouped.map(([slug, items]) => (
        <div key={slug} className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          <div className="text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.07em] text-muted/80">
            {groupById.get(items[0].groupId)?.captions.en ?? items[0].subtitle}
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
            {items.map((preset) => (
              <button
                key={preset.id}
                type="button"
                className="tile flex flex-col overflow-hidden text-left"
                aria-pressed={activePreset === preset.id}
                onClick={() => selectPreset(preset)}
                title={`${preset.title} · ${preset.subtitle}`}
              >
                <span className="h-8 w-full px-2 pt-1.5">
                  <PresetSwatch preset={preset} />
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-col px-2 pb-1.5 pt-0.5">
                  <span className="truncate text-[11px] font-medium leading-tight">{preset.title}</span>
                  <span className="truncate text-[9.5px] text-muted">{preset.subtitle}</span>
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useMemo, type CSSProperties, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { SHAPE_LABEL, type DimensionUnit, type MarkerTip, type TextAlign } from "../lib/annotationTypes";
import { ARROW_HEADS, SWATCHES } from "../lib/presets";
import { inkPath, penStyleOptions } from "../lib/ink";
import type { InkPoint, PenStyle } from "../lib/annotationTypes";
import { isShapeTool, SHAPE_TOOLS } from "../lib/tools";
import type { LineStyleName, ShapeTool, ToolId, ToolProps } from "../lib/types";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { TOOL_ICONS } from "./toolIcons";
import { ColorRow, Segmented, ToggleRow } from "./ui/controls";
import { CustomColorPicker } from "./ui/CustomColorPicker";

const FONTS = ["Arial", "Helvetica", "Times New Roman", "Georgia", "Courier New", "Verdana", "Calibri", "Trebuchet MS"];

const THICKNESS_PRESETS = [0.5, 0.7, 1, 1.5, 2, 2.5, 3, 5, 8, 12, 16];
const THICKNESS_TICKS = [
  { value: 0.5, label: "1" },
  { value: 1, label: "2" },
  { value: 2, label: "3" },
  { value: 4, label: "6" },
  { value: 8, label: "10" },
  { value: 16, label: "24" },
];
const OPACITY_TICKS = [
  { value: 25, label: "25" },
  { value: 50, label: "50" },
  { value: 75, label: "75" },
];
const FONT_SIZES = [8, 10, 12, 14, 18, 24, 32, 48, 64, 72];

const LINE_STYLES: { value: LineStyleName; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "solid", label: "Solid" },
  { value: "dash", label: "Dash" },
  { value: "dot", label: "Dot" },
  { value: "dash-dot", label: "Dash-dot" },
];

const DIMENSION_UNITS: { value: DimensionUnit; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "mm", label: "mm" },
  { value: "cm", label: "cm" },
  { value: "m", label: "m" },
  { value: "in", label: "in" },
  { value: "ft", label: "ft" },
];

const POLYGON_SIDES: { value: string; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "3", label: "3" },
  { value: "4", label: "4" },
  { value: "5", label: "5" },
  { value: "6", label: "6" },
  { value: "8", label: "8" },
];

const MARKER_TIPS: { value: MarkerTip; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "vertical", label: "Vertical" },
  { value: "horizontal", label: "Horizontal" },
  { value: "squared", label: "Squared" },
  { value: "ball", label: "Ball" },
];

function TipIcon({ tip }: { tip: MarkerTip }) {
  if (tip === "vertical") return <rect x="15" y="5" width="8" height="22" rx="1.5" fill="currentColor" />;
  if (tip === "horizontal") return <rect x="5" y="15" width="22" height="8" rx="1.5" fill="currentColor" />;
  if (tip === "squared") return <rect x="9" y="9" width="14" height="14" rx="1.5" fill="currentColor" />;
  return <circle cx="16" cy="16" r="7.5" fill="currentColor" />;
}

const STROKE_STYLES: { value: PenStyle; label: string; hint: string; patch: Partial<ToolProps> }[] = [
  { value: "basic", label: "Basic", hint: "Fixed width", patch: { penStyle: "basic", pressureLevel: 0, pressureSensitive: false, smoothing: false } },
  { value: "pro", label: "Pro", hint: "Variable width", patch: { penStyle: "pro", pressureLevel: 55, pressureSensitive: true, smoothing: false } },
  { value: "calligraphy", label: "Calligraphy", hint: "Dynamic width", patch: { penStyle: "calligraphy", pressureLevel: 85, pressureSensitive: true, smoothing: true } },
];

const DASH: Record<LineStyleName, string | undefined> = {
  solid: undefined,
  dash: "10 6",
  dot: "1.5 5",
  "dash-dot": "10 5 1.5 5",
};

function wavePoints(): InkPoint[] {
  const points: InkPoint[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index <= 72; index += 1) {
    const t = index / 72;
    points.push([6 + t * 308, 36 + Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2.1) * 18, 0.25 + Math.abs(Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2.6)) * 0.75]);
  }
  return points;
}

const WAVE_POINTS = wavePoints();

function strokePreview(style: PenStyle, level: number, thickness: number) {
  return inkPath(WAVE_POINTS, Math.max(0.6, thickness), {
    ...penStyleOptions(style, level),
    simulatePressure: false,
  });
}

/** A live nib always stays slender, however wide the real stroke is. */
function previewNib(tool: ToolId, thicknessMm: number) {
  return tool === "marker"
    ? Math.min(12, 3 + Math.sqrt(thicknessMm) * 1.5)
    : Math.min(9, 1.5 + Math.sqrt(thicknessMm) * 1.4);
}

/** Live ink preview: colour, nib, opacity and stroke style update as you type. */
function WavePreview({ tool, props }: { tool: ToolId; props: ToolProps }) {
  const path = useMemo(
    () => strokePreview(props.penStyle, props.pressureLevel, previewNib(tool, props.thicknessMm)),
    [props, tool],
  );
  const tip = props.tipStyle ?? "vertical";
  const band = tool === "marker" ? (tip === "horizontal" ? 8 : tip === "squared" ? 16 : tip === "ball" ? 16 : 22) : 0;
  return (
    <div
      className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)]"
      style={{ height: 68, background: "linear-gradient(180deg, var(--glass-strong), var(--panel))" }}
    >
      <svg viewBox="0 0 320 72" className="h-full w-full" aria-hidden="true">
        <line x1="6" y1="36" x2="314" y2="36" stroke="var(--border)" strokeDasharray="2 4" />
        {tool === "marker" ? (
          <line
            x1="24"
            y1="36"
            x2="296"
            y2="36"
            stroke={props.color}
            strokeWidth={band}
            strokeLinecap={tip === "squared" ? "square" : tip === "ball" ? "round" : "butt"}
            opacity={props.opacity}
          />
        ) : (
          <path d={path} fill={props.color} opacity={props.opacity} />
        )}
      </svg>
      <span className="absolute bottom-1.5 right-2 text-[9.5px] font-medium text-muted">Live preview</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function MiniStroke({ style, color, selected }: { style: PenStyle; color: string; selected: boolean }) {
  const path = useMemo(
    () =>
      inkPath(
        Array.from({ length: 28 }, (_, index) => {
          const t = index / 27;
          return [3 + t * 50, 14 + Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2) * 7, 0.25 + Math.abs(Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2.4)) * 0.75];
        }),
        2.4,
        { ...penStyleOptions(style, 55), simulatePressure: false },
      ),
    [style],
  );
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 56 28" className="h-7 w-full" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d={path} fill={selected ? color : "var(--muted)"} />
    </svg>
  );
}

/** Slider with tick dots and their scale labels under the track. */
function TickSlider({
  label,
  value,
  min,
  max,
  step,
  ticks,
  color,
  onChange,
  aside,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  step: number;
  ticks: { value: number; label: string }[];
  color?: string;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
  aside?: ReactNode;
}) {
  const percent = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((value - min) / (max - min)) * 100));
  return (
    <div className="field">
      <span className="field-label">
        <span>{label}</span>
        {aside}
      </span>
      <input
        type="range"
        className="range"
        min={min}
        max={max}
        step={step}
        value={value}
        aria-label={label}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(Number(event.target.value))}
        style={
          {
            "--fill": `${percent}%`,
            ...(color
              ? { background: `linear-gradient(to right, ${color} ${percent}%, var(--track) ${percent}%)` }
              : {}),
          } as CSSProperties
        }
      />
      <div className="relative h-3">
        {ticks.map((tick) => {
          const left = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((tick.value - min) / (max - min)) * 100));
          return (
            <span key={tick.value} className="absolute top-0 flex -translate-x-1/2 flex-col items-center gap-0.5" style={{ left: `${left}%` }}>
              <span className="h-1 w-1 rounded-full" style={{ background: "var(--muted)", opacity: 0.65 }} />
              <span className="text-[8.5px] tabular-nums text-muted">{tick.label}</span>
            </span>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Compact dropdown that mirrors the slider value. */
function ValuePill({
  value,
  options,
  unit,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: number;
  options: number[];
  unit: string;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
}) {
  const all = [...new Set([...options, value])].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  return (
    <select
      className="h-6 rounded-md border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--panel)] px-1 text-[10.5px] font-medium tabular-nums outline-none"
      value={value}
      aria-label={`Value in ${unit}`}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(Number(event.target.value))}
    >
      {all.map((option) => (
        <option key={option} value={option}>
          {Number(option.toFixed(2))} {unit}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
}

function ColorGrid({ value, onChange }: { value: string; onChange: (value: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="field">
      <span className="field-label">
        <span>Colour</span>
        <span className="field-value">{value.toUpperCase()}</span>
      </span>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-y-1">
        {SWATCHES.map((color) => (
          <button
            key={color}
            type="button"
            className="swatch"
            aria-label={`Ink ${color}`}
            aria-pressed={color.toLowerCase() === value.toLowerCase()}
            onClick={() => onChange(color)}
            style={{ background: color }}
          />
        ))}
        <CustomColorPicker value={value} onChange={onChange} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StudioInk({ tool, props, setProps }: { tool: ToolId; props: ToolProps; setProps: (patch: Partial<ToolProps>) => void }) {
  const isInk = tool === "pen" || tool === "marker";
  const percent = Math.round(props.opacity * 100);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      {isInk && <WavePreview tool={tool} props={props} />}

      {tool === "marker" && (
        <div className="field">
          <span className="field-label">
            <span>Tip style</span>
            <span className="field-value">{MARKER_TIPS.find((tip) => tip.value === (props.tipStyle ?? "vertical"))?.label}</span>
          </span>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
            {MARKER_TIPS.map((tip) => (
              <button
                key={tip.value}
                type="button"
                className="tile flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 px-1 py-1.5"
                aria-pressed={(props.tipStyle ?? "vertical") === tip.value}
                aria-label={`${tip.label} highlighter tip`}
                title={tip.label}
                onClick={() => setProps({ tipStyle: tip.value })}
              >
                <svg viewBox="0 0 32 32" className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" style={{ color: props.color }}>
                  <TipIcon tip={tip.value} />
                </svg>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-medium">{tip.label}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {tool === "marker" && (
        <ToggleRow
          label="Straight line highlight"
          hint="Snaps the highlight to a straight line between start and end."
          checked={Boolean(props.straightHighlight)}
          onChange={(straightHighlight) => setProps({ straightHighlight })}
        />
      )}

      {tool === "pen" && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
          {STROKE_STYLES.map((style) => {
            const active = props.penStyle === style.value;
            return (
              <button
                key={style.value}
                type="button"
                className="tile flex flex-col items-center overflow-hidden px-1 pb-1.5 pt-1 text-center"
                aria-pressed={active}
                title={style.hint}
                onClick={() => setProps(style.patch)}
              >
                <MiniStroke style={style.value} color={props.color} selected={active} />
                <span className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold leading-tight">{style.label}</span>
                <span className="text-[9px] leading-tight text-muted">{style.hint}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <TickSlider
        label={tool === "marker" ? "Marker width" : tool === "pen" ? "Ink width" : "Stroke"}
        value={props.thicknessMm}
        min={tool === "marker" ? 2 : 0.2}
        max={tool === "marker" ? 32 : 16}
        step={0.1}
        ticks={tool === "marker" ? [{ value: 2, label: "2" }, { value: 10, label: "10" }, { value: 20, label: "20" }, { value: 32, label: "32" }] : THICKNESS_TICKS}
        onChange={(thicknessMm) => setProps({ thicknessMm })}
        aside={<ValuePill value={props.thicknessMm} options={THICKNESS_PRESETS} unit="mm" onChange={(thicknessMm) => setProps({ thicknessMm })} />}
      />

      <ColorGrid value={props.color} onChange={(color) => setProps({ color })} />

      <TickSlider
        label="Opacity"
        value={percent}
        min={5}
        max={100}
        step={1}
        ticks={OPACITY_TICKS}
        color={props.color}
        onChange={(value) => setProps({ opacity: value / 100 })}
        aside={<ValuePill value={percent} options={[10, 25, 50, 70, 80, 100]} unit="%" onChange={(value) => setProps({ opacity: value / 100 })} />}
      />

      {isInk && (
        <details className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)] px-2.5 py-2">
          <summary className="cursor-pointer text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">
            Smart inking
          </summary>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 pt-2.5">
            <TickSlider
              label="Ink pressure"
              value={props.pressureLevel}
              min={0}
              max={100}
              step={1}
              ticks={[{ value: 0, label: "0" }, { value: 50, label: "50" }, { value: 100, label: "100" }]}
              onChange={(pressureLevel) => setProps({ pressureLevel, pressureSensitive: pressureLevel > 0 })}
              aside={
                <ValuePill value={props.pressureLevel} options={[0, 25, 55, 85, 100]} unit="%" onChange={(pressureLevel) => setProps({ pressureLevel, pressureSensitive: pressureLevel > 0 })} />
              }
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              className="chip h-7 justify-center"
              aria-pressed={props.smoothing}
              onClick={() => setProps({ smoothing: !props.smoothing })}
            >
              Auto-smooth
            </button>
          </div>
        </details>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function TextStudio({ props, setProps }: { props: ToolProps; setProps: (patch: Partial<ToolProps>) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <label className="field">
        <span className="field-label">Font</span>
        <select className="text-input" value={props.fontName} onChange={(event) => setProps({ fontName: event.target.value })}>
          {FONTS.map((font) => (
            <option key={font} value={font}>
              {font}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </label>

      <TickSlider
        label="Size"
        value={props.fontSize}
        min={6}
        max={72}
        step={1}
        ticks={[{ value: 6, label: "6" }, { value: 24, label: "24" }, { value: 48, label: "48" }, { value: 72, label: "72" }]}
        onChange={(fontSize) => setProps({ fontSize })}
        aside={<ValuePill value={props.fontSize} options={FONT_SIZES} unit="pt" onChange={(fontSize) => setProps({ fontSize })} />}
      />

      <div className="seg">
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={props.bold} onClick={() => setProps({ bold: !props.bold })} style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>
          B
        </button>
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={props.italic} onClick={() => setProps({ italic: !props.italic })} style={{ fontStyle: "italic" }}>
          I
        </button>
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={props.underline} onClick={() => setProps({ underline: !props.underline })} style={{ textDecoration: "underline" }}>
          U
        </button>
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={props.strikeOut} onClick={() => setProps({ strikeOut: !props.strikeOut })} style={{ textDecoration: "line-through" }}>
          S
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="field">
        <span className="field-label">Align</span>
        <Segmented<TextAlign>
          value={props.textAlign}
          options={[
            { value: "left", label: "Left" },
            { value: "center", label: "Centre" },
            { value: "right", label: "Right" },
          ]}
          onChange={(textAlign) => setProps({ textAlign })}
        />
      </div>

      <ColorGrid value={props.color} onChange={(color) => setProps({ color })} />

      <details className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)] px-2.5 py-2">
        <summary className="cursor-pointer text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">
          Box frame &amp; fill
        </summary>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 pt-2.5">
          <TickSlider
            label="Frame width"
            value={props.thicknessMm}
            min={0.2}
            max={4}
            step={0.1}
            ticks={[{ value: 0.2, label: "0.2" }, { value: 1, label: "1" }, { value: 2, label: "2" }, { value: 4, label: "4" }]}
            onChange={(thicknessMm) => setProps({ thicknessMm })}
          />
          <ColorRow label="Box fill" value={props.fill} allowNone onChange={(fill) => setProps({ fill })} />
          <TickSlider
            label="Box opacity"
            value={Math.round(props.opacity * 100)}
            min={5}
            max={100}
            step={1}
            ticks={OPACITY_TICKS}
            onChange={(value) => setProps({ opacity: value / 100 })}
          />
        </div>
      </details>
    </div>
  );
}

const PREVIEW_STROKE = 2;

function arrowTip(x: number, y: number, direction: 1 | -1, code: number, stroke: string) {
  if (!code) return null;
  const size = 10;
  if (code === 3) {
    return (
      <path
        d={`M${x + direction * size} ${y - 6} L${x} ${y} L${x + direction * size} ${y + 6}`}
        fill="none"
        stroke={stroke}
        strokeWidth={PREVIEW_STROKE}
        strokeLinecap="round"
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <path
      d={`M${x + direction * size} ${y - 6} L${x} ${y} L${x + direction * size} ${y + 6} Z`}
      fill={code === 7 ? "none" : stroke}
      stroke={stroke}
      strokeWidth={PREVIEW_STROKE}
    />
  );
}

/** Live SVG preview of the active shape's stroke, fill, dash and arrowheads. */
function ShapePreview({ tool, props }: { tool: ShapeTool; props: ToolProps }) {
  const stroke = props.color;
  const fill = props.fill ?? "none";
  const common = {
    stroke,
    fill,
    opacity: props.opacity,
    strokeWidth: PREVIEW_STROKE,
    strokeDasharray: DASH[props.lineStyle],
    strokeLinecap: "round" as const,
    strokeLinejoin: "round" as const,
  };
  const flat = { ...common, fill: "none" };

  return (
    <div
      className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)]"
      style={{ height: 86, background: "linear-gradient(180deg, var(--glass-strong), var(--panel))" }}
    >
      <svg viewBox="0 0 320 86" className="h-full w-full" aria-hidden="true">
        {tool === "line" && <line x1="30" y1="62" x2="290" y2="24" {...flat} />}
        {tool === "arrow" && (
          <g {...flat}>
            <line x1="30" y1="62" x2="290" y2="24" />
            {arrowTip(30, 62, -1, props.arrowTail, stroke)}
            {arrowTip(290, 24, 1, props.arrowHead || 5, stroke)}
          </g>
        )}
        {tool === "rectangle" && <rect x="76" y="22" width="168" height="44" rx="3" {...common} />}
        {tool === "ellipse" && <ellipse cx="160" cy="44" rx="84" ry="22" {...common} />}
        {tool === "polygon" && <path d="M160 18 L238 32 L258 62 L160 76 L62 62 L82 32 Z" {...common} />}
        {tool === "dimension" && (
          <g {...flat}>
            <line x1="46" y1="44" x2="274" y2="44" />
            <path d="M46 44l12-6M46 44l12 6M274 44l-12-6M274 44l-12 6" />
          </g>
        )}
      </svg>
      <span className="absolute bottom-1.5 right-2 text-[9.5px] font-medium text-muted">Live preview</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function ShapeGrid({ tool }: { tool: ShapeTool }) {
  const setTool = useToolStore((state) => state.setTool);
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1">
      {SHAPE_TOOLS.map((shape) => {
        const Icon = TOOL_ICONS[shape];
        return (
          <button
            key={shape}
            type="button"
            className="tile flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 px-1 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-medium"
            aria-pressed={shape === tool}
            aria-label={SHAPE_LABEL[shape]}
            onClick={() => setTool(shape)}
          >
            <Icon size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            {SHAPE_LABEL[shape]}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

export function PropertyControls({ tool }: { tool: ToolId }) {
  const props = useToolStore((state) => state.props[tool]);
  const setProps = useToolStore((state) => state.setProps);
  const shape = isShapeTool(tool) ? tool : null;

  if (shape) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <ShapeGrid tool={shape} />
        <ShapePreview tool={shape} props={props} />
        {shape === "polygon" && (
          <div className="field">
            <span className="field-label">
              <span>Sides</span>
              <span className="field-value">{props.polygonSides ?? 6}</span>
            </span>
            <Segmented
              value={String(props.polygonSides ?? 6)}
              options={POLYGON_SIDES}
              onChange={(value) => setProps({ polygonSides: Number(value) as 3 | 4 | 5 | 6 | 8 })}
            />
          </div>
        )}
        <StudioInk tool={tool} props={props} setProps={setProps} />

        {shape !== "line" && shape !== "arrow" && shape !== "dimension" && (
          <ColorRow label="Fill" value={props.fill} allowNone onChange={(fill) => setProps({ fill })} />
        )}

        <div className="field">
          <span className="field-label">Line style</span>
          <Segmented value={props.lineStyle} options={LINE_STYLES} onChange={(lineStyle) => setProps({ lineStyle })} />
        </div>

        {shape === "arrow" && (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <label className="field">
              <span className="field-label">Head</span>
              <select className="text-input" value={props.arrowHead} onChange={(event) => setProps({ arrowHead: Number(event.target.value) })}>
                {ARROW_HEADS.map((head) => (
                  <option key={head.value} value={head.value}>
                    {head.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="field">
              <span className="field-label">Tail</span>
              <select className="text-input" value={props.arrowTail} onChange={(event) => setProps({ arrowTail: Number(event.target.value) })}>
                {ARROW_HEADS.map((head) => (
                  <option key={head.value} value={head.value}>
                    {head.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>
        )}

        {shape === "dimension" && (
          <div className="field">
            <span className="field-label">Unit</span>
            <Segmented value={props.dimensionUnit} options={DIMENSION_UNITS} onChange={(dimensionUnit) => setProps({ dimensionUnit })} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (tool === "text") return <TextStudio props={props} setProps={setProps} />;
  if (tool === "pen" || tool === "marker") return <StudioInk tool={tool} props={props} setProps={setProps} />;
  return null;
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ChevronDown, ChevronUp, Search, X } from "lucide-react";
import { searchTab, useSearchStore } from "../lib/pdfSearch";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";

export function SearchBar() {
  const open = useUIStore((state) => state.searchOpen);
  const setOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setSearchOpen);
  const tabs = useDocStore((state) => state.tabs);
  const activeId = useDocStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === activeId) ?? tabs[0];

  const query = useSearchStore((state) => state.query);
  const matches = useSearchStore((state) => state.matches);
  const current = useSearchStore((state) => state.current);
  const searching = useSearchStore((state) => state.searching);
  const [text, setText] = useState(query);
  const [wholeWord, setWholeWord] = useState(false);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    inputRef.current?.focus();
    inputRef.current?.select();
  }, [open]);

  // A new tab starts a fresh search.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setText("");
    useSearchStore.getState().setQuery("");
    useSearchStore.getState().setMatches([]);
  }, [open, tab?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || !tab) return;
    const value = text.trim();
    if (!value) {
      useSearchStore.getState().setQuery("");
      useSearchStore.getState().setMatches([]);
      useSearchStore.getState().setSearching(false);
      return;
    }
    let cancelled = false;
    useSearchStore.getState().setSearching(true);
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      void searchTab(tab, value, { wholeWord })
        .then((found) => {
          if (cancelled) return;
          useSearchStore.getState().setQuery(value);
          useSearchStore.getState().setMatches(found);
          useSearchStore.getState().setSearching(false);
        })
        .catch(() => {
          if (cancelled) return;
          useSearchStore.getState().setMatches([]);
          useSearchStore.getState().setSearching(false);
        });
    }, 220);
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [open, text, tab?.id, wholeWord]);

  if (!open || !tab) return null;

  const jump = (index: number) => {
    const list = useSearchStore.getState().matches;
    if (list.length === 0) return;
    const next = ((index % list.length) + list.length) % list.length;
    useSearchStore.getState().setCurrent(next);
    useDocStore.getState().setPage(list[next].pageIndex + 1);
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("pm:search-focus", { detail: { pageIndex: list[next].pageIndex } }));
  };

  const close = () => {
    setOpen(false);
    useSearchStore.getState().setMatches([]);
    useSearchStore.getState().setQuery("");
  };

  return (
    <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-3 z-[90] -translate-x-1/2">
      <div className="menu-surface anim-in flex h-10 items-center gap-1 rounded-full pl-3 pr-1.5 shadow-lg">
        <Search size={14} className="shrink-0 text-muted" strokeWidth={1.9} />
        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          className="h-8 w-[220px] bg-transparent text-[12px] outline-none placeholder:text-muted"
          value={text}
          placeholder="Search this document…"
          aria-label="Search document"
          onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(event) => {
            if (event.key === "Enter") {
              event.preventDefault();
              jump(current + (event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1));
            }
            if (event.key === "Escape") {
              event.preventDefault();
              close();
            }
          }}
        />
        <span className="min-w-[42px] shrink-0 text-center text-[10.5px] tabular-nums text-muted" aria-live="polite">
          {searching ? "…" : `${matches.length ? current + 1 : 0}/${matches.length}`}
        </span>
        <div className="mx-0.5 h-5 w-px bg-[var(--border)]" />
        <button
          type="button"
          className="chip h-7 shrink-0 px-1.5 font-mono text-[10px]"
          aria-pressed={wholeWord}
          aria-label="Whole word only"
          title="Whole word only"
          onClick={() => setWholeWord((value) => !value)}
        >
          [ab]
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
          aria-label="Previous match"
          title="Previous match (Shift+Enter)"
          disabled={matches.length === 0}
          onClick={() => jump(current - 1)}
        >
          <ChevronUp size={14} strokeWidth={1.9} />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
          aria-label="Next match"
          title="Next match (Enter)"
          disabled={matches.length === 0}
          onClick={() => jump(current + 1)}
        >
          <ChevronDown size={14} strokeWidth={1.9} />
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7" aria-label="Close search" title="Close (Esc)" onClick={close}>
          <X size={14} strokeWidth={1.9} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
