import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, Command, Download, RotateCcw, Upload, X } from "lucide-react";
import { MEASURE_UNITS, type MeasureUnit } from "../lib/measure";
import { usePrefsStore } from "../lib/prefs";
import {
  DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS,
  SHORTCUT_ACTIONS,
  SHORTCUT_LABELS,
  bindingFromEvent,
  detectConflicts,
  type ShortcutAction,
  useShortcutStore,
} from "../lib/shortcuts";
import { applyWorkspaceStores, parseWorkspaceBackup, storageEstimate, workspaceBackup } from "../lib/storage";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { SectionTitle, Segmented, ToggleRow } from "./ui/controls";

const UNITS = MEASURE_UNITS.map((unit) => ({ value: unit.value as MeasureUnit, label: unit.label }));

function megabytes(bytes: number): string {
  return `${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

export function SettingsModal() {
  const open = useUIStore((state) => state.settingsOpen);
  const setOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setSettingsOpen);
  const setToast = useUIStore((state) => state.setToast);
  const auxLines = useUIStore((state) => state.auxLines);
  const toggleAuxLines = useUIStore((state) => state.toggleAuxLines);
  const setPaletteOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setPaletteOpen);
  const map = useShortcutStore((state) => state.map);
  const bind = useShortcutStore((state) => state.bind);
  const reset = useShortcutStore((state) => state.reset);
  const scratchOut = usePrefsStore((state) => state.scratchOut);
  const measureUnit = usePrefsStore((state) => state.measureUnit);
  const setPrefs = usePrefsStore((state) => state.set);
  const sync = useToolStore((state) => state.sync);
  const setSync = useToolStore((state) => state.setSync);
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState<ShortcutAction | null>(null);
  const [estimate, setEstimate] = useState<{ usage: number; quota: number } | null>(null);
  const importRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const conflicts = useMemo(() => detectConflicts(map), [map]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      setRecording(null);
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    void storageEstimate().then((value) => {
      if (alive) setEstimate(value);
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [open]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const exportBackup = () => {
    const state = useDocStore.getState();
    const blob = workspaceBackup(state.tabs, state.activeId);
    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = `pdf-master-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.pmbackup.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(anchor);
    anchor.click();
    anchor.remove();
    // Revoking immediately can cancel the download in some browsers.
    window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 4000);
    setToast("Workspace backup downloaded");
  };

  const importBackup = async (file: File) => {
    const parsed = parseWorkspaceBackup(await file.text());
    if (!parsed) {
      setToast("That file is not a PDF Master workspace backup");
      return;
    }
    useDocStore.getState().restore(parsed.tabs, parsed.activeId);
    applyWorkspaceStores(parsed.stores);
    const extras = parsed.stores ? " with stamps, scales, shortcuts and preferences" : "";
    setToast(`Restored ${parsed.tabs.length} tab${parsed.tabs.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}${extras}`);
  };

  const quota = estimate && estimate.quota > 0 ? Math.min(100, (estimate.usage / estimate.quota) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center bg-black/25 px-4 pt-[8vh] backdrop-blur-[3px]"
      onPointerDown={(event) => {
        if (event.target === event.currentTarget) setOpen(false);
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="glass-strong anim-in flex max-h-[80vh] w-full max-w-[640px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label="Settings"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-4 py-3">
          <span className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold">Settings</span>
          <span className="text-[10.5px] text-muted">Shortcuts, preferences and storage</span>
          <button type="button" className="icon-btn ml-auto h-7 w-7" aria-label="Close settings" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
            <X size={14} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="scroll-thin flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-5 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4">
          <section className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <SectionTitle
              aside={
                <button type="button" className="chip h-6 px-2" onClick={() => reset()}>
                  <RotateCcw size={11} /> Reset all
                </button>
              }
            >
              Keyboard shortcuts
            </SectionTitle>
            {conflicts.conflicting.size > 0 && (
              <p className="flex items-start gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-[10.5px]" style={{ background: "var(--track)", color: "var(--warning, #b26a00)" }}>
                <AlertTriangle size={13} className="mt-px shrink-0" /> A binding is claimed twice; the earlier action wins and the other is ignored.
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1 sm:grid-cols-2">
              {SHORTCUT_ACTIONS.map((action) => {
                const conflicting = conflicts.conflicting.has(action);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={action}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border px-2 py-1.5"
                    style={{ borderColor: conflicting ? "var(--warning, #b26a00)" : "var(--border)" }}
                  >
                    <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-[11.5px]">{SHORTCUT_LABELS[action]}</span>
                    <span className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
                      <input
                        readOnly
                        value={recording === action ? "Press keys…" : map[action]}
                        aria-label={`${SHORTCUT_LABELS[action]} shortcut`}
                        onFocus={() => setRecording(action)}
                        onBlur={() => setRecording((current) => (current === action ? null : current))}
                        onKeyDown={(event) => {
                          event.stopPropagation();
                          if (event.key === "Escape") {
                            setRecording(null);
                            event.currentTarget.blur();
                            return;
                          }
                          event.preventDefault();
                          const binding = bindingFromEvent(event);
                          if (!binding) return;
                          bind(action, binding);
                          setRecording(null);
                          event.currentTarget.blur();
                        }}
                        className="w-[92px] cursor-pointer rounded-md border border-[var(--border)] bg-transparent px-1.5 py-0.5 text-center text-[10.5px] tabular-nums outline-none"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="icon-btn h-5 w-5"
                        aria-label={`Reset ${SHORTCUT_LABELS[action]}`}
                        title={`Reset to ${DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS[action]}`}
                        onClick={() => reset(action)}
                      >
                        <RotateCcw size={10} />
                      </button>
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <p className="text-[10.5px] text-muted">
              Click a binding and press the new keys. Clicking away keeps the current binding.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
            <SectionTitle>Preferences</SectionTitle>
            <ToggleRow
              label="Scratch out to erase"
              hint="A rapid scribble erases the annotations under it."
              checked={scratchOut}
              onChange={(checked) => setPrefs({ scratchOut: checked })}
            />
            <div className="field">
              <span className="field-label">Default measure unit</span>
              <Segmented value={measureUnit} options={UNITS} onChange={(value) => setPrefs({ measureUnit: value })} />
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <span className="field-label">Tool properties</span>
              <Segmented
                value={sync ? "sync" : "separate"}
                options={[
                  { value: "separate", label: "Separate" },
                  { value: "sync", label: "Synchronous" },
                ]}
                onChange={(value) => setSync(value === "sync")}
              />
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
            <SectionTitle>Interface</SectionTitle>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="chip h-8 justify-center"
              onClick={() => {
                setOpen(false);
                setPaletteOpen(true);
              }}
            >
              <Command size={13} /> Open Command Palette (Ctrl+K)
            </button>
            <ToggleRow
              label="Show alignment guides"
              hint="Draws the page's alignment grid while you position annotations."
              checked={auxLines}
              onChange={toggleAuxLines}
            />
          </section>

          <section className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <SectionTitle
              aside={
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <button type="button" className="chip h-6 px-2" onClick={exportBackup}>
                    <Download size={11} /> Export
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" className="chip h-6 px-2" onClick={() => importRef.current?.click()}>
                    <Upload size={11} /> Import
                  </button>
                </span>
              }
            >
              Workspace storage
            </SectionTitle>
            <input
              ref={importRef}
              type="file"
              accept="application/json,.json"
              className="hidden"
              aria-label="Import workspace backup"
              onChange={(event) => {
                const file = event.target.files?.[0];
                event.target.value = "";
                if (file) void importBackup(file);
              }}
            />
            {estimate ? (
              <>
                <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full" style={{ background: "var(--track)" }}>
                  <div className="h-full rounded-full" style={{ width: `${Math.max(2, quota)}%`, background: "var(--accent)" }} />
                </div>
                <p className="text-[10.5px] text-muted">
                  Autosave uses {megabytes(estimate.usage)} of {megabytes(estimate.quota)} ({quota.toFixed(1)} %). Backups carry every
                  tab, its pages and annotations, plus custom stamps, calibrated scales, shortcuts, preferences and theme.
                </p>
              </>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-[10.5px] text-muted">Storage quota is not reported by this browser.</p>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Bookmark, BookmarkPlus, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, CopyPlus, Eye, EyeOff, FileText, Hand, Heart, Highlighter, LayoutGrid, MessageSquareText, PenLine, Pin, Plus, RotateCw, Search, Sigma, Square, Stamp, Star, Trash2, Type, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { stampById, stamps, userStamps } from "../lib/data";
import { PX_PER_MM } from "../lib/paper";
import { pageSizeMm, pageTemplate } from "../lib/pageUtils";
import { renderPdfPage } from "../lib/pdfEngine";
import { SHAPE_LABEL, type Annotation } from "../lib/annotationTypes";
import type { DocumentPage, DocumentTab } from "../lib/types";
import { usePrefsStore } from "../lib/prefs";
import type { GridStyle, SidebarTab, ViewMode } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { useAnnotationStore } from "../stores/useAnnotationStore";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { PaperSheet } from "./canvas/PaperSheet";
import { Segmented, SectionTitle, Switch } from "./ui/controls";

const TABS: { id: SidebarTab; label: string; Icon: typeof FileText }[] = [
  { id: "pages", label: "Pages", Icon: FileText },
  { id: "bookmarks", label: "Bookmarks", Icon: Bookmark },
  { id: "annotations", label: "Annotations", Icon: MessageSquareText },
  { id: "stamps", label: "Stamps / Library", Icon: Stamp },
];

function EmptyState({ Icon, title, body }: { Icon: typeof FileText; title: string; body: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 px-3 py-8 text-center">
      <div className="grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-xl" style={{ background: "var(--track)", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
        <Icon size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </div>
      <div className="text-[12px] font-medium">{title}</div>
      <p className="max-w-[180px] text-[10.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted">{body}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function PageThumb({ tab, page, active, index, onClick }: { tab: DocumentTab; page: DocumentPage; active: boolean; index: number; onClick: () => void }) {
  const size = pageSizeMm(tab, page);
  const rotation = page.rotation === 90 || page.rotation === 270;
  const width = rotation ? size.heightMm : size.widthMm;
  const height = rotation ? size.widthMm : size.heightMm;
  const boxWidth = 48;
  const boxHeight = 62;
  const scale = Math.min(boxWidth / (width * PX_PER_MM), boxHeight / (height * PX_PER_MM));
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const holderRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  // Rendering 100+ PDF pages must wait until a thumbnail is close to the viewport.
  useEffect(() => {
    const node = holderRef.current;
    if (!node || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") {
      setVisible(true);
      return;
    }
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (!entries.some((entry) => entry.isIntersecting)) return;
        setVisible(true);
        observer.disconnect();
      },
      { rootMargin: "160px" },
    );
    observer.observe(node);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const crop = page.cropBox;
  const viewWidthMm = crop?.widthMm ?? size.widthMm;
  const viewHeightMm = crop?.heightMm ?? size.heightMm;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible || !page.pdf || !canvasRef.current) return;
    void renderPdfPage(page.pdf.url, page.pdf.pageNumber, canvasRef.current, 96, 96 * (height / width), 1);
  }, [visible, page.pdf, width, height]);

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className="tile group flex flex-col gap-1 p-1.5 text-left"
      aria-pressed={active}
      title={`Page ${index + 1}${page.annotations.length ? ` · ${page.annotations.length} annotations` : ""}`}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <span
        ref={holderRef}
        className="relative grid place-items-center overflow-hidden rounded-[3px] bg-[var(--paper)]"
        style={{ width: boxWidth, height: boxHeight, boxShadow: "var(--shadow-soft)" }}
      >
        {page.pdf ? (
          <span
            className="absolute block"
            style={{
              left: `${-((crop?.xMm ?? 0) / viewWidthMm) * 100}%`,
              top: `${-((crop?.yMm ?? 0) / viewHeightMm) * 100}%`,
              width: `${(size.widthMm / viewWidthMm) * 100}%`,
              height: `${(size.heightMm / viewHeightMm) * 100}%`,
            }}
          >
            <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="block h-full w-full" />
          </span>
        ) : (
          <span style={{ display: "block", transform: `scale(${scale})`, transformOrigin: "center" }}>
            <PaperSheet template={pageTemplate(page)} landscape={tab.landscape} />
          </span>
        )}
        {page.annotations.length > 0 && (
          <span
            className="absolute right-0.5 top-0.5 rounded-full px-1 text-[9px] font-semibold"
            style={{ background: "var(--accent)", color: "var(--accent-ink)" }}
          >
            {page.annotations.length}
          </span>
        )}
      </span>
      <span className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-muted">
        <span>{index + 1}</span>
        <span>{page.rotation ? `${page.rotation}°` : ""}</span>
      </span>
    </button>
  );
}

function annotationLabel(annotation: Annotation): string {
  if (annotation.type === "pen") return "Pen";
  if (annotation.type === "marker") return "Highlighter";
  if (annotation.type === "stamp") {
    const factory = stampById.get(annotation.stampId);
    return factory?.title.en ?? userStamps.find((stamp) => stamp.id === annotation.stampId)?.name ?? "Stamp";
  }
  if (annotation.type === "text") return "Text";
  if (annotation.type === "formula") return "Formula";
  return SHAPE_LABEL[annotation.shapeType];
}

function annotationColor(annotation: Annotation): string | null {
  if (annotation.type === "pen" || annotation.type === "marker" || annotation.type === "formula") return annotation.color;
  if (annotation.type === "shape") return annotation.strokeColor;
  if (annotation.type === "text") return annotation.textColor;
  return null;
}

function AnnotationIcon({ annotation }: { annotation: Annotation }) {
  const props = { size: 13, strokeWidth: 1.75 } as const;
  if (annotation.type === "pen") return <PenLine {...props} />;
  if (annotation.type === "marker") return <Highlighter {...props} />;
  if (annotation.type === "stamp") return <Stamp {...props} />;
  if (annotation.type === "text") return <Type {...props} />;
  if (annotation.type === "formula") return <Sigma {...props} />;
  return <Square {...props} />;
}

const TAB_TITLES: Record<SidebarTab, string> = {
  pages: "Pages",
  bookmarks: "Bookmarks",
  annotations: "Annotations",
  stamps: "Stamps / Library",
};

const VIEW_MODES: { id: ViewMode; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "one-page", label: "One page" },
  { id: "one-continuous", label: "One continuous" },
  { id: "two-continuous", label: "Two continuous" },
  { id: "two-page", label: "Two page" },
];

const GRID_STYLES: { id: GridStyle; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "none", label: "None" },
  { id: "squared-5", label: "Squared 5 mm" },
  { id: "squared-10", label: "Squared 10 mm" },
  { id: "ruled-8", label: "Ruled 8 mm" },
  { id: "dots-5", label: "Dots 5 mm" },
  { id: "millimeter", label: "Millimeter" },
];

export function Sidebar() {
  const sidebarOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.sidebarOpen);
  const sidebarTab = useUIStore((state) => state.sidebarTab);
  const setSidebarTab = useUIStore((state) => state.toggleSidebarTab);
  const drawerPinned = useUIStore((state) => state.drawerPinned);
  const toggleDrawerPinned = useUIStore((state) => state.toggleDrawerPinned);
  const favoritesOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.favoritesOpen);
  const toggleFavorites = useUIStore((state) => state.toggleFavorites);
  const setLibraryOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setLibraryOpen);
  const setNewDocOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setNewDocOpen);
  const auxLines = useUIStore((state) => state.auxLines);
  const toggleAuxLines = useUIStore((state) => state.toggleAuxLines);
  const tabs = useDocStore((state) => state.tabs);
  const activeId = useDocStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const setLandscape = useDocStore((state) => state.setLandscape);
  const pageIndex = useDocStore((state) => state.page);
  const setPage = useDocStore((state) => state.setPage);
  const addPage = useDocStore((state) => state.addPage);
  const duplicatePage = useDocStore((state) => state.duplicatePage);
  const rotatePage = useDocStore((state) => state.rotatePage);
  const reorderPage = useDocStore((state) => state.reorderPage);
  const deletePage = useDocStore((state) => state.deletePage);
  const addBookmark = useDocStore((state) => state.addBookmark);
  const selection = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.selection);
  const select = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.select);
  const remove = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.remove);
  const update = useAnnotationStore((state) => state.update);
  const recentStamps = useToolStore((state) => state.recentStamps);
  const selectStamp = useToolStore((state) => state.selectStamp);
  const viewMode = useUIStore((state) => state.viewMode);
  const setViewMode = useUIStore((state) => state.setViewMode);
  const separateCoverPage = useUIStore((state) => state.separateCoverPage);
  const toggleSeparateCoverPage = useUIStore((state) => state.toggleSeparateCoverPage);
  const gridStyle = useUIStore((state) => state.gridStyle);
  const setGridStyle = useUIStore((state) => state.setGridStyle);
  const searchOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.searchOpen);
  const setSearchOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setSearchOpen);
  const touchDrawEnabled = usePrefsStore((state) => state.touchDrawEnabled);
  const setPrefs = usePrefsStore((state) => state.set);
  const [viewOpen, setViewOpen] = useState(false);

  const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === activeId) ?? tabs[0];
  const railRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const drawerRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const viewRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // An unpinned drawer closes on the first click anywhere on the page.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sidebarOpen || drawerPinned) return;
    const onPointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      const target = event.target as Node;
      if (railRef.current?.contains(target) || drawerRef.current?.contains(target) || viewRef.current?.contains(target)) return;
      useUIStore.getState().toggleSidebar();
    };
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown, true);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown, true);
  }, [sidebarOpen, drawerPinned]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!viewOpen) return;
    const onPointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (!viewRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) setViewOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown, true);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown, true);
  }, [viewOpen]);

  const recent = recentStamps
    .map((id) => stamps.find((stamp) => stamp.id === id) ?? userStamps.find((stamp) => stamp.id === id))
    .filter((stamp): stamp is NonNullable<typeof stamp> => Boolean(stamp));

  const jumpTo = (pageNumber: number, annotationId?: string) => {
    setPage(pageNumber);
    if (annotationId) select([annotationId]);
  };

  return (
    <div ref={railRef} className="sidebar absolute left-3 top-3 z-40">
      <div className="glass flex w-11 flex-col items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl py-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn"
          aria-label="View settings"
          title="View"
          aria-pressed={viewOpen}
          onClick={() => setViewOpen((open) => !open)}
        >
          <LayoutGrid size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
        <div className="my-0.5 h-px w-6 bg-[var(--border)]" />
        {TABS.map(({ id, label, Icon }) => (
          <button
            key={id}
            type="button"
            className="icon-btn"
            aria-label={label}
            title={label}
            aria-pressed={sidebarOpen && sidebarTab === id}
            onClick={() => setSidebarTab(id)}
          >
            <Icon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </button>
        ))}
        <div className="my-0.5 h-px w-6 bg-[var(--border)]" />
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn"
          aria-label="Search document"
          title="Search (Ctrl+F)"
          aria-pressed={searchOpen}
          onClick={() => setSearchOpen(!searchOpen)}
        >
          <Search size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn"
          aria-label="Favorite tools"
          title="Favorite tools"
          aria-pressed={favoritesOpen}
          onClick={toggleFavorites}
        >
          <Heart size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} style={favoritesOpen ? { fill: "currentColor" } : undefined} />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn"
          aria-label="Finger draws"
          title={touchDrawEnabled ? "Finger draws · tap to pan with one finger" : "One finger pans · tap to draw with a finger"}
          aria-pressed={touchDrawEnabled}
          onClick={() => setPrefs({ touchDrawEnabled: !touchDrawEnabled })}
        >
          <Hand size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
      </div>

      {viewOpen && (
        <div ref={viewRef} className="glass-strong anim-in absolute left-[52px] top-0 z-50 w-[232px] rounded-xl p-2">
          <div className="px-1 pb-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">Page view</div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
            {VIEW_MODES.map((mode) => (
              <button
                key={mode.id}
                type="button"
                className="chip h-7 w-full justify-start px-2"
                aria-pressed={viewMode === mode.id}
                onClick={() => setViewMode(mode.id)}
              >
                {mode.label}
              </button>
            ))}
            {(viewMode === "two-page" || viewMode === "two-continuous") && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="chip h-7 w-full justify-start px-2"
                aria-pressed={separateCoverPage}
                onClick={toggleSeparateCoverPage}
              >
                Separate cover page
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="divider my-1.5" />
          <label className="field">
            <span className="field-label">Grid line</span>
            <select className="text-input" value={gridStyle} onChange={(event) => setGridStyle(event.target.value as GridStyle)} aria-label="Grid line">
              {GRID_STYLES.map((style) => (
                <option key={style.id} value={style.id}>
                  {style.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
        </div>
      )}

      {sidebarOpen && (
        <aside
          ref={drawerRef}
          className="glass-strong anim-in absolute left-[52px] top-0 z-40 flex w-[268px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl"
          style={{ maxHeight: "calc(100vh - 120px)", height: "auto" }}
          aria-label={TAB_TITLES[sidebarTab]}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-3 py-2">
            <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[12.5px] font-semibold">{TAB_TITLES[sidebarTab]}</span>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
              aria-label={drawerPinned ? "Unpin drawer" : "Pin drawer"}
              aria-pressed={drawerPinned}
              title={drawerPinned ? "Unpinned: closes when you click away" : "Pinned: stays open"}
              onClick={toggleDrawerPinned}
            >
              <Pin size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
              aria-label="Close drawer"
              title="Close"
              onClick={() => useUIStore.getState().toggleSidebar()}
            >
              <X size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="no-scrollbar flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-3 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto p-3">
        {sidebarTab === "pages" && (
          <>
            <SectionTitle aside={<span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{tab.pages.length} pages</span>}>Pages</SectionTitle>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
              {tab.pages.map((item, index) => (
                <PageThumb
                  key={item.id}
                  tab={tab}
                  page={item}
                  index={index}
                  active={index === pageIndex - 1}
                  onClick={() => setPage(index + 1)}
                />
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1">
              <button type="button" className="chip h-7 min-w-0 justify-center gap-1 px-1 text-[10.5px]" title="Add page" onClick={() => addPage()}>
                <Plus size={11} /> Add
              </button>
              <button type="button" className="chip h-7 min-w-0 justify-center gap-1 px-1 text-[10.5px]" title="Duplicate page" onClick={() => duplicatePage()}>
                <CopyPlus size={11} /> Copy
              </button>
              <button type="button" className="chip h-7 min-w-0 justify-center gap-1 px-1 text-[10.5px]" title="Rotate page 90°" onClick={() => rotatePage()}>
                <RotateCw size={11} /> Rotate
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="chip h-7 min-w-0 justify-center px-1"
                title="Delete page"
                aria-label="Delete page"
                disabled={tab.pages.length <= 1}
                onClick={() => deletePage()}
              >
                <Trash2 size={11} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="chip h-7 flex-1 justify-center"
                title="Move page up"
                disabled={pageIndex <= 1}
                onClick={() => reorderPage(pageIndex - 1, pageIndex - 2)}
              >
                <ArrowUp size={12} /> Move up
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="chip h-7 flex-1 justify-center"
                title="Move page down"
                disabled={pageIndex >= tab.pages.length}
                onClick={() => reorderPage(pageIndex - 1, pageIndex)}
              >
                <ArrowDown size={12} /> Move down
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
              <button type="button" className="chip h-7 flex-1 justify-center" disabled={pageIndex <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(pageIndex - 1)}>
                <ChevronLeft size={12} /> Prev
              </button>
              <span className="text-[10.5px] text-muted">
                {pageIndex} / {tab.pages.length}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="chip h-7 flex-1 justify-center"
                disabled={pageIndex >= tab.pages.length}
                onClick={() => setPage(pageIndex + 1)}
              >
                Next <ChevronRight size={12} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="divider" />
            <SectionTitle>Document setup</SectionTitle>
            <Segmented
              value={tab.landscape ? "landscape" : "portrait"}
              options={[
                { value: "portrait", label: "Portrait" },
                { value: "landscape", label: "Landscape" },
              ]}
              onChange={(value) => setLandscape(value === "landscape")}
            />
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="text-[11.5px]">Alignment guides</span>
              <Switch checked={auxLines} onChange={toggleAuxLines} label="Alignment guides" />
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="chip h-8 justify-center" onClick={() => setNewDocOpen(true)}>
              <Plus size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Change template…
            </button>
          </>
        )}

        {sidebarTab === "bookmarks" && (
          <>
            <SectionTitle aside={<span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{tab.pdf?.bookmarks.length ?? 0}</span>}>Bookmarks</SectionTitle>
            {tab.pdf && (
              <button type="button" className="chip h-8 justify-center" onClick={() => addBookmark(`Page ${pageIndex}`)}>
                <BookmarkPlus size={13} /> Bookmark page {pageIndex}
              </button>
            )}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
              {(tab.pdf?.bookmarks ?? []).map((bookmark) => (
                <button
                  key={bookmark.id}
                  type="button"
                  className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[11.5px] hover:bg-[var(--hover)]"
                  style={{ paddingLeft: 8 + bookmark.depth * 12 }}
                  onClick={() => setPage(bookmark.pageIndex + 1)}
                >
                  <Bookmark size={12} className="shrink-0 text-muted" strokeWidth={1.75} />
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{bookmark.title}</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{bookmark.pageIndex + 1}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            {tab.pdf && tab.pdf.bookmarks.length === 0 && (
              <EmptyState Icon={Bookmark} title="No bookmarks" body="This PDF has no outline. Add a bookmark for the current page." />
            )}
          </>
        )}

        {sidebarTab === "annotations" && (
          <>
            <SectionTitle
              aside={<span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{tab.pages.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.annotations.length, 0)}</span>}
            >
              Annotations
            </SectionTitle>
            {tab.pages.every((item) => item.annotations.length === 0) ? (
              <EmptyState
                Icon={MessageSquareText}
                title="No annotations yet"
                body="Ink, shapes and stamps you draw are listed here per page."
              />
            ) : (
              tab.pages.map((item, index) => {
                if (item.annotations.length === 0) return null;
                return (
                  <div key={item.id} className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
                    <div className="px-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">Page {index + 1}</div>
                    {item.annotations.map((annotation) => (
                      <div
                        key={annotation.id}
                        className={`group flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-1.5 py-1 ${
                          selection.includes(annotation.id) ? "bg-[var(--accent-soft)]" : "hover:bg-[var(--hover)]"
                        }`}
                      >
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-1.5 text-left"
                          onClick={() => jumpTo(index + 1, annotation.id)}
                        >
                          <span
                            className="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md"
                            style={{ background: "var(--track)", color: annotationColor(annotation) ?? "var(--muted)" }}
                          >
                            <AnnotationIcon annotation={annotation} />
                          </span>
                          <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[11.5px]">{annotationLabel(annotation)}</span>
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="icon-btn h-5 w-5 opacity-60 group-hover:opacity-100"
                          aria-label={annotation.hidden ? "Show" : "Hide"}
                          title={annotation.hidden ? "Show" : "Hide"}
                          onClick={() => {
                            jumpTo(index + 1);
                            update([annotation.id], { hidden: !annotation.hidden });
                          }}
                        >
                          {annotation.hidden ? <EyeOff size={11} /> : <Eye size={11} />}
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="icon-btn h-5 w-5 opacity-60 group-hover:opacity-100"
                          aria-label="Delete annotation"
                          title="Delete"
                          onClick={() => {
                            jumpTo(index + 1);
                            remove([annotation.id]);
                          }}
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={11} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
          </>
        )}

        {sidebarTab === "stamps" && (
          <>
            <button type="button" className="chip h-8 justify-center" onClick={() => setLibraryOpen(true)}>
              <Stamp size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Open stamp library
            </button>
            <SectionTitle aside={<span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{recent.length}</span>}>Recent stamps</SectionTitle>
            {recent.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState Icon={Star} title="Nothing pinned yet" body="A stamp picked from the gallery shows up here for one-click reuse." />
            ) : (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
                {recent.map((stamp) => {
                  const name = "title" in stamp ? stamp.title.en : stamp.name;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={stamp.id}
                      type="button"
                      className="tile checker grid h-[52px] place-items-center p-1"
                      title={name}
                      onClick={() => selectStamp(stamp.id)}
                    >
                      <img src={stamp.imageUrl ?? ""} alt={name} className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain" />
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
          </div>
        </aside>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\StampGallery.tsx (8.1 KB)
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Maximize2, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { resolveStamp, useCustomStampStore } from "../lib/customStamps";
import { stampGroups, stamps, userStamps } from "../lib/data";
import type { UserStamp } from "../lib/types";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { SectionTitle } from "./ui/controls";

interface GalleryItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  sub: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

const FACTORY_ITEMS: GalleryItem[] = stamps.map((stamp) => ({
  id: stamp.id,
  name: stamp.title.en,
  sub: stamp.title.de,
  imageUrl: stamp.imageUrl ?? "",
  width: stamp.width,
  height: stamp.height,
}));
const FACTORY_GROUP = new Map(stamps.map((stamp) => [stamp.id, stamp.groupSlug]));

const USER_ITEMS: GalleryItem[] = userStamps.map((stamp: UserStamp) => ({
  id: stamp.id,
  name: stamp.name,
  sub: stamp.groupName,
  imageUrl: stamp.imageUrl,
  width: stamp.inkWidth,
  height: stamp.inkHeight,
}));

const TILE_PX = 72;
const GAP = 8;

export function StampGallery({ variant = "panel", onPick }: { variant?: "panel" | "library"; onPick?: () => void }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [group, setGroup] = useState("all");
  const [scrollTop, setScrollTop] = useState(0);
  const [height, setHeight] = useState(320);
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const selectedStampId = useToolStore((state) => state.selectedStampId);
  const selectStamp = useToolStore((state) => state.selectStamp);
  const setLibraryOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setLibraryOpen);
  const customStamps = useCustomStampStore((state) => state.stamps);
  const library = variant === "library";
  const groups = useMemo(stampGroups, []);
  const columns = library ? 7 : 3;

  const customItems = useMemo<GalleryItem[]>(
    () =>
      customStamps.map((stamp) => ({
        id: stamp.id,
        name: stamp.name,
        sub: "Saved stamp",
        imageUrl: stamp.imageUrl,
        width: stamp.width,
        height: stamp.height,
      })),
    [customStamps],
  );

  const items = useMemo(() => {
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    const source =
      group === "user"
        ? USER_ITEMS
        : group === "custom"
          ? customItems
          : group === "all"
            ? [...FACTORY_ITEMS, ...USER_ITEMS, ...customItems]
            : FACTORY_ITEMS.filter((item) => FACTORY_GROUP.get(item.id) === group);
    if (!needle) return source;
    return source.filter(
      (item) => item.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle) || item.sub.toLowerCase().includes(needle),
    );
  }, [customItems, group, query]);

  // A filter change starts the list over; a stale scrollTop would show blank rows.
  useEffect(() => {
    setScrollTop(0);
    scrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }, [group, query]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const element = scrollRef.current;
    if (!element) return;
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => setHeight(element.clientHeight));
    observer.observe(element);
    setHeight(element.clientHeight);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const stride = TILE_PX + GAP;
  const rows = Math.ceil(items.length / columns);
  const firstRow = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / stride) - 1);
  const lastRow = Math.min(rows, Math.ceil((scrollTop + height) / stride) + 1);
  const visible = items.slice(firstRow * columns, lastRow * columns);
  const total = stamps.length + userStamps.length + customStamps.length;

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-2.5">
      <SectionTitle
        aside={
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-[10px] tabular-nums text-muted">
              {items.length} / {total}
            </span>
            {!library && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="icon-btn h-6 w-6"
                title="Open the full stamp library"
                aria-label="Open the full stamp library"
                onClick={() => setLibraryOpen(true)}
              >
                <Maximize2 size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
              </button>
            )}
          </span>
        }
      >
        Stamps
      </SectionTitle>

      <label className="relative block">
        <Search size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" />
        <input
          className="text-input pl-7"
          placeholder={`Search ${total} stamps and your own…`}
          value={query}
          onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
          aria-label="Search stamps"
        />
      </label>

      <div className="scroll-thin -mx-1 flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1">
        <button type="button" className="chip" aria-pressed={group === "all"} onClick={() => setGroup("all")}>
          All
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="chip" aria-pressed={group === "custom"} onClick={() => setGroup("custom")}>
          Custom <span className="opacity-60">{customStamps.length}</span>
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="chip" aria-pressed={group === "user"} onClick={() => setGroup("user")}>
          My stamps <span className="opacity-60">{userStamps.length}</span>
        </button>
        {groups.map((entry) => (
          <button
            key={entry.slug}
            type="button"
            className="chip"
            aria-pressed={group === entry.slug}
            onClick={() => setGroup(entry.slug)}
          >
            {entry.name} <span className="opacity-60">{entry.count}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div
        ref={scrollRef}
        className="scroll-thin min-h-0 overflow-y-auto pr-1"
        style={library ? { maxHeight: "58vh" } : { maxHeight: 320 }}
        onScroll={(event) => setScrollTop(event.currentTarget.scrollTop)}
      >
        <div className="relative" style={{ height: rows * stride }}>
          <div
            className="absolute left-0 right-0 grid"
            style={{
              top: firstRow * stride,
              gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${columns}, minmax(0, 1fr))`,
              gap: GAP,
              gridAutoRows: TILE_PX,
            }}
          >
            {visible.map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                className="tile group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden text-left"
                aria-pressed={selectedStampId === item.id}
                title={`${item.name} — ${item.sub}`}
                onClick={() => {
                  const resolved = resolveStamp(item.id);
                  if (resolved) selectStamp(resolved.id);
                  onPick?.();
                }}
              >
                <span className="checker flex h-[52px] w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-center p-1.5">
                  <img
                    src={item.imageUrl}
                    alt={item.name}
                    loading="lazy"
                    decoding="async"
                    className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain drop-shadow-sm"
                  />
                </span>
                <span className="truncate px-1.5 py-1 text-[9.5px] leading-tight text-muted group-hover:text-fg">
                  {item.name}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        {items.length === 0 && (
          <p className="py-6 text-center text-[11.5px] text-muted">No stamp matches “{query}”.</p>
        )}
      </div>

      {library && (
        <p className="text-[10.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
          {stamps.length} factory stamps migrated · 3 recipes have no recoverable bitmap and keep their vector ink for
          Phase 3 · {userStamps.length} from your Tools folder · {customStamps.length} saved from snapshots.
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function StampLibrary() {

FILE: src\components\ToolBar.tsx (3.1 KB)

import { Shapes } from "lucide-react";
import { isShapeTool, NO_POPOVER_TOOLS, TOOLBAR, TOOL_BY_ID } from "../lib/tools";
import type { ToolId } from "../lib/types";
import { useAnnotationStore } from "../stores/useAnnotationStore";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { TOOL_ICONS } from "./toolIcons";

export function ToolBar() {
  const activeTool = useToolStore((state) => state.activeTool);
  const setTool = useToolStore((state) => state.setTool);
  const shapeActive = isShapeTool(activeTool);
  const ShapeIcon = shapeActive ? TOOL_ICONS[activeTool] : Shapes;

  const pickTool = (tool: ToolId) => {
    const ui = useUIStore.getState();
    const selection = useAnnotationStore.getState().selection;
    if (useToolStore.getState().activeTool === tool) {
      if (!NO_POPOVER_TOOLS.includes(tool)) ui.toggleToolPopover();
      return;
    }
    setTool(tool);
    if (NO_POPOVER_TOOLS.includes(tool)) {
      if (tool === "select" && selection.length > 0) ui.openToolPopover();
      else ui.closeToolPopover();
    } else {
      ui.openToolPopover();
    }
    if (tool === "image") window.dispatchEvent(new Event("pm:pick-image"));
    if (tool === "formula") window.dispatchEvent(new Event("pm:open-formula"));
  };

  const renderButton = (entry: ToolId | "shapes") => {
    if (entry === "shapes") {
      return (
        <div key="shapes" className="tooltip tooltip-left">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="tool-btn"
            aria-pressed={shapeActive}
            aria-label="Shapes"
            onClick={() => {
              const tools = useToolStore.getState();
              const ui = useUIStore.getState();
              if (!isShapeTool(tools.activeTool)) tools.setTool(tools.lastShapeTool);
              ui.openToolPopover();
            }}
          >
            <ShapeIcon size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </button>
          <span className="tooltip-body">{shapeActive ? `${TOOL_BY_ID.get(activeTool)?.name}` : "Shapes"}</span>
        </div>
      );
    }
    const meta = TOOL_BY_ID.get(entry);
    const Icon = TOOL_ICONS[entry];
    if (!meta) return null;
    return (
      <div key={entry} className="tooltip tooltip-left">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="tool-btn"
          aria-pressed={activeTool === entry}
          aria-label={meta.name}
          onClick={() => pickTool(entry)}
        >
          <Icon size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
        <span className="tooltip-body">
          {meta.name} <span className="text-muted">{meta.shortcut}</span>
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="tool-rail absolute right-[18px] top-1/2 z-30 -translate-y-1/2">
      <div className="glass no-scrollbar relative flex flex-col items-center gap-1 rounded-[18px] p-1">
        {TOOLBAR.map((group, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
            {index > 0 && <div className="my-0.5 h-px w-6 bg-[var(--border)]" />}
            {group.map(renderButton)}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import {
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Combine,
  FileDown,
  FilePlus,
  FolderOpen,
  Play,
  Plus,
  Printer,
  Redo2,
  Save,
  Settings,
  Undo2,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import { combinePdfs, exportInputFromTab, exportPdf } from "../lib/pdfEngine";
import { printActiveDocument, saveActiveDocument } from "../lib/save";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";
import { ThemeSwitcher } from "./ThemeSwitcher";
import { ZoomPill } from "./ZoomPill";

function FileAction({
  label,
  shortcut,
  icon,
  onClick,
}: {
  label: string;
  shortcut?: string;
  icon: ReactNode;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[11.5px] hover:bg-[var(--hover)]"
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <span className="text-muted">{icon}</span>
      <span className="flex-1">{label}</span>
      {shortcut && <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{shortcut}</span>}
    </button>
  );
}

function PagePill() {
  const tabs = useDocStore((state) => state.tabs);
  const activeId = useDocStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const page = useDocStore((state) => state.page);
  const setPage = useDocStore((state) => state.setPage);
  const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === activeId) ?? tabs[0];
  const count = tab?.pages.length ?? 1;
  const current = Math.min(Math.max(1, page), count);

  return (
    <div className="pill">
      <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7" aria-label="Previous page" disabled={current <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(current - 1)}>
        <ChevronLeft size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>
      <select
        className="h-7 min-w-[64px] max-w-[96px] cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-transparent px-0.5 text-center text-[11.5px] font-medium tabular-nums outline-none"
        aria-label="Jump to page"
        value={current}
        onChange={(event) => setPage(Number(event.target.value))}
      >
        {tab.pages.map((item, index) => (
          <option key={item.id} value={index + 1}>
            {index + 1} / {count}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7" aria-label="Next page" disabled={current >= count} onClick={() => setPage(current + 1)}>
        <ChevronRight size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

export function TopBar() {
  const tabs = useDocStore((state) => state.tabs);
  const activeId = useDocStore((state) => state.activeId);
  const setActive = useDocStore((state) => state.setActive);
  const closeTab = useDocStore((state) => state.closeTab);
  const setNewDocOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setNewDocOpen);
  const setPresentation = useUIStore((state) => state.setPresentation);
  const setSettingsOpen = useUIStore((state) => state.setSettingsOpen);
  const saving = useUIStore((state) => state.saving);
  const setToast = useUIStore((state) => state.setToast);

  const [filesOpen, setFilesOpen] = useState(false);
  const [combineOpen, setCombineOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedTabIds, setSelectedTabIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [combineFiles, setCombineFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const filesRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const combineRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const tabStripRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const pdfTabs = tabs;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!filesOpen) return;
    const onPointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (filesRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) return;
      setFilesOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);
  }, [filesOpen]);

  // A mouse wheel over the tab strip walks through tabs instead of the page.
  useEffect(() => {
    const strip = tabStripRef.current;
    if (!strip) return;
    const onWheel = (event: WheelEvent) => {
      if (event.ctrlKey || Math.abs(event.deltaX) > Math.abs(event.deltaY)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      strip.scrollLeft -= event.deltaY;
    };
    strip.addEventListener("wheel", onWheel, { passive: false });
    return () => strip.removeEventListener("wheel", onWheel);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!combineOpen) return;
    setSelectedTabIds(pdfTabs.map((tab) => tab.id));
    setCombineFiles([]);
    // Only the tab list at opening time matters.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [combineOpen]);

  const combine = async () => {
    const parts: Uint8Array[] = [];
    try {
      for (const id of selectedTabIds) {
        const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === id);
        // Export each tab so annotations, crops, page order and paper pages survive.
        if (tab) parts.push(await exportPdf(exportInputFromTab(tab, "flattened")));
      }
      for (const file of combineFiles) parts.push(new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()));
      if (parts.length < 2) {
        setToast("Choose at least two documents to combine");
        return;
      }
      const bytes = await combinePdfs(parts);
      await useDocStore.getState().openPdfBytes(bytes, `Combined ${parts.length} documents`);
      setCombineOpen(false);
      setToast(`Combined ${parts.length} documents`);
    } catch (error) {
      setToast(`Could not combine: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
    }
  };

  const closeWithUndo = (id: string) => {
    const tab = tabs.find((item) => item.id === id);
    if (!tab) return;
    closeTab(id);
    if (useDocStore.getState().lastClosedTab?.tab.id === id) {
      setToast(`Closed "${tab.title}"`, () => useDocStore.getState().undoCloseTab());
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <header className="top-bar glass relative z-[120] flex h-14 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-x-0 border-t-0 px-2.5">
        <div ref={filesRef} className="relative">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="chip h-8 gap-1.5 px-2.5"
            aria-label="Files menu"
            aria-expanded={filesOpen}
            onClick={() => setFilesOpen((open) => !open)}
          >
            <FolderOpen size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Files
          </button>
          {filesOpen && (
            <div className="menu-surface anim-in absolute left-0 top-[calc(100%+6px)] z-[100] w-[228px] rounded-xl p-1.5">
              <FileAction
                label="Open Document..."
                shortcut="Ctrl+O"
                icon={<FolderOpen size={13} />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setFilesOpen(false);
                  document.getElementById("pm-pdf-input")?.click();
                }}
              />
              <FileAction
                label="Create Document..."
                icon={<FilePlus size={13} />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setFilesOpen(false);
                  setNewDocOpen(true);
                }}
              />
              <FileAction
                label="Combine Documents..."
                icon={<Combine size={13} />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setFilesOpen(false);
                  setCombineOpen(true);
                }}
              />
              <div className="divider my-1" />
              <FileAction
                label="Save"
                shortcut="Ctrl+S"
                icon={<Save size={13} />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setFilesOpen(false);
                  void saveActiveDocument("editable");
                }}
              />
              <FileAction
                label="Save As..."
                icon={<Save size={13} />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setFilesOpen(false);
                  void saveActiveDocument("editable", true);
                }}
              />
              <FileAction
                label="Save Flattened PDF..."
                icon={<FileDown size={13} />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setFilesOpen(false);
                  void saveActiveDocument("flattened");
                }}
              />
              <div className="divider my-1" />
              <FileAction
                label="Print..."
                shortcut="Ctrl+P"
                icon={<Printer size={13} />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setFilesOpen(false);
                  void printActiveDocument();
                }}
              />
              <FileAction
                label="Print Current Page"
                icon={<Printer size={13} />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setFilesOpen(false);
                  window.print();
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="mx-0.5 h-7 w-px shrink-0 bg-[var(--border)]" />

        <div ref={tabStripRef} className="scroll-thin flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-0.5">
          {tabs.map((tab) => {
            const selected = tab.id === activeId;
            return (
              <div
                key={tab.id}
                className={`group flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-[10px] border pr-1 transition-colors ${
                  selected ? "border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--glass-strong)] shadow-sm" : "border-transparent hover:bg-[var(--hover)]"
                }`}
              >
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="max-w-[190px] truncate pl-2.5 pr-1 text-[11.5px] font-medium"
                  style={{ color: selected ? "var(--fg)" : "var(--muted)" }}
                  onClick={() => setActive(tab.id)}
                  title={tab.title}
                >
                  {tab.title}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="grid h-5 w-5 place-items-center rounded-md text-muted opacity-0 transition-opacity hover:bg-[var(--active)] hover:text-fg group-hover:opacity-100"
                  aria-label={`Close ${tab.title}`}
                  onClick={() => closeWithUndo(tab.id)}
                >
                  <X size={12} strokeWidth={2} />
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          <button
            type="button"
            className="icon-btn h-8 w-8 shrink-0"
            aria-label="New document"
            title="New document"
            onClick={() => setNewDocOpen(true)}
          >
            <Plus size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="hidden sm:block">
          <PagePill />
        </div>
        <div className="hidden md:block">
          <ZoomPill />
        </div>

        <div className="mx-0.5 hidden h-7 w-px bg-[var(--border)] sm:block" />

        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn hidden sm:grid"
          aria-label="Undo"
          title="Undo (Ctrl+Z)"
          onClick={() => useDocStore.getState().undo()}
        >
          <Undo2 size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn hidden sm:grid"
          aria-label="Redo"
          title="Redo (Ctrl+Y)"
          onClick={() => useDocStore.getState().redo()}
        >
          <Redo2 size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn hidden sm:grid"
          aria-label="Save editable PDF"
          title="Save as PDF with editable annotations (Ctrl+S)"
          disabled={saving}
          onClick={() => void saveActiveDocument("editable")}
        >
          <Save size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn hidden sm:grid"
          aria-label="Presentation mode"
          title="Presentation mode"
          onClick={() => setPresentation(true)}
        >
          <Play size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="icon-btn hidden sm:grid"
          aria-label="Settings"
          title="Settings (Ctrl+,)"
          onClick={() => setSettingsOpen(true)}
        >
          <Settings size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </button>
        <div className="ml-1 hidden sm:block">
          <ThemeSwitcher />
        </div>
      </header>

      {combineOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/25 p-4 backdrop-blur-[3px]"
          onPointerDown={(event) => {
            if (event.target === event.currentTarget) setCombineOpen(false);
          }}
        >
          <div className="glass-strong anim-in flex w-full max-w-[440px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Combine documents">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-4 py-3">
              <Combine size={16} />
              <span className="flex-1 text-[13px] font-semibold">Combine documents</span>
              <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7" aria-label="Close combine dialog" onClick={() => setCombineOpen(false)}>
                <X size={14} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex max-h-[60vh] flex-col gap-3 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-3">
              <span className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">Open tabs</span>
              {pdfTabs.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">No tabs are open. Choose files below.</p>
              ) : (
                pdfTabs.map((tab) => (
                  <label key={tab.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-1 py-0.5 text-[11.5px] hover:bg-[var(--hover)]">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={selectedTabIds.includes(tab.id)}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setSelectedTabIds((ids) =>
                          event.target.checked ? [...ids, tab.id] : ids.filter((id) => id !== tab.id),
                        )
                      }
                    />
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{tab.title}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{tab.pages.length} pages</span>
                  </label>
                ))
              )}

              <div className="divider" />
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">PDF files</span>
                <button type="button" className="chip h-7" onClick={() => combineRef.current?.click()}>
                  <FilePlus size={12} /> Choose files
                </button>
              </div>
              {combineFiles.length > 0 && (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  {combineFiles.map((file, index) => (
                    <div key={`${file.name}-${index}`} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11.5px]">
                      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{file.name}</span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="icon-btn h-5 w-5"
                        aria-label={`Remove ${file.name}`}
                        onClick={() => setCombineFiles((list) => list.filter((_, position) => position !== index))}
                      >
                        <X size={11} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
              <input
                ref={combineRef}
                type="file"
                multiple
                accept="application/pdf,.pdf"
                className="hidden"
                aria-label="Choose PDF files to combine"
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setCombineFiles((list) => [...list, ...Array.from(event.target.files ?? [])]);
                  event.target.value = "";
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-[var(--border)] px-4 py-3">
              <button type="button" className="chip h-8" onClick={() => setCombineOpen(false)}>
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button type="button" className="chip h-8" aria-pressed="true" onClick={() => void combine()}>
                <Combine size={13} /> Combine
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

import type { CSSProperties, ReactNode } from "react";
import { SWATCHES } from "../../lib/presets";
import { CustomColorPicker } from "./CustomColorPicker";

export function Segmented<T extends string>({
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: T;
  options: { value: T; label: string }[];
  onChange: (value: T) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="seg" role="group">
      {options.map((option) => (
        <button
          key={option.value}
          type="button"
          aria-pressed={option.value === value}
          onClick={() => onChange(option.value)}
        >
          {option.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Switch({
  checked,
  onChange,
  label,
}: {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
  label: string;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
      className="relative h-[22px] w-[38px] shrink-0 rounded-full border transition-colors duration-150"
      style={{
        background: checked ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--track)",
        borderColor: checked ? "transparent" : "var(--border)",
      }}
    >
      <span
        className="absolute top-1/2 h-[18px] w-[18px] -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-white shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgba(16,24,40,0.35)] transition-all duration-150"
        style={{ left: checked ? 18 : 2 }}
      />
    </button>
  );
}

export function ToggleRow({
  label,
  hint,
  checked,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  hint?: string;
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <div className="text-[12px] font-medium">{label}</div>
        {hint && <div className="text-[10.5px] leading-snug text-muted">{hint}</div>}
      </div>
      <Switch checked={checked} onChange={onChange} label={label} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function SliderRow({
  label,
  value,
  min,
  max,
  step = 0.1,
  unit,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  step?: number;
  unit?: string;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
}) {
  const fill = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((value - min) / (max - min)) * 100));
  return (
    <label className="field">
      <span className="field-label">
        <span>{label}</span>
        <span className="field-value">
          {Number(value.toFixed(2))}
          {unit ? ` ${unit}` : ""}
        </span>
      </span>
      <input
        type="range"
        className="range"
        min={min}
        max={max}
        step={step}
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(Number(event.target.value))}
        style={{ "--fill": `${fill}%` } as CSSProperties}
      />
    </label>
  );
}

export function SectionTitle({ children, aside }: { children: ReactNode; aside?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
      <h3 className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">{children}</h3>
      {aside}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ColorRow({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  allowNone = false,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string | null;
  onChange: (value: string | null) => void;
  allowNone?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="field">
      <div className="field-label">
        <span>{label}</span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="field-value">{value ?? "None"}</span>
          {allowNone && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="chip h-5 shrink-0 px-1.5 text-[9px] normal-case tracking-normal"
              aria-pressed={value === null}
              onClick={() => onChange(null)}
            >
              None
            </button>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        {SWATCHES.map((swatch) => (
          <button
            key={swatch}
            type="button"
            className="swatch"
            style={{ background: swatch }}
            aria-label={`${label} ${swatch}`}
            aria-pressed={value?.toLowerCase() === swatch.toLowerCase()}
            onClick={() => onChange(swatch)}
          />
        ))}
        <CustomColorPicker value={value} onChange={onChange} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
