import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Pipette } from "lucide-react";
import { SWATCHES } from "../../lib/presets";

declare global {
  interface Window {
    EyeDropper?: new () => { open: () => Promise<{ sRGBHex: string }> };
  }
}

interface Hsv {
  h: number;
  s: number;
  v: number;
}

const RECENT_KEY = "pm.recentColors";

function clamp01(value: number): number {
  return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, value));
}

function parseHex(hex: string): [number, number, number] {
  const clean = hex.replace("#", "");
  const value = clean.length === 3 ? clean.split("").map((part) => part + part).join("") : clean.padEnd(6, "0");
  const number = Number.parseInt(value.slice(0, 6), 16);
  return [(number >> 16) & 255, (number >> 8) & 255, number & 255];
}

function rgbToHex(r: number, g: number, b: number): string {
  const part = (value: number) => Math.round(Math.min(255, Math.max(0, value))).toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
  return `#${part(r)}${part(g)}${part(b)}`.toUpperCase();
}

export function hexToHsv(hex: string): Hsv {
  const [r, g, b] = parseHex(hex).map((value) => value / 255);
  const max = Math.max(r, g, b);
  const min = Math.min(r, g, b);
  const delta = max - min;
  let h = 0;
  if (delta > 0) {
    if (max === r) h = ((g - b) / delta) % 6;
    else if (max === g) h = (b - r) / delta + 2;
    else h = (r - g) / delta + 4;
  }
  return { h: (h * 60 + 360) % 360, s: max === 0 ? 0 : delta / max, v: max };
}

export function hsvToHex({ h, s, v }: Hsv): string {
  const c = v * s;
  const x = c * (1 - Math.abs(((h / 60) % 2) - 1));
  const m = v - c;
  const sector = Math.floor(h / 60) % 6;
  const [r, g, b] =
    sector === 0 ? [c, x, 0] :
    sector === 1 ? [x, c, 0] :
    sector === 2 ? [0, c, x] :
    sector === 3 ? [0, x, c] :
    sector === 4 ? [x, 0, c] :
    [c, 0, x];
  return rgbToHex((r + m) * 255, (g + m) * 255, (b + m) * 255);
}

function readRecent(): string[] {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return [];
  try {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RECENT_KEY) ?? "[]") as unknown;
    return Array.isArray(saved) ? saved.filter((value): value is string => typeof value === "string") : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function saveRecent(colors: string[]): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem(RECENT_KEY, JSON.stringify(colors.slice(0, 8)));
}

const QUICK = SWATCHES.slice(0, 9);

/** Apple-style glass colour studio: SV square, hue slider, eyedropper, hex and RGB. */
export function CustomColorPicker({
  value,
  onChange,
  className = "",
}: {
  value: string | null;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [hsv, setHsv] = useState<Hsv>(() => hexToHsv(value ?? "#101114"));
  const [hexText, setHexText] = useState(value ?? "#101114");
  const [recent, setRecent] = useState<string[]>(readRecent);
  const dragging = useRef(false);
  const squareRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const next = hexToHsv(value ?? "#101114");
    setHsv(next);
    setHexText(hsvToHex(next));
  }, [open, value]);

  const commit = (next: Hsv) => {
    setHsv(next);
    const hex = hsvToHex(next);
    setHexText(hex);
    onChange(hex);
  };

  const remember = (color: string) => {
    setRecent((previous) => {
      const next = [color, ...previous.filter((item) => item.toLowerCase() !== color.toLowerCase())].slice(0, 8);
      saveRecent(next);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const pick = (clientX: number, clientY: number) => {
    const rect = squareRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!rect) return;
    commit({ ...hsv, s: clamp01((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width), v: 1 - clamp01((clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) });
  };

  const applyHex = (text: string) => {
    setHexText(text);
    if (/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(text)) {
      const next = hexToHsv(text);
      setHsv(next);
      onChange(text.toUpperCase());
    }
  };

  const [r, g, b] = parseHex(hsvToHex(hsv));
  const rgb = { r, g, b };
  const solid = value ?? hsvToHex(hsv);

  const dropColor = async () => {
    if (!window.EyeDropper) return;
    try {
      const result = await new window.EyeDropper().open();
      applyHex(result.sRGBHex);
      remember(result.sRGBHex);
    } catch {
      // The user cancelled the eyedropper.
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`relative ${className}`}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="relative grid h-6 w-6 shrink-0 place-items-center overflow-hidden rounded-[7px] border border-[var(--border)]"
        title="Custom colour"
        aria-label="Custom colour"
        aria-pressed={open}
        onClick={() => setOpen((state) => !state)}
        style={{
          backgroundColor: solid,
          backgroundImage: value ? undefined : "conic-gradient(#ff3b30, #ffcc00, #34c759, #0a84ff, #5e5ce6, #ff2d55, #ff3b30)",
        }}
      >
        {!value && <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-white drop-shadow">+</span>}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          className="menu-surface anim-in absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] z-[100] w-[244px] rounded-2xl p-2.5"
          onPointerDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
        >
          <div
            ref={squareRef}
            className="relative h-[132px] w-full cursor-crosshair overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--border)]"
            style={{
              background: `linear-gradient(to top, #000, transparent), linear-gradient(to right, #fff, hsl(${hsv.h} 100% 50%))`,
            }}
            onPointerDown={(event) => {
              event.preventDefault();
              dragging.current = true;
              event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
              pick(event.clientX, event.clientY);
            }}
            onPointerMove={(event) => {
              if (dragging.current) pick(event.clientX, event.clientY);
            }}
            onPointerUp={(event) => {
              dragging.current = false;
              if (event.currentTarget.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) event.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);
              remember(hsvToHex(hsv));
            }}
            onPointerCancel={() => {
              dragging.current = false;
            }}
          >
            <span
              className="pointer-events-none absolute h-3.5 w-3.5 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full border-2 border-white shadow-[0_1px_4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.55)]"
              style={{ left: `${hsv.s * 100}%`, top: `${(1 - hsv.v) * 100}%`, background: solid }}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="icon-btn h-7 w-7 shrink-0"
              title="Eyedropper"
              aria-label="Eyedropper"
              disabled={!window.EyeDropper}
              onClick={() => void dropColor()}
            >
              <Pipette size={13} />
            </button>
            <input
              type="range"
              className="hue-range min-w-0 flex-1"
              min={0}
              max={360}
              step={1}
              value={Math.round(hsv.h)}
              aria-label="Hue"
              onChange={(event) => commit({ ...hsv, h: Number(event.target.value) })}
            />
            <span
              className="h-6 w-6 shrink-0 rounded-[7px] border border-[var(--border)]"
              style={{ background: solid }}
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <input
              className="text-input h-7 min-w-0 flex-1 text-[11px] uppercase"
              value={hexText}
              aria-label="Hex colour"
              placeholder="#0044FF"
              onChange={(event) => applyHex(event.target.value)}
              onBlur={() => remember(hsvToHex(hsv))}
            />
            {(["r", "g", "b"] as const).map((channel) => (
              <label key={channel} className="flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-md border border-[var(--border)] px-1 text-[9px] uppercase text-muted">
                {channel}
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  max={255}
                  className="h-6 w-8 bg-transparent text-[10.5px] tabular-nums outline-none"
                  value={rgb[channel]}
                  aria-label={`${channel.toUpperCase()} channel`}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    const next = { ...rgb, [channel]: Number(event.target.value) };
                    applyHex(rgbToHex(next.r, next.g, next.b));
                  }}
                />
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-9 gap-1">
            {QUICK.map((color) => (
              <button
                key={color}
                type="button"
                className="h-5 w-full rounded-[5px] border border-[var(--border)]"
                style={{ background: color }}
                aria-label={`Colour ${color}`}
                onClick={() => {
                  applyHex(color);
                  remember(color);
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>

          {recent.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-muted">Recent</span>
              {recent.slice(0, 6).map((color) => (
                <button
                  key={color}
                  type="button"
                  className="h-4 w-4 rounded-full border border-[var(--border)]"
                  style={{ background: color }}
                  aria-label={`Recent colour ${color}`}
                  onClick={() => applyHex(color)}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src\components\ZoomPill.tsx (1.7 KB)
================================================================================
import { Maximize2, Minus, MoveHorizontal, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";

export function ZoomPill() {
  const zoom = useDocStore((state) => state.zoom);
  const fitMode = useDocStore((state) => state.fitMode);
  const zoomBy = useDocStore((state) => state.zoomBy);
  const setFit = useDocStore((state) => state.setFit);
  const setZoom = useDocStore((state) => state.setZoom);

  return (
    <div className="pill">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
        aria-label="Fit to page"
        aria-pressed={fitMode === "page"}
        title="Fit to page"
        onClick={() => setFit("page")}
      >
        <Maximize2 size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="icon-btn h-7 w-7"
        aria-label="Fit to width"
        aria-pressed={fitMode === "width"}
        title="Fit to width"
        onClick={() => setFit("width")}
      >
        <MoveHorizontal size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="h-7 min-w-[54px] rounded-lg px-1 text-center text-[11.5px] font-medium tabular-nums hover:bg-[var(--hover)]"
        title="Reset to 100 %"
        onClick={() => setZoom(1)}
      >
        {Math.round(zoom * 100)}%
      </button>
      <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7" aria-label="Zoom out" onClick={() => zoomBy(-1)}>
        <Minus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>
      <button type="button" className="icon-btn h-7 w-7" aria-label="Zoom in" onClick={() => zoomBy(1)}>
        <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { FormulaAnnotation, TextAnnotation } from "./annotationTypes";
import { annotationBounds, hitTest, regularPolygonPoints, rotateAnnotation, scaleAnnotation } from "./annotationGeometry";

const text: TextAnnotation = {
  id: "text-1",
  type: "text",
  xMm: 20,
  yMm: 30,
  widthMm: 60,
  heightMm: 16,
  text: "Hello",
  fontFamily: "Helvetica",
  fontSizePt: 12,
  textColor: "#101114",
  bold: false,
  italic: false,
  underline: false,
  strikeOut: false,
  align: "left",
  script: "normal",
  boxFillColor: "#ffffff",
  boxFillTransparent: true,
  frameType: 0,
  frameColor: "#101114",
  lineWidthMm: 0.4,
  autoFit: false,
  rotation: 0,
  opacity: 1,
};

const formula: FormulaAnnotation = {
  id: "formula-1",
  type: "formula",
  latex: "E = mc^2",
  xMm: 100,
  yMm: 40,
  widthMm: 40,
  heightMm: 20,
  color: "#0a84ff",
  fontSizePt: 14,
  fillColor: "#ffffff",
  fillTransparent: true,
  rotation: 0,
  opacity: 1,
};

describe("box annotation geometry", () => {
  it("bounds an unrotated text box by its frame", () => {
    expect(annotationBounds(text)).toEqual({ x: 20, y: 30, width: 60, height: 16 });
  });

  it("swaps width and height when the box is rotated 90°", () => {
    const bounds = annotationBounds({ ...text, rotation: 90 });
    expect(bounds.width).toBeCloseTo(16);
    expect(bounds.height).toBeCloseTo(60);
    expect(bounds.x + bounds.width / 2).toBeCloseTo(50);
    expect(bounds.y + bounds.height / 2).toBeCloseTo(38);
  });

  it("hit-tests inside a rotated formula and misses outside it", () => {
    const rotated = { ...formula, rotation: 90 };
    expect(hitTest(rotated, [120, 60])).toBe(true);
    expect(hitTest(rotated, [140, 40])).toBe(false);
  });

  it("grows the font with the box and keeps text centred", () => {
    const scaled = scaleAnnotation(text, [50, 38], 2, 2);
    expect(scaled.type).toBe("text");
    if (scaled.type !== "text") return;
    expect(scaled.widthMm).toBeCloseTo(120);
    expect(scaled.heightMm).toBeCloseTo(32);
    expect(scaled.fontSizePt).toBeCloseTo(24);
    expect(scaled.xMm + scaled.widthMm / 2).toBeCloseTo(50);
    expect(scaled.yMm + scaled.heightMm / 2).toBeCloseTo(38);
  });

  it("rotates text about its centre and normalises the angle", () => {
    const rotated = rotateAnnotation(text, [50, 38], 400);
    expect(rotated.type).toBe("text");
    if (rotated.type !== "text") return;
    expect(rotated.rotation).toBeCloseTo(40);
    expect(rotated.xMm + rotated.widthMm / 2).toBeCloseTo(50);
    expect(rotated.yMm + rotated.heightMm / 2).toBeCloseTo(38);
  });
});

describe("regular polygon drag", () => {
  it("fits a hexagon inside the drag box", () => {
    const points = regularPolygonPoints([10, 20], [60, 70], 6);
    expect(points).toHaveLength(6);
    for (const [x, y] of points) {
      expect(x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(10 - 1e-9);
      expect(x).toBeLessThanOrEqual(60 + 1e-9);
      expect(y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(20 - 1e-9);
      expect(y).toBeLessThanOrEqual(70 + 1e-9);
    }
    expect(new Set(points.map(([x]) => Number(x.toFixed(4)))).size).toBeGreaterThan(2);
  });
});

================================================================================
FILE: src\lib\annotationGeometry.ts (14.6 KB)

import type { Annotation, BBox, InkPoint, Pt } from "./annotationTypes";

const RAD = Math.PI / 180;

export function normalizeAngle(angle: number): number {
  const wrapped = angle % 360;
  return wrapped < 0 ? wrapped + 360 : wrapped;
}

export function ptDistance(a: Pt, b: Pt): number {
  return Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]);
}

export function pointSegmentDistance(p: Pt, a: Pt, b: Pt): number {
  const dx = b[0] - a[0];
  const dy = b[1] - a[1];
  const lengthSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
  if (lengthSq === 0) return ptDistance(p, a);
  const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((p[0] - a[0]) * dx + (p[1] - a[1]) * dy) / lengthSq));
  return ptDistance(p, [a[0] + t * dx, a[1] + t * dy]);
}

function pointsBounds(points: Pt[]): BBox {
  let minX = Infinity;
  let minY = Infinity;
  let maxX = -Infinity;
  let maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const [x, y] of points) {
    if (x < minX) minX = x;
    if (y < minY) minY = y;
    if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
    if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(minX)) return { x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };
  return { x: minX, y: minY, width: maxX - minX, height: maxY - minY };
}

export function inflate(box: BBox, by: number): BBox {
  return { x: box.x - by, y: box.y - by, width: box.width + by * 2, height: box.height + by * 2 };
}

export function unionBounds(boxes: BBox[]): BBox {
  if (boxes.length === 0) return { x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };
  let minX = Infinity;
  let minY = Infinity;
  let maxX = -Infinity;
  let maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const box of boxes) {
    if (box.x < minX) minX = box.x;
    if (box.y < minY) minY = box.y;
    if (box.x + box.width > maxX) maxX = box.x + box.width;
    if (box.y + box.height > maxY) maxY = box.y + box.height;
  }
  return { x: minX, y: minY, width: maxX - minX, height: maxY - minY };
}

export function boundsIntersect(a: BBox, b: BBox): boolean {
  return a.x < b.x + b.width && a.x + a.width > b.x && a.y < b.y + b.height && a.y + a.height > b.y;
}

export function boundsContain(outer: BBox, inner: BBox): boolean {
  return (
    inner.x >= outer.x &&
    inner.y >= outer.y &&
    inner.x + inner.width <= outer.x + outer.width &&
    inner.y + inner.height <= outer.y + outer.height
  );
}

export function boxCenter(box: BBox): Pt {
  return [box.x + box.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2];
}

/** Corners of a rotated box, in page-local millimetres. */
export function boxCorners(xMm: number, yMm: number, widthMm: number, heightMm: number, rotation: number): Pt[] {
  const cx = xMm + widthMm / 2;
  const cy = yMm + heightMm / 2;
  const angle = rotation * RAD;
  const cos = Math.cos(angle);
  const sin = Math.sin(angle);
  const hw = widthMm / 2;
  const hh = heightMm / 2;
  return [
    [-hw, -hh],
    [hw, -hh],
    [hw, hh],
    [-hw, hh],
  ].map(([x, y]) => [cx + x * cos - y * sin, cy + x * sin + y * cos] as Pt);
}

export function isBoxAnnotation(
  annotation: Annotation,
): annotation is Extract<Annotation, { type: "stamp" | "text" | "formula" }> {
  return annotation.type === "stamp" || annotation.type === "text" || annotation.type === "formula";
}

export function annotationBounds(annotation: Annotation): BBox {
  switch (annotation.type) {
    case "pen":
    case "marker":
      return inflate(pointsBounds(annotation.points.map(([x, y]) => [x, y] as Pt)), annotation.thicknessMm / 2);
    case "shape":
      return inflate(
        pointsBounds([annotation.start, annotation.end, ...annotation.points]),
        Math.max(annotation.lineWidthMm, 0) / 2,
      );
    case "stamp":
    case "text":
    case "formula":
      return pointsBounds(
        boxCorners(annotation.xMm, annotation.yMm, annotation.widthMm, annotation.heightMm, annotation.rotation),
      );
  }
}

export function selectionBounds(annotations: Annotation[]): BBox | null {
  if (annotations.length === 0) return null;
  return unionBounds(annotations.map(annotationBounds));
}

function distanceToInk(points: InkPoint[], p: Pt): number {
  let best = Infinity;
  if (points.length === 1) return ptDistance(p, [points[0][0], points[0][1]]);
  for (let index = 1; index < points.length; index += 1) {
    const distance = pointSegmentDistance(p, [points[index - 1][0], points[index - 1][1]], [points[index][0], points[index][1]]);
    if (distance < best) best = distance;
  }
  return best;
}

function distanceToPolygon(points: Pt[], p: Pt, closed: boolean): number {
  if (points.length === 0) return Infinity;
  if (points.length === 1) return ptDistance(p, points[0]);
  let best = Infinity;
  for (let index = 1; index < points.length; index += 1) {
    best = Math.min(best, pointSegmentDistance(p, points[index - 1], points[index]));
  }
  if (closed) best = Math.min(best, pointSegmentDistance(p, points[points.length - 1], points[0]));
  return best;
}

export function pointInPolygon(point: Pt, polygon: Pt[]): boolean {
  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = polygon.length - 1; i < polygon.length; j = i, i += 1) {
    const [xi, yi] = polygon[i];
    const [xj, yj] = polygon[j];
    if (yi > point[1] !== yj > point[1] && point[0] < ((xj - xi) * (point[1] - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) {
      inside = !inside;
    }
  }
  return inside;
}

/** True when a pointer at `p` is close enough to grab the annotation. */
export function hitTest(annotation: Annotation, p: Pt, toleranceMm = 1.4): boolean {
  if (annotation.hidden) return false;
  switch (annotation.type) {
    case "pen":
    case "marker":
      return distanceToInk(annotation.points, p) <= annotation.thicknessMm / 2 + toleranceMm;
    case "shape": {
      const reach = annotation.lineWidthMm / 2 + toleranceMm;
      const vertices = [annotation.start, annotation.end, ...annotation.points];
      if (annotation.shapeType === "polygon") {
        return distanceToPolygon(vertices, p, annotation.points.length >= 3) <= reach;
      }
      if (annotation.shapeType === "rectangle" || annotation.shapeType === "ellipse") {
        const box = pointsBounds([annotation.start, annotation.end]);
        const corners: Pt[] = [
          [box.x, box.y],
          [box.x + box.width, box.y],
          [box.x + box.width, box.y + box.height],
          [box.x, box.y + box.height],
        ];
        const filled = Boolean(annotation.fillColor) && !annotation.fillTransparent;
        if (annotation.shapeType === "rectangle") {
          return distanceToPolygon(corners, p, true) <= reach || (filled && pointInBox(p, box));
        }
        const radius = ellipseRadius(box, p);
        if (filled && radius <= 1) return true;
        return Math.abs(radius - 1) * (Math.min(box.width, box.height) / 2) <= reach;
      }
      return pointSegmentDistance(p, annotation.start, annotation.end) <= reach;
    }
    case "stamp": {
      const local = toStampLocal(annotation, p);
      return (
        local[0] >= 0 && local[1] >= 0 && local[0] <= annotation.widthMm && local[1] <= annotation.heightMm
      );
    }
    case "text":
    case "formula": {
      const local = toBoxLocal(annotation, p);
      return (
        local[0] >= -toleranceMm &&
        local[1] >= -toleranceMm &&
        local[0] <= annotation.widthMm + toleranceMm &&
        local[1] <= annotation.heightMm + toleranceMm
      );
    }
  }
}

function pointInBox(p: Pt, box: BBox): boolean {
  return p[0] >= box.x && p[0] <= box.x + box.width && p[1] >= box.y && p[1] <= box.y + box.height;
}

/** Normalised ellipse radius: 1 on the outline, <1 inside. */
function ellipseRadius(box: BBox, p: Pt): number {
  const rx = Math.max(box.width / 2, 0.001);
  const ry = Math.max(box.height / 2, 0.001);
  const nx = (p[0] - (box.x + rx)) / rx;
  const ny = (p[1] - (box.y + ry)) / ry;
  return Math.hypot(nx, ny);
}

function toStampLocal(annotation: Extract<Annotation, { type: "stamp" }>, p: Pt): Pt {
  const local = toBoxLocal(annotation, p);
  return [annotation.flipH ? annotation.widthMm - local[0] : local[0], annotation.flipV ? annotation.heightMm - local[1] : local[1]];
}

function toBoxLocal(
  annotation: Pick<Extract<Annotation, { type: "text" }>, "xMm" | "yMm" | "widthMm" | "heightMm" | "rotation">,
  p: Pt,
): Pt {
  const cx = annotation.xMm + annotation.widthMm / 2;
  const cy = annotation.yMm + annotation.heightMm / 2;
  const angle = -annotation.rotation * RAD;
  const dx = p[0] - cx;
  const dy = p[1] - cy;
  return [dx * Math.cos(angle) - dy * Math.sin(angle) + annotation.widthMm / 2, dx * Math.sin(angle) + dy * Math.cos(angle) + annotation.heightMm / 2];
}

function mapPoints<T extends Annotation>(annotation: T, mapper: (p: Pt) => Pt): T {
  if (annotation.type === "pen" || annotation.type === "marker") {
    return {
      ...annotation,
      points: annotation.points.map(([x, y, pressure]) => {
        const [nx, ny] = mapper([x, y]);
        return [nx, ny, pressure] as InkPoint;
      }),
    };
  }
  if (annotation.type === "shape") {
    return {
      ...annotation,
      start: mapper(annotation.start),
      end: mapper(annotation.end),
      points: annotation.points.map(mapper),
    };
  }
  const [x, y] = mapper([annotation.xMm + annotation.widthMm / 2, annotation.yMm + annotation.heightMm / 2]);
  return { ...annotation, xMm: x - annotation.widthMm / 2, yMm: y - annotation.heightMm / 2 };
}

export function translateAnnotation<T extends Annotation>(annotation: T, dx: number, dy: number): T {
  return mapPoints(annotation, ([x, y]) => [x + dx, y + dy]);
}

export function scaleAnnotation(
  annotation: Annotation,
  origin: Pt,
  scaleX: number,
  scaleY: number,
): Annotation {
  const map = ([x, y]: Pt): Pt => [origin[0] + (x - origin[0]) * scaleX, origin[1] + (y - origin[1]) * scaleY];
  const next = mapPoints(annotation, map);
  const factor = (Math.abs(scaleX) + Math.abs(scaleY)) / 2;
  if (next.type === "pen" || next.type === "marker") {
    return { ...next, thicknessMm: Math.max(0.1, next.thicknessMm * factor) };
  }
  if (next.type === "shape") {
    return { ...next, lineWidthMm: Math.max(0.05, next.lineWidthMm * factor) };
  }
  const source = annotation as Extract<Annotation, { type: "stamp" | "text" | "formula" }>;
  const target = next as Extract<Annotation, { type: "stamp" | "text" | "formula" }>;
  const scaled = {
    ...target,
    xMm: origin[0] + (source.xMm - origin[0]) * scaleX,
    yMm: origin[1] + (source.yMm - origin[1]) * scaleY,
    widthMm: Math.max(0.5, source.widthMm * Math.abs(scaleX)),
    heightMm: Math.max(0.5, source.heightMm * Math.abs(scaleY)),
  };
  if (scaled.type === "text" || scaled.type === "formula") {
    return { ...scaled, fontSizePt: Math.max(4, scaled.fontSizePt * factor) };
  }
  return scaled;
}

export function rotateAnnotation<T extends Annotation>(annotation: T, center: Pt, degrees: number): T {
  const angle = degrees * RAD;
  const cos = Math.cos(angle);
  const sin = Math.sin(angle);
  const map = ([x, y]: Pt): Pt => {
    const dx = x - center[0];
    const dy = y - center[1];
    return [center[0] + dx * cos - dy * sin, center[1] + dx * sin + dy * cos];
  };
  const next = mapPoints(annotation, map);
  if (next.type === "stamp" || next.type === "text" || next.type === "formula") {
    return { ...next, rotation: normalizeAngle(next.rotation + degrees) };
  }
  return next;
}

export function flipAnnotation<T extends Annotation>(annotation: T, axis: "h" | "v", center: Pt): T {
  const map = (p: Pt): Pt => (axis === "h" ? [center[0] * 2 - p[0], p[1]] : [p[0], center[1] * 2 - p[1]]);
  const next = mapPoints(annotation, map);
  if (next.type === "stamp") {
    return axis === "h" ? { ...next, flipH: !next.flipH } : { ...next, flipV: !next.flipV };
  }
  return next;
}

export function formatDimension(valueMm: number, unit: "mm" | "cm" | "m" | "in" | "ft"): string {
  if (unit === "cm") return `${(valueMm / 10).toFixed(2)} cm`;
  if (unit === "m") return `${(valueMm / 1000).toFixed(3)} m`;
  if (unit === "in") return `${(valueMm / 25.4).toFixed(3)} in`;
  if (unit === "ft") return `${(valueMm / 304.8).toFixed(3)} ft`;
  return `${valueMm.toFixed(1)} mm`;
}

export interface ScratchOutOptions {
  minReversals?: number;
  maxSizeMm?: number;
  minTravelMm?: number;
  /** Minimum width/height ratio; a real scribble is wide and flat. */
  minAspect?: number;
}

/** Vertices of a regular N-gon inscribed in the drag box (start/end opposite corners). */
export function regularPolygonPoints(start: Pt, end: Pt, sides: number): Pt[] {
  const count = Math.max(3, Math.round(sides));
  const cx = (start[0] + end[0]) / 2;
  const cy = (start[1] + end[1]) / 2;
  const rx = Math.abs(end[0] - start[0]) / 2;
  const ry = Math.abs(end[1] - start[1]) / 2;
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => {
    const angle = -Math.PI / 2 + (index * Math.PI * 2) / count;
    return [cx + rx * Math.cos(angle), cy + ry * Math.sin(angle)] as Pt;
  });
}

/**
 * A rapid horizontal scribble: several sharp left/right reversals inside a
 * tight box. Used to turn a scribble into "erase what is underneath".
 */
export function detectScratchOut(points: InkPoint[], options: ScratchOutOptions = {}): boolean {
  const { minReversals = 4, maxSizeMm = 26, minTravelMm = 25, minAspect = 0 } = options;
  if (points.length < 8) return false;
  const xy = points.map(([x, y]) => [x, y] as Pt);
  const box = pointsBounds(xy);
  if (box.width > maxSizeMm || box.height > maxSizeMm) return false;
  if (minAspect > 0 && box.width / Math.max(0.001, box.height) < minAspect) return false;

  let travel = 0;
  for (let index = 1; index < xy.length; index += 1) travel += ptDistance(xy[index - 1], xy[index]);
  if (travel < minTravelMm) return false;

  let direction = 0;
  let reversals = 0;
  let anchorX = xy[0][0];
  for (let index = 1; index < xy.length; index += 1) {
    const dx = xy[index][0] - anchorX;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 0.6) continue;
    const sign = Math.sign(dx);
    if (direction !== 0 && sign !== direction) reversals += 1;
    direction = sign;
    anchorX = xy[index][0];
  }
  return reversals >= minReversals;
}

/** Splits ink into continuous runs that stay outside the eraser circle. */
export function subtractEraser(points: InkPoint[], center: Pt, radiusMm: number): InkPoint[][] {
  const runs: InkPoint[][] = [];
  let current: InkPoint[] = [];
  for (const point of points) {
    if (ptDistance([point[0], point[1]], center) > radiusMm) {
      current.push(point);
    } else if (current.length > 0) {
      runs.push(current);
      current = [];
    }
  }
  if (current.length > 0) runs.push(current);
  return runs.filter((run) => run.length > 1);
}

/** Page-local millimetres, never pixels: 0..pageWidthMm / 0..pageHeightMm. */
export type Pt = [number, number];

  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

FILE: src\lib\bookmarks.test.ts (1.9 KB)

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { mapPageIndex, remapBookmarksOnPageOp } from "./bookmarks";
import type { PdfBookmark } from "./types";

const bookmark = (pageIndex: number, pageId?: string): PdfBookmark => ({
  id: `bm-${pageIndex}`,
  title: `Page ${pageIndex + 1}`,
  pageIndex,
  depth: 0,
  pageId,
});

describe("bookmark remapping", () => {
  it("shifts indices for add, duplicate and delete", () => {
    expect(mapPageIndex(0, { kind: "add", index: 0 })).toBe(1);
    expect(mapPageIndex(2, { kind: "duplicate", index: 1 })).toBe(3);
    expect(mapPageIndex(3, { kind: "delete", index: 1 })).toBe(2);
    expect(mapPageIndex(1, { kind: "delete", index: 1 })).toBe(1);
  });

  it("follows reorder in both directions", () => {
    expect(mapPageIndex(0, { kind: "reorder", from: 0, to: 2 })).toBe(2);
    expect(mapPageIndex(2, { kind: "reorder", from: 0, to: 2 })).toBe(1);
    expect(mapPageIndex(2, { kind: "reorder", from: 2, to: 0 })).toBe(0);
    expect(mapPageIndex(1, { kind: "reorder", from: 2, to: 0 })).toBe(2);
  });

  it("keeps a bookmark on its page id when pages move", () => {
    const pages = [{ id: "p2" }, { id: "p1" }, { id: "p3" }];
    const [moved] = remapBookmarksOnPageOp([bookmark(0, "p1")], pages, { kind: "reorder", from: 0, to: 1 });
    expect(moved).toMatchObject({ pageId: "p1", pageIndex: 1 });
  });

  it("clamps a bookmark whose page was deleted", () => {
    const pages = [{ id: "p1" }, { id: "p2" }];
    const [kept] = remapBookmarksOnPageOp([bookmark(2, "p3")], pages, { kind: "delete", index: 2 });
    expect(kept.pageIndex).toBe(1);
  });

  it("falls back to the numeric shift for legacy bookmarks without an id", () => {
    const pages = [{ id: "p1" }, { id: "p2" }, { id: "p3" }];
    const [shifted] = remapBookmarksOnPageOp([bookmark(1)], pages, { kind: "add", index: 0 });
    expect(shifted.pageIndex).toBe(2);
  });
});

FILE: src\lib\bookmarks.ts (1.5 KB)

import type { PdfBookmark } from "./types";

/** A page mutation that shifts later 0-based indices. */
export type PageOp =
  | { kind: "add" | "duplicate"; index: number }
  | { kind: "delete"; index: number }
  | { kind: "reorder"; from: number; to: number };

/** Where a 0-based page index lands after the operation. */
export function mapPageIndex(index: number, op: PageOp): number {
  switch (op.kind) {
    case "add":
    case "duplicate":
      return index >= op.index ? index + 1 : index;
    case "delete":
      return index > op.index ? index - 1 : index;
    case "reorder":
      if (index === op.from) return op.to;
      if (op.from < op.to) return index > op.from && index <= op.to ? index - 1 : index;
      return index >= op.to && index < op.from ? index + 1 : index;
  }
}

/**
 * Keeps bookmarks pointing at their page. `pageId` wins when the page still
 * exists; otherwise the numeric index follows the operation.
 */
export function remapBookmarksOnPageOp(
  bookmarks: PdfBookmark[],
  nextPages: { id: string }[],
  op: PageOp,
): PdfBookmark[] {
  const last = Math.max(0, nextPages.length - 1);
  return bookmarks.map((bookmark) => {
    if (bookmark.pageId) {
      const found = nextPages.findIndex((page) => page.id === bookmark.pageId);
      if (found >= 0) return found === bookmark.pageIndex ? bookmark : { ...bookmark, pageIndex: found };
    }
    const pageIndex = Math.min(mapPageIndex(bookmark.pageIndex, op), last);
    return pageIndex === bookmark.pageIndex ? bookmark : { ...bookmark, pageIndex };
  });
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { paperTemplates } from "./data";
import { parseBootArgs, resolvePaperTarget } from "./bootArgs";

describe("boot arguments", () => {
  it("reads the PDFAnnotator-style command line", () => {
    expect(parseBootArgs('--new "A4 Landscape" --fullscreen --print')).toEqual({
      newDoc: "A4 Landscape",
      fullscreen: true,
      print: true,
    });
  });

  it("reads the slash and colon spelling used by the original launcher", () => {
    expect(parseBootArgs('/new:"A4 Landscape" /fullscreen /print')).toEqual({
      newDoc: "A4 Landscape",
      fullscreen: true,
      print: true,
    });
  });

  it("reads the same contract from a URL query", () => {
    expect(parseBootArgs("?new=Letter+Landscape&fullscreen=1&print=1")).toEqual({
      newDoc: "Letter Landscape",
      fullscreen: true,
      print: true,
    });
    expect(parseBootArgs("?new=A4%20Landscape")).toEqual({
      newDoc: "A4 Landscape",
      fullscreen: false,
      print: false,
    });
  });

  it("keeps explicit off values off and empty input inert", () => {
    expect(parseBootArgs("--print=0 --fullscreen=no")).toEqual({ newDoc: null, fullscreen: false, print: false });
    expect(parseBootArgs("")).toEqual({ newDoc: null, fullscreen: false, print: false });
    expect(parseBootArgs("--new")).toEqual({ newDoc: null, fullscreen: false, print: false });
  });
});

describe("paper target", () => {
  it("maps a size and orientation to a shipped recipe", () => {
    const portrait = resolvePaperTarget("A4", paperTemplates);
    expect(portrait).toEqual({ templateId: "paper-000", landscape: false });

    const landscape = resolvePaperTarget("A4 Landscape", paperTemplates);
    expect(landscape).toEqual({ templateId: "paper-000", landscape: true });

    const letter = resolvePaperTarget("Letter Landscape", paperTemplates);
    expect(letter?.landscape).toBe(true);
    expect(paperTemplates.find((template) => template.id === letter?.templateId)?.page.size).toBe("Letter");
  });

  it("prefers an exact recipe name and rejects unknown paper", () => {
    expect(resolvePaperTarget("A4 Squared 5 mm", paperTemplates)?.templateId).toBe("paper-001");
    expect(resolvePaperTarget("B5", paperTemplates)).toBeNull();
    expect(resolvePaperTarget("  ", paperTemplates)).toBeNull();
  });
});

================================================================================
FILE: src\lib\bootArgs.ts (3.5 KB)
================================================================================
import type { PaperTemplate } from "./types";

/**
 * Desktop launch contract, shared by URL query (`?new=A4+Landscape&fullscreen=1`)
 * and PDFAnnotator.exe-style arguments (`--new "A4 Landscape" --fullscreen`).
 */
export interface BootArgs {
  /** Requested paper recipe, e.g. "A4 Landscape". */
  newDoc: string | null;
  fullscreen: boolean;
  print: boolean;
}

const NEW_KEYS = new Set(["new", "newdoc", "new-doc"]);

function isOn(value: string | null): boolean {
  return value === null || value === "" || ["1", "true", "yes", "on"].includes(value.toLowerCase());
}

/** Splits on whitespace outside quotes, dropping the quotes themselves. */
function tokenize(input: string): string[] {
  const tokens: string[] = [];
  let current = "";
  let quote: '"' | "'" | null = null;
  for (const char of input) {
    if (quote) {
      if (char === quote) quote = null;
      else current += char;
    } else if (char === '"' || char === "'") {
      quote = char;
    } else if (/\s/.test(char)) {
      if (current) {
        tokens.push(current);
        current = "";
      }
    } else {
      current += char;
    }
  }
  if (current) tokens.push(current);
  return tokens;
}

export function parseBootArgs(input: string): BootArgs {
  const raw = input.trim();
  const args: BootArgs = { newDoc: null, fullscreen: false, print: false };
  if (!raw) return args;

  // A query string, or a bare `key=value` list.
  if (raw.startsWith("?") || (!raw.startsWith("-") && !raw.startsWith("/") && raw.includes("="))) {
    for (const [key, value] of new URLSearchParams(raw)) {
      const name = key.replace(/^--?/, "").toLowerCase();
      if (NEW_KEYS.has(name)) args.newDoc = value.trim() || null;
      else if (name === "fullscreen") args.fullscreen = isOn(value);
      else if (name === "print") args.print = isOn(value);
    }
    return args;
  }

  const tokens = tokenize(raw);
  for (let index = 0; index < tokens.length; index += 1) {
    const match = /^(?:--?|\/)([a-z-]+)(?:[:=](.*))?$/i.exec(tokens[index]);
    if (!match) continue;
    const name = match[1].toLowerCase();
    const inline = match[2];
    if (NEW_KEYS.has(name)) {
      const next = inline ?? tokens[index + 1] ?? "";
      if (inline === undefined) index += 1;
      const value = next.trim();
      if (value) args.newDoc = value;
    } else if (name === "fullscreen") {
      args.fullscreen = isOn(inline ?? null);
    } else if (name === "print") {
      args.print = isOn(inline ?? null);
    }
  }
  return args;
}

const STYLE_RANK = ["blank", "ruled", "squared", "grid"];

/** Maps a requested size to a shipped paper recipe, preferring the least busy style. */
export function resolvePaperTarget(
  request: string,
  templates: PaperTemplate[],
): { templateId: string; landscape: boolean } | null {
  const normalized = request.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (!normalized) return null;

  const exact = templates.find((template) => template.name.toLowerCase() === normalized);
  if (exact) return { templateId: exact.id, landscape: exact.page.landscape };

  const landscape = /\b(landscape|wide)\b/.test(normalized);
  const pool = templates.filter((template) => normalized.includes(template.page.size.toLowerCase()));
  if (pool.length === 0) return null;

  const ranked = [...pool].sort(
    (a, b) =>
      (STYLE_RANK.indexOf(a.styleLabel.toLowerCase()) + 1 || 99) -
      (STYLE_RANK.indexOf(b.styleLabel.toLowerCase()) + 1 || 99),
  );
  return { templateId: ranked[0].id, landscape };
}

  it("always lets a pen draw, even with finger drawing switched off", () => {
    expect(drawsWithPointer("pen", false)).toBe(true);
    expect(drawsWithPointer("mouse", false)).toBe(true);
    expect(drawsWithPointer("touch", false)).toBe(false);
    expect(drawsWithPointer("touch", true)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("never hands the canvas to native touch-action", () => {
    // Chromium cancels a pen stroke after 2-3px when touch-action is auto/pan.
    expect(CANVAS_TOUCH_ACTION).toBe("none");
  });

  it("pans with one finger only while finger drawing is off", () => {
    expect(pansWithPointer("touch", false, false)).toBe(true);
    expect(pansWithPointer("touch", true, false)).toBe(false);
    expect(pansWithPointer("pen", false, false)).toBe(false);
    expect(pansWithPointer("pen", false, true)).toBe(true);
  });
});

FILE: src\lib\canvasInput.ts (792 B)

/**
 * Chromium applies `touch-action` to pens as well as fingers, so the canvas keeps
 * `touch-action: none` in every mode; anything else cancels a stylus stroke after
 * a few pixels. Finger scrolling is therefore done in code, not by the browser.
 */
export const CANVAS_TOUCH_ACTION = "none";

/** A stylus or mouse always draws; a finger only when palm rejection is off. */
export function drawsWithPointer(pointerType: string, touchDrawEnabled: boolean): boolean {
  return pointerType !== "touch" || touchDrawEnabled;
}

/** One finger pans only while finger drawing is off; pen and mouse never pan by drag. */
export function pansWithPointer(pointerType: string, touchDrawEnabled: boolean, canPan: boolean): boolean {
  return pointerType === "touch" ? !touchDrawEnabled : canPan;
}

FILE: src\lib\customStamps.ts (5.4 KB)
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { stampById, userStamps } from "./data";

/** A snapshot or converted selection saved as a reusable raster stamp. */
export interface CustomStamp {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  /** Natural raster size in pixels, matching factory stamp metrics. */
  width: number;
  height: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

const KEY = "pm.customStamps";
const DB_NAME = "pdf-master-stamps";
const STORE = "stamps";

export interface StampWriteResult {
  ok: boolean;
  /** The created stamp, present as soon as the store accepts it. */
  stamp?: CustomStamp;
  error?: string;
}

export interface ResolvedStamp {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  /** Natural raster width in pixels. */
  width: number;
  /** Natural raster height in pixels. */
  height: number;
  source: "custom" | "factory" | "user";
}

/** One lookup for every stamp source; saved stamps win over factory ids. */
export function resolveStamp(id: string | null | undefined): ResolvedStamp | null {
  if (!id) return null;
  const custom = useCustomStampStore.getState().stamps.find((stamp) => stamp.id === id);
  if (custom) {
    return { id: custom.id, name: custom.name, imageUrl: custom.imageUrl, width: custom.width, height: custom.height, source: "custom" };
  }
  const factory = stampById.get(id);
  if (factory?.imageUrl) {
    return { id: factory.id, name: factory.title.en, imageUrl: factory.imageUrl, width: factory.width, height: factory.height, source: "factory" };
  }
  const user = userStamps.find((stamp) => stamp.id === id);
  if (user) return { id: user.id, name: user.name, imageUrl: user.imageUrl, width: user.inkWidth, height: user.inkHeight, source: "user" };
  return null;
}

function openDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase | null> {
  if (typeof indexedDB === "undefined") return Promise.resolve(null);
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, 1);
    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      if (!request.result.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE)) request.result.createObjectStore(STORE, { keyPath: "id" });
    };
    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
    request.onerror = () => resolve(null);
  });
}

/** localStorage holds the fast copy; IndexedDB keeps the durable one. */
async function mirror(stamps: CustomStamp[]): Promise<StampWriteResult> {
  const db = await openDb();
  if (!db) return { ok: false, error: "IndexedDB is unavailable" };
  try {
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      const transaction = db.transaction(STORE, "readwrite");
      const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE);
      store.clear();
      for (const stamp of stamps) store.put(stamp);
      transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
      transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error ?? new Error("stamp store write failed"));
      transaction.onabort = () => reject(transaction.error ?? new Error("stamp store write aborted"));
    });
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

/** Durable copy, read once at startup. */
export async function loadMirroredStamps(): Promise<CustomStamp[] | null> {
  const db = await openDb();
  if (!db) return null;
  const stamps = await new Promise<CustomStamp[] | null>((resolve) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(STORE, "readonly");
    const request = transaction.objectStore(STORE).getAll();
    request.onsuccess = () => resolve((request.result as CustomStamp[]).filter((stamp) => stamp?.imageUrl));
    request.onerror = () => resolve(null);
  });
  db.close();
  return stamps?.length ? stamps : null;
}

function readStamps(): CustomStamp[] {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return [];
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) ?? "[]") as CustomStamp[];
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((stamp) => stamp?.imageUrl) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function persist(stamps: CustomStamp[]): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(stamps));
  } catch {
    // Quota exceeded: the stamp stays usable for this session.
  }
}

interface CustomStampState {
  stamps: CustomStamp[];
  add: (stamp: Omit<CustomStamp, "id" | "createdAt">) => Promise<StampWriteResult>;
  remove: (id: string) => void;
  /** Replaces the library from a workspace backup. */
  restore: (stamps: CustomStamp[]) => void;
}

export const useCustomStampStore = create<CustomStampState>((set, get) => ({
  stamps: readStamps(),
  add: async (stamp) => {
    const created: CustomStamp = {
      ...stamp,
      id: `custom-${typeof crypto !== "undefined" && "randomUUID" in crypto ? crypto.randomUUID() : Date.now().toString(36)}`,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    const stamps = [created, ...get().stamps];
    persist(stamps);
    set({ stamps });
    return { ...(await mirror(stamps)), stamp: created };
  },
  remove: (id) => {
    const stamps = get().stamps.filter((stamp) => stamp.id !== id);
    persist(stamps);
    set({ stamps });
    void mirror(stamps);
  },
  restore: (stamps) => {
    const valid = stamps.filter((stamp) => stamp?.id && stamp.imageUrl && stamp.width > 0 && stamp.height > 0);
    persist(valid);
    set({ stamps: valid });
    void mirror(valid);
  },
}));

import groupsJson from "../data/groups.json";
import paperJson from "../data/paper_templates.json";
import presetsJson from "../data/presets.json";
import stampsJson from "../data/stamps.json";
import userStampsJson from "../data/user_stamps.json";
import type { PaperTemplate, Preset, Stamp, ToolGroup, UserStamp } from "./types";

export const groups = groupsJson.groups as unknown as ToolGroup[];
export const presets = presetsJson.presets as unknown as Preset[];
export const stamps = (stampsJson.stamps as unknown as Stamp[]).filter(
  (stamp): stamp is Stamp => stamp.imageUrl !== null,
);
export const paperTemplates = paperJson.templates as unknown as PaperTemplate[];
export const pdfTemplates = paperJson.pdfTemplates as { name: string; bytes: number; sha256: string; url: string }[];
export const userStamps = (userStampsJson.stamps as unknown as UserStamp[]) ?? [];

export const groupById = new Map(groups.map((group) => [group.id, group]));
export const paperById = new Map(paperTemplates.map((template) => [template.id, template]));
export const stampById = new Map(stamps.map((stamp) => [stamp.id, stamp]));

/** First page style used before the user has opened anything. */
export const DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID = paperTemplates.find((t) => t.id === "paper-000")?.id ?? paperTemplates[0].id;

export function stampsOfGroup(groupSlug: string): Stamp[] {
  return stamps.filter((stamp) => stamp.groupSlug === groupSlug);
}

/** Stamp groups in factory order, joined with their localized captions. */
export function stampGroups() {
  const seen: { slug: string; name: string; de: string; count: number }[] = [];
  for (const group of groups) {
    const own = stampsOfGroup(group.slug);
    if (own.length > 0) {
      seen.push({
        slug: group.slug,
        name: group.captions.en,
        de: group.captions.de,
        count: own.length,
      });
    }
  }
  return seen;
}

import "katex/dist/katex.min.css";
import type { RasterImage } from "./raster";
import { rasterizeHtml } from "./raster";

/** KaTeX is loaded on demand so the editor bundle only pays for it once used. */
export async function latexToHtml(latex: string, displayMode = true): Promise<string> {
  const module = await import("katex");
  return module.default.renderToString(latex.trim() || "\\;", {
    displayMode,
    throwOnError: false,
    strict: false,
    output: "html",
  });
}

export async function latexToPng(
  latex: string,
  fontSizePx: number,
  widthPx: number,
  heightPx: number,
  color = "#101114",
): Promise<RasterImage | null> {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return null;
  // Native MathML needs no KaTeX stylesheet inside the raster's isolated SVG document.
  const module = await import("katex");
  const html = module.default.renderToString(latex.trim() || "\\;", {
    displayMode: true,
    throwOnError: false,
    strict: false,
    output: "mathml",
  });
  const wrapper = `<div style="font-size:${fontSizePx}px;line-height:1.15;text-align:center;color:${color};">${html}</div>`;
  return rasterizeHtml(wrapper, widthPx, heightPx, 2);
}

FILE: src\lib\images.ts (1.7 KB)

import type { Pt, StampAnnotation } from "./annotationTypes";
import { PX_PER_MM } from "./paper";
import { pageSizeMm } from "./pageUtils";
import type { RasterImage } from "./raster";
import { makeId, useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useAnnotationStore } from "../stores/useAnnotationStore";
import { useToolStore } from "../stores/useToolStore";

/** Places a raster image as a movable, scalable stamp annotation. */
export function addImageStamp(image: RasterImage, atMm?: Pt): string | null {
  const doc = useDocStore.getState();
  const tab = doc.tabs.find((item) => item.id === doc.activeId);
  const page = tab?.pages[doc.page - 1];
  if (!tab || !page) return null;
  const pageSize = pageSizeMm(tab, page);
  const naturalWidthMm = image.width / PX_PER_MM;
  const widthMm = Math.max(8, Math.min(naturalWidthMm, pageSize.widthMm * 0.8));
  const heightMm = Math.max(4, widthMm * (image.height / Math.max(1, image.width)));
  const center = atMm ?? [pageSize.widthMm / 2, pageSize.heightMm / 2];
  const xMm = Math.max(0, Math.min(center[0] - widthMm / 2, pageSize.widthMm - widthMm));
  const yMm = Math.max(0, Math.min(center[1] - heightMm / 2, pageSize.heightMm - heightMm));
  const annotation: StampAnnotation = {
    id: makeId("a"),
    type: "stamp",
    stampId: makeId("image"),
    imageUrl: image.dataUrl,
    xMm,
    yMm,
    widthMm,
    heightMm,
    rotation: 0,
    flipH: false,
    flipV: false,
    opacity: 1,
  };
  useAnnotationStore.getState().add(annotation);
  // The fresh image is ready to move or resize, not another trip to the file picker.
  useAnnotationStore.getState().select([annotation.id]);
  useToolStore.getState().setTool("select");
  return annotation.id;
}

    expect(penStyleOptions("pro", 240).thinning).toBe(1);

    expect(penStyleOptions("calligraphy", 85).smoothing).toBeGreaterThan(penStyleOptions("pro", 55).smoothing);
  });
});

FILE: src\lib\ink.ts (3.1 KB)

import { getStroke } from "perfect-freehand";
import type { InkPoint, PenStyle, Pt } from "./annotationTypes";

export interface InkOutlineOptions {
  /** Simulated pressure from velocity when the device gives no real pressure. */
  simulatePressure?: boolean;
  /** 0..1: how much the width follows pressure. */
  thinning?: number;
  smoothing?: number;
}

/** Style presets: Basic is a fixed nib, Pro and Calligraphy follow pressure. */
export function penStyleOptions(style: PenStyle = "pro", pressureLevel = 55): Required<Pick<InkOutlineOptions, "thinning" | "smoothing" | "simulatePressure">> {
  const level = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, pressureLevel));
  return {
    thinning: style === "basic" ? 0 : level / 100,
    smoothing: style === "calligraphy" ? 0.85 : 0.5,
    simulatePressure: style !== "basic",
  };
}

function toStrokePoints(points: InkPoint[]) {
  return points.map(([x, y, pressure]) => ({ x, y, pressure }));
}

/** Variable-width outline from perfect-freehand, in millimetres. */
export function inkOutline(points: InkPoint[], thicknessMm: number, options: InkOutlineOptions = {}): number[][] {
  if (points.length === 0) return [];
  return getStroke(toStrokePoints(points), {
    size: Math.max(0.05, thicknessMm),
    thinning: options.thinning ?? 0.55,
    smoothing: options.smoothing ?? 0.5,
    streamline: 0.45,
    simulatePressure: options.simulatePressure ?? true,
    last: true,
  });
}

function outlineToPath(stroke: number[][]): string {
  if (stroke.length === 0) return "";
  const parts: (string | number)[] = ["M", stroke[0][0], stroke[0][1], "Q"];
  for (let index = 0; index < stroke.length; index += 1) {
    const [x0, y0] = stroke[index];
    const [x1, y1] = stroke[(index + 1) % stroke.length];
    parts.push(x0, y0, (x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2);
  }
  parts.push("Z");
  return parts.join(" ");
}

export function inkPath(points: InkPoint[], thicknessMm: number, options: InkOutlineOptions = {}): string {
  return outlineToPath(inkOutline(points, thicknessMm, options));
}

export function polylinePath(points: Pt[], closed = false): string {
  if (points.length === 0) return "";
  const [first, ...rest] = points;
  const parts = [`M ${first[0]} ${first[1]}`];
  for (const [x, y] of rest) parts.push(`L ${x} ${y}`);
  if (closed) parts.push("Z");
  return parts.join(" ");
}

/** Velocity-derived pressure so a mouse still produces calligraphic ink. */
export function pressureFromVelocity(previous: Pt | null, next: Pt, elapsedMs: number): number {
  if (!previous || elapsedMs <= 0) return 0.55;
  const speed = Math.hypot(next[0] - previous[0], next[1] - previous[1]) / elapsedMs;
  return Math.min(1, Math.max(0.2, 1 - speed * 0.55));
}

/**
 * Real pressure only exists on pens. Mice report 0 or 0.5 while idle, so those
 * fall back to velocity instead of flattening the stroke.
 */
export function pointerPressure(
  pointerType: string,
  pressure: number,
  previous: Pt | null,
  next: Pt,
  elapsedMs: number,
): number {
  if (pointerType === "pen" && pressure > 0 && pressure !== 0.5) return Math.min(1, pressure);
  return pressureFromVelocity(previous, next, elapsedMs);
}

FILE: src\lib\measure.ts (6.1 KB)

import { create } from "zustand";
import type { Pt } from "./annotationTypes";

export type MeasureMode = "distance" | "perimeter" | "area" | "angle";
export type MeasureUnit = "mm" | "cm" | "m" | "in" | "ft";

export interface MeasurementScale {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  /** One page millimetre represents this many real-world millimetres. */
  denominator: number;
}

export const MEASURE_MODES: { value: MeasureMode; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "distance", label: "Distance" },
  { value: "perimeter", label: "Perimeter" },
  { value: "area", label: "Area" },
  { value: "angle", label: "Angle" },
];

export const MEASURE_UNITS: { value: MeasureUnit; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "mm", label: "mm" },
  { value: "cm", label: "cm" },
  { value: "m", label: "m" },
  { value: "in", label: "in" },
  { value: "ft", label: "ft" },
];

export const PRESET_SCALES: MeasurementScale[] = [1, 2, 10, 20, 50, 100, 500].map((denominator) => ({
  id: `scale-${denominator}`,
  label: `1:${denominator}`,
  denominator,
}));

const MM_PER_UNIT: Record<MeasureUnit, number> = { mm: 1, cm: 10, m: 1000, in: 25.4, ft: 304.8 };

export function formatNumber(value: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return "0";
  const fixed = Math.abs(value) >= 1000 ? value.toFixed(0) : value.toFixed(2);
  return fixed.replace(/\.?0+$/, "") || "0";
}

export function toRealMm(pageMm: number, scaleDenominator: number): number {
  return pageMm * scaleDenominator;
}

export function formatLength(pageMm: number, scaleDenominator: number, unit: MeasureUnit): string {
  return `${formatNumber(toRealMm(pageMm, scaleDenominator) / MM_PER_UNIT[unit])} ${unit}`;
}

export function formatArea(pageMm2: number, scaleDenominator: number, unit: MeasureUnit): string {
  const factor = MM_PER_UNIT[unit] ** 2;
  return `${formatNumber((pageMm2 * scaleDenominator ** 2) / factor)} ${unit}²`;
}

export function polylineLength(points: Pt[]): number {
  let total = 0;
  for (let index = 1; index < points.length; index += 1) {
    total += Math.hypot(points[index][0] - points[index - 1][0], points[index][1] - points[index - 1][1]);
  }
  return total;
}

/** Shoelace area of a polygon in square millimetres. */
export function polygonArea(points: Pt[]): number {
  if (points.length < 3) return 0;
  let sum = 0;
  for (let index = 0; index < points.length; index += 1) {
    const [x1, y1] = points[index];
    const [x2, y2] = points[(index + 1) % points.length];
    sum += x1 * y2 - x2 * y1;
  }
  return Math.abs(sum) / 2;
}

/** Angle at `vertex` between the rays to `a` and `b`, in degrees (0..180). */
export function angleAt(a: Pt, vertex: Pt, b: Pt): number {
  const ax = a[0] - vertex[0];
  const ay = a[1] - vertex[1];
  const bx = b[0] - vertex[0];
  const by = b[1] - vertex[1];
  const denominator = Math.hypot(ax, ay) * Math.hypot(bx, by);
  if (denominator === 0) return 0;
  const cosine = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (ax * bx + ay * by) / denominator));
  return (Math.acos(cosine) * 180) / Math.PI;
}

export interface DistanceStats {
  distance: number;
  dx: number;
  dy: number;
  /** Screen/page angle of the segment in degrees, 0..360. */
  angle: number;
}

export function distanceStats(a: Pt, b: Pt): DistanceStats {
  const dx = b[0] - a[0];
  const dy = b[1] - a[1];
  const angle = ((Math.atan2(dy, dx) * 180) / Math.PI + 360) % 360;
  return { distance: Math.hypot(dx, dy), dx, dy, angle };
}

/** Denominator from a calibration: `realLength` covers `pageLengthMm` on paper. */
export function calibrateDenominator(pageLengthMm: number, realLength: number, unit: MeasureUnit): number | null {
  if (pageLengthMm <= 0 || realLength <= 0) return null;
  return (realLength * MM_PER_UNIT[unit]) / pageLengthMm;
}

export function customScaleLabel(denominator: number): string {
  return `1:${formatNumber(denominator)}`;
}

const SCALES_KEY = "pm.measure.scales";
const ACTIVE_KEY = "pm.measure.scale";

function readScales(): MeasurementScale[] {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return [];
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SCALES_KEY) ?? "[]") as MeasurementScale[];
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((scale) => scale && scale.denominator > 0) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function readActive(): string {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return PRESET_SCALES[0].id;
  return localStorage.getItem(ACTIVE_KEY) ?? PRESET_SCALES[0].id;
}

interface ScaleState {
  custom: MeasurementScale[];
  activeId: string;
  addCustom: (scale: MeasurementScale) => void;
  setActive: (id: string) => void;
  /** Replaces calibrated scales from a workspace backup. */
  restore: (custom: MeasurementScale[], activeId?: string) => void;
  all: () => MeasurementScale[];
  active: () => MeasurementScale;
}

export const useScaleStore = create<ScaleState>((set, get) => ({
  custom: readScales(),
  activeId: readActive(),
  addCustom: (scale) => {
    const custom = [...get().custom.filter((item) => item.id !== scale.id), scale];
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(SCALES_KEY, JSON.stringify(custom));
    set({ custom, activeId: scale.id });
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_KEY, scale.id);
  },
  setActive: (activeId) => {
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_KEY, activeId);
    set({ activeId });
  },
  restore: (custom, activeId) => {
    const valid = custom.filter((scale) => scale?.id && scale.denominator > 0);
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(SCALES_KEY, JSON.stringify(valid));
    const nextActive = activeId && [...PRESET_SCALES, ...valid].some((scale) => scale.id === activeId)
      ? activeId
      : PRESET_SCALES[0].id;
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_KEY, nextActive);
    set({ custom: valid, activeId: nextActive });
  },
  all: () => [...PRESET_SCALES, ...get().custom],
  active: () => get().all().find((scale) => scale.id === get().activeId) ?? PRESET_SCALES[0],
}));

import { paperById, paperTemplates } from "./data";
import { pageMm } from "./paper";
import type { DocumentPage, DocumentTab, PaperTemplate } from "./types";

export function pageTemplate(page: DocumentPage): PaperTemplate {
  return paperById.get(page.templateId) ?? paperTemplates[0];
}

/** Unrotated page size; rotation is applied as a view transform only. */
export function pageSizeMm(tab: DocumentTab, page: DocumentPage): { widthMm: number; heightMm: number } {
  if (page.pdf) return { widthMm: page.pdf.widthMm, heightMm: page.pdf.heightMm };
  return pageMm(pageTemplate(page), tab.landscape);
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { PX_PER_MM, getWorkspaceInsets, workspaceSafeArea } from "./paper";

const VIEWPORT = 1600;
const A4_WIDTH_PX = 210 * PX_PER_MM;

describe("workspace insets", () => {
  it("follows the floating chrome on the left", () => {
    expect(getWorkspaceInsets({}).leftInset).toBe(68);
    expect(getWorkspaceInsets({ favoritesOpen: true }).leftInset).toBe(124);
    expect(getWorkspaceInsets({ sidebarOpen: true }).leftInset).toBe(348);
    expect(getWorkspaceInsets({ sidebarOpen: true, favoritesOpen: true }).leftInset).toBe(404);
  });

  it("makes room for a pinned inspector on the right", () => {
    expect(getWorkspaceInsets({}).rightInset).toBe(76);
    // An unpinned tool popover floats over the page and closes on the first stroke.
    expect(getWorkspaceInsets({ inspectorOpen: true }).rightInset).toBe(76);
    expect(getWorkspaceInsets({ inspectorOpen: true, inspectorPinned: true }).rightInset).toBe(410);
  });
});

describe("safe-area centring", () => {
  it("centres between the rails for every open/closed combination", () => {
    for (const sidebarOpen of [false, true]) {
      for (const favoritesOpen of [false, true]) {
        const { leftInset, rightInset } = getWorkspaceInsets({ sidebarOpen, favoritesOpen });
        const { safeWidth, safeCenterX } = workspaceSafeArea(VIEWPORT, leftInset, rightInset);
        expect(safeWidth).toBe(VIEWPORT - leftInset - rightInset);
        expect(safeCenterX).toBe(leftInset + (VIEWPORT - leftInset - rightInset) / 2);
      }
    }
  });

  it("fit to width keeps the page inside the rails, never at x = 0", () => {
    for (const sidebarOpen of [false, true]) {
      for (const favoritesOpen of [false, true]) {
        const { leftInset, rightInset } = getWorkspaceInsets({ sidebarOpen, favoritesOpen });
        const { safeWidth, safeCenterX } = workspaceSafeArea(VIEWPORT, leftInset, rightInset);
        const zoom = (safeWidth - 24) / A4_WIDTH_PX;
        const pageWidth = A4_WIDTH_PX * zoom;
        const left = safeCenterX - pageWidth / 2;
        const right = safeCenterX + pageWidth / 2;
        expect(left).toBeCloseTo(leftInset + 12);
        expect(right).toBeCloseTo(VIEWPORT - rightInset - 12);
        expect(left).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(leftInset);
      }
    }
  });

  it("fit to page stays centred in the same strip", () => {
    const { leftInset, rightInset } = getWorkspaceInsets({ favoritesOpen: true });
    const { safeWidth, safeCenterX } = workspaceSafeArea(VIEWPORT, leftInset, rightInset);
    const rowHeightPx = 297 * PX_PER_MM;
    const zoom = Math.min((safeWidth - 24) / A4_WIDTH_PX, (900 - 16) / rowHeightPx);
    const pageWidth = A4_WIDTH_PX * zoom;
    expect(pageWidth).toBeLessThanOrEqual(safeWidth - 24);
    expect(safeCenterX - pageWidth / 2).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(leftInset);
  });

  it("keeps a usable strip on a very narrow window", () => {
    expect(workspaceSafeArea(400, 404, 76).safeWidth).toBe(200);
  });
});

import type { PaperTemplate } from "./types";

/** CSS pixels per millimetre at 100 % (96 dpi). */
export const PX_PER_MM = 96 / 25.4;

export function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

export function pageMm(template: PaperTemplate, landscape: boolean): { widthMm: number; heightMm: number } {
  const { widthMm, heightMm, landscape: sourceLandscape } = template.page;
  const flipped = landscape !== sourceLandscape;
  return flipped ? { widthMm: heightMm, heightMm: widthMm } : { widthMm, heightMm };
}

export interface PaperGeometry {
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
  color: string;
  widthStrokeMm: number;
  base: string;
  minor: string;
  major: string;
  hasGrid: boolean;
}

/**
 * Turns a parametric recipe into three SVG paths (base, every 5th, every 10th line).
 * `line` templates only rule horizontally; `karo`/`mm`/`grid` rule both ways.
 */
export function paperGeometry(template: PaperTemplate, landscape = false): PaperGeometry {
  const { widthMm, heightMm } = pageMm(template, landscape);
  const empty: PaperGeometry = {
    widthMm, heightMm, color: "#aaaaaa", widthStrokeMm: 0.1,
    base: "", minor: "", major: "", hasGrid: false,
  };
  const grid = template.grid;
  if (!grid) return empty;

  const bounds = template.bounds ?? { left: 0, top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0 };
  const x0 = bounds.left;
  const x1 = widthMm - bounds.right;
  const y0 = bounds.top;
  const y1 = heightMm - bounds.bottom;
  if (x1 <= x0 || y1 <= y0) return empty;

  const base: string[] = [];
  const minor: string[] = [];
  const major: string[] = [];

  const bucket = (index: number): string[] => {
    if (grid.highlightMajor && index % grid.highlightMajor === 0) return major;
    if (grid.highlightMinor && index % grid.highlightMinor === 0) return minor;
    return base;
  };
  const round = (value: number) => Number(value.toFixed(3));
  const horizontal = (step: number) => {
    for (let index = 1; y0 + index * step < y1; index += 1) {
      const y = round(y0 + index * step);
      bucket(index).push(`M${round(x0)} ${y}H${round(x1)}`);
    }
  };
  const vertical = (step: number) => {
    for (let index = 1; x0 + index * step < x1; index += 1) {
      const x = round(x0 + index * step);
      bucket(index).push(`M${x} ${round(y0)}V${round(y1)}`);
    }
  };

  if (grid.kind === "grid") {
    const step = grid.deltaMm ?? 5;
    horizontal(step);
    vertical(step);
  } else {
    if (grid.deltaYMm) horizontal(grid.deltaYMm);
    if (grid.deltaXMm) vertical(grid.deltaXMm);
  }

  return {
    ...empty,
    color: grid.color,
    widthStrokeMm: grid.widthMm,
    base: base.join(""),
    minor: minor.join(""),
    major: major.join(""),
    hasGrid: base.length + minor.length + major.length > 0,
  };
}

/** Horizontal space the floating chrome takes on each side of the page. */
export interface WorkspaceInsets {
  leftInset: number;
  rightInset: number;
}

/** Left rail 12+44, favourite dock 68..112, sidebar drawer to 332, inspector 398..718. */
export function getWorkspaceInsets({
  sidebarOpen = false,
  favoritesOpen = false,
  inspectorOpen = false,
  inspectorPinned = false,
}: {
  sidebarOpen?: boolean;
  favoritesOpen?: boolean;
  inspectorOpen?: boolean;
  inspectorPinned?: boolean;
}): WorkspaceInsets {
  const leftInset = sidebarOpen ? (favoritesOpen ? 404 : 348) : favoritesOpen ? 124 : 68;
  const rightInset = inspectorOpen && inspectorPinned ? 410 : 76;
  return { leftInset, rightInset };
}

/** The page-safe strip between the rails: its width and its centre in viewport pixels. */
export function workspaceSafeArea(
  viewportWidth: number,
  leftInset: number,
  rightInset: number,
): { safeWidth: number; safeCenterX: number } {
  const safeWidth = Math.max(200, viewportWidth - leftInset - rightInset);
  return { safeWidth, safeCenterX: leftInset + safeWidth / 2 };
}

/** Zoom that fits the whole page (or just its width) inside a viewport. */
export function fitScale(
  viewportWidth: number,
  viewportHeight: number,
  page: { widthMm: number; heightMm: number },
  mode: "page" | "width",
  padding = 64,
): number {
  const pageWidthPx = page.widthMm * PX_PER_MM;
  const pageHeightPx = page.heightMm * PX_PER_MM;
  const byWidth = (viewportWidth - padding) / pageWidthPx;
  const byHeight = (viewportHeight - padding) / pageHeightPx;
  return clamp(mode === "width" ? byWidth : Math.min(byWidth, byHeight), 0.1, 6);
}

function input(mode: "editable" | "flattened"): PdfExportInput {

    title: "Round trip",
    mode,
    pages: [
      {
        templateId: "paper-000",
        landscape: false,
