describe("PDF engine", () => {
  it("encodes and decodes the annotation sidecar", () => {
    const serialized = {
      version: 1 as const,
      title: "Round trip",
      savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      pages: [
        {
          templateId: "paper-000",
          landscape: false,
          rotation: 0,
          widthMm: 210,
          heightMm: 297,
          annotations: [rectangle],
        },
      ],
    };
    const decoded = decodeSidecar(encodeSidecar(serialized));
    expect(decoded?.pages[0].annotations[0]).toMatchObject({ id: "shape-1", shapeType: "rectangle" });
  });

  it("exports a vector PDF with an editable sidecar", async () => {
    const bytes = await exportPdf(input("editable"));
    const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(bytes);
    expect(pdf.getPageCount()).toBe(1);
    const page = pdf.getPage(0);
    expect(page.getWidth()).toBeCloseTo((210 * 72) / 25.4, 1);
    expect(page.getHeight()).toBeCloseTo((297 * 72) / 25.4, 1);

    const sidecar = await loadSidecar(bytes);
    expect(sidecar?.pages[0].annotations).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(sidecar?.pages[0].annotations[0].id).toBe("shape-1");
  });

  it("exports a flattened PDF without a sidecar", async () => {
    const bytes = await exportPdf(input("flattened"));
    expect(await loadSidecar(bytes)).toBeNull();
    const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(bytes);
    expect(pdf.getPageCount()).toBe(1);
  });

  it("mirrors flipped stamps in the flattened content", async () => {
    const flipped: StampAnnotation = { ...stamp, flipH: true, flipV: true, rotation: 30 };
    const bytes = await exportPdf({
      ...input("flattened"),
      pages: [{ ...input("flattened").pages[0], annotations: [flipped] }],
    });
    const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(bytes);
    const page = pdf.getPage(0);
    const raw = page.node.Contents() as never;
    const first = (pdf.context.lookup(raw) as unknown as { array?: unknown[] }).array?.[0] ?? raw;
    const contents = pdf.context.lookup(first as never) as unknown as { asUint8Array: () => Uint8Array };
    const decoded = new Blob([contents.asUint8Array() as unknown as BlobPart])
      .stream()
      .pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream("deflate"));
    const operators = await new Response(decoded).text();
    const scales = [...operators.matchAll(/(-?[\d.]+) 0 0 (-?[\d.]+) /g)].map((match) => [Number(match[1]), Number(match[2])]);
    expect(scales.some(([width, height]) => width < 0 && height < 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(await loadSidecar(bytes)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("never bakes annotations into an editable export", async () => {
    const imageObjects = (pdf: PDFDocument) => {
      const xobjects = pdf.getPage(0).node.Resources()?.lookup(PDFName.of("XObject"));
      return xobjects instanceof PDFDict ? xobjects.keys().length : 0;
    };
    expect(imageObjects(await PDFDocument.load(await exportPdf(input("editable"))))).toBe(0);
    expect(imageObjects(await PDFDocument.load(await exportPdf(input("flattened"))))).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect((await loadSidecar(await exportPdf(input("editable"))))?.pages[0].annotations).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("refuses to export a page whose source PDF is unavailable", async () => {
    const page = { ...input("flattened").pages[0], pdfPageIndex: 1 };
    await expect(
      exportPdf({ title: "Missing", mode: "flattened", pages: [page] }),
    ).rejects.toThrow("Source PDF bytes are unavailable or unreadable");
    await expect(
      exportPdf({ title: "Broken", mode: "flattened", sourceBytes: new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3]), pages: [page] }),
    ).rejects.toThrow("Source PDF bytes are unavailable or unreadable");
  });

  it("writes a real outline tree for the bookmarks", async () => {
    const bookmarks = [
      { id: "bm-1", title: "Chapter 1", pageIndex: 0, depth: 0 },
      { id: "bm-2", title: "Section", pageIndex: 0, depth: 1 },
    ];
    const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(await exportPdf({ ...input("flattened"), bookmarks }));
    const outlines = pdf.catalog.lookup(PDFName.of("Outlines"));
    expect(outlines).toBeInstanceOf(PDFDict);
    const first = (outlines as PDFDict).lookup(PDFName.of("First"));
    expect(first).toBeInstanceOf(PDFDict);
    expect(((first as PDFDict).lookup(PDFName.of("Title")) as PDFString).decodeText()).toBe("Chapter 1");
    expect((first as PDFDict).lookup(PDFName.of("Dest"))).toBeInstanceOf(PDFArray);
    const child = (first as PDFDict).lookup(PDFName.of("First"));
    expect(((child as PDFDict).lookup(PDFName.of("Title")) as PDFString).decodeText()).toBe("Section");
  });

  it("merges a sidecar so paper pages reopen without a PDF source", () => {
    const source: DocumentPage = {
      id: "p1",
      templateId: "paper-000",
      rotation: 0,
      annotations: [],
      pdf: { url: "blob:x", pageNumber: 1, widthMm: 210, heightMm: 297 },
    };
    const sidecar = {
      version: 1 as const,
      title: "Paper",
      savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      pages: [
        {
          templateId: "paper-103",
          landscape: false,
          rotation: 90,
          widthMm: 210,
          heightMm: 297,
          annotations: [rectangle],
        },
      ],
    };
    const [paper] = mergeSidecarPages([source], sidecar);
    expect(paper.pdf).toBeUndefined();
    expect(paper.templateId).toBe("paper-103");
    expect(paper.rotation).toBe(90);
    expect(paper.annotations).toHaveLength(1);

    const [fromPdf] = mergeSidecarPages([source], {
      ...sidecar,
      pages: [{ ...sidecar.pages[0], pdfPageIndex: 1 }],
    });
    expect(fromPdf.pdf).toEqual(source.pdf);
  });

  it("re-exports PDF-backed pages without duplicating them", async () => {
    const base = await exportPdf(input("flattened"));
    const bytes = await exportPdf({
      title: "Second pass",
      mode: "editable",
      sourceBytes: base,
      pages: [
        {
          templateId: "paper-000",
          landscape: false,
          rotation: 0,
          widthMm: 210,
          heightMm: 297,
          pdfPageIndex: 1,
          annotations: [rectangle],
        },
      ],
    });
    const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(bytes);
    expect(pdf.getPageCount()).toBe(1);
    const sidecar = await loadSidecar(bytes);
    expect(sidecar?.pages[0].pdfPageIndex).toBe(1);
  });

  it("exports text and formula annotations and keeps both in the sidecar", async () => {
    const bytes = await exportPdf({
      title: "Typography",
      mode: "editable",
      pages: [
        {
          templateId: "paper-000",
          landscape: false,
          rotation: 0,
          widthMm: 210,
          heightMm: 297,
          annotations: [text, formula],
        },
      ],
    });
    const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(bytes);
    expect(pdf.getPageCount()).toBe(1);
    const sidecar = await loadSidecar(bytes);
    expect(sidecar?.pages[0].annotations.map((annotation) => annotation.id)).toEqual(["text-1", "formula-1"]);
    expect(sidecar?.pages[0].annotations[0]).toMatchObject({ type: "text", text: "Round trip" });
    expect(sidecar?.pages[0].annotations[1]).toMatchObject({ type: "formula", latex: "E = mc^2" });
  });

  it("keeps bookmarks in the sidecar across an export round-trip", async () => {
    const bookmarks = [
      { id: "bm-1", title: "Chapter 1", pageIndex: 0, depth: 0 },
      { id: "bm-2", title: "My note", pageIndex: 0, depth: 1, custom: true },
    ];
    const bytes = await exportPdf({ ...input("editable"), bookmarks });
    const sidecar = await loadSidecar(bytes);
    expect(sidecar?.bookmarks).toEqual(bookmarks);
    expect((await loadSidecar(await exportPdf({ ...input("flattened"), bookmarks })))?.bookmarks).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("maps a tab to export input, keeping crop boxes and PDF page indices", () => {
    const tab: DocumentTab = {
      id: "doc-tab",
      title: "Tab",
      templateId: "paper-000",
      landscape: true,
      pageCount: 2,
      pages: [
        {
          id: "page-paper",
          templateId: "paper-000",
          rotation: 90,
          annotations: [rectangle],
          cropBox: { xMm: 5, yMm: 6, widthMm: 200, heightMm: 280 },
        },
        {
          id: "page-pdf",
          templateId: "paper-000",
          rotation: 0,
          annotations: [],
          pdf: { url: "blob:x", pageNumber: 3, widthMm: 100, heightMm: 200 },
        },
      ],
    };

    const result = exportInputFromTab(tab, "editable");

    expect(result.title).toBe("Tab");
    expect(result.sourceBytes).toBeUndefined();
    expect(result.pages[0]).toEqual({
      templateId: "paper-000",
      landscape: true,
      rotation: 90,
      widthMm: 210,
      heightMm: 297,
      cropBox: { xMm: 5, yMm: 6, widthMm: 200, heightMm: 280 },
      annotations: [rectangle],
    });
    expect(result.pages[1]).toMatchObject({ pdfPageIndex: 3, widthMm: 100, heightMm: 200 });
  });

  it("rebuilds the page tree in tab order, count and size", async () => {
    const pt = 72 / 25.4;
    const base = await exportPdf({
      title: "Source",
      mode: "flattened",
      pages: [
        { templateId: "paper-000", landscape: true, rotation: 0, widthMm: 297, heightMm: 210, annotations: [] },
        { templateId: "paper-000", landscape: false, rotation: 0, widthMm: 210, heightMm: 297, annotations: [] },
      ],
    });
    const bytes = await exportPdf({
      title: "Rebuilt",
      mode: "editable",
      sourceBytes: base,
      pages: [
        { templateId: "paper-000", landscape: false, rotation: 0, widthMm: 148, heightMm: 210, annotations: [rectangle] },
        { templateId: "paper-000", landscape: false, rotation: 0, widthMm: 210, heightMm: 297, pdfPageIndex: 2, annotations: [] },
        { templateId: "paper-000", landscape: false, rotation: 0, widthMm: 210, heightMm: 297, pdfPageIndex: 2, annotations: [] },
      ],
    });
    const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(bytes);
    expect(pdf.getPageCount()).toBe(3);
    expect(pdf.getPage(0).getWidth()).toBeCloseTo(148 * pt, 1);
    expect(pdf.getPage(1).getWidth()).toBeCloseTo(210 * pt, 1);
    expect(pdf.getPage(2).getWidth()).toBeCloseTo(210 * pt, 1);
    expect(pdf.getPage(0).getHeight()).toBeCloseTo(210 * pt, 1);
    const sidecar = await loadSidecar(bytes);
    expect(sidecar?.pages.map((page) => page.pdfPageIndex)).toEqual([undefined, 2, 3]);
    expect(sidecar?.pages[0].annotations[0].id).toBe("shape-1");
  });
});

describe("combinePdfs", () => {
  it("joins the pages of every source in order", async () => {
    const first = await PDFDocument.create();
    first.addPage([210 * 2.8346, 297 * 2.8346]);
    first.addPage([210 * 2.8346, 297 * 2.8346]);
    const second = await PDFDocument.create();
    second.addPage([100 * 2.8346, 200 * 2.8346]);

    const bytes = await combinePdfs([await first.save(), await second.save()]);
    const merged = await PDFDocument.load(bytes);

    expect(merged.getPageCount()).toBe(3);
    expect(merged.getPage(0).getWidth()).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 0);
    expect(merged.getPage(2).getWidth()).toBeCloseTo(283.46, 0);
    expect(merged.getPage(2).getHeight()).toBeCloseTo(566.93, 0);
  });

  it("combines annotated flattened exports", async () => {
    const first = await exportPdf(input("flattened"));
    const second = await exportPdf({ ...input("flattened"), pages: [{ ...input("flattened").pages[0], annotations: [rectangle] }] });

    const bytes = await combinePdfs([first, second]);
    const merged = await PDFDocument.load(bytes);

    expect(merged.getPageCount()).toBe(2);
    expect(await loadSidecar(bytes)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("needs at least two documents", async () => {
    const single = await PDFDocument.create();
    single.addPage([100, 100]);
    await expect(combinePdfs([await single.save()])).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});

import {
  PDFDocument,
  PDFName,
  PDFNumber,
  PDFString,
  StandardFonts,
  degrees,
  rgb,
  type PDFDict,
  type PDFFont,
  type PDFImage,
  type PDFPage,
  type PDFRef,
} from "pdf-lib";
import type { DocumentPage, PaperTemplate, DocumentTab, PdfBookmark } from "./types";
import type { Annotation, FormulaAnnotation, Pt, TextAnnotation } from "./annotationTypes";
import { annotationBounds, formatDimension } from "./annotationGeometry";
import { inkPath, penStyleOptions, polylinePath } from "./ink";
import { layoutText, measureTextWidthMm, TEXT_PADDING_MM, PT_TO_MM } from "./textLayout";
import { latexToPng } from "./formula";
import { paperById } from "./data";
import { paperGeometry } from "./paper";
import type { LineStyleName } from "./types";

export const PT_PER_MM = 72 / 25.4;
export const SIDECAR_KEY = "PDFMasterAnnotations";
const SIDECAR_PREFIX = "pm1:";

export interface PdfOutlineEntry {
  title: string;
  pageIndex: number;
  depth: number;
}

export interface InspectedPdf {
  pages: { widthMm: number; heightMm: number }[];
  bookmarks: PdfOutlineEntry[];
}

type PdfjsModule = typeof import("pdfjs-dist");

let pdfjsPromise: Promise<PdfjsModule> | null = null;

async function loadPdfjs(): Promise<PdfjsModule> {
  if (!pdfjsPromise) {
    pdfjsPromise = (async () => {
      const pdfjs = await import("pdfjs-dist");
      const worker = await import("pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.min.mjs?url");
      pdfjs.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = worker.default;
      return pdfjs;
    })();
  }
  return pdfjsPromise;
}

function copyBytes(bytes: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
  // pdf.js transfers the buffer to its worker; handing it our stored copy would detach it.
  return bytes.slice();
}

/** Page sizes (in mm) and the outline of an opened PDF. */
export async function inspectPdf(bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<InspectedPdf> {
  const pdfjs = await loadPdfjs();
  const doc = await pdfjs.getDocument({ data: copyBytes(bytes) }).promise;
  try {
    const pages: InspectedPdf["pages"] = [];
    // Bounded batches keep 500-page documents snappy without flooding the worker.
    const BATCH = 16;
    for (let start = 0; start < doc.numPages; start += BATCH) {
      const end = Math.min(doc.numPages, start + BATCH);
      await Promise.all(
        Array.from({ length: end - start }, async (_, offset) => {
          const index = start + offset;
          const page = await doc.getPage(index + 1);
          const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 1 });
          pages[index] = {
            widthMm: (viewport.width * 25.4) / 72,
            heightMm: (viewport.height * 25.4) / 72,
          };
        }),
      );
    }
    const bookmarks: PdfOutlineEntry[] = [];
    const outline = await doc.getOutline();
    const walk = async (items: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof doc.getOutline>>, depth: number) => {
      if (!items) return;
      for (const item of items) {
        let pageIndex: number | null = null;
        try {
          const dest = typeof item.dest === "string" ? await doc.getDestination(item.dest) : item.dest;
          if (Array.isArray(dest) && dest[0]) pageIndex = await doc.getPageIndex(dest[0] as never);
        } catch {
          pageIndex = null;
        }
        if (pageIndex !== null) {
          bookmarks.push({ title: item.title || "Untitled", pageIndex, depth });
        }
        if (item.items?.length) await walk(item.items, depth + 1);
      }
    };
    await walk(outline, 0);
    return { pages, bookmarks };
  } finally {
    const destroy = (doc as unknown as { destroy?: () => Promise<void> }).destroy;
    if (destroy) await destroy.call(doc);
  }
}

const documentCache = new Map<string, Promise<import("pdfjs-dist").PDFDocumentProxy>>();
const renderTasks = new WeakMap<HTMLCanvasElement, { cancel: () => void }>();

export function getPdfDocument(url: string) {
  let cached = documentCache.get(url);
  if (!cached) {
    cached = loadPdfjs().then((pdfjs) => pdfjs.getDocument({ url }).promise);
    documentCache.set(url, cached);
  }
  return cached;
}

/** Drop a cached document (and its worker memory) when its tab closes. */
export function releasePdfDocument(url: string): void {
  const cached = documentCache.get(url);
  if (!cached) return;
  documentCache.delete(url);
  void cached
    .then((doc) => (doc as unknown as { destroy?: () => Promise<void> }).destroy?.())
    .catch(() => undefined);
}

/** Rasterise one page into a canvas at `quality` device pixels per CSS pixel. */
export async function renderPdfPage(
  url: string,
  pageNumber: number,
  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement,
  cssWidthPx: number,
  cssHeightPx: number,
  quality: number,
): Promise<void> {
  const doc = await getPdfDocument(url);
  const page = await doc.getPage(pageNumber);
  const base = page.getViewport({ scale: 1 });
  const pixelRatio = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, quality));
  canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssWidthPx * pixelRatio));
  canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssHeightPx * pixelRatio));
  canvas.style.width = `${cssWidthPx}px`;
  canvas.style.height = `${cssHeightPx}px`;
  renderTasks.get(canvas)?.cancel();
  const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: canvas.width / base.width });
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!context) return;
  const task = page.render({ canvas, viewport });
  renderTasks.set(canvas, task);
  try {
    await task.promise;
  } catch {
    // A cancelled render is expected while zooming or switching pages.
  }
}

export type PersistenceMode = "editable" | "flattened";

  heightMm: number;

export interface SerializedPage {
  templateId: string;
  landscape: boolean;
  rotation: number;
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
  pdfPageIndex?: number;
  cropBox?: { xMm: number; yMm: number; widthMm: number; heightMm: number };
  annotations: Annotation[];
}

export interface SerializedDocument {
  version: 1;
  title: string;
  savedAt: string;
  pages: SerializedPage[];
  bookmarks?: PdfBookmark[];
}

function base64Encode(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let binary = "";
  for (let index = 0; index < bytes.length; index += 0x8000) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(index, index + 0x8000));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

function base64Decode(text: string): Uint8Array {
  const binary = atob(text);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let index = 0; index < binary.length; index += 1) bytes[index] = binary.charCodeAt(index);
  return bytes;
}

export function encodeSidecar(document: SerializedDocument): string {
  return SIDECAR_PREFIX + base64Encode(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(document)));
}

export function decodeSidecar(value: string): SerializedDocument | null {
  if (!value.startsWith(SIDECAR_PREFIX)) return null;
  try {
    const json = new TextDecoder().decode(base64Decode(value.slice(SIDECAR_PREFIX.length)));
    const parsed = JSON.parse(json) as SerializedDocument;
    return parsed.version === 1 && Array.isArray(parsed.pages) ? parsed : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Async sidecar reader used by the import path and tests. */
export async function loadSidecar(bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<SerializedDocument | null> {
  const doc = await PDFDocument.load(bytes, { updateMetadata: false });
  const value = doc.catalog.lookup(PDFName.of(SIDECAR_KEY));
  return value instanceof PDFString ? decodeSidecar(value.decodeText()) : null;
}

function hexToRgb(hex: string) {
  const clean = hex.replace("#", "");
  const value = clean.length === 3 ? clean.split("").map((c) => c + c).join("") : clean.padEnd(6, "0");
  const number = Number.parseInt(value.slice(0, 6), 16);
  return rgb(((number >> 16) & 255) / 255, ((number >> 8) & 255) / 255, (number & 255) / 255);
}

const DASH: Record<LineStyleName, (width: number) => number[]> = {
  solid: () => [],
  dash: (width) => [Math.max(1.2, width * 4), Math.max(0.8, width * 2.4)],
  dot: (width) => [Math.max(0.2, width), Math.max(0.9, width * 2.2)],
  "dash-dot": (width) => [Math.max(1.4, width * 5), Math.max(0.8, width * 2), Math.max(0.2, width), Math.max(0.8, width * 2)],
};

function arrowHeadPath(tip: Pt, from: Pt, kind: number, size: number): { path: string; filled: boolean } | null {
  if (!kind) return null;
  const angle = Math.atan2(tip[1] - from[1], tip[0] - from[0]);
  const length = size;
  const spread = size * 0.42;
  const wing = (side: 1 | -1): Pt => [
    tip[0] - Math.cos(angle) * length - Math.cos(angle + (Math.PI / 2) * side) * spread,
    tip[1] - Math.sin(angle) * length - Math.sin(angle + (Math.PI / 2) * side) * spread,
  ];
  const left = wing(1);
  const right = wing(-1);
  if (kind === 3) {
    return { path: `M ${left[0]} ${left[1]} L ${tip[0]} ${tip[1]} L ${right[0]} ${right[1]}`, filled: false };
  }
  if (kind === 7) {
    const back: Pt = [tip[0] - Math.cos(angle) * length, tip[1] - Math.sin(angle) * length];
    return {
      path: polylinePath([tip, left, back, right], true),
      filled: true,
    };
  }
  if (kind === 10) {
    const back: Pt = [tip[0] - Math.cos(angle) * length, tip[1] - Math.sin(angle) * length];
    return { path: polylinePath([tip, left, back, right], true), filled: true };
  }
  return { path: polylinePath([tip, left, right], true), filled: true };
}

interface DrawContext {
  pdf: PDFDocument;
  page: PDFPage;
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
  fonts: Map<StandardFonts, Promise<PDFFont>>;
  images: Map<string, Promise<PDFImage | null>>;
  formulas: Map<string, Promise<PDFImage | null>>;
}

function fontFor(context: DrawContext, name: StandardFonts): Promise<PDFFont> {
  let font = context.fonts.get(name);
  if (!font) {
    font = context.pdf.embedFont(name);
    context.fonts.set(name, font);
  }
  return font;
}

function standardFontFor(annotation: TextAnnotation): StandardFonts {
  if (annotation.fontFamily === "Courier") {
    if (annotation.bold && annotation.italic) return StandardFonts.CourierBoldOblique;
    if (annotation.bold) return StandardFonts.CourierBold;
    if (annotation.italic) return StandardFonts.CourierOblique;
    return StandardFonts.Courier;
  }
  if (annotation.fontFamily === "TimesRoman") {
    if (annotation.bold && annotation.italic) return StandardFonts.TimesRomanBoldItalic;
    if (annotation.bold) return StandardFonts.TimesRomanBold;
    if (annotation.italic) return StandardFonts.TimesRomanItalic;
    return StandardFonts.TimesRoman;
  }
  if (annotation.bold && annotation.italic) return StandardFonts.HelveticaBoldOblique;
  if (annotation.bold) return StandardFonts.HelveticaBold;
  if (annotation.italic) return StandardFonts.HelveticaOblique;
  return StandardFonts.Helvetica;
}

/** CSS-style clockwise rotation around a centre, in screen (y-down) millimetres. */
function rotateMm(point: Pt, center: Pt, rotation: number): Pt {
  const angle = (rotation * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cos = Math.cos(angle);
  const sin = Math.sin(angle);
  const dx = point[0] - center[0];
  const dy = point[1] - center[1];
  return [center[0] + dx * cos - dy * sin, center[1] + dx * sin + dy * cos];
}

function toPdfPoint(point: Pt, heightMm: number): { x: number; y: number } {
  return { x: point[0] * PT_PER_MM, y: (heightMm - point[1]) * PT_PER_MM };
}

/** Lower-left corner of a box after rotating it around its own centre. */
function rotatedLowerLeft(
  box: { xMm: number; yMm: number; widthMm: number; heightMm: number },
  rotation: number,
  heightMm: number,
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const center: Pt = [box.xMm + box.widthMm / 2, box.yMm + box.heightMm / 2];
  return toPdfPoint(rotateMm([box.xMm, box.yMm + box.heightMm], center, rotation), heightMm);
}

const STAMP_URL_PREFIXES = ["data:image/", "blob:", "/assets/"];

async function embedStampImage(pdf: PDFDocument, imageUrl: string): Promise<PDFImage | null> {
  // Only self-produced stamps may be fetched; anything else is ignored.
  if (!STAMP_URL_PREFIXES.some((prefix) => imageUrl.startsWith(prefix))) return null;
  const embed = async (bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<PDFImage | null> => {
    const isJpeg = bytes[0] === 0xff && bytes[1] === 0xd8;
    return isJpeg ? pdf.embedJpg(bytes) : pdf.embedPng(bytes);
  };
  try {
    if (imageUrl.startsWith("data:")) {
      const comma = imageUrl.indexOf(",");
      const meta = imageUrl.slice(0, comma);
      const payload = imageUrl.slice(comma + 1);
      if (meta.includes("svg")) return null;
      return await embed(base64ToBytes(payload));
    }
    const response = await fetch(imageUrl);
    if (!response.ok) return null;
    return await embed(new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer()));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function base64ToBytes(payload: string): Uint8Array {
  const binary = atob(payload);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let index = 0; index < binary.length; index += 1) bytes[index] = binary.charCodeAt(index);
  return bytes;
}

async function drawAnnotation(context: DrawContext, annotation: Annotation): Promise<void> {
  const { page, heightMm } = context;
  if (annotation.hidden) return;
  const svgOptions = { x: 0, y: heightMm * PT_PER_MM, scale: PT_PER_MM };

  switch (annotation.type) {
    case "pen": {
      const style = annotation.penStyle ?? (annotation.sensitivity ? "pro" : "basic");
      const path = inkPath(annotation.points, annotation.thicknessMm, {
        ...penStyleOptions(style, annotation.pressureLevel ?? (annotation.sensitivity ? 55 : 0)),
        simulatePressure: false,
      });
      if (!path) return;
      page.drawSvgPath(path, {
        ...svgOptions,
        color: hexToRgb(annotation.color),
        opacity: annotation.opacity,
      });
      return;
    }
    case "marker": {
      if (annotation.points.length < 2) return;
      const path = polylinePath(annotation.points.map(([x, value]) => [x, value] as Pt));
      page.drawSvgPath(path, {
        ...svgOptions,
        borderColor: hexToRgb(annotation.color),
        borderWidth: annotation.thicknessMm,
        borderOpacity: annotation.opacity,
      });
      return;
    }
    case "shape": {
      await drawShape(context, annotation, svgOptions);
      return;
    }
    case "stamp": {
      const image = await context.images.get(annotation.imageUrl);
      if (!image) return;
      const width = annotation.widthMm * PT_PER_MM;
      const height = annotation.heightMm * PT_PER_MM;
      const cx = (annotation.xMm + annotation.widthMm / 2) * PT_PER_MM;
      const cy = (heightMm - annotation.yMm - annotation.heightMm / 2) * PT_PER_MM;
      const radians = (-annotation.rotation * Math.PI) / 180;
      const x = cx - (width / 2) * Math.cos(radians) + (height / 2) * Math.sin(radians);
      const yPos = cy - (width / 2) * Math.sin(radians) - (height / 2) * Math.cos(radians);
      // A negative scale mirrors the image around its lower-left corner, so move that corner first.
      const shiftX = (annotation.flipH ? width * Math.cos(radians) : 0) - (annotation.flipV ? height * Math.sin(radians) : 0);
      const shiftY = (annotation.flipH ? width * Math.sin(radians) : 0) + (annotation.flipV ? height * Math.cos(radians) : 0);
      page.drawImage(image, {
        x: x + shiftX,
        y: yPos + shiftY,
        width: annotation.flipH ? -width : width,
        height: annotation.flipV ? -height : height,
        opacity: annotation.opacity,
        rotate: degrees(-annotation.rotation),
      });
      return;
    }
    case "text": {
      await drawTextBox(context, annotation);
      return;
    }
    case "formula": {
      await drawFormula(context, annotation);
      return;
    }
  }
}

interface BoxFrame {
  xMm: number;
  yMm: number;
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
  rotation: number;
}

function drawBoxFill(
  context: DrawContext,
  box: BoxFrame,
  options: { fillColor?: string; fillTransparent?: boolean; borderColor: string; borderWidth: number; rounded?: boolean; opacity?: number },
): void {
  const { page, heightMm } = context;
  const opacity = options.opacity ?? 1;
  const corner = rotatedLowerLeft(box, box.rotation, heightMm);
  const color = options.fillTransparent ? undefined : options.fillColor ? hexToRgb(options.fillColor) : undefined;
  if (options.rounded && box.rotation === 0) {
    const radius = Math.min(3, box.widthMm / 4, box.heightMm / 4);
    const x = box.xMm;
    const y = box.yMm;
    const w = box.widthMm;
    const h = box.heightMm;
    const path =
      `M ${x + radius} ${y} H ${x + w - radius} Q ${x + w} ${y} ${x + w} ${y + radius} ` +
      `V ${y + h - radius} Q ${x + w} ${y + h} ${x + w - radius} ${y + h} ` +
      `H ${x + radius} Q ${x} ${y + h} ${x} ${y + h - radius} ` +
      `V ${y + radius} Q ${x} ${y} ${x + radius} ${y} Z`;
    page.drawSvgPath(path, {
      x: 0,
      y: heightMm * PT_PER_MM,
      scale: PT_PER_MM,
      color,
      opacity: color ? opacity : undefined,
      borderColor: options.borderWidth > 0 ? hexToRgb(options.borderColor) : undefined,
      borderWidth: options.borderWidth > 0 ? options.borderWidth : undefined,
      borderOpacity: opacity,
    });
    return;
  }
  page.drawRectangle({
    x: corner.x,
    y: corner.y,
    width: box.widthMm * PT_PER_MM,
    height: box.heightMm * PT_PER_MM,
    rotate: degrees(-box.rotation),
    color,
    opacity: color ? opacity : undefined,
    borderColor: options.borderWidth > 0 ? hexToRgb(options.borderColor) : undefined,
    borderWidth: options.borderWidth > 0 ? options.borderWidth : undefined,
    borderOpacity: opacity,
  });
}

async function drawTextBox(context: DrawContext, annotation: TextAnnotation): Promise<void> {
  const { page, heightMm } = context;
  const box: BoxFrame = {
    xMm: annotation.xMm,
    yMm: annotation.yMm,
    widthMm: annotation.widthMm,
    heightMm: annotation.heightMm,
    rotation: annotation.rotation,
  };
  const opacity = annotation.opacity;
  const center: Pt = [annotation.xMm + annotation.widthMm / 2, annotation.yMm + annotation.heightMm / 2];

  if (annotation.frameType === 3) {
    drawBoxFill(
      context,
      { ...box, xMm: box.xMm + 1.3, yMm: box.yMm + 1.3 },
      {
        fillColor: "#14161b",
        fillTransparent: false,
        borderColor: annotation.frameColor,
        borderWidth: 0,
        opacity: annotation.opacity,
      },
    );
  }
  drawBoxFill(context, box, {
    fillColor: annotation.boxFillColor,
    fillTransparent: annotation.boxFillTransparent,
    borderColor: annotation.frameColor,
    borderWidth: annotation.frameType === 0 ? 0 : annotation.lineWidthMm,
    rounded: annotation.frameType === 2,
    opacity: annotation.opacity,
  });

  if (!annotation.text) return;
  const layout = layoutText(annotation);
  const font = await fontFor(context, standardFontFor(annotation));
  const padding = TEXT_PADDING_MM;
  const fontSizeMm = layout.fontSizePt * PT_TO_MM;

  for (const line of layout.lines) {
    if (!line.text) continue;
    const widthMm = measureTextWidthMm(line.text, annotation.fontFamily, annotation.bold, layout.fontSizePt);
    let xMm = annotation.xMm + padding;
    if (annotation.align === "center") xMm = annotation.xMm + (annotation.widthMm - widthMm) / 2;
    if (annotation.align === "right") xMm = annotation.xMm + annotation.widthMm - padding - widthMm;
    const scriptShiftMm =
      annotation.script === "sub" ? fontSizeMm * 0.22 : annotation.script === "super" ? -fontSizeMm * 0.3 : 0;
    const baselineMm =
      annotation.yMm + padding + line.topMm + fontSizeMm * 0.82 + scriptShiftMm;
    const start = toPdfPoint(rotateMm([xMm, baselineMm], center, annotation.rotation), heightMm);
    const size = annotation.script === "normal" ? layout.fontSizePt : layout.fontSizePt * 0.72;
    page.drawText(line.text, {
      x: start.x,
      y: start.y,
      size,
      font,
      color: hexToRgb(annotation.textColor),
      opacity,
      rotate: degrees(-annotation.rotation),
    });
    const under = (offsetMm: number, thickness: number) => {
      const yMm = baselineMm + offsetMm;
      const a = rotateMm([xMm, yMm], center, annotation.rotation);
      const b = rotateMm([xMm + widthMm, yMm], center, annotation.rotation);
      page.drawLine({
        start: toPdfPoint(a, heightMm),
        end: toPdfPoint(b, heightMm),
        thickness,
        color: hexToRgb(annotation.textColor),
        opacity,
      });
    };
    if (annotation.underline) under(0.55, Math.max(0.35, fontSizeMm * 0.07));
    if (annotation.strikeOut) under(-fontSizeMm * 0.28, Math.max(0.3, fontSizeMm * 0.06));
  }
}

async function formulaImage(context: DrawContext, annotation: FormulaAnnotation): Promise<PDFImage | null> {
  const key = `${annotation.latex}::${annotation.fontSizePt}`;
  let pending = context.formulas.get(key);
  if (!pending) {
    const widthPx = Math.max(48, Math.ceil(annotation.widthMm * 8));
    const heightPx = Math.max(24, Math.ceil(annotation.heightMm * 8));
    const fontSizePx = Math.max(8, annotation.fontSizePt * 2);
    pending = latexToPng(annotation.latex, fontSizePx, widthPx, heightPx, annotation.color).then((raster) =>
      raster ? context.pdf.embedPng(raster.dataUrl) : null,
    );
    pending = pending.catch(() => null);
    context.formulas.set(key, pending);
  }
  return pending;
}

async function drawFormula(context: DrawContext, annotation: FormulaAnnotation): Promise<void> {
  const { page, heightMm } = context;
  if (!annotation.fillTransparent) {
    drawBoxFill(
      context,
      {
        xMm: annotation.xMm,
        yMm: annotation.yMm,
        widthMm: annotation.widthMm,
        heightMm: annotation.heightMm,
        rotation: annotation.rotation,
      },
      { fillColor: annotation.fillColor, borderColor: annotation.fillColor, borderWidth: 0, opacity: annotation.opacity },
    );
  }
  const image = await formulaImage(context, annotation);
  const corner = rotatedLowerLeft(
    {
      xMm: annotation.xMm,
      yMm: annotation.yMm,
      widthMm: annotation.widthMm,
      heightMm: annotation.heightMm,
    },
    annotation.rotation,
    heightMm,
  );
  if (image) {
    page.drawImage(image, {
      x: corner.x,
      y: corner.y,
      width: annotation.widthMm * PT_PER_MM,
      height: annotation.heightMm * PT_PER_MM,
      opacity: annotation.opacity,
      rotate: degrees(-annotation.rotation),
    });
    return;
  }
  // A headless run has no canvas for KaTeX: lay the source out as plain text.
  const fallback: TextAnnotation = {
    ...annotation,
    type: "text",
    text: annotation.latex,
    fontFamily: "Helvetica",
    bold: false,
    italic: false,
    textColor: annotation.color,
    underline: false,
    strikeOut: false,
    align: "center",
    script: "normal",
    boxFillColor: annotation.fillColor,
    boxFillTransparent: true,
    frameType: 0,
    frameColor: annotation.fillColor,
    lineWidthMm: 0,
    autoFit: false,
  };
  const layout = layoutText(fallback);
  const font = await fontFor(context, StandardFonts.Helvetica);
  const center: Pt = [annotation.xMm + annotation.widthMm / 2, annotation.yMm + annotation.heightMm / 2];
  for (const line of layout.lines) {
    const widthMm = measureTextWidthMm(line.text, "Helvetica", false, layout.fontSizePt);
    const xMm = annotation.xMm + (annotation.widthMm - widthMm) / 2;
    const baselineMm = annotation.yMm + annotation.heightMm / 2 + line.topMm - (layout.lines.length * layout.lineHeightMm) / 2 + layout.lineHeightMm * 0.75;
    const start = toPdfPoint(rotateMm([xMm, baselineMm], center, annotation.rotation), heightMm);
    page.drawText(line.text, {
      x: start.x,
      y: start.y,
      size: layout.fontSizePt,
      font,
      color: hexToRgb(annotation.color),
      opacity: annotation.opacity,
      rotate: degrees(-annotation.rotation),
    });
  }
}

async function drawShape(
  context: DrawContext,
  annotation: Extract<Annotation, { type: "shape" }>,
  svgOptions: { x: number; y: number; scale: number },
): Promise<void> {
  const { page, heightMm } = context;
  const stroke = hexToRgb(annotation.strokeColor);
  const fill = annotation.fillColor && !annotation.fillTransparent ? hexToRgb(annotation.fillColor) : null;
  const dash = DASH[annotation.lineStyle](annotation.lineWidthMm);
  const options = {
    ...svgOptions,
    borderColor: stroke,
    borderWidth: annotation.lineWidthMm,
    borderOpacity: annotation.opacity,
    borderDashArray: dash.length ? dash : undefined,
    color: fill ?? undefined,
    opacity: fill ? annotation.opacity : undefined,
  };

  if (annotation.shapeType === "rectangle" || annotation.shapeType === "ellipse") {
    const box = annotationBounds(annotation);
    if (annotation.shapeType === "rectangle") {
      page.drawSvgPath(
        `M ${box.x} ${box.y} H ${box.x + box.width} V ${box.y + box.height} H ${box.x} Z`,
        options,
      );
    } else {
      const rx = box.width / 2;
      const ry = box.height / 2;
      const cx = box.x + rx;
      const cy = box.y + ry;
      page.drawSvgPath(
        `M ${cx - rx} ${cy} A ${rx} ${ry} 0 1 0 ${cx + rx} ${cy} A ${rx} ${ry} 0 1 0 ${cx - rx} ${cy} Z`,
        options,
      );
    }
    return;
  }

  if (annotation.shapeType === "polygon") {
    if (annotation.points.length < 2) return;
    page.drawSvgPath(polylinePath(annotation.points, annotation.points.length > 2), options);
    return;
  }

  if (annotation.shapeType === "dimension") {
    const [start, end] = [annotation.start, annotation.end];
    const dx = end[0] - start[0];
    const dy = end[1] - start[1];
    const length = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 0.001;
    const nx = -dy / length;
    const ny = dx / length;
    const offset = Math.max(1.5, annotation.lineWidthMm * 5);
    const a: Pt = [start[0] + nx * offset, start[1] + ny * offset];
    const b: Pt = [end[0] + nx * offset, end[1] + ny * offset];
    page.drawSvgPath(`M ${start[0]} ${start[1]} L ${a[0]} ${a[1]}`, { ...options, borderWidth: annotation.lineWidthMm * 0.6 });
    page.drawSvgPath(`M ${end[0]} ${end[1]} L ${b[0]} ${b[1]}`, { ...options, borderWidth: annotation.lineWidthMm * 0.6 });
    page.drawSvgPath(`M ${a[0]} ${a[1]} L ${b[0]} ${b[1]}`, options);
    const size = Math.max(2, annotation.lineWidthMm * 5);
    for (const [tip, from] of [
      [a, b],
      [b, a],
    ] as [Pt, Pt][]) {
      const head = arrowHeadPath(tip, from, 5, size);
      if (head) page.drawSvgPath(head.path, { ...svgOptions, color: stroke, opacity: annotation.opacity });
    }
    const label = formatDimension(length * annotation.dimensionScale, annotation.dimensionUnit);
    const midpoint: Pt = [(a[0] + b[0]) / 2, (a[1] + b[1]) / 2];
    const textWidth = label.length * 3.4 * 0.55;
    page.drawText(label, {
      x: (midpoint[0] - textWidth / 2) * PT_PER_MM,
      y: (heightMm - midpoint[1] - 1.4) * PT_PER_MM,
      size: 7,
      font: await fontFor(context, StandardFonts.Helvetica),
      color: stroke,
      opacity: annotation.opacity,
    });
    return;
  }

  const [start, end] = [annotation.start, annotation.end];
  page.drawSvgPath(`M ${start[0]} ${start[1]} L ${end[0]} ${end[1]}`, options);
  const size = Math.max(2.2, annotation.lineWidthMm * 5);
  const head = annotation.shapeType === "arrow" ? arrowHeadPath(end, start, annotation.arrowHead || 5, size) : null;
  if (head) {
    page.drawSvgPath(head.path, { ...svgOptions, color: stroke, opacity: annotation.opacity });
  }
  const tail = annotation.shapeType === "arrow" ? arrowHeadPath(start, end, annotation.arrowTail, size) : null;
  if (tail) {
    page.drawSvgPath(tail.path, { ...svgOptions, color: stroke, opacity: annotation.opacity });
  }
}

function drawPaperBackground(page: PDFPage, template: PaperTemplate, landscape: boolean, widthMm: number, heightMm: number) {
  const geometry = paperGeometry(template, landscape);
  page.drawRectangle({
    x: 0,
    y: 0,
    width: widthMm * PT_PER_MM,
    height: heightMm * PT_PER_MM,
    color: rgb(1, 1, 0.973),
  });
  if (!geometry.hasGrid) return;
  const color = hexToRgb(geometry.color);
  const layers: [string, number, number][] = [
    [geometry.base, 0.35, 0.6],
    [geometry.minor, 0.55, 0.8],
    [geometry.major, 0.75, 1],
  ];
  for (const [path, opacity, weight] of layers) {
    if (!path) continue;
    const matches = path.match(/M([\d.]+) ([\d.]+)[HV]([\d.]+)/g) ?? [];
    for (const match of matches) {
      const parsed = /M([\d.]+) ([\d.]+)([HV])([\d.]+)/.exec(match);
      if (!parsed) continue;
      const x = Number(parsed[1]);
      const yMm = Number(parsed[2]);
      const end = Number(parsed[4]);
      page.drawLine({
        start: { x: x * PT_PER_MM, y: (heightMm - yMm) * PT_PER_MM },
        end:
          parsed[3] === "H"
            ? { x: end * PT_PER_MM, y: (heightMm - yMm) * PT_PER_MM }
            : { x: x * PT_PER_MM, y: (heightMm - end) * PT_PER_MM },
        thickness: Math.max(0.25, geometry.widthStrokeMm * weight * PT_PER_MM),
        color,
        opacity,
      });
    }
  }
}

function serializedFromInput(
  input: PdfExportInput,
  copied: boolean[],
): SerializedDocument {
  return {
    version: 1,
    title: input.title,
    savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    ...(input.bookmarks?.length ? { bookmarks: input.bookmarks } : {}),
    pages: input.pages.map((page, index) => ({
      templateId: page.templateId,
      landscape: page.landscape,
      rotation: page.rotation,
      widthMm: page.widthMm,
      heightMm: page.heightMm,
      // In the rebuilt file every page sits at its new 1-based position.
      ...(copied[index] ? { pdfPageIndex: index + 1 } : {}),
      ...(page.cropBox ? { cropBox: page.cropBox } : {}),
      annotations: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(page.annotations)) as Annotation[],
    })),
  };
}

/** Copies annotations and page settings from a sidecar back onto freshly opened pages. */
export function mergeSidecarPages(pages: DocumentPage[], sidecar: SerializedDocument | null): DocumentPage[] {
  if (!sidecar) return pages;
  return pages.map((page, index) => {
    const saved = sidecar.pages[index];
    if (!saved) return page;
    const merged: DocumentPage = {
      ...page,
      annotations: saved.annotations ?? [],
      rotation: ((((saved.rotation ?? 0) % 360) + 360) % 360) as DocumentPage["rotation"],
      ...(saved.cropBox ? { cropBox: saved.cropBox } : {}),
    };
    // No pdfPageIndex means the page was parametric when saved; show paper, not the copied raster.
    if (!saved.pdfPageIndex) return { ...merged, templateId: saved.templateId || page.templateId, pdf: undefined };
    return merged;
  });
}

/** Writes a real outline tree so viewers list the bookmarks, not only the sidecar. */
function writeOutlines(pdf: PDFDocument, bookmarks: PdfBookmark[] | undefined): void {
  if (!bookmarks?.length) return;
  const entries = bookmarks.filter(
    (bookmark) => bookmark.pageIndex >= 0 && bookmark.pageIndex < pdf.getPageCount(),
  );
  if (!entries.length) return;
  const context = pdf.context;
  const rootRef = context.nextRef();
  const root = context.obj({ Type: "Outlines" });
  context.assign(rootRef, root);
  pdf.catalog.set(PDFName.of("Outlines"), rootRef);
  pdf.catalog.set(PDFName.of("PageMode"), PDFName.of("UseOutlines"));

  const parents: PDFRef[] = [rootRef];
  const lastChild = new Map<PDFRef, PDFRef>();
  const descendants = new Map<PDFRef, number>();
  for (const entry of entries) {
    const depth = Math.min(Math.max(0, entry.depth), parents.length - 1);
    parents.length = depth + 1;
    const parentRef = parents[depth];
    const ref = context.nextRef();
    const dict = context.obj({
      Title: PDFString.of(entry.title || "Untitled"),
      Parent: parentRef,
      Dest: context.obj([pdf.getPage(entry.pageIndex).ref, PDFName.of("Fit")]),
    });
    const previous = lastChild.get(parentRef);
    if (previous) {
      dict.set(PDFName.of("Prev"), previous);
      (context.lookup(previous) as PDFDict).set(PDFName.of("Next"), ref);
    }
    context.assign(ref, dict);
    const parent = context.lookup(parentRef) as PDFDict;
    if (!previous) parent.set(PDFName.of("First"), ref);
    parent.set(PDFName.of("Last"), ref);
    lastChild.set(parentRef, ref);
    // Every enclosing node counts this entry, so /Count matches the open subtree.
    for (const ancestor of parents) descendants.set(ancestor, (descendants.get(ancestor) ?? 0) + 1);
    parents.push(ref);
  }
  for (const [ref, count] of descendants) {
    (context.lookup(ref) as PDFDict).set(PDFName.of("Count"), PDFNumber.of(count));
  }
}

/**
 * Rebuilds the page tree from scratch: source pages are copied one by one in
 * tab order, paper pages are drawn fresh, then rotation and crop are applied.
 * Reordering, duplicating or deleting pages therefore survives an export
 * instead of silently keeping the source document's page order. Editable
 * mode writes annotations only to the sidecar, so reopening never double-draws.
 */
export async function exportPdf(input: PdfExportInput): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
  pdf.setTitle(input.title);
  pdf.setProducer("PDF Master");

  const needsSource = input.pages.some((page) => page.pdfPageIndex !== undefined);
  let source: PDFDocument | null = null;
  if (needsSource) {
    try {
      if (!input.sourceBytes) throw new Error("missing source bytes");
      source = await PDFDocument.load(input.sourceBytes, { updateMetadata: false });
    } catch {
      throw new Error("Source PDF bytes are unavailable or unreadable");
    }
  }

  const flatten = input.mode === "flattened";
  const images = new Map<string, Promise<PDFImage | null>>();
  if (flatten) {
    for (const pageInput of input.pages) {
      for (const annotation of pageInput.annotations) {
        if (annotation.type === "stamp" && !images.has(annotation.imageUrl)) {
          images.set(annotation.imageUrl, embedStampImage(pdf, annotation.imageUrl));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const fonts = new Map<StandardFonts, Promise<PDFFont>>();
  const formulas = new Map<string, Promise<PDFImage | null>>();
  const copied: boolean[] = [];

  for (const pageInput of input.pages) {
    const sourceIndex = pageInput.pdfPageIndex;
    let page: PDFPage;
    let fromPdf = false;
    if (sourceIndex !== undefined) {
      if (!source || sourceIndex < 1 || sourceIndex > source.getPageCount()) {
        throw new Error("Source PDF bytes are unavailable or unreadable");
      }
      const [copy] = await pdf.copyPages(source, [sourceIndex - 1]);
      page = pdf.addPage(copy);
      if (pageInput.rotation) {
        page.setRotation(degrees((page.getRotation().angle + pageInput.rotation) % 360));
      }
      fromPdf = true;
    } else {
      page = pdf.addPage([pageInput.widthMm * PT_PER_MM, pageInput.heightMm * PT_PER_MM]);
      if (pageInput.rotation) page.setRotation(degrees(pageInput.rotation));
      const template = paperById.get(pageInput.templateId);
      if (template) drawPaperBackground(page, template, pageInput.landscape, pageInput.widthMm, pageInput.heightMm);
    }
    copied.push(fromPdf);

    if (pageInput.cropBox) {
      const crop = pageInput.cropBox;
      page.setCropBox(
        crop.xMm * PT_PER_MM,
        (pageInput.heightMm - crop.yMm - crop.heightMm) * PT_PER_MM,
        crop.widthMm * PT_PER_MM,
        crop.heightMm * PT_PER_MM,
      );
    }

    if (!flatten) continue;
    for (const annotation of pageInput.annotations) {
      await drawAnnotation(
        { pdf, page, widthMm: pageInput.widthMm, heightMm: pageInput.heightMm, fonts, images, formulas },
        annotation,
      );
    }
  }

  writeOutlines(pdf, input.bookmarks);
  if (input.mode === "editable") {
    pdf.catalog.set(PDFName.of(SIDECAR_KEY), PDFString.of(encodeSidecar(serializedFromInput(input, copied))));
  }

  return pdf.save();
}

/** Merges PDFs page by page into one flat document, in the order given. */
export async function combinePdfs(parts: Uint8Array[]): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  if (parts.length < 2) throw new Error("Combining documents needs at least two PDFs");
  const merged = await PDFDocument.create();
  merged.setProducer("PDF Master");
  for (const bytes of parts) {
    const source = await PDFDocument.load(bytes, { updateMetadata: false });
    const pages = await merged.copyPages(source, source.getPageIndices());
    for (const page of pages) merged.addPage(page);
  }
  return merged.save();
}

/** Convenience wrapper used by the Save button and the tests. */
export function exportInputFromTab(tab: DocumentTab, mode: PersistenceMode): PdfExportInput {
  return {
    title: tab.title,
    mode,
    sourceBytes: tab.pdf?.bytes,
    bookmarks: tab.pdf?.bookmarks,
    pages: tab.pages.map((page) => {
      const template = paperById.get(page.templateId);
      const pdf = page.pdf;
      return {
        templateId: page.templateId,
        landscape: tab.landscape,
        rotation: page.rotation,
        widthMm: pdf?.widthMm ?? template?.page.widthMm ?? 210,
        heightMm: pdf?.heightMm ?? template?.page.heightMm ?? 297,
        pdfPageIndex: pdf?.pageNumber,
        ...(page.cropBox ? { cropBox: page.cropBox } : {}),
        annotations: page.annotations,
      };
    }),
  };
}

export function downloadPdf(bytes: Uint8Array, filename: string): void {
  const blob = new Blob([new Uint8Array(bytes)], { type: "application/pdf" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const anchor = document.createElement("a");
  anchor.href = url;
  anchor.download = filename.endsWith(".pdf") ? filename : `${filename}.pdf`;
  document.body.appendChild(anchor);
  anchor.click();
  anchor.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 4000);
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { findTextMatches, normalizeSearchText, pdfTextRect, searchTextContains } from "./pdfSearch";

const PT_TO_MM = 25.4 / 72;

// "باکتری‌شناسی" with a ZWNJ between the two words.
const ZWNJ_WORD = "\u0628\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u06CC";
const SPACED_WORD = "\u0628\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u06CC";

describe("Persian and multilingual search normalisation", () => {
  it("folds Arabic presentation forms through NFKC", () => {
    expect(normalizeSearchText("\uFEFB")).toBe("\u0644\u0627");
    expect(normalizeSearchText("\uFE91\uFE8E\uFB90\uFE98\uFEAD\uFBFC")).toBe(
      normalizeSearchText("\u0628\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0631\u06CC"),
    );
  });

  it("unifies Arabic yeh/kaf with Persian yeh/kaf", () => {
    expect(normalizeSearchText("\u0639\u0644\u064A")).toBe(normalizeSearchText("\u0639\u0644\u06CC"));
    expect(normalizeSearchText("\u0643\u062A\u0627\u0628")).toBe(normalizeSearchText("\u06A9\u062A\u0627\u0628"));
  });

  it("drops harakat, tatweel and ZWNJ", () => {
    expect(normalizeSearchText("\u0628\u064E\u0627\u0643\u062A\u0631\u064A")).toBe(
      normalizeSearchText("\u0628\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0631\u06CC"),
    );
    expect(normalizeSearchText(ZWNJ_WORD)).toBe(normalizeSearchText(SPACED_WORD));
  });

  it("matches a spaced query against a ZWNJ word and vice versa", () => {
    const needle = normalizeSearchText(SPACED_WORD);
    expect(searchTextContains(ZWNJ_WORD, needle)).toBe(true);
    expect(searchTextContains(SPACED_WORD, normalizeSearchText(ZWNJ_WORD))).toBe(true);
    expect(searchTextContains("\u0645\u062A\u0646 \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631", needle)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("finds a legacy visual order run", () => {
    // "یسانش" is the reversed run of "شناسی".
    expect(searchTextContains("\u06CC\u0633\u0627\u0646\u0634", normalizeSearchText("\u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u06CC"))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("word-level search", () => {
  it("returns original indices for a latin word", () => {
    expect(findTextMatches("Hello world", "world")).toEqual([{ start: 6, end: 11 }]);
    expect(findTextMatches("Hello world", "hello")).toEqual([{ start: 0, end: 5 }]);
  });

  it("rejects a partial word when wholeWord is on", () => {
    expect(findTextMatches("water damage", "water", true)).toEqual([{ start: 0, end: 5 }]);
    expect(findTextMatches("waterfall", "water", true)).toEqual([]);
    expect(findTextMatches("آب", "آب", true)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(findTextMatches("آبدهی", "آب", true)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("boxes a latin slice inside the text item", () => {
    const item = { str: "Hello world", transform: [1, 0, 0, 1, 100, 700], width: 110, height: 12 };
    const rect = pdfTextRect(item, 6, 11, 792);
    expect(rect.xMm).toBeCloseTo(100 * PT_TO_MM + (6 / 11) * 110 * PT_TO_MM);
    expect(rect.widthMm).toBeCloseTo((5 / 11) * 110 * PT_TO_MM);
    expect(rect.yMm).toBeCloseTo((792 - 700 - 12) * PT_TO_MM);
  });

  it("boxes a Persian slice from the right edge of the run", () => {
    const item = { str: "آبدهی", transform: [1, 0, 0, 1, 200, 500], width: 50, height: 14 };
    const right = pdfTextRect(item, 0, 2, 792);
    // "آب" starts the logical string, so it sits at the visual right edge.
    expect(right.xMm).toBeCloseTo(200 * PT_TO_MM + 0.6 * 50 * PT_TO_MM);
    expect(right.widthMm).toBeCloseTo((2 / 5) * 50 * PT_TO_MM);
    expect(right.xMm).toBeGreaterThan(200 * PT_TO_MM + 25 * PT_TO_MM);

    const left = pdfTextRect(item, 2, 5, 792);
    expect(left.xMm).toBeCloseTo(200 * PT_TO_MM);
    expect(left.widthMm).toBeCloseTo((3 / 5) * 50 * PT_TO_MM);
  });
});

================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { getPdfDocument } from "./pdfEngine";
import type { DocumentPage, DocumentTab } from "./types";

const PDF_PT_TO_MM = 25.4 / 72;
const MAX_PAGES = 500;

export interface SearchMatch {
  /** 0-based page index inside the tab. */
  pageIndex: number;
  xMm: number;
  yMm: number;
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
  source: "pdf" | "text";
}

/**
 * NFKC folds Arabic presentation forms to base letters; the replacements unify
 * Arabic/Persian yeh and kaf, drop harakat, tatweel and bidi marks, and remove
 * ZWNJ and whitespace so "باکتری شناسی" and "باکتری‌شناسی" compare equal.
 */
export function normalizeSearchText(input: string): string {
  return input
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .replace(/[\u064A\u0649]/g, "\u06CC")
    .replace(/\u0643/g, "\u06A9")
    .replace(/[\u064B-\u065F\u0640]/g, "")
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200F\u202A-\u202E\u2066-\u2069\uFEFF]/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, "")
    .toLowerCase();
}

/** Also matches legacy RTL fonts that store the visual (reversed) run. */
export function searchTextContains(haystack: string, needle: string): boolean {
  if (!needle) return false;
  const text = normalizeSearchText(haystack);
  if (text.includes(needle)) return true;
  const reversedNeedle = [...needle].reverse().join("");
  return [...text].reverse().join("").includes(needle) || text.includes(reversedNeedle);
}

export interface TextRunMatch {
  /** Original string index range, end exclusive. */
  start: number;
  end: number;
}

interface NormalizedRun {
  text: string;
  /** normalized index → index in the original string. */
  map: number[];
}

/**
 * Per-character normalisation that keeps a 1-to-1 map back to `item.str`.
 * Whitespace and combining marks collapse out of `text`, never out of the map.
 */
function normalizeWithMap(value: string): NormalizedRun {
  const chars: string[] = [];
  const map: number[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
    const normalized = normalizeSearchText(value[index]);
    for (const char of normalized) {
      chars.push(char);
      map.push(index);
    }
  }
  return { text: chars.join(""), map };
}

function isWordChar(char: string | undefined): boolean {
  return char !== undefined && /[\p{L}\p{N}]/u.test(char);
}

/** Matches of `query` inside `value`, in original string indices. */
export function findTextMatches(value: string, query: string, wholeWord = false): TextRunMatch[] {
  const needle = normalizeSearchText(query);
  if (!needle) return [];
  const run = normalizeWithMap(value);
  const matches: TextRunMatch[] = [];
  let from = 0;
  while (from <= run.text.length - needle.length) {
    const index = run.text.indexOf(needle, from);
    if (index < 0) break;
    const start = run.map[index] ?? 0;
    const end = (run.map[index + needle.length - 1] ?? value.length - 1) + 1;
    if (!wholeWord || (!isWordChar(value[start - 1]) && !isWordChar(value[end]))) {
      matches.push({ start, end });
    }
    from = index + Math.max(1, needle.length);
  }
  // Legacy fonts drawing visual-order runs cannot be sliced word by word.
  if (!wholeWord && matches.length === 0 && searchTextContains(value, needle)) {
    matches.push({ start: 0, end: value.length });
  }
  return matches;
}

interface PdfTextItem {
  str?: string;
  transform?: number[];
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
}

/** Horizontal slice of one text item that covers `[startIndex, endIndex)`. */
export function pdfTextRect(
  item: PdfTextItem,
  startIndex: number,
  endIndex: number,
  viewportHeight: number,
): Pick<SearchMatch, "xMm" | "yMm" | "widthMm" | "heightMm"> {
  const transform = item.transform ?? [1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0];
  const fontSize = Math.hypot(transform[2] ?? 0, transform[3] ?? 0) || 10;
  const heightPt = item.height && item.height > 0 ? item.height : fontSize;
  const text = item.str ?? "";
  const total = Math.max(1, text.length);
  const start = Math.min(total, Math.max(0, startIndex));
  const end = Math.min(total, Math.max(start, endIndex));
  const widthMm = Math.max(2, (item.width ?? 0) * PDF_PT_TO_MM);
  // RTL text starts at the right edge and flows left.
  const rtl = /[\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(text);
  const offsetRatio = rtl ? (total - end) / total : start / total;
  return {
    xMm: Math.max(0, transform[4] * PDF_PT_TO_MM + offsetRatio * widthMm),
    yMm: Math.max(0, (viewportHeight - transform[5] - heightPt) * PDF_PT_TO_MM),
    widthMm: Math.max(1.5, ((end - start) / total) * widthMm),
    heightMm: Math.max(2, heightPt * PDF_PT_TO_MM),
  };
}

function pdfPageMatches(
  page: DocumentPage,
  pageIndex: number,
  needle: string,
  viewportHeight: number,
  items: PdfTextItem[],
  wholeWord: boolean,
): SearchMatch[] {
  const matches: SearchMatch[] = [];
  for (const item of items) {
    if (typeof item.str !== "string" || !item.str.trim() || !Array.isArray(item.transform)) continue;
    for (const run of findTextMatches(item.str, needle, wholeWord)) {
      matches.push({ pageIndex, ...pdfTextRect(item, run.start, run.end, viewportHeight), source: "pdf" });
    }
  }
  for (const annotation of page.annotations) {
    if (annotation.type !== "text" || findTextMatches(annotation.text, needle, wholeWord).length === 0) continue;
    matches.push({
      pageIndex,
      xMm: annotation.xMm,
      yMm: annotation.yMm,
      widthMm: Math.max(2, annotation.widthMm),
      heightMm: Math.max(2, annotation.heightMm),
      source: "text",
    });
  }
  return matches;
}

async function pageMatches(page: DocumentPage, pageIndex: number, needle: string, wholeWord: boolean): Promise<SearchMatch[]> {
  if (!page.pdf) return pdfPageMatches(page, pageIndex, needle, 0, [], wholeWord);
  const pdf = await getPdfDocument(page.pdf.url);
  const pdfPage = await pdf.getPage(page.pdf.pageNumber);
  const viewport = pdfPage.getViewport({ scale: 1 });
  const content = await pdfPage.getTextContent();
  return pdfPageMatches(page, pageIndex, needle, viewport.height, content.items as PdfTextItem[], wholeWord);
}

/** Every text hit in the tab, in page order. PDF text and text-box annotations both count. */
export async function searchTab(
  tab: DocumentTab,
  query: string,
  options: { wholeWord?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<SearchMatch[]> {
  const needle = normalizeSearchText(query);
  if (!needle) return [];
  const wholeWord = Boolean(options.wholeWord);
  const matches: SearchMatch[] = [];
  const limit = Math.min(tab.pages.length, MAX_PAGES);
  for (let index = 0; index < limit; index += 1) {
    try {
      matches.push(...(await pageMatches(tab.pages[index], index, needle, wholeWord)));
    } catch {
      // A broken page must not stop the search.
    }
  }
  return matches;
}

interface SearchState {
  query: string;
  matches: SearchMatch[];
  current: number;
  searching: boolean;
  setQuery: (query: string) => void;
  setMatches: (matches: SearchMatch[]) => void;
  setCurrent: (index: number) => void;
  setSearching: (searching: boolean) => void;
}

export const useSearchStore = create<SearchState>((set) => ({
  query: "",
  matches: [],
  current: 0,
  searching: false,
  setQuery: (query) => set({ query }),
  setMatches: (matches) => set({ matches, current: 0 }),
  setCurrent: (current) => set({ current }),
  setSearching: (searching) => set({ searching }),
}));

import { create } from "zustand";
import type { MeasureUnit } from "./measure";

export type SnapshotDpi = 72 | 150 | 300;

export interface Prefs {
  /** A rapid scribble erases what is underneath instead of drawing ink. */
  scratchOut: boolean;
  measureUnit: MeasureUnit;
  snapshotDpi: SnapshotDpi;
  /** Finger touch draws; off means single-finger touch pans and stylus/mouse still draw. */
  touchDrawEnabled: boolean;
}

const PREFS_KEY = "pm.prefs";

const DEFAULTS: Prefs = { scratchOut: false, measureUnit: "mm", snapshotDpi: 150, touchDrawEnabled: false };

function readPrefs(): Prefs {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return DEFAULTS;
  try {
    return { ...DEFAULTS, ...(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PREFS_KEY) ?? "{}") as Partial<Prefs>) };
  } catch {
    return DEFAULTS;
  }
}

interface PrefState extends Prefs {
  set: (patch: Partial<Prefs>) => void;
  /** Replaces preferences from a workspace backup. */
  replace: (prefs: Partial<Prefs>) => void;
}

export const usePrefsStore = create<PrefState>((set, get) => ({
  ...readPrefs(),
  set: (patch) => {
    const next = { ...get(), ...patch };
    const value: Prefs = {
      scratchOut: next.scratchOut,
      measureUnit: next.measureUnit,
      snapshotDpi: next.snapshotDpi,
      touchDrawEnabled: next.touchDrawEnabled,
    };
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(value));
    set(patch);
  },
  replace: (prefs) => {
    const value: Prefs = { ...DEFAULTS, ...prefs };
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(value));
    set(value);
  },
}));

import type { LineStyleName, Preset, PresetFamily, ToolId, ToolProps } from "./types";

const LINE_STYLE_BY_CODE: Record<number, LineStyleName> = {
  0: "solid",
  1: "dash",
  2: "dot",
  3: "dash-dot",
  5: "dash-dot",
};

/** Which factory family (and graphical type) feeds a tool's preset drawer. */
export const PRESET_QUERY: Partial<Record<ToolId, { family: PresetFamily; typeCode?: number; note?: string }>> = {
  pen: { family: "pen" },
  marker: { family: "marker" },
  text: { family: "text" },
  formula: { family: "text", note: "Formula ink borrows the text colour and size." },
  line: { family: "graphical", typeCode: 0 },
  arrow: { family: "graphical", typeCode: 1 },
  rectangle: { family: "graphical", typeCode: 2 },
  ellipse: { family: "graphical", typeCode: 3 },
  polygon: { family: "graphical", typeCode: 2, note: "The factory set has no polygon preset; rectangle styles apply." },
  dimension: { family: "graphical", typeCode: 6 },
  stamp: { family: "stamp" },
};

export function presetsFor(tool: ToolId, all: Preset[]): Preset[] {
  const query = PRESET_QUERY[tool];
  if (!query) return [];
  return all.filter(
    (preset) =>
      preset.family === query.family &&
      (query.typeCode === undefined || preset.typeCode === query.typeCode),
  );
}

/** The preset's attributes as a patch on the unified ink property model. */
export function presetPatch(preset: Preset): Partial<ToolProps> {
  const props = preset.props;
  const patch: Partial<ToolProps> = {};
  const color = props.color?.hex ?? props.fontColor?.hex;
  if (color) patch.color = color;
  const thickness = props.thicknessMm ?? props.lineWidthMm;
  if (thickness !== undefined && thickness > 0) patch.thicknessMm = thickness;
  if (props.inkTransparency !== undefined) patch.opacity = 1 - props.inkTransparency / 100;
  if (props.lineStyle !== undefined) patch.lineStyle = LINE_STYLE_BY_CODE[props.lineStyle] ?? "solid";
  if (props.arrowHead !== undefined) patch.arrowHead = props.arrowHead;
  if (props.arrowTail !== undefined) patch.arrowTail = props.arrowTail;
  if (props.backColor !== undefined) patch.fill = props.backColor.transparent ? null : props.backColor.hex;
  if (props.fontSize !== undefined) patch.fontSize = props.fontSize;
  if (props.fontName !== undefined) patch.fontName = props.fontName;
  if (props.bold !== undefined) patch.bold = props.bold;
  if (props.italic !== undefined) patch.italic = props.italic;
  if (props.underline !== undefined) patch.underline = props.underline;
  if (props.strikeOut !== undefined) patch.strikeOut = props.strikeOut;
  if (props.pressureSensitive !== undefined) patch.pressureSensitive = props.pressureSensitive;
  return patch;
}

export const BASE_PROPS: ToolProps = {
  color: "#101114",
  thicknessMm: 1,
  opacity: 1,
  lineStyle: "solid",
  arrowHead: 0,
  arrowTail: 0,
  fill: null,
  fontSize: 12,
  fontName: "Arial",
  bold: false,
  italic: false,
  underline: false,
  strikeOut: false,
  textAlign: "left",
  dimensionUnit: "mm",
  pressureSensitive: true,
  smoothing: false,
  penStyle: "pro",
  pressureLevel: 55,
  tipStyle: "vertical",
  polygonSides: 6,
  straightHighlight: false,
};

export const ARROW_HEADS = [
  { value: 0, label: "None" },
  { value: 3, label: "Open" },
  { value: 5, label: "Filled" },
  { value: 7, label: "Hollow" },
  { value: 10, label: "Solid" },
];

/** The one palette every colour picker in the app shares. */
export const SWATCHES = ["#EF4444", "#2563EB", "#000000", "#10B981", "#8B5CF6", "#F97316", "#FACC15", "#EC4899", "#06B6D4"];

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { computeCanvasCropRect } from "./raster";

describe("canvas crop rect", () => {
  it("maps a region onto a portrait A4 canvas using the page height for Y", () => {
    const rect = computeCanvasCropRect({ x: 0, y: 0, width: 210, height: 297 }, 2100, 2970, 210, 297);
    expect(rect).toEqual({ sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: 2100, sh: 2970 });
  });

  it("samples the vertical axis by page height, not width", () => {
    const rect = computeCanvasCropRect({ x: 105, y: 148.5, width: 105, height: 148.5 }, 595, 842, 210, 297);
    expect(rect.sx).toBeCloseTo(297.5, 6);
    expect(rect.sy).toBeCloseTo(421, 6);
    expect(rect.sw).toBeCloseTo(297.5, 6);
    expect(rect.sh).toBeCloseTo(421, 6);
  });
});

FILE: src\lib\raster.ts (6.7 KB)

import { PX_PER_MM } from "./paper";
import type { BBox } from "./annotationTypes";

export interface RasterImage {
  dataUrl: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

function loadImage(src: string): Promise<HTMLImageElement> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const image = new Image();
    image.onload = () => resolve(image);
    image.onerror = () => reject(new Error("image could not be decoded"));
    image.src = src;
  });
}

/** Reads any browser-decodable image (including SVG) and normalises it to PNG. */
export async function readImageFile(file: File): Promise<RasterImage> {
  const source = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result));
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error("image could not be read"));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
  const image = await loadImage(source);
  const maxSide = 2400;
  const width = image.naturalWidth || 800;
  const height = image.naturalHeight || 800;
  const scale = Math.min(1, maxSide / Math.max(width, height));
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(width * scale));
  canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(height * scale));
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!context) throw new Error("canvas unavailable");
  context.drawImage(image, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  return { dataUrl: canvas.toDataURL("image/png"), width: canvas.width, height: canvas.height };
}

/** Renders an HTML fragment (used for KaTeX) to a PNG via an SVG foreignObject. */
export async function rasterizeHtml(html: string, widthPx: number, heightPx: number, pixelRatio = 2): Promise<RasterImage | null> {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return null;
  const width = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(widthPx));
  const height = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(heightPx));
  const scale = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, pixelRatio));
  const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${width}" height="${height}">
<foreignObject width="100%" height="100%">
<div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" style="width:${width}px;height:${height}px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#101114;background:transparent;">${html}</div>
</foreignObject></svg>`;
  try {
    const image = await loadImage(`data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encodeURIComponent(svg)}`);
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    canvas.width = Math.round(width * scale);
    canvas.height = Math.round(height * scale);
    const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!context) return null;
    context.drawImage(image, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    return { dataUrl: canvas.toDataURL("image/png"), width: canvas.width, height: canvas.height };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

const CSS_VARS = [
  "--paper",
  "--accent",
  "--accent-soft",
  "--fg",
  "--muted",
  "--border",
  "--grid-ink",
  "--glass-strong",
];

function resolvedVars(): Record<string, string> {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return {};
  const computed = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
  const map: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const name of CSS_VARS) {
    const value = computed.getPropertyValue(name).trim();
    if (value) map[name] = value;
  }
  return map;
}

function inlineVars(markup: string): string {
  const vars = resolvedVars();
  return markup.replace(/var\((--[a-z-]+?)(?:\s*,[^)]*)?\)/gi, (match, name: string) => vars[name] ?? match);
}

async function drawSvgElement(
  context: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  element: SVGSVGElement,
  region: BBox,
  widthPx: number,
  heightPx: number,
): Promise<void> {
  const clone = element.cloneNode(true) as SVGSVGElement;
  clone.setAttribute("xmlns", "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg");
  clone.setAttribute("viewBox", `${region.x} ${region.y} ${region.width} ${region.height}`);
  clone.setAttribute("width", String(widthPx));
  clone.setAttribute("height", String(heightPx));
  clone.setAttribute("preserveAspectRatio", "none");
  const markup = inlineVars(new XMLSerializer().serializeToString(clone));
  const image = await loadImage(`data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encodeURIComponent(markup)}`);
  context.drawImage(image, 0, 0, widthPx, heightPx);
}

/**
 * Maps a crop region in page millimetres onto a canvas that covers the whole
 * page. The vertical axis uses the page height, not the width.
 */
export function computeCanvasCropRect(
  region: BBox,
  canvasWidth: number,
  canvasHeight: number,
  pageWidthMm: number,
  pageHeightMm: number,
): { sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } {
  return {
    sx: (region.x / pageWidthMm) * canvasWidth,
    sy: (region.y / pageHeightMm) * canvasHeight,
    sw: (region.width / pageWidthMm) * canvasWidth,
    sh: (region.height / pageHeightMm) * canvasHeight,
  };
}

/**
 * Snapshots a page region (paper or PDF background + annotations) at a chosen
 * DPI. Returns null when there is no live page DOM, as in unit tests.
 */
export async function captureRegion(
  pageElement: HTMLElement | null,
  region: BBox,
  dpi = 150,
): Promise<RasterImage | null> {
  if (typeof document === "undefined" || !pageElement) return null;
  const pxPerMm = dpi / 25.4;
  const widthPx = Math.max(1, Math.round(region.width * pxPerMm));
  const heightPx = Math.max(1, Math.round(region.height * pxPerMm));
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = widthPx;
  canvas.height = heightPx;
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!context) return null;

  context.fillStyle = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue("--paper").trim() || "#fffdf8";
  context.fillRect(0, 0, widthPx, heightPx);

  const paper = pageElement.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>('svg[role="img"]');
  if (paper) await drawSvgElement(context, paper, region, widthPx, heightPx);

  const pageWidthMm = (pageElement.clientWidth || 1) / PX_PER_MM;
  const pageHeightMm = (pageElement.clientHeight || 1) / PX_PER_MM;
  for (const source of pageElement.querySelectorAll("canvas")) {
    const { sx, sy, sw, sh } = computeCanvasCropRect(region, source.width, source.height, pageWidthMm, pageHeightMm);
    try {
      context.drawImage(source, sx, sy, sw, sh, 0, 0, widthPx, heightPx);
    } catch {
      // A PDF canvas that has not painted yet simply stays transparent.
    }
  }

  const annotations = pageElement.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>("svg[data-annotation-layer]");
  if (annotations) {
    await drawSvgElement(context, annotations, region, widthPx, heightPx);
  }

  return { dataUrl: canvas.toDataURL("image/png"), width: widthPx, height: heightPx };
}

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { saveActiveDocument } from "./save";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";

describe("saveActiveDocument", () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  });

  it("writes in place through the remembered file handle", async () => {
    const writes: Uint8Array[] = [];
    const createWritable = vi.fn(async () => ({
      write: async (data: Uint8Array) => {
        writes.push(data);
      },
      close: async () => {},
    }));
    const picker = vi.fn(async () => ({ createWritable }));
    vi.stubGlobal("window", { showSaveFilePicker: picker });

    useDocStore.setState({
      activeId: "doc-save-test",
      tabs: [
        {
          id: "doc-save-test",
          title: "Save test",
          templateId: "paper-000",
          landscape: false,
          pageCount: 1,
          pages: [{ id: "page-save-test", templateId: "paper-000", rotation: 0, annotations: [] }],
        },
      ],
      page: 1,
    });

    await saveActiveDocument("flattened");
    await saveActiveDocument("flattened");

    expect(picker).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(createWritable).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(writes).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(writes[0].byteLength).toBeGreaterThan(100);
  });
});

FILE: src\lib\save.ts (4.4 KB)

import { downloadPdf, exportInputFromTab, exportPdf, type PersistenceMode } from "./pdfEngine";
import { useDocStore } from "../stores/useDocStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";

interface SaveFileHandle {
  createWritable: () => Promise<{ write: (data: Uint8Array) => Promise<void>; close: () => Promise<void> }>;
}

interface SaveFilePickerWindow {
  showSaveFilePicker?: (options: {
    suggestedName: string;
    types: { description: string; accept: Record<string, string[]> }[];
  }) => Promise<SaveFileHandle>;
}

function fileName(title: string, mode: PersistenceMode): string {
  const clean = title.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]+/g, "-").trim() || "document";
  return `${clean}${mode === "flattened" ? "-flattened" : ""}.pdf`;
}

/** Reused by Save and Ctrl+S so a saved tab writes in place instead of reopening the picker. */
const saveHandles = new Map<string, SaveFileHandle>();

async function writeHandle(handle: SaveFileHandle, bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<void> {
  const writable = await handle.createWritable();
  await writable.write(bytes);
  await writable.close();
}

/** Native save dialog where available, otherwise a download. */
async function writeFile(bytes: Uint8Array, name: string, alwaysAsk = false, tabId?: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const cached = tabId && !alwaysAsk ? saveHandles.get(tabId) : undefined;
  if (cached) {
    try {
      await writeHandle(cached, bytes);
      return true;
    } catch {
      if (tabId) saveHandles.delete(tabId);
    }
  }
  const picker = (window as unknown as SaveFilePickerWindow).showSaveFilePicker;
  if (typeof picker === "function") {
    try {
      const handle = await picker({
        suggestedName: name,
        types: [{ description: "PDF document", accept: { "application/pdf": [".pdf"] } }],
      });
      await writeHandle(handle, bytes);
      if (tabId) saveHandles.set(tabId, handle);
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      if ((error as DOMException | undefined)?.name === "AbortError") return false;
      // An unavailable picker falls through to the download path.
    }
  }
  let target = name;
  if (alwaysAsk) {
    const answer = window.prompt("Save as", name);
    if (answer === null) return false;
    target = answer.trim() || name;
    if (!/\.pdf$/i.test(target)) target += ".pdf";
  }
  downloadPdf(bytes, target);
  return true;
}

/** One place for both Save buttons and Ctrl+S. */
export async function saveActiveDocument(mode: PersistenceMode, alwaysAsk = false): Promise<void> {
  const ui = useUIStore.getState();
  if (ui.saving) return;
  const state = useDocStore.getState();
  const tab = state.tabs.find((item) => item.id === state.activeId);
  if (!tab) return;
  ui.setSaving(true);
  ui.setToast(mode === "flattened" ? "Flattening PDF…" : "Preparing editable PDF…");
  try {
    const bytes = await exportPdf(exportInputFromTab(tab, mode));
    const name = fileName(tab.title, mode);
    const saved = await writeFile(bytes, name, alwaysAsk, tab.id);
    useUIStore.getState().setToast(saved ? `Saved ${name}` : "Save cancelled");
  } catch (error) {
    useUIStore
      .getState()
      .setToast(`Could not save: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  } finally {
    useUIStore.getState().setSaving(false);
  }
}

/** Prints every page by handing the flattened export to the browser. */
export async function printActiveDocument(): Promise<void> {
  const ui = useUIStore.getState();
  const state = useDocStore.getState();
  const tab = state.tabs.find((item) => item.id === state.activeId);
  if (!tab || ui.saving) return;
  ui.setSaving(true);
  try {
    const bytes = await exportPdf(exportInputFromTab(tab, "flattened"));
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([new Uint8Array(bytes)], { type: "application/pdf" }));
    const frame = document.createElement("iframe");
    frame.style.cssText = "position:fixed;right:0;bottom:0;width:0;height:0;border:0;";
    frame.src = url;
    frame.onload = () => {
      frame.contentWindow?.print();
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        frame.remove();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      }, 60000);
    };
    document.body.appendChild(frame);
  } catch (error) {
    ui.setToast(`Could not print: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  } finally {
    ui.setSaving(false);
  }
}
