import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS,
  SHORTCUT_ACTIONS,
  bindingFromEvent,
  detectConflicts,
  normalizeBinding,
  useShortcutStore,
  type ShortcutAction,
} from "./shortcuts";

describe("shortcuts", () => {
  it("normalises modifier order and key case", () => {
    expect(normalizeBinding("shift+ctrl+s")).toBe("Ctrl+Shift+S");
    expect(normalizeBinding("meta+K")).toBe("Ctrl+K");
    expect(normalizeBinding("ctrl+,")).toBe("Ctrl+,");
    expect(normalizeBinding(" 0 ")).toBe("0");
  });

  it("reads a binding from a keyboard event and ignores bare modifiers", () => {
    expect(bindingFromEvent({ key: "s", ctrlKey: true, metaKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: true })).toBe("Ctrl+Shift+S");
    expect(bindingFromEvent({ key: "t", ctrlKey: false, metaKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false })).toBe("T");
    expect(bindingFromEvent({ key: "Shift", ctrlKey: false, metaKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: true })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("resolves the physical key on a Persian keyboard layout", () => {
    expect(bindingFromEvent({ key: "ر", code: "KeyV", ctrlKey: true, metaKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false })).toBe("Ctrl+V");
    expect(bindingFromEvent({ key: "ح", code: "KeyP", ctrlKey: false, metaKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false })).toBe("P");
    expect(bindingFromEvent({ key: "ظ", code: "KeyZ", ctrlKey: true, metaKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false })).toBe("Ctrl+Z");
    expect(bindingFromEvent({ key: "۱", code: "Digit1", ctrlKey: true, metaKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false })).toBe("Ctrl+1");
    expect(bindingFromEvent({ key: "،", code: "Comma", ctrlKey: true, metaKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false })).toBe("Ctrl+,");
  });

  it("defaults are conflict free", () => {
    const { conflicting, byBinding } = detectConflicts(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS);
    expect(conflicting.size).toBe(0);
    expect(byBinding.size).toBe(SHORTCUT_ACTIONS.length);
  });

  it("flags both actions when a binding is claimed twice", () => {
    const map: Record<ShortcutAction, string> = { ...DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS, pen: "T" };
    const { conflicting } = detectConflicts(map);
    expect(conflicting.has("pen")).toBe(true);
    expect(conflicting.has("text")).toBe(true);
    expect(conflicting.has("select")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("exposes the clipboard and grouping actions", () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS.copy).toBe("Ctrl+C");
    expect(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS["select-all"]).toBe("Ctrl+A");
    expect(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS.ungroup).toBe("Ctrl+Shift+G");
  });

  it("moves an action when its binding is changed", () => {
    useShortcutStore.getState().bind("copy", "Ctrl+Shift+C");
    const resolved = useShortcutStore.getState().resolve();
    expect(resolved.get("Ctrl+Shift+C")).toBe("copy");
    expect(resolved.get("Ctrl+C")).toBeUndefined();
    useShortcutStore.getState().reset("copy");
    expect(useShortcutStore.getState().resolve().get("Ctrl+C")).toBe("copy");
  });
});

import { create } from "zustand";
import { TOOLS } from "./tools";
import type { ToolId } from "./types";

export type ShortcutAction =
  | ToolId
  | "settings"
  | "palette"
  | "save"
  | "open"
  | "print"
  | "undo"
  | "redo"
  | "cut"
  | "copy"
  | "paste"
  | "duplicate"
  | "select-all"
  | "group"
  | "ungroup"
  | "presentation"
  | "fit-page"
  | "search"
  | "view-one-page"
  | "view-one-continuous"
  | "view-two-continuous"
  | "view-two-page"
  | "toggle-cover-page";

export const SHORTCUT_LABELS: Record<ShortcutAction, string> = {
  ...(Object.fromEntries(TOOLS.map((tool) => [tool.id, tool.name])) as Record<ToolId, string>),
  settings: "Open settings",
  palette: "Command palette",
  save: "Save editable PDF",
  open: "Open PDF",
  print: "Print page",
  undo: "Undo",
  redo: "Redo",
  cut: "Cut selection",
  copy: "Copy selection",
  paste: "Paste",
  duplicate: "Duplicate selection",
  "select-all": "Select all annotations",
  group: "Group selection",
  ungroup: "Ungroup selection",
  presentation: "Presentation mode",
  "fit-page": "Fit page",
  search: "Search document",
  "view-one-page": "One page",
  "view-one-continuous": "One page continuous",
  "view-two-continuous": "Two pages continuous",
  "view-two-page": "Two pages",
  "toggle-cover-page": "Separate cover page",
};

/** `⇧S` from the migration table becomes a real binding. */
function fromToolShortcut(shortcut: string): string {
  return shortcut.startsWith("⇧") ? `Shift+${shortcut.slice(1)}` : shortcut;
}

export const DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS: Record<ShortcutAction, string> = {
  ...(Object.fromEntries(TOOLS.map((tool) => [tool.id, fromToolShortcut(tool.shortcut)])) as Record<ToolId, string>),
  settings: "Ctrl+,",
  palette: "Ctrl+K",
  save: "Ctrl+S",
  open: "Ctrl+O",
  print: "Ctrl+P",
  undo: "Ctrl+Z",
  redo: "Ctrl+Y",
  cut: "Ctrl+X",
  copy: "Ctrl+C",
  paste: "Ctrl+V",
  duplicate: "Ctrl+D",
  "select-all": "Ctrl+A",
  group: "Ctrl+G",
  ungroup: "Ctrl+Shift+G",
  presentation: "Ctrl+Shift+P",
  "fit-page": "0",
  search: "Ctrl+F",
  "view-one-page": "Ctrl+1",
  "view-one-continuous": "Ctrl+2",
  "view-two-continuous": "Ctrl+3",
  "view-two-page": "Ctrl+4",
  "toggle-cover-page": "Ctrl+7",
};

export const SHORTCUT_ACTIONS = Object.keys(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS) as ShortcutAction[];

/** Canonical order and spelling: Ctrl, Alt, Shift, then the key. */
export function normalizeBinding(binding: string): string {
  const parts = binding
    .split("+")
    .map((part) => part.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  const key = parts.pop() ?? "";
  const mods = new Set(parts.map((part) => part.toLowerCase()));
  const ordered: string[] = [];
  if (mods.has("ctrl") || mods.has("control") || mods.has("meta") || mods.has("cmd") || mods.has("⌘")) ordered.push("Ctrl");
  if (mods.has("alt") || mods.has("option")) ordered.push("Alt");
  if (mods.has("shift") || mods.has("⇧")) ordered.push("Shift");
  const normalizedKey = key.length === 1 ? key.toUpperCase() : key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
  ordered.push(normalizedKey);
  return ordered.join("+");
}

/** Physical key from `event.code`, so a Persian or other non-Latin layout still matches. */
function physicalKey(code: string | undefined): string | null {
  if (!code) return null;
  const letter = /^Key([A-Z])$/.exec(code);
  if (letter) return letter[1];
  const digit = /^Digit([0-9])$/.exec(code);
  if (digit) return digit[1];
  if (code === "Comma") return ",";
  if (code === "Minus" || code === "NumpadSubtract") return "-";
  if (code === "Equal" || code === "NumpadAdd") return "+";
  return null;
}

export function bindingFromEvent(event: {
  key: string;
  code?: string;
  ctrlKey: boolean;
  metaKey: boolean;
  altKey: boolean;
  shiftKey: boolean;
}): string | null {
  const key = physicalKey(event.code) ?? event.key;
  if (!key || ["Control", "Meta", "Alt", "Shift", "CapsLock", "Dead"].includes(key)) return null;
  const mods: string[] = [];
  if (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) mods.push("Ctrl");
  if (event.altKey) mods.push("Alt");
  if (event.shiftKey) mods.push("Shift");
  const printable = key.length === 1 ? key.toUpperCase() : key;
  return [...mods, printable].join("+");
}

export interface ConflictMap {
  /** Binding -> every action that claims it, in table order. */
  byBinding: Map<string, ShortcutAction[]>;
  /** Actions whose binding is shared with at least one other action. */
  conflicting: Set<ShortcutAction>;
}

export function detectConflicts(map: Record<ShortcutAction, string>): ConflictMap {
  const byBinding = new Map<string, ShortcutAction[]>();
  for (const action of SHORTCUT_ACTIONS) {
    const raw = map[action]?.trim();
    if (!raw) continue;
    const binding = normalizeBinding(raw);
    byBinding.set(binding, [...(byBinding.get(binding) ?? []), action]);
  }
  const conflicting = new Set<ShortcutAction>();
  for (const actions of byBinding.values()) {
    if (actions.length > 1) actions.forEach((action) => conflicting.add(action));
  }
  return { byBinding, conflicting };
}

const STORE_KEY = "pm.shortcuts";

function readShortcuts(): Record<ShortcutAction, string> {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return { ...DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS };
  try {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY) ?? "{}") as Partial<Record<ShortcutAction, string>>;
    return { ...DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS, ...saved };
  } catch {
    return { ...DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS };
  }
}

interface ShortcutState {
  map: Record<ShortcutAction, string>;
  bind: (action: ShortcutAction, binding: string) => void;
  reset: (action?: ShortcutAction) => void;
  /** Replaces the whole table from a workspace backup, filling gaps with defaults. */
  replace: (map: Partial<Record<ShortcutAction, string>>) => void;
  conflicts: () => ConflictMap;
  resolve: () => Map<string, ShortcutAction>;
}

export const useShortcutStore = create<ShortcutState>((set, get) => ({
  map: readShortcuts(),
  bind: (action, binding) => {
    const map = { ...get().map, [action]: normalizeBinding(binding) };
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
    set({ map });
  },
  reset: (action) => {
    const map = action ? { ...get().map, [action]: DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS[action] } : { ...DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS };
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
    set({ map });
  },
  replace: (incoming) => {
    const map = { ...DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS };
    for (const action of SHORTCUT_ACTIONS) {
      const binding = incoming[action];
      if (typeof binding === "string" && binding.trim()) map[action] = normalizeBinding(binding);
    }
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
    set({ map });
  },
  conflicts: () => detectConflicts(get().map),
  resolve: () => {
    const resolved = new Map<string, ShortcutAction>();
    for (const action of SHORTCUT_ACTIONS) {
      const binding = get().map[action]?.trim();
      // A conflict keeps the first action in table order and ignores the later one.
      if (binding && !resolved.has(normalizeBinding(binding))) resolved.set(normalizeBinding(binding), action);
    }
    return resolved;
  },
}));

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS } from "./shortcuts";
import {
  applyWorkspaceStores,
  collectWorkspaceStores,
  parseWorkspaceBackup,
  workspaceBackup,
  type WorkspaceStores,
} from "./storage";
import type { DocumentTab } from "./types";

const tabs: DocumentTab[] = [
  {
    id: "doc-a",
    title: "A4 Blank",
    templateId: "paper-000",
    landscape: false,
    pageCount: 1,
    pages: [{ id: "page-a", templateId: "paper-000", rotation: 0, annotations: [] }],
  },
];

const stores: WorkspaceStores = {
  customStamps: [
    {
      id: "custom-1",
      name: "Selection 10:00",
      imageUrl: "data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgo=",
      width: 240,
      height: 120,
      createdAt: "2026-01-01T10:00:00.000Z",
    },
  ],
  customScales: [{ id: "scale-2.5", label: "1:2.5", denominator: 2.5 }],
  activeScaleId: "scale-2.5",
  shortcuts: { ...DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS, pen: "Ctrl+Shift+9" },
  prefs: { scratchOut: false, measureUnit: "in", snapshotDpi: 300, touchDrawEnabled: false },
  theme: "sepia",
};

describe("workspace backup", () => {
  it("round-trips tabs and every preference store", async () => {
    const text = await workspaceBackup(tabs, "doc-a", stores).text();
    const parsed = parseWorkspaceBackup(text);

    expect(parsed?.version).toBe(2);
    expect(parsed?.activeId).toBe("doc-a");
    expect(parsed?.tabs).toEqual(tabs);
    expect(parsed?.stores).toEqual(stores);
  });

  it("restores the stores from a backup and reads them back", () => {
    applyWorkspaceStores(stores);
    const restored = collectWorkspaceStores();

    expect(restored.customStamps).toEqual(stores.customStamps);
    expect(restored.customScales).toEqual(stores.customScales);
    expect(restored.activeScaleId).toBe("scale-2.5");
    expect(restored.shortcuts.pen).toBe("Ctrl+Shift+9");
    expect(restored.prefs).toEqual(stores.prefs);
    expect(restored.theme).toBe("sepia");
  });

  it("still accepts a version 1 file without stores", () => {
    const legacy = JSON.stringify({ version: 1, savedAt: "2025-01-01", activeId: "doc-a", tabs });
    const parsed = parseWorkspaceBackup(legacy);
    expect(parsed?.tabs).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(parsed?.stores).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("rejects malformed or empty backups", () => {
    expect(parseWorkspaceBackup("not json")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseWorkspaceBackup(JSON.stringify({ version: 2, tabs: [] }))).toBeNull();
    expect(parseWorkspaceBackup(JSON.stringify({ version: 3, tabs }))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("ignores absent stores on import", () => {
    expect(() => applyWorkspaceStores(undefined)).not.toThrow();
  });

  it("round-trips PDF bytes as base64", async () => {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array([37, 80, 68, 70, 45, 49]);
    const withPdf: DocumentTab[] = [
      { ...tabs[0], id: "doc-pdf", pdf: { url: "", name: "source.pdf", pageCount: 1, bookmarks: [], bytes } },
    ];
    const text = await workspaceBackup(withPdf, "doc-pdf", stores).text();

    expect(text).toContain("bytesBase64");
    const parsed = parseWorkspaceBackup(text);
    expect(parsed?.tabs[0].pdf?.bytes).toEqual(bytes);
  });

  it("reads bytes from a legacy numeric-key object", () => {
    const legacy = JSON.stringify({
      version: 2,
      savedAt: "2025-01-01",
      activeId: "doc-pdf",
      tabs: [
        {
          ...tabs[0],
          id: "doc-pdf",
          pdf: { url: "", name: "source.pdf", pageCount: 1, bookmarks: [], bytes: { 0: 37, 1: 80, 2: 68 } },
        },
      ],
    });

    expect(parseWorkspaceBackup(legacy)?.tabs[0].pdf?.bytes).toEqual(new Uint8Array([37, 80, 68]));
  });
});

import type { DocumentTab, ThemeName } from "./types";
import { useCustomStampStore, type CustomStamp } from "./customStamps";
import { useScaleStore, type MeasurementScale } from "./measure";
import { useShortcutStore, type ShortcutAction } from "./shortcuts";
import { usePrefsStore, type Prefs } from "./prefs";
import { useFavoritesStore, type FavoriteTool } from "../stores/useFavoritesStore";
import { useUIStore } from "../stores/useUIStore";

/** Everything a `.pmbackup.json` carries besides tabs, pages and annotations. */
export interface WorkspaceStores {
  customStamps: CustomStamp[];
  customScales: MeasurementScale[];
  activeScaleId: string;
  shortcuts: Record<ShortcutAction, string>;
  prefs: Prefs;
  theme: ThemeName;
  /** Absent in backups written before the favourite tools dock. */
  favorites?: FavoriteTool[];
}

export interface SavedWorkspace {
  version: 1 | 2;
  savedAt: string;
  activeId: string;
  tabs: DocumentTab[];
  stores?: WorkspaceStores;
}

/** Version 1 files (before stores were included) still import. */
const DB_NAME = "pdf-master";
const STORE_NAME = "workspace";
const BLOB_STORE = "pdf-blobs";
const KEY = "current";
const THEMES: ThemeName[] = ["light", "dark", "sepia"];

export interface SavedWorkspaceRecord {
  version: 1 | 2;
  savedAt: string;
  activeId: string;
  tabs: DocumentTab[];
}

function openDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase | null> {
  if (typeof indexedDB === "undefined") return Promise.resolve(null);
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, 2);
    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      const db = request.result;
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) db.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME);
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(BLOB_STORE)) db.createObjectStore(BLOB_STORE);
    };
    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
    request.onerror = () => resolve(null);
  });
}

/**
 * Structural data and PDF bytes live in separate stores: re-saving after an
 * annotation edit no longer rewrites every page of every document.
 */
const savedBlobs = new Map<string, Uint8Array>();

/** PDF bytes cannot survive `JSON.stringify`, so a backup carries base64 text. */
function bytesToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let binary = "";
  const chunk = 0x8000;
  for (let index = 0; index < bytes.length; index += chunk) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(index, index + chunk));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

function base64ToBytes(text: string): Uint8Array {
  const binary = atob(text);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let index = 0; index < binary.length; index += 1) bytes[index] = binary.charCodeAt(index);
  return bytes;
}

/** Frees a closed tab's blob and any orphan key no open tab owns. */
export async function deleteWorkspaceBlob(tabId: string, keepIds?: string[]): Promise<void> {
  savedBlobs.delete(tabId);
  const db = await openDb();
  if (!db) return;
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(BLOB_STORE, "readwrite");
    const store = transaction.objectStore(BLOB_STORE);
    store.delete(tabId);
    if (keepIds) {
      const keys = store.getAllKeys();
      keys.onsuccess = () => {
        for (const key of keys.result) {
          if (typeof key === "string" && !keepIds.includes(key)) {
            store.delete(key);
            savedBlobs.delete(key);
          }
        }
      };
    }
    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => resolve();
    transaction.onabort = () => resolve();
  });
  db.close();
}

export async function saveWorkspace(tabs: DocumentTab[], activeId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const db = await openDb();
  if (!db) return false;
  const blobs = new Map<string, Uint8Array>();
  const stripped = tabs.map((tab) => {
    if (!tab.pdf?.bytes) return tab;
    blobs.set(tab.id, tab.pdf.bytes);
    return { ...tab, pdf: { ...tab.pdf, bytes: undefined } };
  });
  const ok = await new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME, BLOB_STORE], "readwrite");
    transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME).put(
      { version: 2, savedAt: new Date().toISOString(), activeId, tabs: stripped } satisfies SavedWorkspaceRecord,
      KEY,
    );
    const blobStore = transaction.objectStore(BLOB_STORE);
    for (const [id, bytes] of blobs) {
      if (savedBlobs.get(id) === bytes) continue;
      blobStore.put(bytes, id);
    }
    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve(true);
    transaction.onerror = () => resolve(false);
    transaction.onabort = () => resolve(false);
  });
  db.close();
  if (ok) for (const [id, bytes] of blobs) savedBlobs.set(id, bytes);
  return ok;
}

export async function loadWorkspace(): Promise<SavedWorkspaceRecord | null> {
  const db = await openDb();
  if (!db) return null;
  const blobs = new Map<string, Uint8Array>();
  const saved = await new Promise<SavedWorkspaceRecord | null>((resolve) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME, BLOB_STORE], "readonly");
    const blobStore = transaction.objectStore(BLOB_STORE);
    let record: SavedWorkspaceRecord | null = null;
    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve(record);
    transaction.onabort = () => resolve(null);
    const request = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME).get(KEY);
    request.onsuccess = () => {
      record = (request.result as SavedWorkspaceRecord | undefined) ?? null;
      if (!record) return;
      for (const tab of record.tabs) {
        if (!tab.pdf || tab.pdf.bytes) continue;
        const blobRequest = blobStore.get(tab.id);
        blobRequest.onsuccess = () => {
          if (blobRequest.result instanceof Uint8Array) blobs.set(tab.id, blobRequest.result);
        };
      }
    };
  });
  db.close();
  if (!saved || !Array.isArray(saved.tabs) || saved.tabs.length === 0) return null;
  saved.tabs = saved.tabs.map((tab) => {
    const bytes = blobs.get(tab.id) ?? tab.pdf?.bytes;
    if (!bytes) return tab;
    return {
      ...tab,
      pdf: {
        url: tab.pdf?.url ?? "",
        name: tab.pdf?.name ?? tab.title,
        pageCount: tab.pdf?.pageCount ?? tab.pages.length,
        bookmarks: tab.pdf?.bookmarks ?? [],
        bytes,
      },
    };
  });
  return saved;
}

/** Browser-quota figures for the autosave store; null where the API is missing. */
export async function storageEstimate(): Promise<{ usage: number; quota: number } | null> {
  if (typeof navigator === "undefined" || !navigator.storage?.estimate) return null;
  const estimate = await navigator.storage.estimate();
  return { usage: estimate.usage ?? 0, quota: estimate.quota ?? 0 };
}

/** Reads the localStorage-backed stores a backup must keep alongside the document. */
export function collectWorkspaceStores(): WorkspaceStores {
  const prefs = usePrefsStore.getState();
  return {
    customStamps: useCustomStampStore.getState().stamps,
    customScales: useScaleStore.getState().custom,
    activeScaleId: useScaleStore.getState().activeId,
    shortcuts: useShortcutStore.getState().map,
    prefs: {
      scratchOut: prefs.scratchOut,
      measureUnit: prefs.measureUnit,
      snapshotDpi: prefs.snapshotDpi,
      touchDrawEnabled: prefs.touchDrawEnabled,
    },
    theme: useUIStore.getState().theme,
    favorites: useFavoritesStore.getState().favorites,
  };
}

/** Restores the stores from a backup file, leaving absent sections untouched. */
export function applyWorkspaceStores(stores: WorkspaceStores | undefined): void {
  if (!stores) return;
  if (Array.isArray(stores.customStamps)) useCustomStampStore.getState().restore(stores.customStamps);
  if (Array.isArray(stores.customScales)) {
    useScaleStore.getState().restore(stores.customScales, stores.activeScaleId);
  }
  if (stores.shortcuts) useShortcutStore.getState().replace(stores.shortcuts);
  if (stores.prefs) usePrefsStore.getState().replace(stores.prefs);
  if (stores.theme && THEMES.includes(stores.theme)) useUIStore.getState().setTheme(stores.theme);
  if (Array.isArray(stores.favorites)) useFavoritesStore.getState().restore(stores.favorites);
}

export function workspaceBackup(
  tabs: DocumentTab[],
  activeId: string,
  stores: WorkspaceStores = collectWorkspaceStores(),
): Blob {
  const serialized = tabs.map((tab) => {
    if (!tab.pdf?.bytes) return tab;
    const { bytes, ...pdf } = tab.pdf;
    return { ...tab, pdf: { ...pdf, bytesBase64: bytesToBase64(bytes) } };
  });
  const payload: SavedWorkspace = {
    version: 2,
    savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    activeId,
    tabs: serialized as DocumentTab[],
    stores,
  };
  return new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload)], { type: "application/json" });
}

/** A `.pmbackup` JSON file, checked before it is handed to the store. */
export function parseWorkspaceBackup(text: string): SavedWorkspace | null {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(text) as Partial<SavedWorkspace> & { version?: number };
    if ((parsed?.version !== 1 && parsed?.version !== 2) || !Array.isArray(parsed.tabs) || parsed.tabs.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }
    const tabs = parsed.tabs.map((tab) => {
      const pdf = tab.pdf as
        | (NonNullable<DocumentTab["pdf"]> & { bytesBase64?: string; bytes?: unknown })
        | undefined;
      if (!pdf) return tab;
      const { bytesBase64, bytes, ...rest } = pdf;
      let decoded: Uint8Array | undefined;
      if (typeof bytesBase64 === "string" && bytesBase64) {
        try {
          decoded = base64ToBytes(bytesBase64);
        } catch {
          decoded = undefined;
        }
      } else if (bytes instanceof Uint8Array) {
        decoded = bytes;
      } else if (bytes && typeof bytes === "object") {
        // Legacy backups stringified the typed array into {"0":37,"1":80,...}.
        const values = Object.values(bytes as Record<string, number>);
        if (values.every((value) => typeof value === "number")) decoded = new Uint8Array(values);
      }
      return { ...tab, pdf: { ...rest, ...(decoded ? { bytes: decoded } : {}) } } as DocumentTab;
    });
    return { ...parsed, tabs } as SavedWorkspace;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { applyToolPatch, syncTargets } from "./sync";
import type { ToolId, ToolProps } from "./types";

const BASE: ToolProps = {
  color: "#000000",
  thicknessMm: 1,
  opacity: 1,
  lineStyle: "solid",
  arrowHead: 0,
  arrowTail: 0,
  fill: null,
  fontSize: 10,
  fontName: "Arial",
  bold: false,
  italic: false,
  underline: false,
  strikeOut: false,
  textAlign: "left",
  dimensionUnit: "mm",
  pressureSensitive: true,
  smoothing: false,
  penStyle: "pro",
  pressureLevel: 55,
};

const TOOLS: ToolId[] = [
  "hand", "select", "lasso", "pen", "marker", "text", "line", "arrow", "rectangle",
  "ellipse", "polygon", "dimension", "stamp", "eraser", "measure", "formula", "crop", "snapshot",
];

function all(): Record<ToolId, ToolProps> {
  return Object.fromEntries(TOOLS.map((tool) => [tool, { ...BASE }])) as Record<ToolId, ToolProps>;
}

describe("tool property synchronisation", () => {
  it("keeps every tool separate when sync is off", () => {
    const next = applyToolPatch(all(), "pen", { color: "#ff0000" }, false);
    expect(next.pen.color).toBe("#ff0000");
    expect(next.text.color).toBe("#000000");
    expect(next.arrow.color).toBe("#000000");
  });

  it("syncs colour and width from Pen, and never touches Marker", () => {
    const next = applyToolPatch(all(), "pen", { color: "#ff0000", thicknessMm: 2.4 }, true);
    for (const tool of ["pen", "text", "line", "arrow", "rectangle", "ellipse", "polygon", "dimension", "formula"] as ToolId[]) {
      expect(next[tool].color, tool).toBe("#ff0000");
      expect(next[tool].thicknessMm, tool).toBe(2.4);
    }
    expect(next.marker.color).toBe("#000000");
    expect(next.marker.thicknessMm).toBe(1);
  });

  it("does not broadcast changes made on Marker", () => {
    const next = applyToolPatch(all(), "marker", { color: "#ffff00", thicknessMm: 16 }, true);
    expect(next.marker.color).toBe("#ffff00");
    expect(next.pen.color).toBe("#000000");
    expect(next.ellipse.thicknessMm).toBe(1);
  });

  it("syncs line style only across line and arrow", () => {
    expect(syncTargets("lineStyle", "line", true)).toEqual(["line", "arrow"]);
    const next = applyToolPatch(all(), "line", { lineStyle: "dash" }, true);
    expect(next.line.lineStyle).toBe("dash");
    expect(next.arrow.lineStyle).toBe("dash");
    expect(next.rectangle.lineStyle).toBe("solid");
    expect(next.pen.lineStyle).toBe("solid");
  });

  it("syncs fill and opacity only across the area shapes", () => {
    const next = applyToolPatch(all(), "rectangle", { fill: "#00ff00", opacity: 0.5 }, true);
    for (const tool of ["rectangle", "ellipse", "polygon"] as ToolId[]) {
      expect(next[tool].fill, tool).toBe("#00ff00");
      expect(next[tool].opacity, tool).toBe(0.5);
    }
    expect(next.line.fill).toBeNull();
    expect(next.pen.opacity).toBe(1);
  });

  it("leaves fields outside the contract alone", () => {
    const next = applyToolPatch(all(), "text", { fontSize: 18 }, true);
    expect(next.text.fontSize).toBe(18);
    expect(next.pen.fontSize).toBe(10);
  });
});

FILE: src\lib\sync.ts (1.7 KB)
================================================================================
import type { ToolId, ToolProps } from "./types";

/**
 * Separate vs. synchronous tool properties, as shipped by PDF Annotator:
 * Color and LineWidth sync across Pen, Text, Line, Arrow, Rectangle, Ellipse, Polygon,
 * Dimension, Formula; LineStyle across Line and Arrow; FillColor and Opacity across the
 * area shapes. Marker is never synchronised, in either direction.
 */
export const SYNC_TARGETS: Partial<Record<keyof ToolProps, ToolId[]>> = {
  color: ["pen", "text", "line", "arrow", "rectangle", "ellipse", "polygon", "dimension", "formula"],
  thicknessMm: ["pen", "text", "line", "arrow", "rectangle", "ellipse", "polygon", "dimension", "formula"],
  lineStyle: ["line", "arrow"],
  fill: ["rectangle", "ellipse", "polygon"],
  opacity: ["rectangle", "ellipse", "polygon"],
};

export const NEVER_SYNCED: ToolId[] = ["marker"];

export function syncTargets(field: keyof ToolProps, tool: ToolId, sync: boolean): ToolId[] {
  if (!sync || tool === "marker") return [tool];
  const group = SYNC_TARGETS[field];
  if (!group || !group.includes(tool)) return [tool];
  return group.filter((target) => !NEVER_SYNCED.includes(target));
}

/** Applies a property patch and returns a new per-tool property map. */
export function applyToolPatch(
  all: Record<ToolId, ToolProps>,
  tool: ToolId,
  patch: Partial<ToolProps>,
  sync: boolean,
): Record<ToolId, ToolProps> {
  const next = { ...all };
  for (const field of Object.keys(patch) as (keyof ToolProps)[]) {
    const value = patch[field];
    if (value === undefined) continue;
    for (const target of syncTargets(field, tool, sync)) {
      next[target] = { ...next[target], [field]: value } as ToolProps;
    }
  }
  return next;
}

FILE: src\lib\textLayout.ts (4.7 KB)

import type { TextAnnotation, TextLayout, TextLine } from "./annotationTypes";

export const PT_TO_MM = 25.4 / 72;
export const TEXT_PADDING_MM = 1.4;
const MIN_FONT_PT = 4;

const NARROW = new Set("ijl.,:;'`|!()[]{}ftrI ");
const WIDE = new Set("mwMW@%");

/**
 * Approximate glyph width in em units. The screen and the PDF share this
 * model so a wrapped line breaks in the same place in both.
 */
function glyphEm(char: string, family: string, bold: boolean): number {
  if (family === "Courier") return 0.6;
  if (char === " ") return 0.28;
  if (NARROW.has(char)) return 0.3;
  if (WIDE.has(char)) return family === "TimesRoman" ? 0.86 : 0.9;
  if (/[A-Z]/.test(char)) return family === "TimesRoman" ? 0.66 : 0.7;
  if (/[0-9]/.test(char)) return 0.56;
  const base = family === "TimesRoman" ? 0.46 : 0.53;
  return bold ? base * 1.04 : base;
}

export function measureTextWidthMm(text: string, family: string, bold: boolean, fontSizePt: number): number {
  let em = 0;
  for (const char of text) em += glyphEm(char, family, bold);
  return em * fontSizePt * PT_TO_MM;
}

/** CSS stack that matches the PDF standard families. */
export function textFontCss(family: string, bold: boolean, italic: boolean): string {
  const stack =
    family === "Courier"
      ? "'Courier New', Courier, monospace"
      : family === "TimesRoman"
        ? "'Times New Roman', Times, serif"
        : "Helvetica, Arial, 'Helvetica Neue', sans-serif";
  return `${italic ? "italic " : ""}${bold ? "700 " : "400 "}${stack}`;
}

function wrapParagraph(text: string, maxWidthMm: number, family: string, bold: boolean, fontSizePt: number): string[] {
  if (!text) return [""];
  const words = text.split(/(\s+)/);
  const lines: string[] = [];
  let current = "";
  const pushWord = (word: string) => {
    const candidate = current + word;
    if (current && measureTextWidthMm(candidate, family, bold, fontSizePt) > maxWidthMm) {
      lines.push(current.trimEnd());
      current = word.trimStart();
      // A single word longer than the box is broken by character.
      while (measureTextWidthMm(current, family, bold, fontSizePt) > maxWidthMm && current.length > 1) {
        let cut = current.length - 1;
        while (cut > 1 && measureTextWidthMm(current.slice(0, cut), family, bold, fontSizePt) > maxWidthMm) cut -= 1;
        lines.push(current.slice(0, cut));
        current = current.slice(cut);
      }
    } else {
      current = candidate;
    }
  };
  for (const word of words) pushWord(word);
  if (current.trim() || lines.length === 0) lines.push(current.trimEnd());
  return lines;
}

export interface TextLayoutInput {
  text: string;
  fontFamily: string;
  bold: boolean;
  fontSizePt: number;
  autoFit: boolean;
  widthMm: number;
  heightMm: number;
}

export function computeTextLayout(input: TextLayoutInput): TextLayout {
  const maxWidth = Math.max(1, input.widthMm - TEXT_PADDING_MM * 2);
  let fontSizePt = input.fontSizePt;
  let rawLines: string[] = [];

  for (;;) {
    rawLines = input.text.split("\n").flatMap((paragraph) =>
      wrapParagraph(paragraph, maxWidth, input.fontFamily, input.bold, fontSizePt),
    );
    const lineHeight = fontSizePt * 1.25 * PT_TO_MM;
    const fits =
      !input.autoFit ||
      rawLines.length * lineHeight <= Math.max(1, input.heightMm - TEXT_PADDING_MM * 2) ||
      fontSizePt <= MIN_FONT_PT;
    if (fits) break;
    fontSizePt = Math.max(MIN_FONT_PT, fontSizePt - 0.5);
  }

  const lineHeightMm = fontSizePt * 1.25 * PT_TO_MM;
  const lines: TextLine[] = rawLines.map((text, index) => ({ text, topMm: index * lineHeightMm }));
  const widthMm = rawLines.reduce(
    (widest, line) => Math.max(widest, measureTextWidthMm(line, input.fontFamily, input.bold, fontSizePt)),
    0,
  );
  return { lines, fontSizePt, lineHeightMm, widthMm };
}

export function layoutText(annotation: TextAnnotation): TextLayout {
  return computeTextLayout(annotation);
}

/** Ctrl+M: shrink the box to the text it actually holds. */
export function fitTextBox(annotation: TextAnnotation): { widthMm: number; heightMm: number } {
  const layout = computeTextLayout(annotation);
  return {
    widthMm: Math.max(6, layout.widthMm + TEXT_PADDING_MM * 2),
    heightMm: Math.max(4, layout.lines.length * layout.lineHeightMm + TEXT_PADDING_MM * 2),
  };
}

/**
 * A screen-space editor height that keeps the whole text visible while typing,
 * even when the stored box would clip it.
 */
export function editorHeightMm(annotation: TextAnnotation): number {
  const layout = computeTextLayout({ ...annotation, autoFit: false });
  return Math.max(annotation.heightMm, layout.lines.length * layout.lineHeightMm + TEXT_PADDING_MM * 2);
}

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { PenAnnotation, ShapeAnnotation, ShapeType } from "../lib/annotationTypes";
import { annotationBounds } from "../lib/annotationGeometry";
import { useAnnotationStore, selectionForAnnotation } from "./useAnnotationStore";
import { newPage, useDocStore } from "./useDocStore";
import type { DocumentTab } from "../lib/types";

function shape(id: string, x = 10, y = 10, shapeType: ShapeType = "rectangle"): ShapeAnnotation {
  return {
    id,
    type: "shape",
    shapeType,
    start: [x, y],
    end: [x + 20, y + 10],
    points: [],
    strokeColor: "#101114",
    fillColor: null,
    fillTransparent: true,
    lineWidthMm: 0.5,
    lineStyle: "solid",
    opacity: 1,
    arrowHead: 0,
    arrowTail: 0,
    dimensionUnit: "mm",
    dimensionScale: 1,
  };
}

function pen(id: string, y: number): PenAnnotation {
  return {
    id,
    type: "pen",
    points: [[10, y, 0.5], [30, y, 0.5]],
    color: "#101114",
    thicknessMm: 1,
    opacity: 1,
    sensitivity: true,
    smoothed: false,
  };
}

function setup(): DocumentTab {
  const tab: DocumentTab = {
    id: "doc-test",
    title: "Test",
    templateId: "paper-000",
    landscape: false,
    pageCount: 1,
    pages: [newPage("paper-000")],
  };
  useDocStore.setState({ tabs: [tab], activeId: tab.id, page: 1, history: {}, lastError: null });
  useAnnotationStore.setState({ selection: [], clipboard: [] });
  return tab;
}

function annotations() {
  const state = useDocStore.getState();
  const tab = state.tabs.find((item) => item.id === state.activeId);
  return tab?.pages[state.page - 1].annotations ?? [];
}

describe("annotation store", () => {
  beforeEach(() => setup());

  it("adds annotations and selects the new one", () => {
    useAnnotationStore.getState().add(shape("a1"));
    expect(annotations()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(useAnnotationStore.getState().selection).toEqual(["a1"]);
  });

  it("translates every selected annotation", () => {
    useAnnotationStore.getState().add(shape("a1", 10, 10));
    useAnnotationStore.getState().add(shape("a2", 40, 40));
    useAnnotationStore.getState().select(["a1", "a2"]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().nudge(2, -3);
    const [first, second] = annotations();
    expect(first.type === "shape" && first.start).toEqual([12, 7]);
    expect(second.type === "shape" && second.start).toEqual([42, 37]);
  });

  it("aligns and distributes a mixed selection", () => {
    useAnnotationStore.getState().addMany([shape("a1", 10, 10), shape("a2", 40, 30), shape("a3", 90, 60)]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().select(["a1", "a2", "a3"]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().align("left");
    const boxes = annotations().map((annotation) => annotationBounds(annotation));
    expect(new Set(boxes.map((box) => Number(box.x.toFixed(2)))).size).toBe(1);

    useAnnotationStore.getState().align("top");
    const tops = annotations().map((annotation) => annotationBounds(annotation).y);
    expect(new Set(tops.map((top) => Number(top.toFixed(2)))).size).toBe(1);

    useAnnotationStore.getState().select(["a1", "a2", "a3"]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().distribute("x");
    const ordered = annotations()
      .map((annotation) => annotationBounds(annotation))
      .sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
    const gap1 = ordered[1].x - (ordered[0].x + ordered[0].width);
    const gap2 = ordered[2].x - (ordered[1].x + ordered[1].width);
    expect(gap1).toBeCloseTo(gap2, 1);
  });

  it("groups, moves as a group, and ungroups", () => {
    useAnnotationStore.getState().addMany([shape("a1", 10, 10), shape("a2", 40, 40)]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().select(["a1", "a2"]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().groupSelection();
    const groupId = annotations()[0].groupId;
    expect(groupId).toBeTruthy();
    expect(annotations()[1].groupId).toBe(groupId);

    useAnnotationStore.getState().ungroupSelection();
    expect(annotations().every((annotation) => !annotation.groupId)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("undoes and redoes annotation changes per tab", () => {
    const store = useAnnotationStore.getState();
    store.add(shape("a1"));
    expect(annotations()).toHaveLength(1);
    useDocStore.getState().undo();
    expect(annotations()).toHaveLength(0);
    useDocStore.getState().redo();
    expect(annotations()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("scratch-out removes only annotations under the gesture box", () => {
    useAnnotationStore.getState().addMany([shape("near", 10, 10), shape("far", 120, 180)]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().scratchOut({ x: 8, y: 8, width: 8, height: 8 });
    expect(annotations().map((annotation) => annotation.id)).toEqual(["far"]);
  });

  it("split-erases ink and keeps the untouched runs", () => {
    const points = Array.from({ length: 17 }, (_, index) => [10 + index * 2.5, 10, 0.5] as [number, number, number]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().add({
      id: "ink",
      type: "pen",
      points,
      color: "#101114",
      thicknessMm: 1,
      opacity: 1,
      sensitivity: true,
      smoothed: false,
    });
    useAnnotationStore.getState().eraseAt([30, 10], true, 3);
    const runs = annotations();
    expect(runs.length).toBe(2);
    expect(runs.every((annotation) => annotation.type === "pen")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("records one history entry for a whole erase gesture", () => {
    useAnnotationStore.getState().addMany([pen("ink-1", 10), pen("ink-2", 20)]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().eraseAt([20, 10], false, 3, true);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().eraseAt([20, 20], false, 3, false);
    expect(annotations()).toHaveLength(0);

    useDocStore.getState().undo();
    expect(annotations().map((annotation) => annotation.id)).toEqual(["ink-1", "ink-2"]);
  });

  it("selects only the clicked annotation when it has no group", () => {
    useAnnotationStore.getState().addMany([shape("a1", 10, 10), shape("a2", 40, 10), shape("a3", 70, 10)]);
    const hit = annotations()[0];
    const ids = selectionForAnnotation(annotations(), hit);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().select(ids);
    expect(ids).toEqual(["a1"]);
    expect(useAnnotationStore.getState().selection).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(useAnnotationStore.getState().selection).not.toContain("a2");
  });

  it("gives duplicated groups a fresh group id", () => {
    useAnnotationStore.getState().addMany([shape("a1"), shape("a2")]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().select(["a1", "a2"]);
    useAnnotationStore.getState().groupSelection();
    useAnnotationStore.getState().duplicateSelection();

    const all = annotations();
    expect(all).toHaveLength(4);
    const original = all[0].groupId;
    const copies = all.slice(2);
    expect(original).toBeTruthy();
    expect(copies[0].groupId).toBeTruthy();
    expect(copies[0].groupId).toBe(copies[1].groupId);
    expect(copies[0].groupId).not.toBe(original);
  });
});

================================================================================
FILE: src\stores\useAnnotationStore.ts (13.7 KB)

import { create } from "zustand";
import type {
  Annotation,
  AnnotationPatch,
  AlignMode,
  BBox,
  MarkerAnnotation,
  PenAnnotation,
  Pt,
} from "../lib/annotationTypes";
import {
  annotationBounds,
  boundsIntersect,
  flipAnnotation,
  hitTest,
  rotateAnnotation,
  scaleAnnotation,
  selectionBounds,
  subtractEraser,
  translateAnnotation,
} from "../lib/annotationGeometry";
import type { DocumentPage } from "../lib/types";
import { makeId, useDocStore } from "./useDocStore";

export function cloneAnnotations(annotations: Annotation[]): Annotation[] {
  return structuredClone(annotations);
}

/**
 * Ids a click should select. Only a real group id expands to its siblings;
 * ungrouped annotations share `undefined`, which must never mean "all".
 */
export function selectionForAnnotation(annotations: Annotation[], hit: Annotation): string[] {
  const groupId = typeof hit.groupId === "string" && hit.groupId.length > 0 ? hit.groupId : null;
  if (!groupId) return [hit.id];
  return annotations.filter((annotation) => annotation.groupId === groupId).map((annotation) => annotation.id);
}

interface ActiveContext {
  tabId: string;
  pageIndex: number;
  page: DocumentPage;
  pages: DocumentPage[];
}

function activeContext(): ActiveContext | null {
  const state = useDocStore.getState();
  const tab = state.tabs.find((item) => item.id === state.activeId);
  const pageIndex = state.page - 1;
  const page = tab?.pages[pageIndex];
  if (!tab || !page) return null;
  return { tabId: tab.id, pageIndex, page, pages: tab.pages };
}

function replacePage(ctx: ActiveContext, page: DocumentPage): DocumentPage[] {
  return ctx.pages.map((item, index) => (index === ctx.pageIndex ? page : item));
}

interface AnnotationState {
  selection: string[];
  clipboard: Annotation[];
  select: (ids: string[]) => void;
  toggle: (id: string) => void;
  clearSelection: () => void;
  add: (annotation: Annotation) => void;
  addMany: (annotations: Annotation[]) => void;
  update: (ids: string[], patch: AnnotationPatch) => void;
  transform: (fn: (annotation: Annotation) => Annotation) => void;
  mutate: (fn: (annotations: Annotation[]) => Annotation[], history?: boolean) => void;
  remove: (ids: string[]) => void;
  deleteSelection: () => void;
  duplicateSelection: () => void;
  copySelection: () => void;
  cutSelection: () => void;
  paste: () => void;
  groupSelection: () => void;
  ungroupSelection: () => void;
  setLocked: (locked: boolean) => void;
  bringToFront: () => void;
  sendToBack: () => void;
  align: (mode: AlignMode) => void;
  distribute: (axis: "x" | "y") => void;
  nudge: (dxMm: number, dyMm: number) => void;
  eraseAt: (point: Pt, split: boolean, radiusMm?: number, recordHistory?: boolean) => boolean;
  scratchOut: (box: BBox) => void;
}

function selectedAnnotations(state: AnnotationState, ctx: ActiveContext): Annotation[] {
  const wanted = new Set(state.selection);
  return ctx.page.annotations.filter((annotation) => wanted.has(annotation.id));
}

function withHistory(ctx: ActiveContext): void {
  useDocStore.getState().pushHistory(ctx.tabId);
}

export const useAnnotationStore = create<AnnotationState>((set, get) => ({
  selection: [],
  clipboard: [],

  select: (selection) => set({ selection }),
  toggle: (id) =>
    set((state) => ({
      selection: state.selection.includes(id) ? state.selection.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...state.selection, id],
    })),
  clearSelection: () => set({ selection: [] }),

  add: (annotation) => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    withHistory(ctx);
    const pages = replacePage(ctx, { ...ctx.page, annotations: [...ctx.page.annotations, annotation] });
    useDocStore.getState().setPages(ctx.tabId, pages);
    set({ selection: [annotation.id] });
  },
  addMany: (annotations) => {
    if (annotations.length === 0) return;
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    withHistory(ctx);
    useDocStore
      .getState()
      .setPages(ctx.tabId, replacePage(ctx, { ...ctx.page, annotations: [...ctx.page.annotations, ...annotations] }));
    set({ selection: annotations.map((annotation) => annotation.id) });
  },

  update: (ids, patch) => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const wanted = new Set(ids);
    if (!ctx.page.annotations.some((annotation) => wanted.has(annotation.id))) return;
    withHistory(ctx);
    const annotations = ctx.page.annotations.map((annotation) =>
      wanted.has(annotation.id) ? ({ ...annotation, ...patch } as Annotation) : annotation,
    );
    useDocStore.getState().setPages(ctx.tabId, replacePage(ctx, { ...ctx.page, annotations }));
  },

  transform: (fn) => get().mutate((annotations) => annotations.map(fn), false),

  mutate: (fn, history = true) => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const annotations = fn(ctx.page.annotations);
    if (annotations === ctx.page.annotations) return;
    if (history) withHistory(ctx);
    useDocStore.getState().setPages(ctx.tabId, replacePage(ctx, { ...ctx.page, annotations }));
    const alive = new Set(annotations.map((annotation) => annotation.id));
    const selection = get().selection.filter((id) => alive.has(id));
    if (selection.length !== get().selection.length) set({ selection });
  },

  remove: (ids) => {
    const wanted = new Set(ids);
    get().mutate((annotations) => annotations.filter((annotation) => !wanted.has(annotation.id)));
    set((state) => ({ selection: state.selection.filter((id) => !wanted.has(id)) }));
  },

  deleteSelection: () => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx || get().selection.length === 0) return;
    const wanted = new Set(get().selection);
    get().mutate((annotations) => annotations.filter((annotation) => !wanted.has(annotation.id)));
    set({ selection: [] });
  },

  duplicateSelection: () => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    const state = get();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const chosen = selectedAnnotations(state, ctx);
    if (chosen.length === 0) return;
    const groups = new Map<string, string>();
    const copies = chosen.map((annotation) => {
      const copy = structuredClone(annotation);
      copy.id = makeId("a");
      copy.locked = false;
      if (annotation.groupId) {
        const groupId = groups.get(annotation.groupId) ?? makeId("g");
        groups.set(annotation.groupId, groupId);
        copy.groupId = groupId;
      }
      return translateAnnotation(copy, 5, 5);
    });
    get().addMany(copies);
  },

  copySelection: () => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const chosen = selectedAnnotations(get(), ctx);
    if (chosen.length > 0) set({ clipboard: cloneAnnotations(chosen) });
  },
  cutSelection: () => {
    get().copySelection();
    get().deleteSelection();
  },
  paste: () => {
    const source = get().clipboard;
    if (source.length === 0) return;
    const groups = new Map<string, string>();
    const copies = source.map((annotation) => {
      const copy = structuredClone(annotation);
      copy.id = makeId("a");
      if (annotation.groupId) {
        const groupId = groups.get(annotation.groupId) ?? makeId("g");
        groups.set(annotation.groupId, groupId);
        copy.groupId = groupId;
      }
      return translateAnnotation(copy, 5, 5);
    });
    get().addMany(copies);
  },

  groupSelection: () => {
    const ids = new Set(get().selection);
    if (ids.size < 2) return;
    const groupId = makeId("g");
    get().mutate((annotations) =>
      annotations.map((annotation) => (ids.has(annotation.id) ? { ...annotation, groupId } : annotation)),
    );
  },
  ungroupSelection: () => {
    const ids = new Set(get().selection);
    if (ids.size === 0) return;
    get().mutate((annotations) =>
      annotations.map((annotation) => (ids.has(annotation.id) ? { ...annotation, groupId: null } : annotation)),
    );
  },
  setLocked: (locked) => {
    const ids = new Set(get().selection);
    if (ids.size === 0) return;
    get().mutate((annotations) =>
      annotations.map((annotation) => (ids.has(annotation.id) ? { ...annotation, locked } : annotation)),
    );
  },

  bringToFront: () => {
    const ids = new Set(get().selection);
    if (ids.size === 0) return;
    get().mutate((annotations) => [
      ...annotations.filter((annotation) => !ids.has(annotation.id)),
      ...annotations.filter((annotation) => ids.has(annotation.id)),
    ]);
  },
  sendToBack: () => {
    const ids = new Set(get().selection);
    if (ids.size === 0) return;
    get().mutate((annotations) => [
      ...annotations.filter((annotation) => ids.has(annotation.id)),
      ...annotations.filter((annotation) => !ids.has(annotation.id)),
    ]);
  },

  align: (mode) => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const chosen = selectedAnnotations(get(), ctx);
    if (chosen.length < 2) return;
    const outer = selectionBounds(chosen);
    if (!outer) return;
    const moves = new Map<string, Pt>();
    for (const annotation of chosen) {
      const box = annotationBounds(annotation);
      let dx = 0;
      let dy = 0;
      if (mode === "left") dx = outer.x - box.x;
      if (mode === "center") dx = outer.x + outer.width / 2 - (box.x + box.width / 2);
      if (mode === "right") dx = outer.x + outer.width - (box.x + box.width);
      if (mode === "top") dy = outer.y - box.y;
      if (mode === "middle") dy = outer.y + outer.height / 2 - (box.y + box.height / 2);
      if (mode === "bottom") dy = outer.y + outer.height - (box.y + box.height);
      moves.set(annotation.id, [dx, dy]);
    }
    get().mutate((annotations) =>
      annotations.map((annotation) => {
        const move = moves.get(annotation.id);
        return move ? translateAnnotation(annotation, move[0], move[1]) : annotation;
      }),
    );
  },

  distribute: (axis) => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const chosen = selectedAnnotations(get(), ctx);
    if (chosen.length < 3) return;
    const boxes = chosen.map((annotation) => ({ annotation, box: annotationBounds(annotation) }));
    const position = (box: BBox) => (axis === "x" ? box.x : box.y);
    const size = (box: BBox) => (axis === "x" ? box.width : box.height);
    boxes.sort((a, b) => position(a.box) - position(b.box));
    const first = boxes[0].box;
    const last = boxes[boxes.length - 1].box;
    const total = position(last) + size(last) - position(first);
    const used = boxes.reduce((sum, item) => sum + size(item.box), 0);
    const gap = (total - used) / (boxes.length - 1);
    let cursor = position(first);
    const moves = new Map<string, Pt>();
    for (const item of boxes) {
      const delta = cursor - position(item.box);
      moves.set(item.annotation.id, axis === "x" ? [delta, 0] : [0, delta]);
      cursor += size(item.box) + gap;
    }
    get().mutate((annotations) =>
      annotations.map((annotation) => {
        const move = moves.get(annotation.id);
        return move ? translateAnnotation(annotation, move[0], move[1]) : annotation;
      }),
    );
  },

  nudge: (dxMm, dyMm) => {
    const ids = new Set(get().selection);
    if (ids.size === 0) return;
    get().mutate((annotations) =>
      annotations.map((annotation) => (ids.has(annotation.id) ? translateAnnotation(annotation, dxMm, dyMm) : annotation)),
    );
  },

  eraseAt: (point, split, radiusMm = 2.5, recordHistory = true) => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return false;
    let changed = false;
    const next: Annotation[] = [];
    for (const annotation of ctx.page.annotations) {
      const locked = annotation.locked || annotation.hidden;
      if (locked || !hitTest(annotation, point, radiusMm)) {
        next.push(annotation);
        continue;
      }
      changed = true;
      if (!split || (annotation.type !== "pen" && annotation.type !== "marker")) continue;
      const runs = subtractEraser(annotation.points, point, radiusMm);
      if (runs.length === 0) continue;
      for (const run of runs) {
        const copy = structuredClone(annotation) as PenAnnotation | MarkerAnnotation;
        copy.id = makeId("a");
        copy.points = run;
        next.push(copy);
      }
    }
    if (changed) get().mutate(() => next, recordHistory);
    return changed;
  },

  scratchOut: (box) => {
    const ctx = activeContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const remaining = ctx.page.annotations.filter(
      (annotation) => annotation.locked || annotation.hidden || !boundsIntersect(annotationBounds(annotation), box),
    );
    if (remaining.length !== ctx.page.annotations.length) get().mutate(() => remaining);
  },
}));

export function applySelectionTransform(
  kind: "translate" | "scale" | "rotate" | "flip",
  payload: { dx?: number; dy?: number; origin?: Pt; sx?: number; sy?: number; degrees?: number; axis?: "h" | "v" },
): void {
  const transform = useAnnotationStore.getState().transform;
  transform((annotation) => {
    if (kind === "translate") return translateAnnotation(annotation, payload.dx ?? 0, payload.dy ?? 0);
    if (kind === "scale") return scaleAnnotation(annotation, payload.origin ?? [0, 0], payload.sx ?? 1, payload.sy ?? 1);
    if (kind === "rotate") return rotateAnnotation(annotation, payload.origin ?? [0, 0], payload.degrees ?? 0);
    return flipAnnotation(annotation, payload.axis ?? "h", payload.origin ?? [0, 0]);
  });
}

let lastKey = "";
useDocStore.subscribe((state) => {
  const key = `${state.activeId}:${state.page}`;
  if (key !== lastKey) {
    lastKey = key;
    if (useAnnotationStore.getState().selection.length > 0) useAnnotationStore.setState({ selection: [] });
  }
});

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { DocumentPage, DocumentTab } from "../lib/types";
import { useDocStore } from "./useDocStore";

function page(id: string, templateId = "paper-000"): DocumentPage {
  return { id, templateId, rotation: 0, annotations: [] };
}

function tabWith(pages: DocumentPage[]): DocumentTab {
  return {
    id: "doc-bookmarks",
    title: "Bookmarks",
    templateId: "paper-000",
    landscape: false,
    pageCount: pages.length,
    pages,
    pdf: { url: "", name: "doc.pdf", pageCount: pages.length, bookmarks: [] },
  };
}

function bookmarks() {
  return useDocStore.getState().tabs[0].pdf?.bookmarks ?? [];
}

describe("document store bookmarks", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    useDocStore.setState({
      tabs: [tabWith([page("p1"), page("p2"), page("p3")])],
      activeId: "doc-bookmarks",
      page: 1,
      history: {},
    });
  });

  it("adds a bookmark tied to the current page id", () => {
    useDocStore.getState().addBookmark("Start");
    expect(bookmarks()).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ title: "Start", pageIndex: 0, pageId: "p1", custom: true }),
    ]);
  });

  it("follows a reorder, a duplicate and a delete", () => {
    useDocStore.getState().addBookmark("p1");
    useDocStore.getState().reorderPage(0, 2);
    expect(bookmarks()[0]).toMatchObject({ pageIndex: 2, pageId: "p1" });

    useDocStore.getState().duplicatePage(0);
    expect(bookmarks()[0]).toMatchObject({ pageIndex: 3, pageId: "p1" });

    useDocStore.getState().deletePage(0);
    expect(bookmarks()[0]).toMatchObject({ pageIndex: 2, pageId: "p1" });
  });

  it("shifts a bookmark when a page is inserted above it", () => {
    useDocStore.setState({ page: 2 });
    useDocStore.getState().addBookmark("second");
    useDocStore.setState({ page: 1 });
    useDocStore.getState().addPage();
    expect(bookmarks()[0]).toMatchObject({ pageIndex: 2, pageId: "p2" });
  });
});

describe("closing the last tab", () => {
  it("replaces it with a fresh blank tab", () => {
    const blank: DocumentTab = {
      id: "doc-last",
      title: "Last",
      templateId: "paper-000",
      landscape: false,
      pageCount: 1,
      pages: [page("only")],
    };
    useDocStore.setState({ tabs: [blank], activeId: blank.id, page: 1, history: {}, lastClosedTab: null });

    useDocStore.getState().closeTab(blank.id);

    const state = useDocStore.getState();
    expect(state.tabs).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(state.tabs[0].id).not.toBe(blank.id);
    expect(state.activeId).toBe(state.tabs[0].id);
    expect(state.tabs[0].pages).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(state.tabs[0].pages[0].annotations).toEqual([]);
    expect(state.lastClosedTab).toBeNull();
  });
});

import { create } from "zustand";
import { DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID, paperById } from "../lib/data";
import { scaleAnnotation } from "../lib/annotationGeometry";
import { clamp } from "../lib/paper";
import { deleteWorkspaceBlob } from "../lib/storage";
import { ZOOM_STEPS } from "../lib/tools";
import type { DocumentPage, DocumentTab, PdfBookmark } from "../lib/types";
import { inspectPdf, loadSidecar, mergeSidecarPages, releasePdfDocument, type InspectedPdf } from "../lib/pdfEngine";
import { remapBookmarksOnPageOp, type PageOp } from "../lib/bookmarks";

const MILLIMETER_TEMPLATE_ID = "paper-103";

export function makeId(prefix: string): string {
  const random = typeof crypto !== "undefined" && "randomUUID" in crypto ? crypto.randomUUID() : Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
  return `${prefix}-${random}`;
}

export function newPage(templateId: string, annotations: DocumentPage["annotations"] = []): DocumentPage {
  return { id: makeId("page"), templateId, rotation: 0, annotations };
}

/** A clean blank document used when the last tab is closed. */
export function createInitialTab(): DocumentTab {
  return {
    id: makeId("doc"),
    title: "A4 Blank",
    templateId: DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID,
    landscape: false,
    pageCount: 1,
    pages: [newPage(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID)],
  };
}

const initialTabs: DocumentTab[] = [
  {
    id: "doc-a4",
    title: "A4 Blank",
    templateId: DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID,
    landscape: false,
    pageCount: 1,
    pages: [newPage(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID)],
  },
  {
    id: "doc-mm",
    title: "A4 Millimeter 1 mm",
    templateId: MILLIMETER_TEMPLATE_ID,
    landscape: false,
    pageCount: 1,
    pages: [newPage(MILLIMETER_TEMPLATE_ID)],
  },
];

export interface HistoryEntry {
  past: DocumentPage[][];
  future: DocumentPage[][];
}

interface DocState {
  tabs: DocumentTab[];
  activeId: string;
  zoom: number;
  fitMode: "page" | "width" | null;
  page: number;
  history: Record<string, HistoryEntry>;
  lastError: string | null;
  /** The tab closed most recently; kept for 15 s so Undo can put it back. */
  lastClosedTab: { tab: DocumentTab; index: number } | null;
  setActive: (id: string) => void;
  newTab: (templateId: string, title: string, landscape?: boolean) => void;
  closeTab: (id: string) => void;
  undoCloseTab: () => void;
  applyTemplate: (templateId: string, title: string, landscape?: boolean) => void;
  setLandscape: (landscape: boolean) => void;
  setZoom: (zoom: number) => void;
  zoomBy: (direction: 1 | -1) => void;
  setFit: (mode: "page" | "width" | null) => void;
  setPage: (page: number) => void;
  addPage: (templateId?: string) => void;
  duplicatePage: (index?: number) => void;
  rotatePage: (index?: number, delta?: number) => void;
  reorderPage: (from: number, to: number) => void;
  deletePage: (index?: number) => void;
  setPages: (tabId: string, pages: DocumentPage[]) => void;
  updatePage: (tabId: string, index: number, patch: Partial<DocumentPage>) => void;
  openPdfBytes: (bytes: Uint8Array, name: string) => Promise<void>;
  openPdfFile: (file: File) => Promise<void>;
  openPdfUrl: (url: string, name: string) => Promise<void>;
  addBookmark: (title: string) => void;
  restore: (tabs: DocumentTab[], activeId: string) => void;
  pushHistory: (tabId?: string) => void;
  undo: (tabId?: string) => void;
  redo: (tabId?: string) => void;
}

const HISTORY_LIMIT = 40;

/** Structural history stores page snapshots; every mutation replaces objects, so a shallow copy is enough. */
function clonePages(pages: DocumentPage[]): DocumentPage[] {
  return pages.map((page) => ({ ...page, annotations: page.annotations.slice() }));
}

function releaseTab(tab: DocumentTab): void {
  const urls = new Set<string>();
  if (tab.pdf?.url) urls.add(tab.pdf.url);
  for (const page of tab.pages) if (page.pdf?.url) urls.add(page.pdf.url);
  for (const url of urls) releasePdfDocument(url);
}

/** Permanent close: free the rendered document and its autosaved blob. */
function disposeTab(tab: DocumentTab, keepIds: string[]): void {
  releaseTab(tab);
  void deleteWorkspaceBlob(tab.id, keepIds);
}

let closeTimer = 0;

/** Applies a page op to the tab's bookmarks, keeping `pageId` authoritative. */
function withRemappedBookmarks(tab: DocumentTab, pages: DocumentPage[], op: PageOp): DocumentTab {
  if (!tab.pdf?.bookmarks.length) return tab;
  return { ...tab, pdf: { ...tab.pdf, bookmarks: remapBookmarksOnPageOp(tab.pdf.bookmarks, pages, op) } };
}

function withPageCount(tab: DocumentTab, pages: DocumentPage[]): DocumentTab {
  return { ...tab, pages, pageCount: pages.length };
}

function tabOf(state: DocState, tabId?: string): DocumentTab | undefined {
  return state.tabs.find((tab) => tab.id === (tabId ?? state.activeId));
}

function pdfPages(info: InspectedPdf, url: string): DocumentPage[] {
  return info.pages.map((size, index) => ({
    id: makeId("page"),
    templateId: DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID,
    rotation: 0 as const,
    annotations: [],
    pdf: { url, pageNumber: index + 1, widthMm: size.widthMm, heightMm: size.heightMm },
  }));
}

export const useDocStore = create<DocState>((set, get) => ({
  tabs: initialTabs,
  activeId: initialTabs[0].id,
  zoom: 0.8,
  fitMode: "page",
  page: 1,
  history: {},
  lastError: null,
  lastClosedTab: null,

  setActive: (activeId) => set({ activeId, fitMode: "page", zoom: 0.8, page: 1 }),
  newTab: (templateId, title, landscape = false) => {
    const tab: DocumentTab = {
      id: makeId("doc"),
      title,
      templateId,
      landscape,
      pageCount: 1,
      pages: [newPage(templateId)],
    };
    set((state) => ({ tabs: [...state.tabs, tab], activeId: tab.id, fitMode: "page", page: 1 }));
  },
  closeTab: (id) => {
    const { tabs, activeId, history, lastClosedTab } = get();
    const index = tabs.findIndex((tab) => tab.id === id);
    const closing = tabs[index];
    if (!closing) return;
    // The previous closed tab is now beyond undo: free its document and blob.
    if (lastClosedTab) disposeTab(lastClosedTab.tab, tabs.filter((tab) => tab.id !== lastClosedTab.tab.id).map((tab) => tab.id));
    const nextHistory = { ...history };
    delete nextHistory[id];

    // Closing the only tab never traps the user: it becomes a fresh blank tab.
    if (tabs.length === 1) {
      const fresh = createInitialTab();
      const annotated = closing.pages.some((page) => page.annotations.length > 0);
      set({
        tabs: [fresh],
        history: nextHistory,
        activeId: fresh.id,
        page: 1,
        lastClosedTab: annotated ? { tab: closing, index: 0 } : null,
      });
      if (!annotated) {
        disposeTab(closing, [fresh.id]);
        return;
      }
      if (closeTimer) globalThis.clearTimeout(closeTimer);
      closeTimer = globalThis.setTimeout(() => {
        closeTimer = 0;
        const state = get();
        if (state.lastClosedTab?.tab.id !== id) return;
        disposeTab(state.lastClosedTab.tab, state.tabs.map((tab) => tab.id));
        set({ lastClosedTab: null });
      }, 15000);
      return;
    }

    const next = tabs.filter((tab) => tab.id !== id);
    set({
      tabs: next,
      history: nextHistory,
      activeId: activeId === id ? next[Math.max(0, index - 1)].id : activeId,
      page: 1,
      lastClosedTab: { tab: closing, index },
    });
    if (closeTimer) globalThis.clearTimeout(closeTimer);
    closeTimer = globalThis.setTimeout(() => {
      closeTimer = 0;
      const state = get();
      if (state.lastClosedTab?.tab.id !== id) return;
      disposeTab(state.lastClosedTab.tab, state.tabs.map((tab) => tab.id));
      set({ lastClosedTab: null });
    }, 15000);
  },
  undoCloseTab: () => {
    const closed = get().lastClosedTab;
    if (!closed) return;
    if (closeTimer) {
      globalThis.clearTimeout(closeTimer);
      closeTimer = 0;
    }
    const tabs = [...get().tabs];
    tabs.splice(Math.min(closed.index, tabs.length), 0, closed.tab);
    set({ tabs, activeId: closed.tab.id, page: 1, lastClosedTab: null });
  },
  applyTemplate: (templateId, title, landscape = false) => {
    const state = get();
    const tab = tabOf(state);
    if (!tab) return;
    state.pushHistory(tab.id);
    const nextTemplate = paperById.get(templateId);
    const pages = tab.pages.map((page) => {
      if (page.pdf) return page;
      const previous = paperById.get(page.templateId);
      if (!nextTemplate || !previous) return { ...page, templateId };
      const scale = Math.min(
        1,
        nextTemplate.page.widthMm / previous.page.widthMm,
        nextTemplate.page.heightMm / previous.page.heightMm,
      );
      const annotations =
        scale < 1 ? page.annotations.map((annotation) => scaleAnnotation(annotation, [0, 0], scale, scale)) : page.annotations;
      return { ...page, templateId, annotations };
    });
    set((current) => ({
      tabs: current.tabs.map((item) =>
        item.id === tab.id
          ? withPageCount({ ...item, templateId, title: item.pdf ? item.title : title, landscape }, pages)
          : item,
      ),
      fitMode: "page",
    }));
  },
  setLandscape: (landscape) => {
    const tab = tabOf(get());
    if (!tab) return;
    get().pushHistory(tab.id);
    set((state) => ({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) => (item.id === tab.id ? { ...item, landscape } : item)),
      fitMode: "page",
    }));
  },
  setZoom: (zoom) => set({ zoom: clamp(zoom, 0.1, 6), fitMode: null }),
  zoomBy: (direction) => {
    const zoom = get().zoom;
    const next =
      direction === 1
        ? ZOOM_STEPS.find((step) => step > zoom + 0.001) ?? ZOOM_STEPS[ZOOM_STEPS.length - 1]
        : [...ZOOM_STEPS].reverse().find((step) => step < zoom - 0.001) ?? ZOOM_STEPS[0];
    set({ zoom: next, fitMode: null });
  },
  setFit: (fitMode) => set({ fitMode }),
  setPage: (page) => {
    const tab = tabOf(get());
    const next = clamp(page, 1, tab?.pages.length ?? 1);
    set({ page: next });
    // The continuous viewport listens for explicit navigation and scrolls there.
    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("pm:goto-page", { detail: { page: next } }));
    }
  },

  addPage: (templateId) => {
    const tab = tabOf(get());
    if (!tab) return;
    get().pushHistory(tab.id);
    const source = tab.pages[get().page - 1];
    const page = newPage(templateId ?? source?.templateId ?? tab.templateId);
    const pages = [...tab.pages];
    const index = get().page;
    pages.splice(index, 0, page);
    set((state) => ({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) =>
        item.id === tab.id
          ? withPageCount(withRemappedBookmarks(item, pages, { kind: "add", index }), pages)
          : item,
      ),
      page: index + 1,
    }));
  },
  duplicatePage: (index) => {
    const tab = tabOf(get());
    if (!tab) return;
    const sourceIndex = index ?? get().page - 1;
    const source = tab.pages[sourceIndex];
    if (!source) return;
    get().pushHistory(tab.id);
    const copy = structuredClone(source);
    copy.id = makeId("page");
    const groups = new Map<string, string>();
    copy.annotations = copy.annotations.map((annotation) => {
      const next = { ...annotation, id: makeId("a") };
      if (annotation.groupId) {
        const groupId = groups.get(annotation.groupId) ?? makeId("g");
        groups.set(annotation.groupId, groupId);
        next.groupId = groupId;
      }
      return next;
    });
    const pages = [...tab.pages];
    pages.splice(sourceIndex + 1, 0, copy);
    set((state) => ({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) =>
        item.id === tab.id
          ? withPageCount(withRemappedBookmarks(item, pages, { kind: "duplicate", index: sourceIndex }), pages)
          : item,
      ),
      page: sourceIndex + 2,
    }));
  },
  rotatePage: (index, delta = 90) => {
    const tab = tabOf(get());
    if (!tab) return;
    const target = index ?? get().page - 1;
    if (!tab.pages[target]) return;
    get().pushHistory(tab.id);
    const pages = tab.pages.map((page, pageIndex) =>
      pageIndex === target
        ? { ...page, rotation: (((page.rotation + delta) % 360 + 360) % 360) as DocumentPage["rotation"] }
        : page,
    );
    set((state) => ({ tabs: state.tabs.map((item) => (item.id === tab.id ? withPageCount(item, pages) : item)) }));
  },
  reorderPage: (from, to) => {
    const tab = tabOf(get());
    if (!tab || from === to || !tab.pages[from] || to < 0 || to >= tab.pages.length) return;
    get().pushHistory(tab.id);
    const pages = [...tab.pages];
    const [moved] = pages.splice(from, 1);
    pages.splice(to, 0, moved);
    set((state) => ({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) =>
        item.id === tab.id
          ? withPageCount(withRemappedBookmarks(item, pages, { kind: "reorder", from, to }), pages)
          : item,
      ),
      page: to + 1,
    }));
  },
  deletePage: (index) => {
    const tab = tabOf(get());
    if (!tab || tab.pages.length <= 1) return;
    const target = index ?? get().page - 1;
    if (!tab.pages[target]) return;
    get().pushHistory(tab.id);
    const pages = tab.pages.filter((_, pageIndex) => pageIndex !== target);
    set((state) => ({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) =>
        item.id === tab.id
          ? withPageCount(withRemappedBookmarks(item, pages, { kind: "delete", index: target }), pages)
          : item,
      ),
      page: clamp(get().page, 1, pages.length),
    }));
  },

  setPages: (tabId, pages) => {
    set((state) => ({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) => (item.id === tabId ? withPageCount(item, pages) : item)),
      page: clamp(get().page, 1, pages.length),
    }));
  },
  updatePage: (tabId, index, patch) => {
    set((state) => ({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) =>
        item.id === tabId
          ? withPageCount(item, item.pages.map((page, pageIndex) => (pageIndex === index ? { ...page, ...patch } : page)))
          : item,
      ),
    }));
  },

  openPdfBytes: async (bytes, name) => {
    const stored = bytes.slice();
    set({ lastError: null });
    let url = "";
    try {
      url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([new Uint8Array(stored)], { type: "application/pdf" }));
      const [info, sidecar] = await Promise.all([inspectPdf(stored), loadSidecar(stored).catch(() => null)]);
      const pages = mergeSidecarPages(pdfPages(info, url), sidecar);
      const title = name.replace(/\.pdf$/i, "");
      // The sidecar carries outline entries across an export round-trip, including custom ones.
      const bookmarks: PdfBookmark[] = (
        sidecar?.bookmarks?.length
          ? sidecar.bookmarks
          : info.bookmarks.map((entry) => ({
              id: makeId("bm"),
              title: entry.title,
              pageIndex: entry.pageIndex,
              depth: entry.depth,
            }))
      ).map((entry) => ({ ...entry, id: entry.id || makeId("bm"), pageId: pages[entry.pageIndex]?.id }));
      const tab: DocumentTab = {
        id: makeId("doc"),
        title,
        templateId: DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID,
        landscape: (pages[0]?.pdf?.widthMm ?? 210) > (pages[0]?.pdf?.heightMm ?? 297),
        pageCount: pages.length,
        pages,
        pdf: {
          url,
          name,
          pageCount: pages.length,
          bytes: stored,
          bookmarks,
        },
      };
      set((state) => ({ tabs: [...state.tabs, tab], activeId: tab.id, page: 1, fitMode: "page", zoom: 0.8 }));
    } catch (error) {
      if (url) URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      set({ lastError: `Could not open ${name}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });
    }
  },
  openPdfFile: async (file) => {
    await get().openPdfBytes(new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()), file.name);
  },
  openPdfUrl: async (url, name) => {
    set({ lastError: null });
    try {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
      await get().openPdfBytes(new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer()), name);
    } catch (error) {
      set({ lastError: `Could not open ${name}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });
    }
  },

  addBookmark: (title) => {
    const state = get();
    const tab = tabOf(state);
    if (!tab?.pdf) return;
    const bookmark = {
      id: makeId("bm"),
      title,
      pageIndex: state.page - 1,
      depth: 0,
      pageId: tab.pages[state.page - 1]?.id,
      custom: true,
    };
    set({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) =>
        item.id === tab.id && item.pdf ? { ...item, pdf: { ...item.pdf, bookmarks: [...item.pdf.bookmarks, bookmark] } } : item,
      ),
    });
  },

  restore: (tabs, activeId) => {    const previousTabs = get().tabs;
    const restored = tabs.map((tab) => {
      const pages = tab.pages?.length ? tab.pages : [newPage(tab.templateId)];
      if (!tab.pdf?.bytes) return { ...tab, pages, pageCount: pages.length };
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([new Uint8Array(tab.pdf.bytes)], { type: "application/pdf" }));
      return {
        ...tab,
        pageCount: pages.length,
        pages: pages.map((page) => (page.pdf ? { ...page, pdf: { ...page.pdf, url } } : page)),
        pdf: { ...tab.pdf, url },
      };
    });
    for (const tab of previousTabs) releaseTab(tab);
    set({
      tabs: restored,
      activeId: restored.some((tab) => tab.id === activeId) ? activeId : restored[0].id,
      page: 1,
      fitMode: "page",
      zoom: 0.8,
      lastClosedTab: null,
    });
  },

  pushHistory: (tabId) => {
    const state = get();
    const tab = tabOf(state, tabId);
    if (!tab) return;
    const entry = state.history[tab.id] ?? { past: [], future: [] };
    const past = [...entry.past, clonePages(tab.pages)].slice(-HISTORY_LIMIT);
    set({ history: { ...state.history, [tab.id]: { past, future: [] } } });
  },
  undo: (tabId) => {
    const state = get();
    const tab = tabOf(state, tabId);
    if (!tab) return;
    const entry = state.history[tab.id];
    const previous = entry?.past[entry.past.length - 1];
    if (!entry || !previous) return;
    set({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) => (item.id === tab.id ? withPageCount(item, previous) : item)),
      history: {
        ...state.history,
        [tab.id]: { past: entry.past.slice(0, -1), future: [clonePages(tab.pages), ...entry.future] },
      },
      page: clamp(state.page, 1, previous.length),
    });
  },
  redo: (tabId) => {
    const state = get();
    const tab = tabOf(state, tabId);
    if (!tab) return;
    const entry = state.history[tab.id];
    const next = entry?.future[0];
    if (!entry || !next) return;
    set({
      tabs: state.tabs.map((item) => (item.id === tab.id ? withPageCount(item, next) : item)),
      history: {
        ...state.history,
        [tab.id]: { past: [...entry.past, clonePages(tab.pages)], future: entry.future.slice(1) },
      },
      page: clamp(state.page, 1, next.length),
    });
  },
}));

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { applyWorkspaceStores, collectWorkspaceStores, parseWorkspaceBackup, workspaceBackup } from "../lib/storage";
import type { DocumentTab } from "../lib/types";
import { DEFAULT_FAVORITES, useFavoritesStore } from "./useFavoritesStore";

const tabs: DocumentTab[] = [
  {
    id: "doc-a",
    title: "A4 Blank",
    templateId: "paper-000",
    landscape: false,
    pageCount: 1,
    pages: [{ id: "page-a", templateId: "paper-000", rotation: 0, annotations: [] }],
  },
];

beforeEach(() => {
  useFavoritesStore.getState().restore(DEFAULT_FAVORITES);
});

describe("favourite tools", () => {
  it("starts with the ten wireframe shortcuts", () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_FAVORITES).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(useFavoritesStore.getState().favorites.map((item) => item.tool)).toEqual([
      "pen", "pen", "pen", "marker", "marker", "marker", "text", "line", "arrow", "arrow",
    ]);
  });

  it("adds and removes a favourite", () => {
    useFavoritesStore.getState().add({
      label: "Signature pen",
      tool: "pen",
      penStyle: "calligraphy",
      props: { color: "#123456", thicknessMm: 2.4, pressureLevel: 85 },
    });
    const added = useFavoritesStore.getState().favorites.find((item) => item.label === "Signature pen");
    expect(added).toMatchObject({ tool: "pen", penStyle: "calligraphy", props: { color: "#123456", thicknessMm: 2.4 } });
    expect(useFavoritesStore.getState().favorites).toHaveLength(11);

    useFavoritesStore.getState().remove(added?.id ?? "");
    expect(useFavoritesStore.getState().favorites).toHaveLength(10);
  });

  it("travels inside the workspace backup", async () => {
    const favorites = [
      ...DEFAULT_FAVORITES,
      { id: "fav-signature", label: "Signature pen", tool: "pen" as const, penStyle: "calligraphy" as const, props: { color: "#123456", thicknessMm: 2.4 } },
    ];
    const parsed = parseWorkspaceBackup(await workspaceBackup(tabs, "doc-a", { ...collectWorkspaceStores(), favorites }).text());
    expect(parsed?.stores?.favorites).toHaveLength(11);

    useFavoritesStore.getState().restore([]);
    applyWorkspaceStores(parsed?.stores);
    const restored = useFavoritesStore.getState().favorites;
    expect(restored).toHaveLength(11);
    expect(restored.find((item) => item.id === "fav-signature")?.props.color).toBe("#123456");
    expect(restored.find((item) => item.id === "fav-signature")?.penStyle).toBe("calligraphy");
  });
});
