import { create } from "zustand";
import type { PenStyle } from "../lib/annotationTypes";
import type { ToolId, ToolProps } from "../lib/types";

export interface FavoriteTool {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  tool: ToolId;
  props: Partial<ToolProps>;
  penStyle?: PenStyle;
}

const KEY = "pm.favoriteTools";

/** The ten shortcuts the Phase 7 wireframe starts with. */
export const DEFAULT_FAVORITES: FavoriteTool[] = [
  { id: "fav-thin-blue", label: "Thin Blue Pen", tool: "pen", penStyle: "pro", props: { color: "#0044FF", thicknessMm: 0.7, opacity: 1, pressureSensitive: true, pressureLevel: 55 } },
  { id: "fav-medium-blue", label: "Medium Blue Pen", tool: "pen", penStyle: "pro", props: { color: "#0044FF", thicknessMm: 1.5, opacity: 1, pressureSensitive: true, pressureLevel: 55 } },
  { id: "fav-thick-blue", label: "Thick Blue Pen", tool: "pen", penStyle: "calligraphy", props: { color: "#0044FF", thicknessMm: 3, opacity: 1, pressureSensitive: true, pressureLevel: 85, smoothing: true } },
  { id: "fav-yellow", label: "Yellow Highlighter", tool: "marker", props: { color: "#FFE600", thicknessMm: 10, opacity: 0.4 } },
  { id: "fav-cyan", label: "Cyan Highlighter", tool: "marker", props: { color: "#00D4FF", thicknessMm: 10, opacity: 0.4 } },
  { id: "fav-green", label: "Green Highlighter", tool: "marker", props: { color: "#10B981", thicknessMm: 10, opacity: 0.4 } },
  { id: "fav-text", label: "Text Tool", tool: "text", props: { color: "#000000", fontSize: 12 } },
  { id: "fav-blue-line", label: "Thin Blue Line", tool: "line", props: { color: "#0044FF", thicknessMm: 1, lineStyle: "solid" } },
  { id: "fav-blue-arrow", label: "Blue Arrow", tool: "arrow", props: { color: "#0044FF", thicknessMm: 1.2, arrowHead: 5, arrowTail: 0 } },
  { id: "fav-amber-arrow", label: "Amber Arrow", tool: "arrow", props: { color: "#F59E0B", thicknessMm: 1.2, arrowHead: 5, arrowTail: 0 } },
];

function readFavorites(): FavoriteTool[] {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return DEFAULT_FAVORITES;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_FAVORITES;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as FavoriteTool[];
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((item) => item?.id && item?.tool && item?.label) : DEFAULT_FAVORITES;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_FAVORITES;
  }
}

function persist(favorites: FavoriteTool[]): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(favorites));
  } catch {
    // Quota exceeded: the dock stays usable for this session.
  }
}

interface FavoritesState {
  favorites: FavoriteTool[];
  add: (favorite: Omit<FavoriteTool, "id">) => void;
  remove: (id: string) => void;
  /** Replaces the dock from a workspace backup. */
  restore: (favorites: FavoriteTool[]) => void;
}

export const useFavoritesStore = create<FavoritesState>((set, get) => ({
  favorites: readFavorites(),
  add: (favorite) => {
    const created: FavoriteTool = {
      ...favorite,
      id: `fav-${typeof crypto !== "undefined" && "randomUUID" in crypto ? crypto.randomUUID() : Date.now().toString(36)}`,
    };
    const favorites = [...get().favorites, created];
    persist(favorites);
    set({ favorites });
  },
  remove: (id) => {
    const favorites = get().favorites.filter((item) => item.id !== id);
    persist(favorites);
    set({ favorites });
  },
  restore: (favorites) => {
    const valid = favorites.filter((item) => item?.id && item?.tool && item?.label);
    persist(valid);
    set({ favorites: valid });
  },
}));

================================================================================
FILE: src\stores\useToolStore.ts (2.9 KB)
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { presets, stamps } from "../lib/data";
import { BASE_PROPS, presetPatch, presetsFor } from "../lib/presets";
import { applyToolPatch } from "../lib/sync";
import { isShapeTool, TOOLS } from "../lib/tools";
import type { Preset, ShapeTool, ToolId, ToolProps } from "../lib/types";

function initialProps(): Record<ToolId, ToolProps> {
  const map = {} as Record<ToolId, ToolProps>;
  for (const tool of TOOLS) {
    const factory = presetsFor(tool.id, presets)[0];
    map[tool.id] = { ...BASE_PROPS, ...(factory ? presetPatch(factory) : {}) };
  }
  return map;
}

function stampPresetId(preset: Preset): string | null {
  if (preset.family !== "stamp") return null;
  return stamps.find((stamp) => stamp.title.en === preset.title)?.id ?? null;
}

interface ToolState {
  activeTool: ToolId;
  /** The shape the Shapes rail button reopens; remembers the last one picked. */
  lastShapeTool: ShapeTool;
  props: Record<ToolId, ToolProps>;
  sync: boolean;
  activePreset: Partial<Record<ToolId, string>>;
  selectedStampId: string | null;
  recentStamps: string[];
  setTool: (tool: ToolId) => void;
  setProps: (patch: Partial<ToolProps>) => void;
  setSync: (sync: boolean) => void;
  selectPreset: (preset: Preset) => void;
  selectStamp: (stampId: string) => void;
  /** Switches the stamp tool to an id without touching the recent list. */
  setStamp: (stampId: string) => void;
}

export const useToolStore = create<ToolState>((set, get) => ({
  activeTool: "pen",
  lastShapeTool: "rectangle",
  props: initialProps(),
  sync: false,
  activePreset: {},
  selectedStampId: stamps[0]?.id ?? null,
  recentStamps: [],
  setTool: (activeTool) =>
    set(isShapeTool(activeTool) ? { activeTool, lastShapeTool: activeTool } : { activeTool }),
  setProps: (patch) => {
    const { props, activeTool, sync } = get();
    set({ props: applyToolPatch(props, activeTool, patch, sync), activePreset: { ...get().activePreset, [activeTool]: "" } });
  },
  setSync: (sync) => set({ sync }),
  selectPreset: (preset) => {
    const { props, sync } = get();
    // Graphical presets are filtered by type, so they always belong to the active shape tool.
    const target: ToolId = preset.family === "graphical" ? get().activeTool : (preset.family as ToolId);
    const stampId = stampPresetId(preset);
    set({
      props: applyToolPatch(props, target, presetPatch(preset), sync),
      activePreset: { ...get().activePreset, [target]: preset.id },
      selectedStampId: stampId ?? get().selectedStampId,
    });
  },
  selectStamp: (selectedStampId) =>
    set((state) => ({
      selectedStampId,
      activeTool: "stamp",
      recentStamps: [selectedStampId, ...state.recentStamps.filter((id) => id !== selectedStampId)].slice(0, 8),
    })),
  setStamp: (selectedStampId) => set({ selectedStampId }),
}));

FILE: src\stores\useUIStore.ts (5.2 KB)
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import type { ThemeName } from "../lib/types";

const THEMES: ThemeName[] = ["light", "dark", "sepia"];
const stored = typeof localStorage === "undefined" ? null : localStorage.getItem("pa.theme");
const systemDark = typeof matchMedia !== "undefined" && matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;
const initialTheme: ThemeName =
  stored && THEMES.includes(stored as ThemeName) ? (stored as ThemeName) : systemDark ? "dark" : "light";

if (typeof document !== "undefined") document.documentElement.dataset.theme = initialTheme;

export type SidebarTab = "pages" | "bookmarks" | "annotations" | "stamps";

export type MeasureModeUi = "distance" | "perimeter" | "area" | "angle";

export type ViewMode = "one-page" | "one-continuous" | "two-page" | "two-continuous";

export type GridStyle = "none" | "squared-5" | "squared-10" | "ruled-8" | "dots-5" | "millimeter";

interface UIState {
  theme: ThemeName;
  sidebarOpen: boolean;
  sidebarTab: SidebarTab;
  drawerPinned: boolean;
  favoritesOpen: boolean;
  toolPopoverOpen: boolean;
  toolPopoverPinned: boolean;
  auxLines: boolean;
  paletteOpen: boolean;
  libraryOpen: boolean;
  newDocOpen: boolean;
  presentation: boolean;
  settingsOpen: boolean;
  measureMode: MeasureModeUi;
  toast: string | null;
  /** Optional action rendered as an Undo button inside the toast. */
  toastAction: (() => void) | null;
  saving: boolean;
  eraserSplit: boolean;
  eraserSizeMm: number;
  viewMode: ViewMode;
  separateCoverPage: boolean;
  gridStyle: GridStyle;
  searchOpen: boolean;
  setViewMode: (mode: ViewMode) => void;
  toggleSeparateCoverPage: () => void;
  setGridStyle: (style: GridStyle) => void;
  setSearchOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  setEraserSplit: (split: boolean) => void;
  setEraserSize: (sizeMm: number) => void;
  setTheme: (theme: ThemeName) => void;
  toggleSidebar: () => void;
  setSidebarTab: (tab: SidebarTab) => void;
  toggleSidebarTab: (tab: SidebarTab) => void;
  toggleDrawerPinned: () => void;
  toggleFavorites: () => void;
  setFavoritesOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  openToolPopover: () => void;
  closeToolPopover: () => void;
  toggleToolPopover: () => void;
  toggleToolPopoverPinned: () => void;
  toggleAuxLines: () => void;
  setPaletteOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  setLibraryOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  setNewDocOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  setPresentation: (on: boolean) => void;
  setSettingsOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  setMeasureMode: (mode: MeasureModeUi) => void;
  setToast: (toast: string | null, action?: (() => void) | null) => void;
  setSaving: (saving: boolean) => void;
}

export const useUIStore = create<UIState>((set) => ({
  theme: initialTheme,
  sidebarOpen: false,
  sidebarTab: "pages",
  drawerPinned: false,
  favoritesOpen: true,
  toolPopoverOpen: true,
  toolPopoverPinned: false,
  auxLines: false,
  paletteOpen: false,
  libraryOpen: false,
  newDocOpen: false,
  presentation: false,
  settingsOpen: false,
  measureMode: "distance",
  toast: null,
  toastAction: null,
  saving: false,
  eraserSplit: false,
  eraserSizeMm: 5.2,
  viewMode: "one-continuous",
  separateCoverPage: false,
  gridStyle: "none",
  searchOpen: false,
  setViewMode: (viewMode) => set({ viewMode }),
  toggleSeparateCoverPage: () => set((state) => ({ separateCoverPage: !state.separateCoverPage })),
  setGridStyle: (gridStyle) => set({ gridStyle }),
  setSearchOpen: (searchOpen) => set({ searchOpen }),
  setEraserSplit: (eraserSplit) => set({ eraserSplit }),
  setEraserSize: (eraserSizeMm) => set({ eraserSizeMm }),
  setSaving: (saving) => set({ saving }),
  setTheme: (theme) => {
    if (typeof document !== "undefined") document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.setItem("pa.theme", theme);
    set({ theme });
  },
  toggleSidebar: () => set((state) => ({ sidebarOpen: !state.sidebarOpen })),
  setSidebarTab: (sidebarTab) => set({ sidebarTab, sidebarOpen: true }),
  toggleSidebarTab: (sidebarTab) =>
    set((state) => ({
      sidebarTab,
      sidebarOpen: state.sidebarOpen && state.sidebarTab === sidebarTab ? false : true,
    })),
  toggleDrawerPinned: () => set((state) => ({ drawerPinned: !state.drawerPinned })),
  toggleFavorites: () => set((state) => ({ favoritesOpen: !state.favoritesOpen })),
  setFavoritesOpen: (favoritesOpen) => set({ favoritesOpen }),
  openToolPopover: () => set({ toolPopoverOpen: true }),
  closeToolPopover: () => set({ toolPopoverOpen: false }),
  toggleToolPopover: () => set((state) => ({ toolPopoverOpen: !state.toolPopoverOpen })),
  toggleToolPopoverPinned: () => set((state) => ({ toolPopoverPinned: !state.toolPopoverPinned })),
  toggleAuxLines: () => set((state) => ({ auxLines: !state.auxLines })),
  setPaletteOpen: (paletteOpen) => set({ paletteOpen }),
  setLibraryOpen: (libraryOpen) => set({ libraryOpen }),
  setNewDocOpen: (newDocOpen) => set({ newDocOpen }),
  setPresentation: (presentation) => set({ presentation }),
  setSettingsOpen: (settingsOpen) => set({ settingsOpen }),
  setMeasureMode: (measureMode) => set({ measureMode }),
  setToast: (toast, action = null) => set({ toast, toastAction: toast ? action : null }),
}));

FILE: src-tauri\src\main.rs (2.6 KB)
================================================================================
#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

/// Percent-encodes a value for the boot query; spaces become `+` so a paper name
/// such as `A4 Landscape` survives the trip into the webview.
fn encode(value: &str) -> String {
    value
        .bytes()
        .map(|byte| match byte {
            b'A'..=b'Z' | b'a'..=b'z' | b'0'..=b'9' | b'-' | b'_' | b'.' | b'~' => (byte as char).to_string(),
            b' ' => "+".to_string(),
            other => format!("%{other:02X}"),
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Maps `--new "A4 Landscape" --fullscreen --print` onto the same query contract
/// the browser build reads (`?new=A4+Landscape&fullscreen=1&print=1`).
fn boot_query(args: &[String]) -> String {
    let mut query: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut index = 0;
    while index < args.len() {
        let trimmed = args[index].trim_start_matches('-');
        let (name, inline) = match trimmed.split_once(['=', ':']) {
            Some((name, value)) => (name, Some(value.to_string())),
            None => (trimmed, None),
        };
        match name.to_lowercase().as_str() {
            "new" => {
                let value = match inline {
                    Some(value) => Some(value),
                    None if index + 1 < args.len() => {
                        index += 1;
                        Some(args[index].clone())
                    }
                    None => None,
                };
                if let Some(value) = value.filter(|value| !value.trim().is_empty()) {
                    query.push(format!("new={}", encode(value.trim())));
                }
            }
            "fullscreen" | "print" => {
                let on = inline.map(|value| value != "0").unwrap_or(true);
                if on {
                    query.push(format!("{name}=1"));
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        index += 1;
    }
    query.join("&")
}

fn main() {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    let query = boot_query(&args);

    tauri::Builder::default()
        .setup(move |app| {
            if query.is_empty() {
                return Ok(());
            }
            if let Some(window) = tauri::Manager::get_webview_window(app, "main") {
                if let Ok(mut url) = window.url() {
                    url.set_query(Some(&query));
                    window.navigate(url)?;
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        })
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while running PDF Master");
}
